############################################################################################
# DATEI 001/107: scripts/audit-all-de-cardmarket-prices.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Audit all German physical variants against the official Cardmarket price guide.

Bulk stage only: catalogue-level asking/trend data is never an independently
verified German/NM/edition/stamp-specific price. Preserve production values.
"""
import argparse,collections,json,subprocess
from datetime import datetime,timezone
from pathlib import Path
from price_feed_client import fetch_json

URL="https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/priceGuide/price_guide_6.json"

def db(sql):
    p=subprocess.run(["docker","exec","-i","cardyssey-db","psql","-X","-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1",
                      "-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-At"],input=sql,
                     capture_output=True,text=True,check=True,timeout=120)
    return p.stdout.strip()

def quote(v):
    return "'"+str(v).replace("'","''")+"'"

DDL="""
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cardmarket_de_variant_price_audit (
 card_id text NOT NULL,
 variant_id text NOT NULL,
 product_ids jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
 selected_product_id text,
 status text NOT NULL,
 guide_key text,
 guide_value_eur numeric(14,4),
 prior_source text,
 prior_value_eur numeric(14,4),
 is_special boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
 checked_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
 PRIMARY KEY(card_id,variant_id)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS cardmarket_de_variant_price_audit_status
 ON cardmarket_de_variant_price_audit(status,checked_at);
"""

def select_value(guide,is_holo):
    keys=("trend-holo","avg7-holo","avg30-holo","avg-holo") if is_holo else ("trend","avg7","avg30","avg")
    for key in keys:
        value=guide.get(key)
        try:
            price=float(value)
            if 0<price<1e7:return key,round(price,4)
        except (ValueError,TypeError):pass
    return None,None

def calculate(rows,prices):
    results=[]
    for row in rows:
        candidates=row.get("product_ids") or []
        variant=row.get("variant") or {}
        special=bool(variant.get("foil") or variant.get("stamps") or variant.get("subtype"))
        foil=(str(variant.get("type") or "").lower() in ("holo","reverse") or
              str(variant.get("legacyVariant") or row.get("legacy_variant") or "").upper() in ("HOLO","REVERSE") or
              bool(variant.get("foil")))
        status="NO_VERIFIED_EXPANSION_OR_NAME"
        selected=None
        key=None
        value=None
        if len(candidates)>1:
            status="MULTIPLE_PRODUCT_CANDIDATES"
        elif len(candidates)==1:
            selected=str(candidates[0])
            guide=prices.get(selected)
            if guide is None:status="PRODUCT_HAS_NO_GUIDE"
            else:
                key,value=select_value(guide,foil)
                if value is None:status="GUIDE_VALUE_MISSING_FOR_PRINT"
                elif special:status="SPECIAL_PRINT_IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED"
                elif foil and row.get("variant_count",0)>1:
                    status="HOLO_REVERSE_SPLIT_UNVERIFIED"
                else:status="GUIDE_CANDIDATE_NOT_LANGUAGE_NM_VERIFIED"
        results.append(dict(card_id=row["card_id"],variant_id=row["variant_id"],
                            product_ids=candidates,selected_product_id=selected,
                            status=status,guide_key=key,guide_value_eur=value,
                            prior_source=row.get("source"),prior_value_eur=row.get("price"),
                            is_special=special))
    return results

def apply(results):
    db(DDL)
    for i in range(0,len(results),400):
        payload=quote(json.dumps(results[i:i+400],ensure_ascii=False,separators=(",",":")))
        db(f"""
WITH incoming AS (SELECT jsonb_array_elements({payload}::jsonb) AS x),
fields AS (
 SELECT x->>'card_id' AS card_id,x->>'variant_id' AS variant_id,
 x->'product_ids' AS product_ids,x->>'selected_product_id' AS selected_product_id,
 x->>'status' AS status,x->>'guide_key' AS guide_key,
 (x->>'guide_value_eur')::numeric AS guide_value_eur,
 x->>'prior_source' AS prior_source,(x->>'prior_value_eur')::numeric AS prior_value_eur,
 (x->>'is_special')::boolean AS is_special FROM incoming)
INSERT INTO cardmarket_de_variant_price_audit
(card_id,variant_id,product_ids,selected_product_id,status,guide_key,
 guide_value_eur,prior_source,prior_value_eur,is_special)
SELECT card_id,variant_id,product_ids,selected_product_id,status,guide_key,
 guide_value_eur,prior_source,prior_value_eur,is_special FROM fields
ON CONFLICT(card_id,variant_id) DO UPDATE SET
 product_ids=EXCLUDED.product_ids,selected_product_id=EXCLUDED.selected_product_id,
 status=EXCLUDED.status,guide_key=EXCLUDED.guide_key,
 guide_value_eur=EXCLUDED.guide_value_eur,prior_source=EXCLUDED.prior_source,
 prior_value_eur=EXCLUDED.prior_value_eur,is_special=EXCLUDED.is_special,
 checked_at=NOW();""")

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--apply",action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--report",type=Path,default=Path("/tmp/cardyssey-de-full-price-audit.json"))
    args=p.parse_args()
    guide=fetch_json(URL)
    prices={str(g.get("idProduct")):g for g in guide.get("priceGuides") or []}
    query="""
SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(x)), '[]'::jsonb) FROM (
 SELECT p.card_id,p.variant_id,p.legacy_variant,p.source,p.base_eur AS price,
        COALESCE(link.provider_product_ids,'[]'::jsonb) AS product_ids,
        jsonb_array_length(c.variants) AS variant_count,
        COALESCE((SELECT v FROM jsonb_array_elements(c.variants) AS v
            WHERE v->>'id'=p.variant_id LIMIT 1),'{}'::jsonb) AS variant
 FROM tcg_price_cache p
 JOIN catalog_cards_mirror c ON c.card_id=p.card_id AND c.language_code=p.language_code
 LEFT JOIN cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates link ON link.card_id=p.card_id
  AND link.language_code=p.language_code
 WHERE p.language_code='de'
) x;"""
    rows=json.loads(db(query))
    result=calculate(rows,prices)
    if args.apply:apply(result)
    counts=dict(collections.Counter(x["status"] for x in result))
    report={"checked_at":datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            "variants":len(result),"guide_products":len(prices),
            "statuses":counts,"candidate_with_price":sum(x["guide_value_eur"] is not None for x in result),
            "production_prices_changed":0,
            "warning":"Official guide is not filtered by German NM nor print-specific; candidate pricing staged only."}
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False))
if __name__=="__main__":main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 002/107: scripts/audit-cardmarket-catalog-crossrefs.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Verify external variant IDs against the official Cardmarket product catalogue.

Never promote equal idMetacard to equal physical printing: two expansions can
contain the same underlying meta-card. Writes a full read-only review report.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import json
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path


def crosscheck(cases, products):
    catalog = {str(p.get("idProduct")): p for p in products}
    results = []
    for row in cases:
        candidates = [catalog.get(str(p.get("id"))) for p in row.get("products") or []]
        candidates = [p for p in candidates if p]
        direct_id = str((row.get("exact_variant_provider_ids") or {}).get("cardmarket") or "")
        direct = catalog.get(direct_id) if direct_id else None
        same_ids = [p for p in candidates if str(p.get("idProduct")) == direct_id]
        related_metacards = [p for p in candidates if direct and p.get("idMetacard") is not None and p.get("idMetacard") == direct.get("idMetacard")]
        if not direct_id:
            status = "NO_DIRECT_VARIANT_ID"
        elif direct is None:
            status = "DIRECT_ID_NOT_IN_CURRENT_CATALOG"
        elif same_ids:
            status = "DIRECT_ID_IN_SET_REVIEW_PRINT"
        elif related_metacards:
            status = "SAME_METACARD_DIFFERENT_PRODUCT_OR_EXPANSION"
        else:
            status = "DIRECT_ID_UNRELATED_TO_SET_CANDIDATES"
        results.append({
            "language": row.get("language", ""), "card_id": row.get("card_id", ""),
            "variant_id": row.get("variant_id", ""), "set": row.get("set_code", ""),
            "direct_product_id": direct_id, "direct_expansion": direct.get("idExpansion") if direct else "",
            "direct_metacard": direct.get("idMetacard") if direct else "",
            "candidate_products": ",".join(str(p.get("idProduct")) for p in candidates),
            "candidate_expansions": ",".join(str(p.get("idExpansion")) for p in candidates),
            "same_metacard_products": ",".join(str(p.get("idProduct")) for p in related_metacards),
            "crossref_status": status, "safe_to_assign_product_id": False,
        })
    summary = {"inspected": len(results), "by_status": dict(Counter(r["crossref_status"] for r in results)),
               "automatically_assigned": 0, "note": "idMetacard proves a catalogue family, not expansion, printed number, language or foil"}
    return results, summary


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--input", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
        from price_feed_client import fetch_json
    except ImportError:
        from scripts.price_feed_client import fetch_json
    url = "https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json"
    products = fetch_json(url, headers={"User-Agent": "Cardyssey/0.4 catalog-crossref"}).get("products") or []
    cases = json.loads(args.input.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["unresolved"]
    results, summary = crosscheck(cases, products)
    args.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with (args.output_dir / "de_cardmarket_catalog_crossrefs.csv").open("w", encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(results[0])); w.writeheader(); w.writerows(results)
    (args.output_dir / "de_cardmarket_catalog_crossrefs.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 003/107: scripts/audit-cardmarket-feed-first.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Audit fast Cardmarket guide coverage without publishing unverified prices.

Guide is European product/finish aggregate, NOT language-, edition-, condition-
or stamp-specific. Candidate IDs are never silently promoted to verified IDs.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import json
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

PRICE_URL='https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/priceGuide/price_guide_6.json'
DEFAULT_CACHE=Path('/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/price-feeds')/(hashlib.sha256(PRICE_URL.encode()).hexdigest()+'.json')


def classify(row, guide):
    ids=sorted({x.strip() for x in str(row.get('cardmarket_ids') or '').split(',') if x.strip().isdigit()})
    special=bool(str(row.get('sonderdruck') or '').strip() or str(row.get('foil') or '').strip() or str(row.get('stempel') or '').strip() not in ('','[]','null'))
    finish=str(row.get('drucktyp') or '').strip().casefold()
    bucket='avg30-holo' if finish=='reverse' else 'avg30' if finish in ('normal','holo') else None
    if not ids:return 'NO_PRODUCT_ID',None
    if len(ids)>1:return 'MULTIPLE_PRODUCT_IDS',None
    pid=ids[0]
    if row.get('routing_state')!='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID':return 'SINGLE_ID_NEEDS_IDENTITY_VERIFICATION',None
    if special:return 'SPECIAL_PRINT_NEEDS_REVIEW',None
    if bucket is None:return 'UNSUPPORTED_FINISH',None
    entry=guide.get(pid)
    if entry is None:return 'NOT_IN_GUIDE',None
    value=entry.get(bucket)
    if not isinstance(value,(float,int)) or isinstance(value,bool) or value<=0:return 'NO_GUIDE_PRICE',None
    return 'VERIFIED_PRODUCT_GUIDE_PREVIEW',{'product_id':pid,'guide_bucket':bucket,'aggregate_eur':value}


def audit(rows,guide):
    stats=Counter();preview=[]
    for row in rows:
        classification,quote=classify(row,guide)
        stats[classification]+=1
        if quote:
            preview.append({'card_id':row['cardyssey_karte'],'variant_id':row['variante_id'],**quote})
    return dict(stats),preview


def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument('--queue',type=Path,required=True)
    ap.add_argument('--cached-guide',type=Path,default=DEFAULT_CACHE)
    ap.add_argument('--report',type=Path,required=True)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    with args.queue.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:rows=list(csv.DictReader(f))
    payload=json.loads(args.cached_guide.read_text())
    prices={str(x['idProduct']):x for x in payload.get('priceGuides',[])}
    if len(prices)<50000:raise ValueError('Incomplete price guide')
    stats,preview=audit(rows,prices)
    report={'source':'CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL_PRICE_GUIDE','guide_date':payload.get('createdAt'),
        'price_guide_products':len(prices),'variants':len(rows),'counts':stats,
        'preview_count':len(preview),'language_specific':False,'condition_specific':False,
        'edition_specific':False,'print_identity_certified':False,'published':False,
        'notes':'Guide-only reference candidates; no language-specific NM price and no overwrite of tcg_price_cache.',
        'verified_id_previews':preview}
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in report.items() if k!='verified_id_previews'},ensure_ascii=False))

if __name__=='__main__':main()

# DATEI 004/107: scripts/audit-cardmarket-id-coverage.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Audits offline Cardmarket IDs against the official feed; never changes prices."""
import csv
import hashlib
import json
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

URL="https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json"
CACHE=Path("/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/price-feeds")/(hashlib.sha256(URL.encode()).hexdigest()+".json")
QUEUE=Path("/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector")
OUT=Path("/home/tv/cardshop/reports")
OUT.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
feed=json.loads(CACHE.read_text())
products={str(p["idProduct"]):p for p in feed["products"]}
summary={"feed_date":feed.get("createdAt"),"official_product_count":len(products),"languages":{}}
for lang in ("de","en"):
    counts=Counter()
    exceptions=[]
    with (QUEUE/f"queue-{lang}.csv").open(encoding="utf-8-sig",newline="") as src:
        for r in csv.DictReader(src):
            counts["variants"]+=1
            ids=sorted({x.strip() for x in r["cardmarket_ids"].split(",") if x.strip().isdigit()})
            if not ids:
                counts["no_candidate"]+=1
                status="NO_CANDIDATE"
            elif len(ids)>1:
                counts["multiple_candidates"]+=1
                status="MULTIPLE_CANDIDATES"
            elif ids[0] not in products:
                counts["candidate_not_in_official_catalog"]+=1
                status="NOT_IN_OFFICIAL_CATALOG"
            else:
                counts["one_candidate_in_official_catalog"]+=1
                status="ONE_OFFICIAL_PRODUCT_CANDIDATE"
                if r["candidate_status"]=="SINGLE_SET_PRODUCT":
                    counts["verified_set_candidate"]+=1
                elif r["candidate_status"]=="NO_CATALOG_CANDIDATE":
                    counts["source_card_id_candidate"]+=1
            if status!="ONE_OFFICIAL_PRODUCT_CANDIDATE":
                exceptions.append({"language":lang,"card_id":r["cardyssey_karte"],
                  "variant_id":r["variante_id"],"set_id":r["set_id"],
                  "number":r["kartennummer"],"name":r["kartenname_suche"],
                  "status":status,"candidate_ids":",".join(ids),
                  "candidate_product_names":" | ".join(products[x].get("name","") for x in ids[:5] if x in products)})
    summary["languages"][lang]=dict(counts)
    with (OUT/f"cardmarket-id-exceptions-{lang}.csv").open("w",encoding="utf-8",newline="") as f:
        w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=["language","card_id","variant_id","set_id","number","name","status","candidate_ids","candidate_product_names"])
        w.writeheader();w.writerows(exceptions)
path=OUT/"cardmarket-id-coverage.json"
path.write_text(json.dumps(summary,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
print(json.dumps(summary,ensure_ascii=False))


############################################################################################
# DATEI 005/107: scripts/audit-cardmarket-set-crosscheck.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Read-only Cardmarket candidate audit using verified expansion IDs.

Never interpret one product ID as confirmed print/foil/language identity.
Never writes price caches, routing, or candidate tables.
"""
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import subprocess
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

URL="https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json"
CACHE=Path("/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/price-feeds")/(hashlib.sha256(URL.encode()).hexdigest()+".json")
BASE=Path("/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector")
REPORTS=Path("/home/tv/cardshop/reports")
REPORTS.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)

def get_set_crosswalk():
    q="""SELECT json_build_object('card_id',card_id,'language',language_code,
             'expansion',provider_expansion_id,'status',candidate_status)::text
         FROM cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates
         WHERE provider_expansion_id IS NOT NULL
           AND candidate_status IN ('SINGLE_SET_PRODUCT','MULTIPLE_SET_PRODUCTS')
           AND language_code IN ('de','en')"""
    r=subprocess.run(["docker","exec","cardyssey-db","psql","-X","-At",
       "-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-c",q],
       capture_output=True,text=True,check=True)
    return {(x["language"],x["card_id"]):str(x["expansion"])
            for line in r.stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()
            for x in [json.loads(line)]}

def review(products, crosswalk,lang):
    counts=Counter()
    path=REPORTS/f"cardmarket-candidates-checked-{lang}.csv"
    with (BASE/f"queue-{lang}.csv").open(encoding="utf-8-sig",newline="") as infile, path.open("w",encoding="utf-8",newline="") as outfile:
        reader=csv.DictReader(infile)
        fields=["language","card_id","variant_id","set_id","printed_number","card_name",
            "type","foil","stamps","candidate_count","matched_set_count",
            "all_product_ids","same_set_ids","rejected_other_set_ids",
            "classification","next_step"]
        writer=csv.DictWriter(outfile,fieldnames=fields)
        writer.writeheader()
        for row in reader:
            counts["total"]+=1
            card=row["cardyssey_karte"]
            ids=sorted({s for s in row["cardmarket_ids"].split(",") if s.isdigit()})
            expansion=crosswalk.get((lang,card))
            same=[pid for pid in ids if pid in products and str(products[pid].get("idExpansion"))==expansion] if expansion else []
            other=[pid for pid in ids if pid in products and str(products[pid].get("idExpansion"))!=expansion] if expansion else []
            if not ids:
                classification="NO_ID"
                step="LOOKUP_BY_SET_NAME_AND_PRINTED_NUMBER"
            elif expansion and len(same)==1:
                classification="ONE_MATCH_IN_VERIFIED_SET"
                step="VERIFY_PRINT_FOIL_STAMP_LANGUAGE"
                counts["one_matching_set"]+=1
                if len(ids)>1:counts["multi_resolved_by_set"]+=1
            elif expansion and len(same)>1:
                classification="MULTIPLE_MATCHES_WITHIN_SET"
                step="REVIEW_PRINT_OR_PRODUCT"
                counts["multi_same_set"]+=1
            elif expansion:
                classification="NO_MATCH_WITHIN_VERIFIED_SET"
                step="REVIEW_MISMATCH"
                counts["no_set_match"]+=1
            elif len(ids)==1 and ids[0] in products:
                classification="ONE_ID_SET_UNVERIFIED"
                step="VERIFY_SET_NUMBER_PRINT"
                counts["one_id_no_set_proof"]+=1
            else:
                classification="SET_UNVERIFIED_OR_MISSING_ID"
                step="FIND_SET_MATCH"
                counts["set_unverified"]+=1
            counts[classification]+=1
            writer.writerow({
                "language":lang,"card_id":card,"variant_id":row["variante_id"],
                "set_id":row["set_id"],"printed_number":row["kartennummer"],
                "card_name":row["kartenname_suche"],"type":row["drucktyp"],
                "foil":row["foil"],"stamps":row["stempel"],
                "candidate_count":len(ids),"matched_set_count":len(same),
                "all_product_ids":",".join(ids),"same_set_ids":",".join(same),
                "rejected_other_set_ids":",".join(other),
                "classification":classification,"next_step":step})
    return dict(counts),str(path)

def main():
    products={str(p["idProduct"]):p for p in json.loads(CACHE.read_text())["products"]}
    crosswalk=get_set_crosswalk()
    out={"official_products":len(products),"verified_set_crosswalk_cards":len(crosswalk),"langs":{}}
    for lang in ("de","en"):
        stats,path=review(products,crosswalk,lang)
        out["langs"][lang]={"counts":stats,"report":path}
    (REPORTS/"cardmarket-set-crosscheck-summary.json").write_text(
       json.dumps(out,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(out,ensure_ascii=False))
if __name__=="__main__":
    main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 006/107: scripts/audit-cardmarket-variant-evidence.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Offline inspection of all German unresolved Cardmarket variant candidates.

Consumes previously generated evidence, never browser credentials or a live account;
never treats a product-name match as full print identity.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import json
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path


def classify(case):
    products = case.get("products") or []
    candidates = {str(p.get("id")) for p in products}
    external = str((case.get("exact_variant_provider_ids") or {}).get("cardmarket") or "")
    verified_set = bool(case.get("set_verified"))
    if external and external in candidates and verified_set:
        return "DIRECT_ID_SET_MATCH_REVIEW"
    if external:
        return "DIRECT_ID_CONFLICT_REVIEW"
    if verified_set and len(candidates) == 1:
        return "SINGLE_SET_CANDIDATE_REVIEW"
    if verified_set and candidates:
        return "MULTIPLE_SET_CANDIDATES_REVIEW"
    if case.get("name_only_search_hints"):
        return "NAME_ONLY_HINT_NOT_VERIFIED"
    return "NO_PRODUCT_CANDIDATE"


def audit(cases):
    rows = []
    keys = set()
    for case in cases:
        key = (case.get("language"), case.get("card_id"), case.get("variant_id"))
        if key in keys:
            raise ValueError(f"duplicate key: {key}")
        keys.add(key)
        variant = case.get("variant") or {}
        refs = case.get("exact_variant_provider_ids") or {}
        rows.append({
            "language": case.get("language", ""), "card_id": case.get("card_id", ""),
            "variant_id": case.get("variant_id", ""), "set_id": case.get("set_code", ""),
            "number": case.get("number", ""), "variant_type": variant.get("type", ""),
            "subtype": variant.get("subtype", ""), "foil": variant.get("foil", ""),
            "stamps": ",".join(variant.get("stamps") or []),
            "status": classify(case),
            "direct_cardmarket_id": str(refs.get("cardmarket") or ""),
            "in_set_candidate_ids": ",".join(str(p.get("id")) for p in (case.get("products") or [])),
            "name_only_hint_count": len(case.get("name_only_search_hints") or []),
            "remaining_proof": ",".join(case.get("unverified_identity_fields") or []),
            "verified_identity": False,
            "price_auto_published": False,
        })
    summary = {"rows_inspected": len(rows), "unique_variant_keys": len(keys),
               "by_status": dict(Counter(row["status"] for row in rows)),
               "fully_verified_prints": 0, "published_prices": 0,
               "source": "existing official catalogue and variant cross-references; offline review"}
    return rows, summary


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--input", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    a = p.parse_args()
    rows, summary = audit(json.loads(a.input.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["unresolved"])
    a.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with (a.output_dir / "de_full_identity_audit.csv").open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=rows[0].keys())
        writer.writeheader(); writer.writerows(rows)
    (a.output_dir / "de_full_identity_audit.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# DATEI 007/107: scripts/build-cardmarket-identity-worklist.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build safe DE/EN Cardmarket ID candidates and a compact print-review queue.

Read-only against all existing price tables. Candidate set identities are staged
separately and never treated as per-language NM prices or verified foil/stamps.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import re
import subprocess
from collections import Counter,defaultdict
from pathlib import Path

ROOT=Path("/home/tv/cardshop")
QUEUE=Path("/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector")
FEEDS=Path("/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/price-feeds")
CAT_URL="https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json"
OUT=ROOT/"reports"
FIELDS=("language","card_id","variant_id","set_id","number","name","type","foil","subtype","stamps",
        "candidate_ids","matched_set_ids","expansion_id","classification","product_names","requires_confirmation",
        "guide_has_30day","guide_product_id")
def db_json(sql):
    p=subprocess.run(["docker","exec","cardyssey-db","psql","-X","-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1",
                      "-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-At","-c",sql],capture_output=True,text=True,check=True)
    return [json.loads(line) for line in p.stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()]
def official_products():
    p=FEEDS/(hashlib.sha256(CAT_URL.encode()).hexdigest()+".json")
    raw=json.loads(p.read_text())
    return {str(x["idProduct"]):x for x in raw["products"]},raw.get("createdAt")
def set_mapping():
    rows=db_json("""SELECT json_build_object('set',set_id,'exp',provider_expansion_id)::text
       FROM cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates
       WHERE language_code='de' AND provider_expansion_id IS NOT NULL
         AND candidate_status IN ('SINGLE_SET_PRODUCT','MULTIPLE_SET_PRODUCTS')""")
    counts=defaultdict(Counter)
    for x in rows:counts[x["set"]][str(x["exp"])]+=1
    return {sid:next(iter(c)) for sid,c in counts.items() if len(c)==1 and sum(c.values())>=3}
def infer_english_sets(products,known):
    votes=defaultdict(lambda:defaultdict(set))
    with (QUEUE/"queue-en.csv").open(encoding="utf-8-sig",newline="") as f:
        for r in csv.DictReader(f):
            ids={i for i in r["cardmarket_ids"].split(",") if i.isdigit()}
            if len(ids)!=1 or r["set_id"] in known:continue
            p=products.get(next(iter(ids)))
            if p:votes[r["set_id"]][str(p.get("idExpansion"))].add(r["cardyssey_karte"])
    inferred={}
    flagged={}
    for sid,exp in votes.items():
        scores=sorted(((len(cards),eid) for eid,cards in exp.items()),reverse=True)
        total=sum(n for n,_ in scores)
        if scores[0][0]>=6 and scores[0][0]/max(1,total)>=0.98 and (len(scores)==1 or scores[0][0]-scores[1][0]>=6):
            inferred[sid]=scores[0][1]
        else:flagged[sid]=dict((k,len(v)) for k,v in exp.items())
    return inferred,flagged

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--review-limit",type=int,default=2000)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    products,feed_date=official_products()
    sets=set_mapping()
    inferred,flagged=infer_english_sets(products,sets)
    all_sets={**sets,**inferred}
    # A strong duplicate expansion-name list is NOT independent set evidence.
    # Do not promote catalog-only singleton IDs from those sets until their
    # specific Cardmarket expansion is verified separately. Preserve human
    # exact-print routes in the loop below.
    audit_file=ROOT/'reports/cardmarket-set-mapping-risk-audit.json'
    quarantined=set()
    if audit_file.exists():
        audit=json.loads(audit_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        if audit.get('setsChecked',0)>=100:
            quarantined={r['setId'] for r in audit['cases']
                if r['status']=='AMBIGUOUS_OVERLAPPING_EXPANSIONS'
                and not (r.get('independentlyConfirmedOverride') or r.get('variantIdConsensusMatch'))}
            for sid in quarantined:all_sets.pop(sid,None)
    review=[]
    counts={}
    for lang in ("de","en"):
        counts[lang]=Counter()
        out=OUT/f"cardmarket-identity-worklist-{lang}.csv"
        with (QUEUE/f"queue-{lang}.csv").open(encoding="utf-8-sig",newline="") as f,out.open("w",encoding="utf-8",newline="") as w:
            reader=csv.DictReader(f)
            writer=csv.DictWriter(w,fieldnames=FIELDS);writer.writeheader()
            for r in reader:
                counts[lang]["total"]+=1
                ids=sorted({i for i in r["cardmarket_ids"].split(",") if i.isdigit()})
                exp=all_sets.get(r["set_id"])
                matched=[i for i in ids if i in products and exp is not None and str(products[i]["idExpansion"])==exp]
                # When a special print has NO exact Cardmarket product ID,
                # preserve card-level candidates from the independently
                # mapped expansion as UNVERIFIED PRINT candidates. This is
                # NEVER a confirmed physical print or a publishable price.
                print_missing=not matched and not ids
                card_candidates=sorted({i for i in r.get('set_card_candidate_ids','').split(',')
                    if i in products and exp is not None and str(products[i]['idExpansion'])==exp})
                if print_missing and card_candidates:
                    matched=card_candidates
                human_confirmed=(r.get('routing_state')=='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID' and
                    bool(r.get('known_url')) and len(ids)==1 and ids[0] in products)
                if human_confirmed:
                    # One user-confirmed exact print is evidence for THIS print,
                    # not for the whole expansion. Preserve it despite a faulty
                    # old set crosswalk and do not repeatedly ask for the same ID.
                    matched=[ids[0]]
                    exp=str(products[ids[0]].get('idExpansion') or exp or '')
                if exp is None:state="SET_NOT_VERIFIED"
                elif print_missing and matched:state="CARD_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_NOT_IDENTIFIED"
                elif not ids:state="NO_PRODUCT_ID"
                elif not matched:state="NO_MATCH_IN_VERIFIED_SET"
                elif len(matched)>1:state="MULTIPLE_PRODUCTS_IN_VERIFIED_SET"
                else:state="ONE_PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET"
                typ=(r["drucktyp"] or "").casefold()
                finish="REVERSE" if "reverse" in typ else ("HOLO" if "holo" in typ else "NORMAL")
                special=bool(r["sonderdruck"].strip() or r["foil"].strip() or r["stempel"].strip() not in ("","[]","null"))
                # Inferred English set identities are only consensus suggestions.
                # They are not equivalent to the independently mapped DE set crosswalk.
                if lang=="en" and r["set_id"] in inferred:
                    state=state.replace("VERIFIED_SET","INFERRED_SET")
                if human_confirmed:state='HUMAN_CONFIRMED_EXACT_PRINT'
                elif special:state += "_SPECIAL_PRINT"
                counts[lang][state]+=1
                chosen=matched[0] if len(matched)==1 and not print_missing else ""
                data=dict(language=lang,card_id=r["cardyssey_karte"],variant_id=r["variante_id"],
                          set_id=r["set_id"],number=r["kartennummer"],name=r["kartenname_suche"],
                          type=r["drucktyp"],foil=r["foil"],subtype=r["sonderdruck"],stamps=r["stempel"],
                          candidate_ids=",".join(sorted(set(ids+matched))),matched_set_ids=",".join(matched),
                          expansion_id=exp or "",classification=state,
                          product_names=" | ".join(products[i].get("name","") for i in matched[:5]),
                          requires_confirmation="SET_NUMBER_LANGUAGE_PRINT_FOIL_STAMP",
                          guide_has_30day="",guide_product_id=chosen)
                writer.writerow(data)
                if state not in ('ONE_PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET','HUMAN_CONFIRMED_EXACT_PRINT'):
                    review.append(data)
    # Prefer existing ownership and missing price when shown in admin; no automatic price publication.
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    with (OUT/"cardmarket-print-review-all.csv").open("w",encoding="utf-8",newline="") as f:
        w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=FIELDS);w.writeheader();w.writerows(review)
    status={"feed_date":feed_date,"official_catalog_size":len(products),"de_set_candidate_crosswalks":len(sets),
            "inferred_unverified_en_set_mappings":len(inferred),"en_set_conflicts":len(flagged),
            "lang_counts":{k:dict(v) for k,v in counts.items()},
            "manual_review_rows":len(review),
            "catalog_set_ambiguities_held":sorted(quarantined),
            "safety":"CANDIDATES ONLY. No prices or routing rows have been written."}
    (OUT/"cardmarket-identity-pipeline-status.json").write_text(json.dumps(status,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(status,ensure_ascii=False))
if __name__=="__main__":main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 008/107: scripts/Cardmarket-Account-Collector.ps1
############################################################################################

# Cardyssey Cardmarket browser review. Uses the pre-authenticated, dedicated
# Chrome profile via localhost CDP. Does not export credentials or cookies.
param(
  [string]$InputCsv = "C:\Users\TV\AppData\Local\Cardyssey\CardmarketCollector\queue.csv",
  [string]$OutputDir = "C:\Users\TV\AppData\Local\Cardyssey\CardmarketCollector",
  [int]$MaxCases = 1460,
  [int]$DelaySeconds = 12
)
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $OutputDir | Out-Null
$resultsPath = Join-Path $OutputDir "product-page-review.jsonl"
$progressPath = Join-Path $OutputDir "progress.json"
$targets = @(Invoke-RestMethod "http://127.0.0.1:9222/json/list")
$target = @($targets | Where-Object { $_.type -eq "page" -and $_.url -match "cardmarket.com" })[0]
if ($target -is [array]) {$target=$target[0]}
if (!$target) { throw "Open the signed-in Cardmarket page in the isolated Chrome profile with localhost debugging enabled." }
$ws = [Net.WebSockets.ClientWebSocket]::new()
$ws.Options.SetRequestHeader("Origin", "http://localhost")
$ws.ConnectAsync([uri]$target.webSocketDebuggerUrl, [threading.cancellationtoken]::None).GetAwaiter().GetResult() | Out-Null
$script:id = 0
function Send-Cdp([string]$method, [hashtable]$parameters) {
  $script:id += 1
  $currentId = $script:id
  $packet = @{id=$currentId;method=$method;params=$parameters} | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 15 -Compress
  $bytes = [text.encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes($packet)
  $sendCts=[threading.CancellationTokenSource]::new([TimeSpan]::FromSeconds(20))
  try {
    $ws.SendAsync([ArraySegment[byte]]::new($bytes),[Net.WebSockets.WebSocketMessageType]::Text,$true,$sendCts.Token).GetAwaiter().GetResult() | Out-Null
  } finally { $sendCts.Dispose() }
  $buffer = [byte[]]::new(262144)
  $all = New-Object System.IO.MemoryStream
  for ($i=0; $i -lt 200; $i++) {
    $cts=[threading.CancellationTokenSource]::new([TimeSpan]::FromSeconds(20))
    try {
      $response = $ws.ReceiveAsync([ArraySegment[byte]]::new($buffer),$cts.Token).GetAwaiter().GetResult()
    } finally { $cts.Dispose() }
    $all.Write($buffer,0,$response.Count)
    if (!$response.EndOfMessage) {continue}
    $raw = [text.encoding]::UTF8.GetString($all.ToArray())
    $all.SetLength(0)
    $parsed = $raw | ConvertFrom-Json
    if ($parsed.id -ne $currentId) {continue}
    if ($parsed.error) { throw "Browser protocol error: $($parsed.error.message)" }
    return $parsed
  }
  throw "Browser protocol timed out"
}
function Js([string]$expression) {
  $reply = Send-Cdp "Runtime.evaluate" @{expression=$expression;returnByValue=$true;awaitPromise=$true}
  if ($reply.result.exceptionDetails) {throw "Browser script exception"}
  return $reply.result.result.value
}
function Move-To([string]$url) {
  Send-Cdp "Page.navigate" @{url=$url} | Out-Null
  $desired = [uri]$url
  for ($i=0;$i -lt 38;$i++) {
    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 600
    $state=Js 'JSON.stringify({path:location.pathname,href:location.href,ready:document.readyState,body:document.body?.innerText.length||0})'
    if (!$state) {continue}
    $s=$state | ConvertFrom-Json
    $actual=[uri]$s.href
    $expectedQuery=[uri]::UnescapeDataString($desired.Query.TrimStart('?').Replace('+',' '))
    $actualQuery=[uri]::UnescapeDataString($actual.Query.TrimStart('?').Replace('+',' '))
    $sameQuery=($expectedQuery -eq $actualQuery) -or ($desired.AbsolutePath -ne '/de/Pokemon/Products/Search')
    if ($s.path -eq $desired.AbsolutePath -and $sameQuery -and $s.ready -eq "complete" -and $s.body -gt 300) {return $true}
  }
  return $false
}
function Save-Progress([int]$done,[int]$total,[string]$status,[string]$card) {
  $s = @{done=$done;total=$total;status=$status;last_card=$card;updated_at=[DateTime]::UtcNow.ToString("o");file=$resultsPath}
  $s | ConvertTo-Json -Compress | Set-Content -Encoding UTF8 -Path $progressPath
}
$cases = @(Import-Csv -Path $InputCsv -Encoding UTF8)
$processed = [Collections.Generic.HashSet[string]]::new()
$latest = @{}
if (Test-Path $resultsPath) {
  Get-Content $resultsPath | ForEach-Object {
    try {
      $prev=$_ | ConvertFrom-Json
      $key=[string]$prev.key
      if ($key) { $latest[$key]=$prev }
    } catch {}
  }
}
$nowUtc=[datetime]::UtcNow
# Reuse a previously observed canonical product page across physical variants
# of the same card ONLY if its product ID is independently listed as a
# candidate for the next variant. Variant pricing is never auto-approved.
$knownProductPages=@{}
foreach ($record in $latest.Values) {
  if ($record.status -notin @('FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE','NO_CONFIRMED_FILTERED_OFFERS')) {continue}
  $id=[string]$record.product_id
  if ($id -notmatch '^[0-9]+$') {continue}
  try {
    $candidate=[uri](([string]$record.url).Split('?')[0])
    if ($candidate.Scheme -ne 'https' -or $candidate.Host -ne 'www.cardmarket.com' -or
        !$candidate.AbsolutePath.StartsWith('/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/')) {continue}
    $knownProductPages["$($record.card_id)|$id"]=$candidate.AbsoluteUri
  } catch {}
}
foreach ($case in $cases) {
  $key="{0}|{1}" -f $case.cardyssey_karte,$case.variante_id
  if (!$latest.ContainsKey($key)) { continue }
  $prev=$latest[$key]
  $tier=[string]$case.scan_tier
  $refreshDays=if($tier -in @('0','1')){7}elseif($tier -eq '2'){14}else{30}
  $isRecent=$false
  try { $isRecent=([datetime]::Parse($prev.seen_at).ToUniversalTime() -gt $nowUtc.AddDays(-$refreshDays)) } catch {}
  if (($tier -eq '4' -and $prev.status -eq 'SKIPPED_BULK_PRICE_FEED') -or
      ($prev.status -ne 'ERROR' -and $isRecent)) { [void]$processed.Add($key) }
}
$completed=0
$runTotal=[Math]::Min($cases.Count,$MaxCases)
Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal "RUNNING" ""
try {
  foreach ($case in ($cases | Select-Object -First $MaxCases)) {
    $key="{0}|{1}" -f $case.cardyssey_karte,$case.variante_id
    if ($processed.Contains($key)) {continue}
    if ([string]$case.scan_tier -eq '4' -and [string]$case.needs_price -eq 'NO') {
      $skip=[ordered]@{key=$key;card_id=$case.cardyssey_karte;variant_id=$case.variante_id;status='SKIPPED_BULK_PRICE_FEED';seen_at=[datetime]::UtcNow.ToString('o')}
      ($skip | ConvertTo-Json -Compress) | Add-Content -Encoding UTF8 -Path $resultsPath
      [void]$processed.Add($key)
      Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'RUNNING' $case.cardyssey_karte
      continue
    }
    $out=[ordered]@{key=$key;card_id=$case.cardyssey_karte;variant_id=$case.variante_id;number=$case.kartennummer;set_id=$case.set_id;status="STARTED";query="";product_id="";url="";offers_seen=0;de_nm_offers=0;sample_median_eur=$null;price_basis="lowest_page_active_asking_sample_not_sold";seen_at=[DateTime]::UtcNow.ToString("o")}
    try {
      $auth=Js 'document.body?.innerText.includes("AUSLOGGEN")===true'
      if (!$auth) {
        Start-Sleep -Seconds 3
        $auth=Js 'document.body?.innerText.includes("AUSLOGGEN")===true'
      }
      if (!$auth) { Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal "LOGIN_REQUIRED" $case.cardyssey_karte;break }
      $name=[string]$case.kartenname_suche
      $number=[string]$case.kartennummer
      $out.query="$name $number"
      $savedUrl=""
      $allowedIds=@([string]$case.cardmarket_ids -split ",")
      if ($latest.ContainsKey($key)) {
        $prior=$latest[$key]
        $sameProduct=([string]$prior.product_id -in $allowedIds)
        if ($sameProduct -and $prior.status -in @('FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE','NO_CONFIRMED_FILTERED_OFFERS')) {
          try {
            $candidate=[uri](([string]$prior.url).Split('?')[0])
            if ($candidate.Scheme -eq 'https' -and $candidate.Host -eq 'www.cardmarket.com' -and $candidate.AbsolutePath.StartsWith('/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/')) {
              $savedUrl=$candidate.AbsoluteUri
            }
          } catch {}
        }
      }
      if (!$savedUrl) {
        $reuse=@($allowedIds | Where-Object { $_ -and $knownProductPages.ContainsKey("$($case.cardyssey_karte)|$_") })
        # A single candidate prevents silently choosing a different printing.
        if ($reuse.Count -eq 1) {
          $savedUrl=$knownProductPages["$($case.cardyssey_karte)|$($reuse[0])"]
        }
      }
      if ($savedUrl) {
        $out.search_hits=1
        $likely=@([pscustomobject]@{url=$savedUrl;text="$name $number"})
      } else {
        # Fetch the search document inside the *existing* authenticated Chrome
        # origin; no extra page navigation, cookie export, or TLS impersonation.
        $encoded=[uri]::EscapeDataString($out.query)
        $searchExpr='(async()=>{const c=new AbortController();const tm=setTimeout(()=>c.abort(),12000);try{const r=await fetch("/de/Pokemon/Products/Search?searchString='+$encoded+'",{credentials:"include",signal:c.signal});const t=await r.text();const d=new DOMParser().parseFromString(t,"text/html");const links=[...d.querySelectorAll("a[href*=\u0027/Products/Singles/\u0027]")].map(a=>({text:a.textContent.trim().slice(0,140),url:new URL(a.getAttribute("href"),location.origin).href})).filter(x=>x.text);return JSON.stringify({status:r.status,blocked:/captcha|robot check|access denied|zugriff verweigert|too many requests/i.test(d.title+" "+(d.body?.innerText||"").slice(0,1600)),links:links})}catch(e){return JSON.stringify({status:0,blocked:false,error:e.name==="AbortError"?"FETCH_TIMEOUT":"FETCH_FAILED",links:[]})}finally{clearTimeout(tm)}})()'
        $searchResult=(Js $searchExpr) | ConvertFrom-Json
        if ($searchResult.error -in @("FETCH_TIMEOUT","FETCH_FAILED")) { Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal "PROVIDER_TIMEOUT" $case.cardyssey_karte; break }
        if ($searchResult.blocked -or $searchResult.status -in @(403,429,503)) {
          Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal "BLOCKED" $case.cardyssey_karte
          break
        }
        if ($searchResult.status -ne 200) {throw "SEARCH_HTTP_STATUS_$($searchResult.status)"}
        $links=@($searchResult.links)
        $out.search_hits=$links.Count
        $likely=@($links | Where-Object { $_.text -match "\b0*$([regex]::Escape($number))\b" })
      }
      if ($likely.Count -eq 1) {
        $out.url=$likely[0].url
        if (!(Move-To $out.url)) {throw "PRODUCT_PAGE_TIMEOUT"}
        $candidateId=[string](Js 'document.querySelector("input[name=idProduct]")?.value||""')
        $out.product_id=$candidateId
        $allowed=[string]$case.cardmarket_ids -split ","
        if ($allowed.Count -gt 0 -and $allowed[0] -and $candidateId -notin $allowed) {
          $out.status="PRODUCT_ID_NOT_IN_VERIFIED_SET"
        } elseif (!$candidateId) {
          $out.status="NO_PRODUCT_ID"
        } else {
          $out.status="PRODUCT_PAGE_MATCH_REVIEW"
          $filterOk = Js '(()=>{let f=[...document.forms].find(f=>f.action.includes("Product_Filter_FilterProduct"));let l=f?.querySelector("input[name=\"language[3]\"]");if(!l)return false;l.checked=true;f.querySelector("[name=minCondition]").value="2";f.querySelector("[name=\"extra[isSigned]\"]").value="N";f.querySelector("[name=\"extra[isAltered]\"]").value="N";f.requestSubmit(f.querySelector("[name=apply]"));return true})()'
          if ($filterOk) {
            Start-Sleep -Seconds 2
            $data = Js 'JSON.stringify({filtered:document.querySelector("input[name=\"language[3]\"]")?.checked===true&&document.querySelector("[name=minCondition]")?.value==="2",rows:[...document.querySelectorAll(".article-row")].slice(0,100).map(r=>({id:r.id,condition:r.querySelector(".article-condition")?.getAttribute("data-bs-original-title")||r.querySelector(".article-condition")?.getAttribute("title"),language:[...r.querySelectorAll("[data-bs-original-title],[title]")].map(n=>n.getAttribute("data-bs-original-title")||n.getAttribute("title")).find(t=>t==="Deutsch"||t==="Englisch")||null,price:r.querySelector(".price-container")?.innerText.trim()}))})' | ConvertFrom-Json
            $out.offers_seen=@($data.rows).Count
            $accepted=@($data.rows | Where-Object { $_.language -eq "Deutsch" -and $_.condition -in @("Near Mint","Mint") -and $_.price -match "[0-9]" })
            $out.de_nm_offers=$accepted.Count
            if ($data.filtered -and $accepted.Count -ge 1) {
              $nums=@($accepted | ForEach-Object { $m=[regex]::Match($_.price,"\d+(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?");if($m.Success){[double]::Parse($m.Value.Replace(",","."),[globalization.cultureinfo]::InvariantCulture)} } | Sort-Object)
              if ($nums.Count -gt 0) { $mid=[int][Math]::Floor($nums.Count/2);$out.sample_median_eur=if($nums.Count%2){$nums[$mid]}else{[math]::Round(($nums[$mid-1]+$nums[$mid])/2,2)} }
              $out.status="FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE"
            } else {$out.status="NO_CONFIRMED_FILTERED_OFFERS"}
            if ($out.status -in @('FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE','NO_CONFIRMED_FILTERED_OFFERS') -and $out.product_id -match '^[0-9]+$') {
              $knownProductPages["$($case.cardyssey_karte)|$($out.product_id)"]=$out.url.Split('?')[0]
            }
          } else {$out.status="FILTER_UNAVAILABLE"}
        }
      } else { $out.status=if($likely.Count -gt 1){"MULTIPLE_SEARCH_HITS"}else{"NO_EXACT_NUMBER_SEARCH_HIT"} }
    } catch {
      $message=$_.Exception.Message
      # A timed-out CDP receive aborts the WebSocket permanently. Stop this
      # run immediately; the launcher will reconnect with a new CDP socket.
      # Do not mark the current card as completed and do not loop over the
      # remainder of the queue with a dead socket.
      if ($ws.State -ne [Net.WebSockets.WebSocketState]::Open -or
          $message -match 'WebSocket|websocket|abgebrochen|cancelled|canceled|CDP.*timed out') {
        [pscustomobject]@{at=[DateTime]::UtcNow.ToString("o");card=$case.cardyssey_karte;message=$message;socket=[string]$ws.State} | ConvertTo-Json -Compress | Add-Content -Encoding UTF8 -Path (Join-Path $OutputDir "connection-errors.jsonl")
        Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal "CONNECTION_LOST" $case.cardyssey_karte
        break
      }
      $out.status="ERROR"
      $out.error=$message.Substring(0,[Math]::Min(180,$message.Length))
    }
    ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress) | Add-Content -Encoding UTF8 -Path $resultsPath
    [void]$processed.Add($key)
    Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal "RUNNING" $case.cardyssey_karte
    # Failed discovery requests do not fetch/filter an entire offer page.
    # Keep a conservative cooldown and reduce idle waiting only for these cases.
    $pause=if($out.status -in @('MULTIPLE_SEARCH_HITS','NO_EXACT_NUMBER_SEARCH_HIT','PRODUCT_ID_NOT_IN_VERIFIED_SET')) {
      [Math]::Max(6,[Math]::Min(8,$DelaySeconds))
    } else { [Math]::Max(3,$DelaySeconds) }
    Start-Sleep -Seconds $pause
  }
  if ((Get-Content $progressPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).status -notin @("LOGIN_REQUIRED","BLOCKED","CONNECTION_LOST")) {
    $lastStatuses=@{}
    if (Test-Path $resultsPath) {
      Get-Content $resultsPath | ForEach-Object {
        try {
          $entry=$_ | ConvertFrom-Json
          if ($entry.key) { $lastStatuses[[string]$entry.key]=[string]$entry.status }
        } catch {}
      }
    }
    $errorCount=@($lastStatuses.Values | Where-Object { $_ -eq "ERROR" }).Count
    $endStatus=if($errorCount -gt 0){"COMPLETED_WITH_ERRORS"}else{"COMPLETED_OR_LIMIT_REACHED"}
    Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal $endStatus ""
  }
} finally { $ws.Dispose() }

# DATEI 009/107: scripts/cardmarket-apply-print-page-hints.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Attach verified *card page* leads; never claim exact print/offer price.

Validated against expected card/set/collector slug. A link to a shared
product page is NOT evidence of a specific stamp, foil, edition or language.
"""
import argparse,csv,json,os,tempfile
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlparse
ROOT=Path('/home/tv/cardshop')
BASE=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector')

def apply(language,base=BASE,root=ROOT):
    if language not in ('de','en'):raise ValueError('invalid language')
    rules=json.loads((root/'config/cardmarket-known-print-page-hints.json').read_text())
    path=base/f'queue-{language}.csv'
    with path.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
        r=csv.DictReader(f);fields=list(r.fieldnames or []);rows=list(r)
    for col in ('reference_product_url','reference_print_verified','reference_evidence','reference_standard_filter_supported','catalog_singleton_id','catalog_expansion_id'):
        if col not in fields:fields.append(col)
    counts={'language':language,'rows':len(rows),'knownPages':0,'printConfirmedByReference':0,'standardOfferFilterCandidates':0}
    seen=set()
    for row in rows:
        row['reference_product_url']='';row['reference_print_verified']='NO';row['reference_evidence']='';row['reference_standard_filter_supported']='NO'
        card=row['cardyssey_karte'];var=row['variante_id']
        if card not in rules:continue
        rule=rules[card];choice=rule['variants'].get(var)
        if choice is None:continue
        assert card not in ('',None) and row.get('set_id')==card.rsplit('-',1)[0],(card,row.get('set_id'))
        assert choice['slug'].endswith('-'+rule['collectorSuffix']),(card,choice)
        assert choice.get('printVerified') is False,'Hint cannot claim exact print proof'
        assert all('/' not in choice[k] for k in ('slug',)),choice
        url='https://www.cardmarket.com/'+language+'/Pokemon/Products/Singles/'+rule['setSlug']+'/'+choice['slug']
        assert urlparse(url).hostname=='www.cardmarket.com'
        row['reference_product_url']=url
        row['reference_print_verified']='NO'
        row['reference_evidence']=choice['evidence']
        standard=choice.get('standardFilterProductId')
        if standard:
            assert str(standard).isdigit() and str(choice.get('standardFilterExpansionId') or '').isdigit()
            assert row['drucktyp'].strip().lower() in ('normal','reverse'),row
            assert str(row.get('stempel') or '') in ('','[]') and not str(row.get('foil') or '') and not str(row.get('sonderdruck') or ''),'special print must not become standard'
            assert choice['slug'].startswith('Sprigatito-V1-') and card=='sv01-013','standard evidence must be inspected'
            # Official set-repair should have populated the complete list.
            # In dry-runs/temporary queues, this independently browser-verified
            # product is still safe as a *candidate*; never publish it as a
            # reviewed physical print or price just because the list was empty.
            if str(standard) not in str(row.get('cardmarket_ids') or '').split(','):
                current=[x for x in str(row.get('cardmarket_ids') or '').split(',') if x.isdigit()]
                row['cardmarket_ids']=','.join(sorted(set(current+[str(standard)]),key=int))
            # This is a *standard offer-filter candidate*, not human confirmation.
            # Preserve current human-confirmed IDs and prices in all cases.
            if row.get('routing_state')!='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID':
                row['catalog_singleton_id']=str(standard)
                row['catalog_expansion_id']=str(choice['standardFilterExpansionId'])
                row['reference_standard_filter_supported']='YES'
                counts['standardOfferFilterCandidates']+=1
        key=(card,var)
        assert key not in seen,'duplicate reference'
        seen.add(key);counts['knownPages']+=1
    fd,tmp=tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f'queue-{language}-refs-',suffix='.csv.tmp',dir=base)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd,'w',newline='',encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
            w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=fields);w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)
        os.replace(tmp,path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(tmp):os.unlink(tmp)
    return counts
if __name__=='__main__':
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser();parser.add_argument('--language',required=True,choices=('de','en'));a=parser.parse_args()
    print(json.dumps(apply(a.language)))

# DATEI 010/107: scripts/cardmarket-build-guide-diagnostics.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Compact read-only, PRODUCT-WIDE Cardmarket guide context for the browser.

A product-level trend/avg30 has no DE/EN/NM/foil guarantee. This file is
diagnostic evidence only: never publish into tcg_price_cache or routing.
"""
import argparse,csv,hashlib,json,os,tempfile
from datetime import datetime,timezone
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path('/home/tv/cardshop')
BASE=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector')
URL='https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/priceGuide/price_guide_6.json'
CACHE=Path('/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/price-feeds')
def build(base=BASE,cache=CACHE):
    source=cache/(hashlib.sha256(URL.encode()).hexdigest()+'.json')
    feed=json.loads(source.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    if len(feed.get('priceGuides') or [])<50000:raise ValueError('Price guide incomplete')
    needed=set()
    for lang in ('de','en'):
        with (base/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
            for row in csv.DictReader(f):
                for pid in (str(row.get('cardmarket_ids') or '')+','+
                            str(row.get('catalog_singleton_id') or '')).split(','):
                    if pid.isdigit():needed.add(pid)
    prices={}
    for item in feed['priceGuides']:
        pid=str(item.get('idProduct') or '')
        if pid not in needed:continue
        prices[pid]={k:item.get(k) for k in ('trend','trend-holo','avg30','avg30-holo')
                     if isinstance(item.get(k),(int,float)) and item.get(k)>0}
    return {'createdAt':feed.get('createdAt'),'extractedAt':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            'scope':'PRODUCT_AGGREGATE_NOT_LANGUAGE_CONDITION_OR_EXACT_PRINT',
            'canPublishAsLanguagePrintPrice':False,'products':prices}
if __name__=='__main__':
    a=argparse.ArgumentParser();a.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True);args=a.parse_args()
    d=build();args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    fd,temp=tempfile.mkstemp(prefix='price-guide-diagnostic-',suffix='.tmp',dir=args.output.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd,'w',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(d,f,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(',',':'))
        os.replace(temp,args.output)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temp):os.unlink(temp)
    print(json.dumps({'guideDate':d['createdAt'],'products':len(d['products']),
                       'canPublishAsLanguagePrintPrice':False}))

# DATEI 011/107: scripts/cardmarket-build-metacard-family-index.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Official catalog metacard-family *candidate* index for the full DE/EN queue.

idMetacard groups printings including separate Additional expansions, but
does not certify Cardyssey identity, collector number, foil or price. Index
keeps original official expansion IDs to avoid cross-set autopromotion.
"""
import argparse,csv,hashlib,json,os,re,tempfile
from collections import defaultdict,Counter
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path('/home/tv/cardshop')
BASE=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector')
URL='https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json'
FEEDS=Path('/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/price-feeds')
def build(base=BASE,feeds=FEEDS):
    p=feeds/(hashlib.sha256(URL.encode()).hexdigest()+'.json')
    products=json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))['products']
    by_id={str(x['idProduct']):x for x in products if x.get('idProduct')}
    by_meta=defaultdict(list)
    for x in products:
        if x.get('idMetacard'):by_meta[str(x['idMetacard'])].append(x)
    result={};summary=Counter()
    for lang in ('de','en'):
        cards=defaultdict(lambda:{'setId':None,'seeds':set(),'variants':0})
        with (base/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
            for r in csv.DictReader(f):
                card=r['cardyssey_karte'];d=cards[card];d['setId']=r['set_id'];d['variants']+=1
                if r.get('candidate_status')=='SET_EXPANSION_AMBIGUOUS':continue
                for val in (str(r.get('cardmarket_ids') or '')+','+
                            str(r.get('set_card_candidate_ids') or '')+','+
                            str(r.get('catalog_singleton_id') or '')).split(','):
                    if re.fullmatch('[0-9]{4,12}',val) and val in by_id:d['seeds'].add(val)
        for card,d in cards.items():
            possible=defaultdict(set)
            for pid in d['seeds']:
                meta=str(by_id[pid].get('idMetacard') or '')
                if meta and meta!='0':possible[meta].add(pid)
            if not possible:continue
            # Distinct families for a single card are ambiguous and cannot
            # safely be merged: retain them as separate, unverified options.
            meta_groups=[]
            for meta,seeds in sorted(possible.items()):
                members=by_meta.get(meta,[])
                if len(members)<2:continue
                meta_groups.append({'metacardId':meta,'seedProductIds':sorted(seeds),
                  'candidateProducts':[{'id':str(x['idProduct']),
                     'expansionId':str(x.get('idExpansion') or ''),
                     'name':str(x.get('name') or '')[:170]}
                    for x in members[:30]],'truncated':len(members)>30,
                  'multipleExpansions':len({str(x.get('idExpansion')) for x in members})>1,
                  'printIdentityVerified':False})
            if meta_groups:
                result[lang+'|'+card]={'cardId':card,'language':lang,'setId':d['setId'],
                    'seedIsVerifiedPrint':False,'families':meta_groups}
                summary[lang+'_cards']+=1
                if len(meta_groups)>1:summary[lang+'_multipleFamilies']+=1
                if any(x['multipleExpansions'] for x in meta_groups):summary[lang+'_crossExpansionFamilies']+=1
    return {'source':'CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL_PRODUCT_CATALOG_METACARD_ONLY',
            'printIdentityVerified':False,'pricesPublished':0,
            'summary':dict(summary),'cards':result}
def write(data,path:Path):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    fd,temp=tempfile.mkstemp(prefix='cm-family-',suffix='.json.tmp',dir=path.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd,'w',encoding='utf-8') as f:
            json.dump(data,f,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(',',':'))
        os.replace(temp,path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temp):os.unlink(temp)
if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True)
    a=p.parse_args();x=build();write(x,a.output)
    print(json.dumps({'summary':x['summary'],'indexedCards':len(x['cards']),
       'printVerified':False,'pricesPublished':0},ensure_ascii=False))


############################################################################################
# DATEI 012/107: scripts/cardmarket-build-observed-set-guards.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Derive only independently repeated set/slug evidence from completed
official-catalog direct-ID browser lookups. No ID/print/price publication.
The existing human set guards always override this optional cache.
"""
import argparse,csv,json,re
from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import datetime,timezone
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
ROOT=Path('/home/tv/cardshop')
BASE=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector')
PATTERN=re.compile(r'^/(de|en)/Pokemon/Products/Singles/([A-Za-z0-9-]+)/([A-Za-z0-9-]+)$')
def derive(base=BASE,minimum=5):
    with (base/'queue-de.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
        rows={(r['cardyssey_karte'],r['variante_id']):r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
    latest={}
    with (base/'browser-observations-de.jsonl').open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
        for line in f:
            try:
                item=json.loads(line);latest[(item['card_id'],item['variant_id'])]=item
            except (ValueError,TypeError,KeyError):continue
    evidence=defaultdict(lambda:defaultdict(set))
    for key,item in latest.items():
        row=rows.get(key)
        if not row or item.get('catalog_direct_id_verified') is not True or item.get('card_number_url_verified') is not True:continue
        pid=str(item.get('product_id') or '')
        if not pid.isdigit() or pid!=str(row.get('catalog_singleton_id') or ''):continue
        if not str(row.get('catalog_expansion_id') or '').isdigit():continue
        try:u=urlsplit(item.get('url',''))
        except (ValueError,TypeError):continue
        if u.scheme!='https' or u.hostname!='www.cardmarket.com':continue
        m=PATTERN.fullmatch(u.path)
        if not m:continue
        evidence[row['set_id']][m.group(2)].add(row['cardyssey_karte'])
    approved={};excluded={}
    for sid,group in sorted(evidence.items()):
        if len(group)==1:
            slug,ids=next(iter(group.items()))
            if len(ids)>=minimum:approved[sid]={'setSlug':slug,'distinctLiveCards':len(ids),'proof':'OFFICIAL_PRODUCT_ID_AND_LIVE_NUMBER_AND_UNIQUE_SET_PATH'}
        else:excluded[sid]={s:len(v) for s,v in group.items()}
    return {'generatedAt':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            'minimumDistinctCards':minimum,'setSlugs':approved,
            'ambiguousExcluded':excluded,'verifiedPrints':0,'publishedPrices':0}
if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--output',type=Path,required=True);p.add_argument('--min-cards',type=int,default=5)
    a=p.parse_args()
    if not 5<=a.min_cards<=100:raise SystemExit('min-cards out of range')
    d=derive(minimum=a.min_cards)
    a.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    import os,tempfile
    fd,temp=tempfile.mkstemp(prefix='observed-set-',suffix='.tmp',dir=a.output.parent)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd,'w',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(d,f,indent=2,ensure_ascii=False)
        os.replace(temp,a.output)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temp):os.unlink(temp)
    print(json.dumps({'approved':len(d['setSlugs']),'excluded':d['ambiguousExcluded'],
                      'setSlugs':{k:v['setSlug'] for k,v in d['setSlugs'].items()}},ensure_ascii=False))

# Install an automatic resume shortcut for the existing isolated browser scanner.
$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$base = Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA "Cardyssey\CardmarketCollector"
$launcher = Join-Path $base "Cardmarket-Collector-Launcher.ps1"
if (!(Test-Path $launcher)) {throw "Collector launcher does not exist"}
$startup = [Environment]::GetFolderPath("Startup")
$shortcutPath = Join-Path $startup "Cardyssey Cardmarket Collector.lnk"
$shell = New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell
$shortcut = $shell.CreateShortcut($shortcutPath)
$shortcut.TargetPath = "$env:SystemRoot\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe"
$shortcut.Arguments = "-NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -WindowStyle Hidden -File `"$launcher`""
$shortcut.WorkingDirectory = $base
$shortcut.Description = "Cardyssey: resume filtered Cardmarket review after login"
$shortcut.WindowStyle = 7
$shortcut.Save()
$action = New-ScheduledTaskAction -Execute "$env:SystemRoot\System32\WindowsPowerShell\v1.0\powershell.exe" -Argument "-NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -WindowStyle Hidden -File `"$launcher`""
$trigger = New-ScheduledTaskTrigger -Daily -At '04:35'
$principal = New-ScheduledTaskPrincipal -UserId ([System.Security.Principal.WindowsIdentity]::GetCurrent().Name) -LogonType Interactive -RunLevel Limited
$settings = New-ScheduledTaskSettingsSet -ExecutionTimeLimit (New-TimeSpan -Minutes 10) -StartWhenAvailable
Register-ScheduledTask -TaskName 'Cardyssey Cardmarket Daily Refresh' -Action $action -Trigger $trigger -Principal $principal -Settings $settings -Force | Out-Null
Write-Output "startup_installed=$shortcutPath daily_refresh_installed=true"

# DATEI 014/107: scripts/Cardmarket-Collector-Launcher.ps1
