        # the family URL for later independent version-page checks. This adds
        # no Cardmarket navigation and NEVER verifies a foil or price.
        if($candidateId -match '^[0-9]{4,12}$'){
          $familyLink=[string](Js '(()=>{const a=[...document.querySelectorAll("a[href]")].find(x=>{try{const u=new URL(x.href);return u.origin==="https://www.cardmarket.com"&&/^\/(de|en)\/Pokemon\/Cards\/[^/]+\/Versions$/.test(u.pathname)}catch{return false}});return a?a.href.split("?")[0]:""})()')
          if($familyLink -match '^https://www[.]cardmarket[.]com/(de|en)/Pokemon/Cards/[^/]+/Versions$'){
            $out.version_page_url=$familyLink
            $out.version_family_status='PAGE_LINK_FOUND_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'
          }
        }
        $allowed = @([string]$case.cardmarket_ids -split ',' | Where-Object {$_ -match '^[0-9]+$'})
        # The Cardyssey print can lack a product ID while the exact CARD is
        # already known in the independently matched Cardmarket expansion.
        # These are card candidates, NOT verified physical-print identities.
        $cardCandidates=@([string]$case.set_card_candidate_ids -split ',' | Where-Object {$_ -match '^[0-9]+$'})
        $numberEvidence=$false
        $observedPath=([string]$out.url).Split('?')[0]
        # Only the FINAL slug suffix is a candidate collector number.
        # Never accidentally parse -V1 as card #1 in -V1-BS13.
        $numberTail=[regex]::Match($observedPath,'(?i)-[A-Z]{1,8}([0-9]{1,4})$')
        if($numberTail.Success){
          try{$numberEvidence=([int]$numberTail.Groups[1].Value -eq [int]([string]$case.kartennummer))}catch{}
        }
        $out.card_number_url_verified=$numberEvidence
        if (!$candidateId) {
          $out.status = 'NO_PRODUCT_ID'
          Append-Observation $unverifyPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
        } elseif([string]$out.score_matching_status -eq 'SET_NOT_INDEPENDENTLY_VERIFIED'){
          # A perfect-looking number and title still cannot prove the set.
          $out.status='PRODUCT_ID_NOT_VERIFIED_FOR_CARD'
          $out.error='SEARCH_RANK_SET_NOT_INDEPENDENTLY_VERIFIED'
          Append-Observation $unverifyPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
        } elseif($out.score_page_candidate_only -eq $true -and
                 ($out.non_filterable_print_attributes.Count -gt 0 -or [string]$case.catalog_singleton_id -notmatch '^[0-9]{4,12}$')){
          # A scored title is not independent evidence of the exact print.
          # The card/number/actual page are recorded for review only.
          if($numberEvidence -and ([string]$candidateId -in $allowed -or
                                  [string]$candidateId -in $cardCandidates)){
            $out.status='PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'
            $out.error='SCORED_PAGE_IN_CATALOG_PRINT_NOT_CONFIRMED'
          }else{
            $out.status='PRODUCT_ID_NOT_VERIFIED_FOR_CARD'
            $out.error=if($numberEvidence){'SCORED_PAGE_NOT_IN_CATALOG_CANDIDATES'}else{'SCORED_PAGE_NUMBER_FAILED'}
          }
          Append-Observation $unverifyPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
        } elseif($out.version_family_candidate_used -eq $true){
          # V1/V2 product ID proves the actual page within the observed family.
          # It does not distinguish Pokeball/Masterball or other print details.
          $sameProduct=([string]$candidateId -eq [string]$out.version_family_candidate_id -and
            ([string]$out.url).Split('?')[0] -eq [string]$out.version_family_candidate_url)
          if($sameProduct -and $numberEvidence){
            $out.status='PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'
            $out.error='VERSION_FAMILY_PAGE_AND_NUMBER_PROVED_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'
          } else {
            $out.status='PRODUCT_ID_NOT_VERIFIED_FOR_CARD'
            $out.error='VERSION_FAMILY_LIVE_PRODUCT_PAGE_MISMATCH'
          }
          Append-Observation $unverifyPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
        } elseif ($setSlugGuards.ContainsKey([string]$case.set_id) -and
                  ([string]$out.url -notmatch ('/Products/Singles/'+[regex]::Escape($setSlugGuards[[string]$case.set_id])+'(?:/|-)'))) {
          $out.status='PRODUCT_SET_SLUG_MISMATCH'
          $out.error='PRODUCT_IS_IN_DIFFERENT_CARDMARKET_EXPANSION'
          Append-Observation $unverifyPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
        } elseif ($out.catalog_direct_id_verified -eq $true -and
                  (([string]$case.foil) -or
                   ([string]$case.stempel -notin @('','[]') -and
                    [string]$case.stempel -notmatch '(?i)1[.] auflage|1st.edition|first.edition') -or
                   ([string]$case.sonderdruck -notin @('','unbegrenzt','unlimited')))) {
          # Exact special art / stamped / rare foil print cannot be proved
          # solely by a page's canonical idProduct. Never reuse the ordinary
          # offer filter price for a hidden physical variant.
          $out.status='PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'
          $out.error='CATALOG_ID_AND_CARD_NUMBER_VERIFIED_EXACT_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'
          Append-Observation $unverifyPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
        } elseif ($out.reference_page_used -eq $true -and [string]$case.reference_print_verified -eq 'NO' -and [string]$case.reference_standard_filter_supported -ne 'YES') {
          # Direct URL proves the page/print family, not the special edition.
          # A single WP4 product is shared by different movie-stamp variants;
          # Aerodactyl V3 includes more than one prerelease foil pattern.
          # No exact-print median/route may be published from this status.
          if($numberEvidence -and $setSlugGuards.ContainsKey([string]$case.set_id)){
            $out.status='PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'
            $out.error='EXACT_PRINT_STAMP_OR_FOIL_NEEDS_INDEPENDENT_EVIDENCE'
          }else{
            $out.status='PRODUCT_ID_NOT_VERIFIED_FOR_CARD'
            $out.error='REFERENCE_URL_CARD_NUMBER_OR_SET_UNVERIFIED'
          }
          Append-Observation $unverifyPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
        } elseif ($candidateId -ne [string]$case.catalog_singleton_id -and
                  -not ([string]$case.routing_state -eq 'VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID' -and $candidateId -in $allowed)) {
          # A product can be proven in the CORRECT CARD/SET but not be proven
          # to match this particular physical edition (1999-2000, 1st Ed,
          # shadowless, reverse, V1/V2). These are different states.
          if(($candidateId -in $cardCandidates -or $candidateId -in $allowed) -and $numberEvidence -and
             $setSlugGuards.ContainsKey([string]$case.set_id)) {
            $out.status = 'PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'
            $out.error = 'CARD_AND_COLLECTOR_NUMBER_MATCH_PRINT_NEEDS_EVIDENCE'
          } else {
            $out.status = 'PRODUCT_ID_NOT_VERIFIED_FOR_CARD'
            $out.error = 'SET_CARD_NUMBER_OR_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'
          }
          Append-Observation $unverifyPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
        } else {
          # Identitaet bestaetigt: ID + URL dauerhaft, unabhaengig vom Preis.
          $out.identity_confirmed = $true
          if($out.catalog_direct_id_verified -eq $true -or $out.reference_page_used -eq $true){$out.exact_print_verified=$false}
          if($out.reference_page_used -eq $true -and [string]$case.reference_standard_filter_supported -eq 'YES'){
            $out.standard_offer_filter_candidate=$true
          }
          if (-not $priceNeeded) {
            $out.status = 'MAPPED_NO_PRICE_REQUIRED'
          } else {
            $out.status = 'PRODUCT_PAGE_MATCH_REVIEW'
            $reverseCode = if ([string]$case.drucktyp -match '(?i)^reverse$') { 'Y' } elseif ([string]$case.drucktyp -match '(?i)^(normal|nonholo|common|holo)$') { 'N' } else { '0' }
            $firstCode = if ([string]$case.stempel -match '(?i)1\. auflage|1st.edition|first.edition') { 'Y' } elseif ([string]$case.sonderdruck -match '(?i)^(unbegrenzt|unlimited|copyright 1999-2000|1999-2000-copyright)$') { 'N' } else { '0' }
            $out.expected_reverse_holo = $reverseCode
            $out.expected_first_edition = $firstCode
            # A provider filter cannot distinguish ©1999–2000, special
            # cosmo foil patterns or collection stamps; never claim the
            # filtered result proves that physical special print.
            $out.exact_print_filter_supported=(@($out.non_filterable_print_attributes).Count -eq 0 -and $reverseCode -ne '0' -and $firstCode -ne '0')
            $filterName = 'input[name=\"language[' + $LangCfg.LanguageId + ']\"]'
            $langNames = ($LangCfg.LanguageNames | ForEach-Object { '"'+$_+'"' }) -join ','
            $filterOk=$false
            # Prefer canonical server-side parameters; verify the returned
            # language, condition and print flags before trusting any rows.
            # If the provider does not honor these parameters, restore the
            # product page and use the existing validated form as fallback.
            if($reverseCode -ne '0' -and $directUrlFilterSupported){
              try {
                $targetFilter=New-CardmarketOfferFilterUrl -ProductUrl ([string]$out.url) -LanguageId ([int]$LangCfg.LanguageId) -ReverseCode $reverseCode -FirstEditionCode $firstCode
                $out.direct_filter_attempted=$true
                if(Move-To $targetFilter){
                  $firstCheck=if($firstCode -eq '0'){'true'}else{'u.searchParams.get("isFirstEd")==="'+$firstCode+'"'}
                  $filterValid=Js ('(()=>{const u=new URL(location.href);const f=[...document.forms].find(x=>x.action.includes("Product_Filter_FilterProduct"));const l=f?.querySelector("' + $filterName + '");const lang=[...document.querySelectorAll("input")].filter(x=>x.name.startsWith("language[")&&x.checked);return !!f&&!!l&&l.checked&&lang.length===1&&u.searchParams.get("language")==="'+$LangCfg.LanguageId+'"&&u.searchParams.get("minCondition")==="2"&&u.searchParams.get("isSigned")==="N"&&u.searchParams.get("isAltered")==="N"&&f.elements.namedItem("extra[isSigned]")?.value==="N"&&f.elements.namedItem("extra[isAltered]")?.value==="N"&&u.searchParams.get("isReverseHolo")==="'+$reverseCode+'"&&'+$firstCheck+'})()')
                  if($filterValid){$filterOk=$true;$out.filter_mode='CANONICAL_URL_DOM_VERIFIED'}else{$directUrlFilterSupported=$false;$out.direct_filter_fallback='PROVIDER_URL_FILTER_NOT_VERIFIED'}
                }
              } catch {
                if([string]$_ -match 'CARDMARKET_PROVIDER_CHALLENGE|CARDMARKET_LOGIN_REQUIRED'){throw}
                $out.direct_filter_fallback='PARAMETERS_NOT_VERIFIED'
                $directUrlFilterSupported=$false
              }
            }
            if(!$filterOk){
              if($out.direct_filter_attempted){
                $restoreUrl=([string]$out.url).Split('?')[0]
                if(-not (Move-To $restoreUrl)){throw 'FILTER_RESTORE_PRODUCT_FAILED'}
              }
              Wait-CardmarketRequestBudget
              $filterOk = Js ('(()=>{let f=[...document.forms].find(f=>f.action.includes("Product_Filter_FilterProduct"));let l=f?.querySelector("' + $filterName + '");if(!l)return false;[...f.querySelectorAll("input")].filter(x=>x.name.startsWith("language[")).forEach(x=>x.checked=false);l.checked=true;f.querySelector("[name=minCondition]").value="2";f.querySelector("[name=\"extra[isSigned]\"]").value="N";f.querySelector("[name=\"extra[isAltered]\"]").value="N";const choices=[["extra[isReverseHolo]","'+$reverseCode+'"],["extra[isFirstEd]","'+$firstCode+'"]];for(const [name,want] of choices){const el=f.elements.namedItem(name);if(!el){if(want==="Y")return false;continue;}el.value=want;}f.requestSubmit(f.querySelector("[name=apply]"));return true})()')
              if($filterOk){$out.filter_mode='FORM_SUBMISSION_DOM_VERIFIED'}
            }
            if ($filterOk) {
              # A deliberately bounded but larger collector window.
              # Never mark a market as complete if the provider still exposes
              # more offers at this limit.
              $offerCap=1200
              $maxOfferPages=24
              $rowsExpr = 'JSON.stringify({filtered:document.querySelector("' + $filterName + '")?.checked===true&&[...document.querySelectorAll("input")].filter(x=>x.name.startsWith("language[")&&x.checked).length===1&&new URL(location.href).searchParams.get("language")==="'+$LangCfg.LanguageId+'"&&new URL(location.href).searchParams.get("minCondition")==="2"&&document.querySelector("[name=minCondition]")?.value==="2"&&new URL(location.href).searchParams.get("isSigned")==="N"&&new URL(location.href).searchParams.get("isAltered")==="N"&&[["isReverseHolo","'+$reverseCode+'"],["isFirstEd","'+$firstCode+'"]].every(([k,v])=>{const u=new URL(location.href);const f=[...document.forms].find(x=>x.action.includes("Product_Filter_FilterProduct"));const e=f?.elements.namedItem("extra["+k+"]");return v==="0"?(!u.searchParams.has(k)||u.searchParams.get(k)==="0"):(e?(u.searchParams.get(k)===v&&e.value===v):(v==="N"&&!u.searchParams.has(k)))}),rows:[...document.querySelectorAll(".article-row")].slice(0,1200).map(r=>({id:r.id,condition:r.querySelector(".article-condition")?.getAttribute("data-bs-original-title")||r.querySelector(".article-condition")?.getAttribute("title"),language:[...r.querySelectorAll("[data-bs-original-title],[title]")].map(n=>n.getAttribute("data-bs-original-title")||n.getAttribute("title")).find(t=>[' + $langNames + '].includes(t)),price:r.querySelector(".price-container")?.innerText.trim(),comment:r.querySelector(".article-comment,.article-remarks,.article-comment-text,[data-testid=article-comment]")?.textContent?.trim()||null,commentVisible:!!r.querySelector(".article-comment,.article-remarks,.article-comment-text,[data-testid=article-comment]"),flags:[...r.querySelectorAll("[title],[data-bs-original-title]")].map(x=>x.getAttribute("data-bs-original-title")||x.getAttribute("title")).filter(x=>x==="Reverse Holo"||x==="First Edition")})),hasMore:!!document.querySelector("#loadMoreButton"),page:Number(document.querySelector("#articlePage")?.value||0),totalRows:document.querySelectorAll(".article-row").length})'
              $data = $null
              for ($fi = 0; $fi -lt 12; $fi++) {
                Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500
                $data = Js $rowsExpr | ConvertFrom-Json
                if ($data -and $data.filtered) { break }
              }
              if (!$data) { throw 'FILTER_STATE_UNREADABLE' }
              $out.offer_pages_attempted=0
              $out.pagination_failure=''
              # Public mode collects visible rows, without assuming that a
              # public session can paginate or that its sample is exhaustive.
              if (-not $auth) { $out.pagination_failure='PUBLIC_SESSION_LIMITED_SAMPLE' }
              # Logged-in mode progressively appends offer blocks (provider cap unknown).
              if ($data.filtered -and $auth) {
                for ($pageAttempt=0; $pageAttempt -lt $maxOfferPages -and $data.hasMore; $pageAttempt++) {
                  $beforeRows=[int]$data.totalRows
                  if ($beforeRows -ge $offerCap) { $out.pagination_failure='CARDMARKET_COLLECTOR_CAP';break }
                  $out.offer_pages_attempted++
                  # Do not mistake a hidden/disabled load-more button for a successful click.
                  Wait-CardmarketRequestBudget
                  $clicked=Js '(()=>{const b=document.querySelector("#loadMoreButton");if(!b)return "MISSING";if(b.disabled||b.classList.contains("d-none")||getComputedStyle(b).display==="none")return "DISABLED";b.click();return "CLICKED"})()'
                  if ($clicked -ne 'CLICKED') {
                    $out.pagination_failure=if($clicked -eq 'DISABLED') {'LOAD_MORE_DISABLED_OR_LIMIT_REACHED'} else {'LOAD_MORE_BUTTON_LOST'}
                    break
                  }
                  $progressed=$false
                  for ($wait=0; $wait -lt 18; $wait++) {
                    Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 500
                    $next=Js $rowsExpr | ConvertFrom-Json
                    if (!$next.filtered) { $out.pagination_failure='FILTER_CHANGED_DURING_PAGINATION';break }
                    if ([int]$next.totalRows -gt $beforeRows) {
                      $progressed=$true
                      $data=$next
                      break
                    }
                  }
                  if (!$progressed) {
                    if (!$out.pagination_failure) {
                      $disabled=Js '(()=>{const b=document.querySelector("#loadMoreButton");return !!b&&(b.disabled||b.classList.contains("d-none")||getComputedStyle(b).display==="none")})()'
                      $out.pagination_failure=if ($disabled) {'LOAD_MORE_DISABLED_OR_LIMIT_REACHED'} else {'LOAD_MORE_NO_NEW_OFFERS'}
                    }
                    break
                  }
                  Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 800
                }
              }
              # A bounded iteration count is not proof of the end.
              if([int]$data.totalRows -ge $offerCap -and !$out.pagination_failure){
                $out.pagination_failure='CARDMARKET_COLLECTOR_CAP'
              }
              if($data.hasMore -and !$out.pagination_failure){
                $out.pagination_failure='MORE_OFFERS_STILL_VISIBLE'
              }
              # Login may expire while fetching pages; a public result is
              # never promoted into a full-market price on that transition.
              $stillSignedIn = (Js '!!document.querySelector("a[href*=User_Logout]")') -and (-not $ForcePublicMode)
              if (-not $stillSignedIn) {
                $auth=$false
                $out.access_mode='PUBLIC_LIMITED'
                if ($RequireLogin) { throw 'CARDMARKET_LOGIN_REQUIRED' }
                if (-not $out.pagination_failure) { $out.pagination_failure='SESSION_EXPIRED_OR_PUBLIC' }
              }
              # Re-read the actual final DOM after AJAX pagination: an old
              # `hasMore` snapshot can remain true after the last successful
              # batch, while the load-more element has disappeared. Require
              # a stable, strictly matching row count + final missing button;
              # a hidden/disabled button alone never proves completeness.
              if($auth -and $data.filtered -and [int]$data.totalRows -gt 0 -and
                 [int]$data.totalRows -lt $offerCap -and $out.pagination_failure -in
                   @('LOAD_MORE_DISABLED_OR_LIMIT_REACHED','LOAD_MORE_BUTTON_LOST','LOAD_MORE_NO_NEW_OFFERS')) {
                $stableEnd=$false
                $finalCount=[int]$data.totalRows
                for($settle=0;$settle -lt 4;$settle++){
                  Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 700
                  $last=Js $rowsExpr | ConvertFrom-Json
                  if(!$last -or !$last.filtered -or [int]$last.totalRows -ne $finalCount){break}
                  # The end of Cardmarket's AJAX list is indicated by removal
                  # of #loadMoreButton, not just a disabled element.
                  $lastButton=Js '(()=>{const b=document.querySelector("#loadMoreButton");return b?"PRESENT":"ABSENT"})()'
                  if($lastButton -ne 'ABSENT'){break}
                  if($settle -eq 3){$stableEnd=$true;$data=$last}
                }
                if($stableEnd){$out.pagination_failure='';$out.pagination_end_evidence='BUTTON_REMOVED_STABLE_4_CHECKS'}
              }
              $out.all_pages_complete=($auth -and $data.filtered -and
                -not $data.hasMore -and [int]$data.totalRows -lt $offerCap -and
                -not $out.pagination_failure)
              # Qualitaetskontrolle (Punkt 5): nur Zeilen, die Sprach- UND
              # Zustandskriterium einzeln erfuellen, zaehlen als Filtertreffer.
              $out.offers_seen = @($data.rows).Count
              $accepted = @($data.rows | Where-Object {
                $_.language -in $LangCfg.LanguageNames -and
                $_.condition -in $LangCfg.ConditionNames -and
                ($_.condition -notmatch 'Played|Poor|Damaged') -and
                $_.price -match '[0-9]' -and
                ($reverseCode -eq '0' -or
                  (($reverseCode -eq 'Y') -eq ('Reverse Holo' -in @($_.flags)))) -and
                ($firstCode -eq '0' -or
                  (($firstCode -eq 'Y') -eq ('First Edition' -in @($_.flags))))
              })
              # Seller comments can reject explicitly mislabeled special
              # prints but cannot automatically prove an unlabeled foil.
              $targetPrint=''
              $f=[string]$case.foil
              if($f -match '(?i)meisterball|masterball'){$targetPrint='masterball'}
              elseif($f -match '(?i)pok[eé]ball|poke.?ball'){$targetPrint='pokeball'}
              elseif([string]$case.stempel -match '(?i)professorenprogramm|professor.program'){$targetPrint='stamp:professor-program'}
              elseif([string]$case.stempel -match '(?i)play.?pokemon'){$targetPrint='stamp:play-pokemon'}
              elseif([string]$case.stempel -match '(?i)prerelease'){$targetPrint='stamp:prerelease'}
              if($targetPrint){
                $counts=@{positive=0;contradiction=0;ambiguous=0;missing=0;not_applicable=0;comment_fields_visible=0}
                foreach($row in $accepted){
                  if($row.commentVisible){$counts.comment_fields_visible++}
                  $verdict=Get-CardmarketOfferCommentClassification -Comment ([string]$row.comment) -TargetPrint $targetPrint
                  $keyName=$verdict.ToLowerInvariant()
                  $counts[$keyName]++
                }
                $out.special_print_comment_evidence=@{
                    target=$targetPrint;counts=$counts;source='VISIBLE_SELLER_COMMENTS_ONLY';
                    unlabeled_offers_not_verified=$true;not_print_proof=$true;
                    all_pages_complete=[bool]$out.all_pages_complete
                }
              }
              $out.verified_lang_nm_offers = $accepted.Count
              $out.language_offers_observed=($accepted.Count -gt 0)
              $seenOfferIds=@($data.rows | ForEach-Object { [string]$_.id })
              $uniqueOfferIds=@($seenOfferIds | Sort-Object -Unique)
              if ($uniqueOfferIds.Count -ne [int]$data.totalRows -or $seenOfferIds -contains '') {
                $out.all_pages_complete=$false
                $out.pagination_failure='DUPLICATE_OR_MISSING_OFFER_ID'
              }
              if ($accepted.Count -ne [int]$data.totalRows) {
                $out.all_pages_complete=$false
                if (!$out.pagination_failure) { $out.pagination_failure='MISMATCHED_OFFER_FILTER_FLAGS' }
              }

              if ($data.filtered -and $accepted.Count -ge 1) {
                $nums = @($accepted | ForEach-Object {
                  Parse-Cardmarket-Euro ([string]$_.price)
                } | Where-Object { $_ -ne $null } | Sort-Object)
                if ($nums.Count -gt 0) {
                  $out.lowest_ask_eur=$nums[0]
                  $cheapest=@($nums | Select-Object -First 5)
                  $out.entry_five_median_eur=if($cheapest.Count%2) {$cheapest[[int][Math]::Floor($cheapest.Count/2)]}else{[Math]::Round(($cheapest[$cheapest.Count/2-1]+$cheapest[$cheapest.Count/2])/2,2)}
                  $mid = [int][Math]::Floor($nums.Count/2)
                  $out.sample_median_eur = if ($nums.Count % 2) { $nums[$mid] } else { [math]::Round(($nums[$mid-1]+$nums[$mid])/2, 2) }
                }
                $out.median_all_offers_eur=if($out.all_pages_complete) {$out.sample_median_eur} else {$null}
                $out.status=if($out.all_pages_complete) {'COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'} else {'FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE'}
              } elseif ($data.filtered) {
                $out.status = 'FILTER_UNVERIFIED_EMPTY'
              } else {
                $out.status = 'FILTER_NOT_APPLIED'
              }
            } else {
              $out.status = 'FILTER_UNAVAILABLE'
            }
          }
          if ($out.identity_confirmed -and $out.product_id -match '^[0-9]+$') {
            $knownProductPages["$($case.cardyssey_karte)|$($out.product_id)"] = $out.url.Split('?')[0]
          }
        }
      } else {
        $out.status = if ($likely.Count -gt 1) { 'MULTIPLE_SEARCH_HITS' } elseif ($out.search_results_unverified_empty -eq $true) { 'DEFERRED_SEARCH_RESULTS_UNVERIFIED' } else { 'NO_EXACT_NUMBER_SEARCH_HIT' }
        if ($out.status -eq 'NO_EXACT_NUMBER_SEARCH_HIT') { $out.error='NAME_NUMBER_TEXT_SEARCH_NOT_CONCLUSIVE' }
        Append-Observation $unverifyPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
      }
    } catch {
      $message = $_.Exception.Message
      if ($message -match 'CARDMARKET_LOGIN_REQUIRED') {
        $out.status='LOGIN_REQUIRED'
        $out.error='CARDMARKET_LOGIN_REQUIRED'
        $out.elapsed_ms=[int]$caseTimer.ElapsedMilliseconds
        Append-Observation $resultsPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
        Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'LOGIN_REQUIRED' $case.cardyssey_karte
        break
      }
      if ($message -match 'CARDMARKET_PROVIDER_CHALLENGE') {
        $out.status = 'BLOCKED'
        $out.error = 'CARDMARKET_PROVIDER_CHALLENGE'
        $out.elapsed_ms=[int]$caseTimer.ElapsedMilliseconds
        Append-Observation $resultsPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
        Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'BLOCKED' $case.cardyssey_karte
        break
      }
      $socketDead = ($ws.State -ne [Net.WebSockets.WebSocketState]::Open) -or
                    ($message -match 'CDP_STREAM_DEAD|CDP_TIMEOUT|WebSocket|websocket|abgebrochen|cancelled|canceled')
      if ($socketDead) {
        $out.status = 'DEFERRED_SOCKET'
        $out.error = $message.Substring(0, [Math]::Min(180, $message.Length))
        Append-Observation $resultsPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
        [pscustomobject]@{at=[DateTime]::UtcNow.ToString('o');card=$case.cardyssey_karte;message=$message;socket=[string]$ws.State} |
          ConvertTo-Json -Compress | ForEach-Object { Append-Observation $errorPath $_ }
        [void]$processed.Add($key)
        $deferredCount++
        Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'RUNNING' $case.cardyssey_karte
        if ($deferredCount -gt 25) {
          Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'CONNECTION_LOST' $case.cardyssey_karte
          break
        }
        Start-Sleep -Seconds 5
        try {
          Connect-Cdp
          # Re-evaluate access mode on the next card; lost login is not fatal.
          $auth = (Js '!!document.querySelector("a[href*=User_Logout]")') -and (-not $ForcePublicMode)
        } catch {
          Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'CONNECTION_LOST' $case.cardyssey_karte
          break
        }
        continue
      }
      $out.status = 'ERROR'
      $out.error = $message.Substring(0, [Math]::Min(180, $message.Length))
    }

    $caseTimer.Stop()
    $out.elapsed_ms=[int]$caseTimer.ElapsedMilliseconds
    Append-Observation $resultsPath ($out | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 6 -Compress)
    [void]$processed.Add($key)
    Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'RUNNING' $case.cardyssey_karte

    if ($out.status -in @('MULTIPLE_SEARCH_HITS','NO_EXACT_NUMBER_SEARCH_HIT','PRODUCT_ID_NOT_IN_VERIFIED_SET')) {
      Start-Sleep -Seconds ([Math]::Max(4, [Math]::Min(6, $DelaySeconds)))
    } else {
      Start-Sleep -Seconds ([Math]::Max(3, $DelaySeconds))
    }
  }

  # --- Qualitaetskontroll-Bericht (Punkt 7) --------------------------------
  $currentStatus=(Get-Content $progressPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).status
  if ($currentStatus -notin @('LOGIN_REQUIRED','BLOCKED','PROVIDER_TIMEOUT','CONNECTION_LOST')) {
    if ($deferredCount -gt 0) { Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'COMPLETED_WITH_DEFERRED' '' }
    elseif ($processed.Count -ge $runTotal) { Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'COMPLETED' '' }
    else { Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'BATCH_COMPLETE_MORE_PENDING' '' }
  }
  $statuses = @{}
  if (Test-Path $resultsPath) {
    Get-Content $resultsPath | ForEach-Object {
      try { $e = $_ | ConvertFrom-Json; if ($e.key) { $statuses[[string]$e.key] = $e } } catch {}
    }
  }
  $validIdentityStatuses = @('FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE','NO_CONFIRMED_FILTERED_OFFERS','FILTER_UNAVAILABLE','FILTER_UNVERIFIED_EMPTY','MAPPED_NO_PRICE_REQUIRED','FILTER_NOT_APPLIED','COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED')
  $confirmed = @($statuses.Values | Where-Object { $_.identity_confirmed -eq $true -and $_.status -in $validIdentityStatuses })
  $withUrl   = @($confirmed | Where-Object { $_.url })
  # Nur bestaetigte Angebots-Mediane zaehlen als verifizierte Preise.
  # Angebotsmedian != offizieller Cardmarket-Trendpreis (daher die Kennzeichnung).
  $withPrice = @($statuses.Values | Where-Object { $_.sample_median_eur -ne $null -and
                 $_.status -in @('FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE','COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED') })
  $missing = @($selected | Where-Object {
      $k = '{0}|{1}' -f $_.cardyssey_karte,$_.variante_id
      (-not $statuses.ContainsKey($k)) -or ($statuses[$k].identity_confirmed -ne $true) -or ($statuses[$k].status -notin $validIdentityStatuses)
  })
  $reasons = @($missing | ForEach-Object {
      $k = '{0}|{1}' -f $_.cardyssey_karte,$_.variante_id
      if (!$statuses.ContainsKey($k)) { 'NOT_ATTEMPTED' } else { [string]$statuses[$k].status }
  } | Group-Object | ForEach-Object { '{0}: {1}' -f $_.Name,$_.Count })
  $report = [ordered]@{
    language               = $Language
    expected_variants      = $runTotal
    bulk_feed_only_variants = $bulkFeedOnly
    search_cache_hits = $searchCacheHits
    search_cache_misses = $searchCacheMisses
    progress_status        = (Get-Content $progressPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json).status
    reviewed_variants      = $statuses.Count
    assigned_product_ids   = $confirmed.Count
    stored_urls            = $withUrl.Count
    filtered_offer_sample_quotes = $withPrice.Count
    complete_filtered_asking_medians_print_unverified = @($statuses.Values | Where-Object { $_.status -eq 'COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED' }).Count
    verified_language_variant_prices = 0
    price_basis            = 'active_asking_offers_not_sold; full_only_if_completed'
    missing_assignments    = $missing.Count
    missing_by_reason      = $reasons
    unreviewed_variants    = [Math]::Max(0,$runTotal-$statuses.Count)
    deferred_socket_errors = $deferredCount
    generated_at           = [DateTime]::UtcNow.ToString('o')
  }
  ($report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 4) | Set-Content -Encoding UTF8 -Path (Join-Path $OutputDir "qc-report-$Language.json")
  Write-Host ($report | ConvertTo-Json -Depth 4)
} finally {
  try { $script:rxCts.Cancel() } catch {}
  try { $ws.Dispose() } catch {}
}


############################################################################################
# DATEI 021/107: scripts/cardmarket-live-failures.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Paginated admin-only scan failures, read directly from scanner JSONL logs.
The worklist remains distinct from catalog print review. No guesses or writes.
"""
import argparse,csv,json,importlib.util,subprocess
from datetime import datetime,timezone
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit
BASE=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector')
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('filter_readiness',Path(__file__).with_name('cardmarket-filter-readiness.py'))
filter_readiness=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(filter_readiness)
SUCCESS={'MAPPED_NO_PRICE_REQUIRED','COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED','COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN','SOURCE_ONLY','SUCCESS','SET_MAPPING_PENDING'}
PRICE_LIMIT_STATUSES={'FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE','NO_CONFIRMED_FILTERED_OFFERS','FILTER_UNAVAILABLE','FILTER_UNVERIFIED_EMPTY','FILTER_NOT_APPLIED'}
TECHNICAL={'BLOCKED','LOGIN_REQUIRED','ERROR','PROVIDER_ERROR','PROVIDER_TIMEOUT','CONNECTION_LOST','FILTER_UNAVAILABLE','FILTER_UNVERIFIED_EMPTY','FILTER_NOT_APPLIED','NO_CONFIRMED_FILTERED_OFFERS','FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE'}

def classify(status,entry=None,queue=None):
    if status=='PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED' and entry and queue:
        if (entry.get('catalog_direct_id_verified') is True and
            str(entry.get('product_id') or '')==str(queue.get('catalog_singleton_id') or '') and
            str(queue.get('catalog_expansion_id') or '').isdigit()):
            # Only unfilterable special prints need a human. A standard
            # normal/holo/reverse can be handled by the existing filtered
            # price pipeline; identity evidence by itself is never a price.
            attrs=filter_readiness.analyze(queue)
            if attrs['unsupported'] or attrs['missing'] or str(queue.get('sonderdruck') or '').strip():
                return 'DRUCKPRUEFUNG'
            return 'PREISPRUEFUNG'
    # A separately known and set-guarded card page makes same-name search
    # failures redundant: the only missing information is the physical stamp
    # or foil. Keep these in a DISTINCT print-review tab; do not falsely
    # suggest the user must identify which Pokémon or set it is again.
    if queue and status in ('MULTIPLE_SEARCH_HITS','NO_EXACT_NUMBER_SEARCH_HIT','PRODUCT_ID_NOT_VERIFIED_FOR_CARD'):
        ref=str(queue.get('reference_product_url') or '')
        stamp=str(queue.get('stempel') or '[]')
        foil=str(queue.get('foil') or '')
        sid=str(queue.get('set_id') or '')
        guard_path=Path('/home/tv/cardshop/config/cardmarket-set-url-guards.json')
        try:guards=json.loads(guard_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        except (OSError,ValueError):guards={}
        try:
            parsed=urlsplit(ref)
            approved=parsed.scheme=='https' and parsed.hostname=='www.cardmarket.com' and f'/Pokemon/Products/Singles/{guards.get(sid,"__UNKNOWN__")}/' in parsed.path
        except ValueError:approved=False
        if approved and (stamp not in ('','[]') or foil):return 'DRUCKPRUEFUNG'
    # Card+product match is established. Incomplete price pagination does NOT
    # create an identity/print decision for the founder. Scope this rule to
    # 30th sets until other releases have been independently audited.
    if status in PRICE_LIMIT_STATUSES and entry and queue and str(queue.get('set_id') or '').startswith('30th'):
        pid=str(entry.get('product_id') or '')
        candidates={x.strip() for x in str(queue.get('cardmarket_ids') or '').split(',') if x.strip()}
        known_route=str(queue.get('known_url') or '')
        if entry.get('identity_confirmed') is True and pid.isdigit() and pid in candidates and (known_route or queue.get('routing_state')=='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'):
            return 'PREISPRUEFUNG'
        if entry.get('identity_confirmed') is True and pid.isdigit() and candidates and pid not in candidates:
            return 'ZUORDNUNG'

    if status=='MISSING_REQUIRED_SCAN_FIELDS':return 'DATEN_FEHLEN'
    if status in SUCCESS:return None
    if status in TECHNICAL or status.startswith('DEFERRED') or status.startswith('FILTER_') or status.startswith('NO_CONFIRMED'):return 'TECHNISCH'
    if status.startswith('COMPLETE_') or status in ('STARTED','RUNNING'):return None
    return 'ZUORDNUNG'

def reviewed_decisions(language):
    """Current decisions for exact (card, physical variant), DB read only."""
    if language not in ('de','en'):raise ValueError('invalid_language')
    sql=("SELECT card_id,variant_id,status,reviewed_at::text,COALESCE(selected_product_id,'') "
         "FROM cardmarket_print_identity_review WHERE language_code='"+language+
         "' AND status IN ('CONFIRMED_PRINT','NO_MATCH','DEFERRED') AND reviewed_at IS NOT NULL")
    try:
        p=subprocess.run(['docker','exec','cardyssey-db','psql','-X','-qAt','-v','ON_ERROR_STOP=1','-F','|','-U','cardyssey','-d','cardyssey','-c',sql],
                         capture_output=True,text=True,check=True,timeout=12)
    except (subprocess.SubprocessError,OSError):return {}
    out={}
    for line in p.stdout.splitlines():
        fields=line.split('|')
        if len(fields)==5:out[(fields[0],fields[1])]={'status':fields[2],'reviewedAt':fields[3],'selectedProductId':fields[4]}
    return out

def resolved_after_scan(observation,decision):
    if not decision or decision.get('status') not in ('CONFIRMED_PRINT','NO_MATCH','DEFERRED'):return False
    # Once the exact physical print has been human-confirmed, a subsequent
    # name-only/no-result browser failure does not erase that confirmation.
    # A NEW positive contradictory product ID is still surfaced for debugging.
    if decision['status']=='CONFIRMED_PRINT':
        product=str(observation.get('product_id') or '')
        chosen=str(decision.get('selectedProductId') or '')
        if chosen and (not product or product==chosen):return True
    try:
        reviewed=datetime.fromisoformat(str(decision['reviewedAt']).replace('Z','+00:00'))
        seen=datetime.fromisoformat(str(observation.get('seen_at') or '').replace('Z','+00:00'))
        if reviewed.tzinfo is None:reviewed=reviewed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        if seen.tzinfo is None:seen=seen.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        return reviewed>=seen
    except (TypeError,ValueError):return False

def list_failures(base=BASE,language='de',page=0,search='',scope='current',kind='karten',decisions=None):
    if language not in ('de','en'):raise ValueError('invalid_language')
    if scope not in ('current','all'):raise ValueError('invalid_scope')
    if kind not in ('karten','preise','technik','drucke','alle'):raise ValueError('invalid_kind')
    if not 0<=page<=2000:raise ValueError('invalid_page')
    epoch=None
    ep=base/'full-rescan-epoch.json'
    if ep.exists():
        try:epoch=datetime.fromisoformat(json.loads(ep.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))['requestedAt'].replace('Z','+00:00'))
        except (ValueError,KeyError):pass
    rows={}
    p=base/f'browser-observations-{language}.jsonl'
    if p.exists():
        with p.open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
            for line in f:
                try:entry=json.loads(line)
                except (TypeError,ValueError):continue
                card=str(entry.get('card_id') or '');var=str(entry.get('variant_id') or '')
                if not card or not var:continue
                if scope=='current' and epoch:
                    try:
                        seen=datetime.fromisoformat(str(entry.get('seen_at','')).replace('Z','+00:00'))
                        if seen<epoch:continue
                    except ValueError:continue
                rows[(card,var)]=entry
    queue={}
    with (base/f'queue-{language}.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
        for r in csv.DictReader(f):queue[(r.get('cardyssey_karte'),r.get('variante_id'))]=r
    if decisions is None:decisions=reviewed_decisions(language) if base==BASE else {}
    output=[]
    for key,entry in rows.items():
        if resolved_after_scan(entry,decisions.get(key)):continue
        status=str(entry.get('status') or '')
        q=queue.get(key,{})
        group=classify(status,entry,q)
        if not group:continue
        card,var=key
        readiness=filter_readiness.analyze(q) if q else {'missing':[],'unsupported':[]}
        item={'printStamp':str(q.get('stempel') or '[]'),'printFoil':str(q.get('foil') or ''),
          'referenceUrl':str(q.get('reference_product_url') or ''),
          'missingFields':entry.get('missing_fields') or readiness['missing'],
          'unfilterablePrintFeatures':readiness['unsupported'],
          'cardId':card,'variantId':var,'setId':q.get('set_id') or entry.get('set_id') or card.rsplit('-',1)[0],
          'name':q.get('kartenname_suche') or entry.get('name') or card,
          'language':language,'status':status,'type':group,
          'detail':str(entry.get('error') or entry.get('pagination_failure') or entry.get('reason') or '')[:220],
          'productId':str(entry.get('product_id') or ''), 'seenAt':entry.get('seen_at') or '',
          'scoreMatchState':str(entry.get('score_matching_status') or ''),
          'scorePageCandidateOnly':entry.get('score_page_candidate_only') is True,
          'scoreCandidateUrls':[u for u in (entry.get('score_top_candidate_urls') or [])[:5]
             if isinstance(u,str) and u.startswith('https://www.cardmarket.com/')],
          'filterMode':str(entry.get('filter_mode') or ''),
          'officialProductGuide':entry.get('official_guide_product_aggregate') or None,
          'specialPrintComments':entry.get('special_print_comment_evidence') or None}
        if search.strip() and search.strip().casefold() not in ' '.join(str(v) for v in item.values()).casefold():continue
        output.append(item)
    output.sort(key=lambda e:e['seenAt'],reverse=True)
    full_counts={'assignment':sum(x['type']=='ZUORDNUNG' for x in output),
            'technical':sum(x['type']=='TECHNISCH' for x in output),
            'printChecks':sum(x['type']=='DRUCKPRUEFUNG' for x in output),
            'priceChecks':sum(x['type']=='PREISPRUEFUNG' for x in output),
            'missingData':sum(x['type']=='DATEN_FEHLEN' for x in output)}
    if kind=='karten':output=[x for x in output if x['type'] in ('ZUORDNUNG','DATEN_FEHLEN')]
    elif kind=='preise':output=[x for x in output if x['type']=='PREISPRUEFUNG']
    elif kind=='technik':output=[x for x in output if x['type']=='TECHNISCH']
    elif kind=='drucke':output=[x for x in output if x['type']=='DRUCKPRUEFUNG']
    return {'language':language,'scope':scope,'kind':kind,'total':len(output),'page':page,'pageSize':50,
            **full_counts,
            'items':output[page*50:(page+1)*50]}
if __name__=='__main__':
    a=argparse.ArgumentParser();a.add_argument('--language',choices=['de','en'],default='de');a.add_argument('--scope',choices=['all','current'],default='current');a.add_argument('--page',type=int,default=0);a.add_argument('--search',default='');a.add_argument('--kind',choices=['karten','preise','technik','drucke','alle'],default='karten');v=a.parse_args()
    print(json.dumps(list_failures(language=v.language,page=v.page,search=v.search,scope=v.scope,kind=v.kind),ensure_ascii=False))

# DATEI 023/107: scripts/cardmarket-manual-product-review.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Preview a user-supplied Cardmarket product link, then record a human-reviewed
exact-card identity as a durable learning example. No guess-based prices.
"""
import argparse
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import secrets
import subprocess
import sys
from datetime import datetime,timezone,timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit,parse_qs

ROOT=Path('/home/tv/cardshop')
BASE=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector')
STORE=Path('/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/manual-product-previews')
PS='/mnt/c/Windows/System32/WindowsPowerShell/v1.0/powershell.exe'
PRODUCT_URL='https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json'
GUIDE_URL='https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/priceGuide/price_guide_6.json'

def valid_link(url):
    if not isinstance(url,str) or len(url)>750:return False
    try:
        p=urlsplit(url)
        if p.scheme!='https' or p.netloc!='www.cardmarket.com' or p.fragment or p.username or p.password:return False
        if re.fullmatch(r'/(de|en)/Pokemon/Products/Singles/[a-zA-Z0-9\-_.~%/]+',p.path):return True
        if p.path=='/Pokemon/Products' and re.fullmatch(r'[0-9]{3,12}',parse_qs(p.query).get('idProduct',[''])[0]):return True
    except ValueError:pass
    return False

def catalog(feed):
    cache=Path('/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/price-feeds')/(hashlib.sha256(feed.encode()).hexdigest()+'.json')
    data=json.loads(cache.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    return {str(x['idProduct']):x for x in data.get('products' if feed==PRODUCT_URL else 'priceGuides',[])}

def db(sql):
    p=subprocess.run(['docker','exec','cardyssey-db','psql','-X','-qAt','-v','ON_ERROR_STOP=1','-U','cardyssey','-d','cardyssey','-c',sql],capture_output=True,text=True)
    if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError(p.stderr[-450:])
    return p.stdout.strip()

def quote(s):return "'"+str(s).replace("'","''")+"'"

def record(lang,card,variant):
    if lang not in ('de','en') or not re.fullmatch(r'[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,128}',card or '') or not re.fullmatch(r'[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,128}',variant or ''):
        raise ValueError('invalid_card_identity')
    sql=f"""SELECT row_to_json(r)::text FROM (
     SELECT language_code,card_id,variant_id,set_id,printed_number,card_name,print_type,
     foil,subtype,stamps,provider_expansion_id,matched_set_ids,status,
     selected_product_id,reviewer_note FROM cardmarket_print_identity_review
     WHERE language_code={quote(lang)} AND card_id={quote(card)} AND variant_id={quote(variant)}
     ) r"""
    text=db(sql)
    if not text:raise ValueError('review_case_not_found')
    return json.loads(text)

def preview(data):
    lang,card,variant=data.get('language'),data.get('cardId'),data.get('variantId')
    item=record(lang,card,variant)
    url=str(data.get('url') or '').strip()
    if not valid_link(url):raise ValueError('invalid_cardmarket_url')
    guards=json.loads((ROOT/'config/cardmarket-set-url-guards.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    expected_slug=guards.get(item['set_id'])
    current_path=urlsplit(url).path
    if expected_slug and '/Products/Singles/' in current_path:
        actual_slug=current_path.split('/')[5] if len(current_path.split('/'))>5 else ''
        if actual_slug!=expected_slug:
            raise ValueError('wrong_set_url_expected_'+expected_slug)
    cmd=[PS,'-NoProfile','-ExecutionPolicy','Bypass','-File',r'C:\Users\TV\AppData\Local\Cardyssey\CardmarketCollector\cardmarket-product-link-preview.ps1','-ProductUrl',url]
    try:proc=subprocess.run(cmd,capture_output=True,text=True,encoding='utf-8',errors='replace',timeout=44)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:raise ValueError('cardmarket_browser_timeout')
    if proc.returncode or '-File-Parameter' in proc.stderr or 'f\u00fcr den -File-Parameter' in proc.stderr:
        if 'CARDMARKET_PROVIDER_CHALLENGE' in proc.stderr:raise ValueError('cardmarket_cloudflare_blocked_try_later')
        if 'PRODUCT_ID_NOT_VISIBLE_IN_BROWSER' in proc.stderr:raise ValueError('product_id_not_visible')
        raise ValueError('cardmarket_browser_unavailable')
    try:page=json.loads(proc.stdout.strip().splitlines()[-1])
    except (IndexError,json.JSONDecodeError):raise ValueError('invalid_browser_result')
    pid=str(page.get('id') or '')
    resolved=str(page.get('url') or '')
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9]{3,12}',pid) or not valid_link(resolved) or '/Singles/' not in resolved:
        raise ValueError('browser_result_unverified')
    if expected_slug and urlsplit(resolved).path.split('/')[5]!=expected_slug:
        raise ValueError('resolved_product_belongs_to_other_set')
    products=catalog(PRODUCT_URL)
    meta=products.get(pid)
    if meta is None:raise ValueError('product_id_missing_in_official_catalog')
    prices=catalog(GUIDE_URL).get(pid,{})
    expansion=str(meta.get('idExpansion') or '')
    expected=str(item.get('provider_expansion_id') or '')
    warnings=[]
    if expected and expansion!=expected:warnings.append('SET_ID_CONFLICT: Katalog-Kandidat und eingegebener Produktlink liegen in unterschiedlichen Cardmarket-Sets.')
    if pid not in (item.get('matched_set_ids') or []):warnings.append('PRODUCT_NOT_IN_PREVIOUS_CANDIDATES: Der frühere automatische Vorschlag war unvollständig oder falsch.')
    warnings.append('PRINT_UNVERIFIED: Der Produktfeed beweist Holo/Reverse/Edition nicht. Kartenbild und Nummer selbst vergleichen.')
    warnings.append('LANGUAGE_UNVERIFIED: Die Sprache der Cardmarket-Webseite beweist keine deutsche oder englische Kartenausgabe.')
    token=secrets.token_urlsafe(24)
    snap={'token':token,'created':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
          'language':lang,'cardId':card,'variantId':variant,'productId':pid,'productUrl':resolved.split('?')[0],
          'productName':str(meta.get('name') or ''),'expansionId':expansion,
          'pageHeading':str(page.get('heading') or '')[:200],
          'pageBreadcrumbs':page.get('crumbs') if isinstance(page.get('crumbs'),list) else [],
          'cardSet':item.get('set_id'),'cardNumber':item.get('printed_number'),'cardName':item.get('card_name'),
          'printType':item.get('print_type'),'warnings':warnings,'previousCandidates':item.get('matched_set_ids') or [],
          'guide':{k:prices.get(k) for k in ('avg30','avg30-holo','trend','trend-holo')},
          'languageSpecificPrice':None,'languageSpecificPriceStatus':'NOT_SCANNED'}
    STORE.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    target=STORE/(token+'.json');target.write_text(json.dumps(snap,ensure_ascii=False),encoding='utf-8');os.chmod(target,0o600)
    return snap

def confirm(data):
    token=str(data.get('token') or '')
    if not re.fullmatch(r'[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{20,80}',token):raise ValueError('invalid_preview_token')
    path=STORE/(token+'.json')
    if not path.exists():raise ValueError('preview_expired_or_missing')
    snap=json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    born=datetime.fromisoformat(snap['created'])
    if datetime.now(timezone.utc)-born>timedelta(minutes=15):raise ValueError('preview_expired')
    if data.get('exactPrintConfirmed') is not True:raise ValueError('exact_print_confirmation_required')
    if any('CONFLICT' in w or 'NOT_IN_PREVIOUS' in w for w in snap['warnings']) and data.get('overrideMismatch') is not True:
        raise ValueError('acknowledge_previous_catalog_mismatch')
    lang,card,variant=snap['language'],snap['cardId'],snap['variantId']
    current=record(lang,card,variant)
    if current['status'] not in ('PENDING_REVIEW','DEFERRED'):
        raise ValueError('review_case_already_decided')
    pid,url=snap['productId'],snap['productUrl']
    original=','.join(str(x) for x in current.get('matched_set_ids') or [])
    # One transaction preserves a complete audit of the input and the old candidates.
    sql=f"""BEGIN;
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cardmarket_manual_identity_examples (
  id BIGSERIAL PRIMARY KEY,language_code TEXT NOT NULL,card_id TEXT NOT NULL,
  variant_id TEXT NOT NULL,card_set TEXT,card_number TEXT,card_name TEXT,
  print_type TEXT,product_id TEXT NOT NULL,product_url TEXT NOT NULL,
  product_expansion_id TEXT,product_name TEXT,previous_candidate_ids TEXT,
  correction_reason TEXT,created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()
);
WITH reviewed AS (
 UPDATE cardmarket_print_identity_review SET status='CONFIRMED_PRINT',selected_product_id={quote(pid)},
 reviewer_note={quote('Manual link correction: '+str(data.get('note') or '')[:300])},reviewed_at=NOW(),
 candidate_ids=CASE WHEN candidate_ids ? {quote(pid)} THEN candidate_ids ELSE candidate_ids || to_jsonb({quote(pid)}::text) END,
 matched_set_ids=CASE WHEN matched_set_ids ? {quote(pid)} THEN matched_set_ids ELSE matched_set_ids || to_jsonb({quote(pid)}::text) END,
 classification='HUMAN_CONFIRMED_URL_OVERRIDE'
 WHERE language_code={quote(lang)} AND card_id={quote(card)} AND variant_id={quote(variant)}
 AND status IN ('PENDING_REVIEW','DEFERRED') RETURNING card_id
), mapped AS (
 INSERT INTO price_source_routing(language_code,card_id,variant_id,primary_provider,provider_product_id,
 provider_product_url,source_evidence,verification_state,fallback_provider,refresh_days,next_attempt_at)
 SELECT {quote(lang)},{quote(card)},{quote(variant)},'CARDMARKET',{quote(pid)},{quote(url)},
 'Human confirmed exact set, number, language, edition, foil and stamp; supplied URL','VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID','TCGDEX',7,NOW()
 FROM reviewed
 ON CONFLICT(language_code,card_id,variant_id) DO UPDATE SET
 primary_provider='CARDMARKET',provider_product_id=EXCLUDED.provider_product_id,
 provider_product_url=EXCLUDED.provider_product_url,source_evidence=EXCLUDED.source_evidence,
 verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID',next_attempt_at=NOW(),updated_at=NOW()
 WHERE price_source_routing.verification_state<>'VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'
 OR (price_source_routing.primary_provider='CARDMARKET' AND price_source_routing.provider_product_id=EXCLUDED.provider_product_id)
 RETURNING card_id
), trained AS (
 INSERT INTO cardmarket_manual_identity_examples(language_code,card_id,variant_id,card_set,card_number,card_name,
 print_type,product_id,product_url,product_expansion_id,product_name,previous_candidate_ids,correction_reason)
 SELECT {quote(lang)},{quote(card)},{quote(variant)},{quote(snap['cardSet'])},{quote(snap['cardNumber'])},
 {quote(snap['cardName'])},{quote(snap['printType'])},{quote(pid)},{quote(url)},
 {quote(snap['expansionId'])},{quote(snap['productName'])},{quote(original)},
 {quote(' | '.join(snap['warnings']))} FROM mapped RETURNING id
)
SELECT json_build_object('reviewed',(SELECT count(*) FROM reviewed),'mapped',(SELECT count(*) FROM mapped),
 'exampleSaved',(SELECT count(*) FROM trained))::text;
DO $block$ BEGIN
 IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM price_source_routing WHERE language_code={quote(lang)}
  AND card_id={quote(card)} AND variant_id={quote(variant)}
  AND primary_provider='CARDMARKET' AND provider_product_id={quote(pid)}
  AND verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID') THEN
 RAISE EXCEPTION 'existing_verified_mapping_conflict'; END IF;
END $block$;
COMMIT;"""
    output=db(sql)
    result=json.loads(next((x for x in output.splitlines() if x.startswith('{')), '{}'))
    if result.get('mapped')!=1:raise ValueError('existing_verified_mapping_conflict')
    # Queue uses the exact same persisted ID and URL on the next scan.
    from importlib.util import spec_from_file_location,module_from_spec
    src=ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-print-review-admin.py'
    spec=spec_from_file_location('review',src);m=module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(m)
    queueUpdated=m.sync_queue_identity(lang,card,variant,pid,product_url=url)
    cacheUpdated=m.refresh_print_mapping_cache()
    path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
    return {'ok':True,'productId':pid,'productUrl':url,'queueUpdated':queueUpdated,
        'exampleSaved':result.get('exampleSaved')==1,
        'mappingCacheUpdated':cacheUpdated,
        'priceStatus':'PENDING_VERIFIED_LANGUAGE_AND_PRINT_FILTER_SCAN'}

def main():
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser();ap.add_argument('--action',choices=['preview','confirm'],required=True);a=ap.parse_args()
    try:
        req=json.load(sys.stdin)
        if not isinstance(req,dict):raise ValueError('invalid_payload')
        print(json.dumps(preview(req) if a.action=='preview' else confirm(req),ensure_ascii=False))
    except (ValueError,TypeError,RuntimeError,OSError) as e:
        print(json.dumps({'error':str(e)[:250]},ensure_ascii=False));sys.exit(2)
if __name__=='__main__':main()

############################################################################################
# DATEI 025/107: scripts/cardmarket-median-all-offers.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Median of ALL eligible Cardmarket asking offers, not the cheapest subset.

Fail closed on incomplete pages or print/language mismatches. One listing counts
once regardless of its quantity; quantity-weighted median is diagnostic only.
Never writes collection/price cache.
"""
import argparse
import json
import re
import statistics
from collections import Counter
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
from pathlib import Path

VALID_LANGUAGE={"de","en"}
VALID_CONDITION={"NM","MT","NEAR MINT","MINT"}
def money(value):
    if isinstance(value,(int,float,Decimal)):
        v=Decimal(str(value))
    elif isinstance(value,str):
        s=value.strip().replace("€","").replace(" ","")
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9.,]+",s):raise ValueError("invalid price text")
        if "." in s and "," in s:
            s=s.replace(".","").replace(",",".") if s.rfind(",")>s.rfind(".") else s.replace(",","")
        elif "," in s:s=s.replace(",",".")
        elif re.fullmatch(r"\d{1,3}(?:\.\d{3})+",s):s=s.replace(".","")
        v=Decimal(s)
    else:raise ValueError("invalid price type")
    if not v.is_finite() or v<=0:raise ValueError("nonpositive price")
    return v
def median_eur(values):
    return str(statistics.median(values).quantize(Decimal("0.01")))
def evaluate(data):
    expected=data.get("expected") or {}
    lang=expected.get("language")
    pid=str(expected.get("product_id") or "")
    if lang not in VALID_LANGUAGE or not pid.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("expected product ID and de/en required")
    finish=expected.get("finish")
    edition=expected.get("edition")
    if finish not in ("normal","holo","reverse","cosmos"):
        raise ValueError("explicit finish required")
    if edition not in ("unlimited","first_edition","other"):
        raise ValueError("explicit edition required")
    rows=data.get("offers")
    if not isinstance(rows,list):raise ValueError("offers list required")
    prices=[];weighted=[];seen=set();reject=Counter()
    for row in rows:
        if not isinstance(row,dict):reject["invalid_row"]+=1;continue
        id=str(row.get("offer_id") or "").strip()
        if not id or id in seen:reject["duplicate_or_missing_id"]+=1;continue
        seen.add(id)
        if str(row.get("product_id"))!=pid:reject["product"]+=1;continue
        if row.get("language")!=lang:reject["language"]+=1;continue
        if str(row.get("condition") or "").upper() not in VALID_CONDITION:reject["condition"]+=1;continue
        if row.get("finish")!=finish:reject["finish"]+=1;continue
        if row.get("edition")!=edition:reject["edition"]+=1;continue
        if row.get("signed") is not False or row.get("altered") is not False or row.get("graded") is not False:
            reject["modified_or_unverified"]+=1;continue
        if row.get("bundle") is not False:reject["bundle_or_unverified"]+=1;continue
        try:
            price=money(row.get("price_eur"))
            qty=row.get("quantity")
            if type(qty) is not int or qty<1:raise ValueError("missing quantity")
        except (InvalidOperation,ValueError,TypeError):
            reject["price_or_quantity"]+=1;continue
        prices.append(price)
        if len(weighted)+qty<=100000:weighted.extend([price]*qty)
    pages=data.get("pagination") or {}
    count=pages.get("total_offers")
    complete=(
        pages.get("all_pages_fetched") is True and pages.get("capped") is False
        and type(count) is int and count==len(seen)
        and reject["duplicate_or_missing_id"]==0
    )
    result={"product_id":pid,"language":lang,"finish":finish,"edition":edition,
        "listed_offers":len(seen),"accepted_offers":len(prices),
        "reject_reasons":dict(reject),
        "median_all_offers_eur":median_eur(prices) if prices else None,
        "median_quantity_weighted_eur":median_eur(weighted) if weighted else None,
        "all_pages_complete":complete,
        "status":"COMPLETE_ASKING_MEDIAN" if prices and complete else
            "PARTIAL_ASKING_MEDIAN" if prices else "NO_MATCHING_OFFERS",
        "price_basis":"ACTIVE_ASKING_NOT_SOLD",
        "can_publish_print_price":bool(prices and complete and not reject and
           data.get("print_identity_independently_verified") is True)}
    return result
def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("input",type=Path)
    p.add_argument("--output",type=Path)
    a=p.parse_args()
    result=evaluate(json.loads(a.input.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")))
    out=json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n"
    if a.output:a.output.write_text(out,encoding="utf-8")
    print(out,end="")
if __name__=="__main__":main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 026/107: scripts/cardmarket-offer-comments.ps1
############################################################################################

# Cardmarket seller remarks are diagnostic evidence, not an automatic print ID.
# In particular an absent comment does not mean Pokeball for Japanese reverse.
function Get-CardmarketOfferCommentClassification {
 param([string]$Comment,[string]$TargetPrint)
 $text=[string]$Comment
 $target=[string]$TargetPrint
 if([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($text)){return 'MISSING'}
 $mb=($text -match '(?i)(?<!\w)(?:master\s*ball|meister\s*ball|masterball|meisterball)(?!\w)')
 $pb=($text -match '(?i)(?<!\w)(?:pok[eé]\s*ball|pok[eé]ball)(?!\w)')
 $no=($text -match '(?i)\b(?:no|not|kein(?:e|en|er|es)?|ohne|non|nicht|sin|sans)\s+(?:(?:a|ein|eine|an)\s+)?(?:master\s*ball|meister\s*ball|masterball|pok[eé]\s*ball|pok[eé]ball)')
 if($no){return 'AMBIGUOUS'}
 if($target -eq 'masterball'){
   if($pb){return 'CONTRADICTION'}
   if($mb){return 'POSITIVE'}
   return 'MISSING'
 }
 if($target -eq 'pokeball'){
   if($mb){return 'CONTRADICTION'}
   if($pb){return 'POSITIVE'}
   return 'MISSING'
 }
 if($target -like 'stamp:*'){
   $stamp=$target.Substring(6)
   switch($stamp){
     'prerelease' {$ok=($text -match '(?i)\b(?:prerelease|pre.release)\b')}
     'professor-program' {$ok=($text -match '(?i)\b(?:professor.?s?\s+program(?:me)?|professorenprogramm)\b')}
     'play-pokemon' {$ok=($text -match '(?i)\bplay!?\s*pok[eé]mon\b')}
     'cosmos' {$ok=($text -match '(?i)\bcosmos\b')}
     default {$ok=$false}
   }
   if($ok){return 'POSITIVE'}
   return 'MISSING'
 }
 return 'NOT_APPLICABLE'
}
