# Read-only diagnosis of the SAME persistent Cardmarket Edge profile.
# Never accesses password manager contents, credentials, cookies or tokens.
$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$base=Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'Cardyssey\CardmarketCollector'
$dest=Join-Path $base 'browser-session-status.json'
$state=[ordered]@{checkedAt=[DateTime]::UtcNow.ToString('o');provider='CARDMARKET';status='UNKNOWN';reason='';login='UNKNOWN';profileExists=(Test-Path (Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'Cardyssey\CardmarketEdgeProfile'))}
$ws=$null
try {
  $targets=Invoke-RestMethod -Uri 'http://127.0.0.1:9223/json/list' -TimeoutSec 4
  $page=$targets | Where-Object {$_.type -eq 'page' -and ([string]$_.url) -match '^https://www[.]cardmarket[.]com/'} | Select-Object -First 1
  if(!$page){$state.status='NO_CARDMARKET_TAB';$state.reason='Browser tab unavailable'}
  else {
    if([string]$page.title -match '(?i)nur einen moment|just a moment|attention required|security check|checking your browser|access denied|cloudflare to restrict access|too many requests'){
      $state.status='BLOCKED';$state.reason='CLOUDFLARE_CHALLENGE'
    } elseif (!$page.webSocketDebuggerUrl){
      $state.status='BROWSER_UNAVAILABLE'
    } else {
      $ws=[Net.WebSockets.ClientWebSocket]::new()
      $ws.Options.SetRequestHeader('Origin','http://localhost')
      $cts=[Threading.CancellationTokenSource]::new(5000)
      try{$ws.ConnectAsync([uri]$page.webSocketDebuggerUrl,$cts.Token).GetAwaiter().GetResult()|Out-Null}finally{$cts.Dispose()}
      $js='JSON.stringify({title:document.title,blocked:/(nur einen moment|just a moment|attention required|checking your browser|captcha|access denied|cloudflare to restrict access|too many requests)/i.test(document.title)||/(?:you have been blocked|you are unable to access|access denied|error 1020|too many requests|temporarily rate.limited)/i.test((document.body?.innerText||"").slice(0,1700)),loginForm:!!document.querySelector("form[action*=Login],form[action*=login],input[type=password]"),logoutLink:!!document.querySelector("a[href*=User_Logout],form[action*=User_Logout]"),accountHint:!!document.querySelector("a[href*=User_Profile],a[href*=User_Account],a[href*=MyAccount]"),pageReady:document.readyState})'
      $packet=@{id=7;method='Runtime.evaluate';params=@{expression=$js;returnByValue=$true}}|ConvertTo-Json -Compress -Depth 5
      $buffer=[Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetBytes($packet)
      $ws.SendAsync([ArraySegment[byte]]::new($buffer),[Net.WebSockets.WebSocketMessageType]::Text,$true,[Threading.CancellationToken]::None).GetAwaiter().GetResult()|Out-Null
      $result=$null
      $deadline=[DateTime]::UtcNow.AddSeconds(5)
      while([DateTime]::UtcNow -lt $deadline){
        $bytes=New-Object byte[] 65536
        $wait=[Threading.CancellationTokenSource]::new(1500)
        try{$answer=$ws.ReceiveAsync([ArraySegment[byte]]::new($bytes),$wait.Token).GetAwaiter().GetResult()}catch{break}finally{$wait.Dispose()}
        if(!$answer.EndOfMessage){continue}
        $frame=[Text.Encoding]::UTF8.GetString($bytes,0,$answer.Count)|ConvertFrom-Json
        if($frame.id -ne 7){continue}
        if($frame.result.result.value){$result=$frame.result.result.value | ConvertFrom-Json}
        break
      }
      if(!$result){$state.status='BROWSER_STATE_UNREADABLE'}
      elseif($result.blocked){$state.status='BLOCKED';$state.reason='CLOUDFLARE_CHALLENGE'}
      elseif($result.logoutLink){$state.status='AVAILABLE';$state.login='AUTHENTICATED';$state.reason='VISIBLE_LOGOUT_LINK'}
      elseif($result.loginForm){$state.status='AVAILABLE';$state.login='LOGIN_REQUIRED';$state.reason='VISIBLE_LOGIN_FORM'}
      else{$state.status='AVAILABLE';$state.login='UNVERIFIED';$state.reason='NO_RELIABLE_LOGIN_INDICATOR'}
    }
  }
} catch {$state.status='BROWSER_UNAVAILABLE';$state.reason='LOCAL_BROWSER_CONNECTION_ERROR'}
finally{if($ws){try{$ws.Dispose()}catch{}}}
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force -Path $base|Out-Null
($state|ConvertTo-Json -Compress -Depth 3)|Set-Content -Encoding UTF8 -Path $dest
$state|ConvertTo-Json -Compress -Depth 3


############################################################################################
# DATEI 039/107: scripts/cardmarket-set-identity-audit.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Read-only audit: ambiguous Cardyssey/Cardmarket expansion matches.

The official singles product feed has idExpansion but not independently proven
Cardyssey set IDs, numbers, prints or languages. Therefore a shared name list
is an ambiguity, not proof of a wrong mapping. Never auto-reassign products.
"""
import argparse,collections,hashlib,json,subprocess
from datetime import datetime,timezone
from pathlib import Path
import importlib.util
mapper_spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('cardmarket_mapper',Path(__file__).with_name('map-all-de-cardmarket-catalog.py'))
mapper=importlib.util.module_from_spec(mapper_spec);mapper_spec.loader.exec_module(mapper)
sem_name=mapper.sem_name

ROOT=Path('/home/tv/cardshop')
FEEDS=Path('/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/price-feeds')
PRODUCT_URL='https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json'

def assess(names, current_id, expansions, top_limit=5):
    wanted=collections.Counter(sem_name(n) for n in names if sem_name(n))
    total=sum(wanted.values())
    if not total:return {'status':'NO_CARD_NAMES','top':[],'currentHits':0,'matchPercent':0}
    scored=[]
    for expansion_id,available in expansions.items():
        hits=sum(min(n,available.get(name,0)) for name,n in wanted.items())
        if hits:scored.append((hits,str(expansion_id)))
    scored.sort(key=lambda r:(-r[0],r[1]))
    selected=str(current_id or '')
    current_hits=next((hits for hits,exp in scored if exp==selected),0)
    top=scored[:top_limit]
    highest=scored[0][0] if scored else 0
    close=[(hits,exp) for hits,exp in scored if highest-hits<=max(1,round(total*.03)) and hits/total>=.88]
    if current_hits==0:status='SELECTED_EXPANSION_NO_NAME_OVERLAP'
    elif highest-current_hits>=max(3,round(total*.12)):status='SELECTED_EXPANSION_NEEDS_REVIEW'
    elif len(close)>1:status='AMBIGUOUS_OVERLAPPING_EXPANSIONS'
    else:status='NO_STRONG_CONFLICT_FOUND'
    return {'status':status,'currentHits':current_hits,'matchPercent':round(current_hits/total*100,1),
            'top':[{'expansionId':exp,'nameMatches':hits} for hits,exp in top],
            'competingNearEqualExpansions':len(close)}

def pg(query):
    r=subprocess.run(['docker','exec','cardyssey-db','psql','-X','-qAt','-v','ON_ERROR_STOP=1',
      '-U','cardyssey','-d','cardyssey','-c',query],capture_output=True,text=True,check=True,timeout=60)
    return json.loads(r.stdout.strip())

def run(root=ROOT,feeds=FEEDS):
    meta=json.loads((feeds/(hashlib.sha256(PRODUCT_URL.encode()).hexdigest()+'.json')).read_text())
    products=meta['products']
    expansions=collections.defaultdict(collections.Counter)
    for p in products:
        name=sem_name(p['name'])
        if name:expansions[str(p['idExpansion'])][name]+=1
    cards=pg("""SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('set',set_id,'name',name,'set_id',set_id,'card_id',card_id,'variants',variants)), '[]'::jsonb)::text
      FROM catalog_cards_mirror WHERE language_code='en'""")
    chosen=pg("""SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('set',set_id,'exp',provider_expansion_id)), '[]'::jsonb)::text
      FROM cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates WHERE language_code='de' AND provider_expansion_id IS NOT NULL""")
    consensus=mapper.calculate_variant_set_consensus(cards,products)
    names=collections.defaultdict(list)
    for c in cards:names[c['set']].append(c['name'])
    selected=collections.defaultdict(collections.Counter)
    for x in chosen:selected[x['set']][str(x['exp'])]+=1
    overrides=json.loads((root/'config/cardmarket-verified-set-overrides.json').read_text())
    cases=[]
    for sid,expvotes in selected.items():
        current=expvotes.most_common(1)[0][0]
        analysis=assess(names[sid],current,expansions)
        cases.append({'setId':sid,'currentExpansion':current,'cardCount':len(names[sid]),
                      'independentlyConfirmedOverride':sid in overrides,
                      'variantIdConsensusMatch':consensus.get(sid)==current,**analysis})
    cases.sort(key=lambda x:(x['status']!='NO_STRONG_CONFLICT_FOUND',x['cardCount']),reverse=True)
    counts=collections.Counter(x['status'] for x in cases)
    report={'checkedAt':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),'feedDate':meta.get('createdAt'),
            'setsChecked':len(cases),'variantConsensusSets':len(consensus),'statusCounts':dict(counts),'cases':cases,
            'safety':'Diagnostic only: matching card names do not prove set, collector number, language or print. Confirmed set overrides are kept; no routes or prices modified.'}
    out=root/'reports/cardmarket-set-mapping-risk-audit.json'
    out.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n',encoding='utf-8')
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in report.items() if k!='cases'},ensure_ascii=False))
    return report
if __name__=='__main__':run()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 040/107: scripts/cardmarket-sync-print-mapping-cache.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Persist confirmed print decisions + unverified version-family candidates.

Reads only local human reviews and archived browser observations. Never maps
V1/V2 by order or price. Never changes price_source_routing/tcg_price_cache.
Exports a tiny verified-only file consumed by the Cardmarket browser collector.
"""
import argparse,json,os,re,subprocess,tempfile
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path('/home/tv/cardshop')
BASE=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector')
EVIDENCE=ROOT/'config/cardmarket-version-family-evidence.json'
def sql_quote(s):return "'"+str(s).replace("'","''")+"'"
def run(sql):
    p=subprocess.run(['docker','exec','-i','cardyssey-db','psql','-X','-qAt',
      '-v','ON_ERROR_STOP=1','-U','cardyssey','-d','cardyssey'],
      input=sql,text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=45)
    if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError(p.stderr[-700:])
    return p.stdout.strip()
def pending_queries(data):
    statements=[]
    for cid,item in data.items():
        if (item.get('cardId')!=cid or item.get('status')!='VERSION_FAMILY_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'
              or not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9_.:-]{1,120}',cid)):continue
        rows=item.get('sameSetCardNumberCandidates') or []
        for v in rows:
            pid=str(v.get('productId') or '')
            url=str(v.get('productUrl') or '')
            if not v.get('productIdChecked') or not re.fullmatch(r'[0-9]{4,12}',pid):continue
            if not url.startswith('https://www.cardmarket.com/') or '/Pokemon/Products/Singles/' not in url:continue
            setslug=v.get('setSlug') or ''
            if not re.fullmatch(r'[A-Za-z0-9-]{2,90}',setslug):continue
            # An exact observed family is evidence for a page candidate,
            # NOT evidence that e.g. V1 is Pokéball or V2 Master Ball.
            evid=json.dumps({'version':v.get('version'),'pageUrl':url,
                 'observedFrom':item.get('versionPageUrl'),
                 'exactPrintVerified':False,'priceVerified':False},ensure_ascii=False)
            statements.append(f"""INSERT INTO cardmarket_variant_mappings
 (language_code,card_id,variant_id,product_id,print_type,set_slug,product_url,
  is_reverse_holo,required_comment_pattern,mapping_status,confidence,evidence_type,evidence)
 SELECT r.language_code,r.card_id,r.variant_id,{sql_quote(pid)},
  'unknown',{sql_quote(setslug)},{sql_quote(url)},NULL,NULL,
  'PENDING_REVIEW','LOW','BROWSER_VERSION_FAMILY',{sql_quote(evid)}::jsonb
 FROM cardmarket_print_identity_review r
 WHERE r.card_id={sql_quote(cid)} AND r.language_code IN ('de','en')
 ON CONFLICT (language_code,card_id,variant_id,product_id) DO NOTHING;""")
    return statements

HUMAN_SYNC="""
INSERT INTO cardmarket_variant_mappings
(language_code,card_id,variant_id,product_id,print_type,set_slug,product_url,
 is_reverse_holo,required_comment_pattern,mapping_status,confidence,
 evidence_type,evidence,confirmed_by,verified_at)
SELECT r.language_code,r.card_id,r.variant_id,r.selected_product_id,
 CASE
 WHEN lower(COALESCE(r.foil,'')) LIKE '%meisterball%' OR lower(COALESCE(r.foil,'')) LIKE '%masterball%' THEN 'masterball'
 WHEN lower(COALESCE(r.foil,'')) LIKE '%pok%ball%' THEN 'pokeball'
 WHEN COALESCE(r.stamps,'[]') NOT IN ('','[]') THEN 'stamp'
 WHEN lower(COALESCE(r.print_type,'')) LIKE '%reverse%' THEN 'reverse'
 WHEN lower(COALESCE(r.print_type,''))='normal' THEN 'normal'
 WHEN lower(COALESCE(r.print_type,'')) LIKE '%holo%' THEN 'holo'
 ELSE 'unknown' END,
 NULL,
 CASE WHEN route.provider_product_url LIKE 'https://www.cardmarket.com/%'
  THEN route.provider_product_url ELSE NULL END,
 CASE WHEN lower(COALESCE(r.print_type,'')) LIKE '%reverse%' THEN TRUE
      WHEN lower(COALESCE(r.print_type,''))='normal' THEN FALSE ELSE NULL END,
 NULL,
 'HUMAN_CONFIRMED','HIGH','CARDYSSEY_HUMAN_REVIEW',
 jsonb_build_object('reviewedAt',r.reviewed_at,'reviewerNote',r.reviewer_note,
                    'source','cardmarket_print_identity_review'),
 'cardyssey-admin',COALESCE(r.reviewed_at,NOW())
FROM cardmarket_print_identity_review r
LEFT JOIN price_source_routing route
 ON route.language_code=r.language_code AND route.card_id=r.card_id
 AND route.variant_id=r.variant_id AND route.primary_provider='CARDMARKET'
 AND route.provider_product_id=r.selected_product_id
WHERE r.status='CONFIRMED_PRINT' AND r.selected_product_id ~ '^[0-9]{4,12}$'
ON CONFLICT (language_code,card_id,variant_id,product_id) DO UPDATE SET
 mapping_status='HUMAN_CONFIRMED', confidence='HIGH',
 print_type=EXCLUDED.print_type,
 is_reverse_holo=EXCLUDED.is_reverse_holo,
 product_url=COALESCE(EXCLUDED.product_url,cardmarket_variant_mappings.product_url),
 evidence_type='CARDYSSEY_HUMAN_REVIEW', evidence=EXCLUDED.evidence,
 confirmed_by=EXCLUDED.confirmed_by, verified_at=EXCLUDED.verified_at,
 updated_at=NOW();
"""
def sync(apply=False,base=BASE,evidence_file=EVIDENCE):
    families=json.loads(evidence_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    pending=pending_queries(families)
    if apply:
        # Revoke an identity if the earlier manual review was undone.
        revoke="""UPDATE cardmarket_variant_mappings m
           SET mapping_status='PENDING_REVIEW',confidence='LOW',
               confirmed_by=NULL,verified_at=NULL,
               evidence_type='REVIEW_UNDONE',updated_at=NOW()
           WHERE mapping_status='HUMAN_CONFIRMED'
             AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM cardmarket_print_identity_review r
                 WHERE r.language_code=m.language_code AND r.card_id=m.card_id
                   AND r.variant_id=m.variant_id AND r.status='CONFIRMED_PRINT'
                   AND r.selected_product_id=m.product_id);"""
        run("BEGIN;\n"+revoke+"\n"+"\n".join(pending)+"\n"+HUMAN_SYNC+"\nCOMMIT;")
    data=json.loads(run("""SELECT json_build_object(
      'confirmedCount',(SELECT count(*) FROM cardmarket_variant_mappings WHERE mapping_status='HUMAN_CONFIRMED'),
      'candidateCount',(SELECT count(*) FROM cardmarket_variant_mappings WHERE mapping_status='PENDING_REVIEW'),
      'confirmed',COALESCE((SELECT json_agg(z) FROM
        (SELECT language_code,card_id,variant_id,product_id,print_type,
          product_url,set_slug,is_reverse_holo,mapping_status
         FROM cardmarket_variant_mappings WHERE mapping_status='HUMAN_CONFIRMED'
         ORDER BY language_code,card_id,variant_id) z),'[]'::json))::text"""))
    output={'schema':1,'source':'CARDYSSEY_HUMAN_CONFIRMED_PRINT_ONLY',
       'confirmedMappings':data.get('confirmed') or [],
       'confirmedCount':data['confirmedCount'],'pendingCandidates':data['candidateCount'],
       'familyCandidateStatements':len(pending),'pricesUpdated':0}
    if apply:
        base.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
        dest=base/'cardmarket-verified-print-mapping-cache.json'
        fd,tmp=tempfile.mkstemp(prefix='cm-confirmed-',suffix='.tmp',dir=base)
        try:
            with os.fdopen(fd,'w',encoding='utf-8') as f:json.dump(output,f,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(',',':'))
            os.replace(tmp,dest)
        finally:
            if os.path.exists(tmp):os.unlink(tmp)
    return {'mode':'APPLIED' if apply else 'DRY_RUN',
      'confirmedCount':output['confirmedCount'],
      'pendingCandidates':output['pendingCandidates'],
      'familyCandidateStatements':len(pending),
      'exportedVerifiedOnly':len(output['confirmedMappings']),
      'pricesUpdated':0}
if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument('--apply',action='store_true')
    args=p.parse_args()
    print(json.dumps(sync(apply=args.apply),ensure_ascii=False))


############################################################################################
# DATEI 041/107: scripts/cardmarket-version-family-resolver.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Parse Cardmarket's *observed* /Cards/.../Versions DOM into safe candidate evidence.

The real browser probe runs in a separate tab of the authenticated browser.
A version page can prove a reprint family and its set/number links, NOT the
physical foil, stamp, edition, sale price, or even product ID without opening
the corresponding product page. No automatic price publication or DB writes.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import json
import re
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

BASE = "https://www.cardmarket.com"
SINGLES = re.compile(r"^/(de|en)/Pokemon/Products/Singles/([^/]+)/([^/]+)$")
VERSIONS = re.compile(r"^/(de|en)/Pokemon/Cards/[^/]+/Versions$")
PRODUCT_ID = re.compile(r"^[0-9]{4,12}$")


def safe_url(value: str):
    try:
        u = urlsplit(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    if u.scheme != "https" or u.hostname != "www.cardmarket.com" or u.port not in (None, 443):
        return None
    if u.username or u.password:
        return None
    return u


def resolve_version_family(raw: dict, *, card_id: str, set_slug: str,
                           collector_suffix: str, observations: list[dict] = (),
                           catalog: dict | None = None,
                           additional_print_groups: list[dict] = ()) -> dict:
    page = safe_url(raw.get("href", ""))
    if not page or not VERSIONS.fullmatch(page.path):
        raise ValueError("A verified Cardmarket Cards/.../Versions page is required")
    if raw.get("challenge") or raw.get("ready") != "complete":
        raise ValueError("Browser challenge/incomplete page: refuse candidate extraction")
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9-]{2,90}", set_slug):
        raise ValueError("Unsafe set slug")
    if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9-]{2,32}", collector_suffix):
        raise ValueError("Unsafe collector code")
    groups = [(set_slug, collector_suffix)]
    for group in additional_print_groups:
        gs, gn = str(group.get('setSlug', '')), str(group.get('collectorSuffix', ''))
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9-]{2,90}", gs) or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9-]{2,32}", gn):
            raise ValueError("Unsafe additional group")
        groups.append((gs, gn))
    expected_path = "/" + page.path.strip("/").split("/")[0] + "/Pokemon/Products/Singles/"
    seen = set()
    all_versions, matched = [], []
    for item in raw.get("anchors", []):
        link = safe_url(item.get("url", ""))
        if not link:
            continue
        m = SINGLES.fullmatch(link.path)
        if not m or (expected_path + m.group(2) + "/" + m.group(3)) != link.path:
            continue
        if link.path in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(link.path)
        product_slug = m.group(3)
        version = re.search(r"(?:^|-)V([1-9][0-9]?)(?:-|$)", product_slug, re.I)
        record = {
            "productUrl": BASE + link.path,
            "setSlug": m.group(2),
            "productSlug": product_slug,
            "version": "V" + version.group(1) if version else None,
            "versionLabel": str(item.get("text", ""))[:160],
            "productId": None,
            "productIdChecked": False,
            "exactPrintVerified": False,
            "priceVerified": False,
        }
        all_versions.append(record)
        if any(m.group(2) == gs and product_slug.upper().endswith("-" + gn.upper()) for gs, gn in groups):
            matched.append(record)
    if not all_versions:
        raise ValueError("No Cardmarket version links visible")
    # A live product-page observation (matching path AND official catalog metadata)
    # is required before recording the exact idProduct. Never guess by V1/V2.
    observations_by_path = {}
    for o in observations:
        u = safe_url(o.get("url", ""))
        pid = str(o.get("id", ""))
        if not u or not SINGLES.fullmatch(u.path) or not PRODUCT_ID.fullmatch(pid):
            continue
        source = next((im.get("src") for im in o.get("imgs", [])
                       if str(im.get("src", "")).startswith("https://product-images.s3.cardmarket.com/")
                       and str(im.get("src", "")).endswith("/" + pid + "/" + pid + ".jpg")), None)
        observations_by_path[u.path] = (pid, source)
    for entry in matched:
        path = urlsplit(entry["productUrl"]).path
        verified = observations_by_path.get(path)
        if verified:
            pid, source_image = verified
            metadata = (catalog or {}).get(pid)
            if catalog is not None and (not metadata or not metadata.get("idExpansion")):
                continue
            entry["productId"] = pid
            entry["productIdChecked"] = True
            if source_image:
                entry["imageUrl"] = source_image
            if metadata:
                entry["expansionId"] = str(metadata["idExpansion"])
                entry["metacardId"] = str(metadata.get("idMetacard", ""))
    # A version family is a candidate set only. Different sets using the same
    # artwork/attacks must not be assigned to this card.
    return {
        "cardId": card_id,
        "versionPageUrl": BASE + page.path,
        "pageHeading": str(raw.get("heading", ""))[:180],
        "totalVersionsObserved": len(all_versions),
        "otherSetVersionsExcluded": len(all_versions) - len(matched),
        "sameSetCardNumberCandidates": matched,
        "candidateCount": len(matched),
        "allCandidatesHaveCheckedProductId": bool(matched) and all(x["productIdChecked"] for x in matched),
        "exactPrintVerified": False,
        "safeToAutoPrice": False,
        "status": "VERSION_FAMILY_PRINT_UNVERIFIED" if matched else "NO_SAME_SET_NUMBER_CANDIDATE",
    }


def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--case-file", required=True, help="JSON case with versionsFile, productsFiles, setSlug, collectorSuffix, cardId")
    p.add_argument("--output", required=True)
    a = p.parse_args()
    case = json.loads(Path(a.case_file).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    raw = json.loads(Path(case["versionsFile"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    observations = [json.loads(Path(name).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")) for name in case.get("productsFiles", [])]
    catalog = None
    if case.get("catalogFile"):
        feed = json.loads(Path(case["catalogFile"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
        catalog = {str(x["idProduct"]): x for x in feed["products"]}
    result = resolve_version_family(raw, card_id=case["cardId"], set_slug=case["setSlug"],
        collector_suffix=case["collectorSuffix"], observations=observations, catalog=catalog,
        additional_print_groups=case.get("additionalPrintGroups", []))
    Path(a.output).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    Path(a.output).write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({"cardId": result["cardId"], "familyVersions": result["totalVersionsObserved"],
        "matched": result["candidateCount"], "allIdChecked": result["allCandidatesHaveCheckedProductId"],
        "status": result["status"]}, ensure_ascii=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 042/107: scripts/cardmarket_offer_median.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Offline median of authorized Cardmarket offer exports; never calls blocked pages.

Prices are ASKING prices, not sales. Missing condition is 'assumed NM' only for
explicit German offers; language/edition/finish are never guessed.
"""
import argparse
from collections import Counter
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
import json
from pathlib import Path
import statistics
try:
    from .market_condition_policy import classify_condition
except ImportError:  # script invocation
    from market_condition_policy import classify_condition

GOOD={'MT','NM'}
BAD={'EX','GD','LP','PL','PO'}

def evaluate(data):
    expected=data.get('expected') or {}
    if str(expected.get('language','')).lower() != 'de':
        raise ValueError('German-only rollout: expected.language must be de')
    if not str(expected.get('cardmarket_product_id','')).isdigit():
        raise ValueError('Verified numeric Cardmarket product id required')
    if expected.get('finish') not in ('normal','holo','reverse'):
        raise ValueError('Expected finish must be normal, holo or reverse')
    if expected.get('first_edition') not in (True,False):
        raise ValueError('Expected first_edition must be explicitly true or false')
    records=data.get('offers')
    if not isinstance(records,list):raise ValueError('offers must be an array')
    rejected=Counter(); accepted=[]; seen=set(); assumed=0
    for item in records:
        if not isinstance(item,dict):rejected['invalid_offer']+=1;continue
        oid=str(item.get('id') or '').strip()
        if not oid or oid in seen:rejected['missing_or_duplicate_offer_id']+=1;continue
        seen.add(oid)
        if str(item.get('product_id') or '')!=str(expected['cardmarket_product_id']):
            rejected['wrong_product']+=1;continue
        if str(item.get('language') or '').strip().lower() not in ('de','german','deutsch'):
            rejected['wrong_or_unknown_language']+=1;continue
        if item.get('finish') != expected['finish']:
            rejected['wrong_or_unknown_finish']+=1;continue
        if item.get('first_edition') is not expected['first_edition']:
            rejected['wrong_or_unknown_edition']+=1;continue
        if item.get('signed') is not False or item.get('altered') is not False or item.get('graded') is not False:
            rejected['signed_altered_graded_or_unverified']+=1;continue
        if item.get('bundle_size',1)!=1 or item.get('is_playset',False) is not False:
            rejected['bundle_or_playset']+=1;continue
        condition=str(item.get('condition') or '').strip().upper()
        state=('explicit_nm' if condition in GOOD else 'rejected' if condition in BAD else classify_condition(item.get('condition'),language='de'))
        if state not in ('explicit_nm','assumed_nm'):
            rejected['condition_not_nm']+=1;continue
        try:
            amount=Decimal(str(item['price_eur']))
            if not amount.is_finite() or amount<=0:raise InvalidOperation
        except (InvalidOperation,KeyError,TypeError,ValueError):
            rejected['invalid_price']+=1;continue
        accepted.append(amount)
        if state=='assumed_nm':assumed+=1
    pages=data.get('pagination') or {}
    # An offer sample is not complete unless source explicitly certifies all pages.
    complete=(pages.get('all_pages_fetched') is True and pages.get('capped') is False
        and isinstance(pages.get('total_offers'),int)
        and pages['total_offers'] == len(seen))
    result={'product_id':str(expected['cardmarket_product_id']), 'language':'de',
            'basis':'active_asking_offers_not_sales','accepted':len(accepted),
            'assumed_nm':assumed,'rejected':dict(rejected),
            'all_pages_complete':complete,'median_eur':None,'status':'NO_ELIGIBLE_OFFERS'}
    if accepted:
        med=statistics.median(accepted)
        result['median_eur']=str(med.quantize(Decimal('0.01')))
        result['status']='COMPLETE_ASKING_MEDIAN' if complete else 'PARTIAL_SAMPLE_MEDIAN_NOT_MARKET_WIDE'
    result['safe_to_auto_publish']=bool(accepted and complete and assumed==0)
    return result

if __name__=='__main__':
    ap=argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    ap.add_argument('json_file',type=Path)
    ap.add_argument('--report',type=Path)
    args=ap.parse_args()
    output=evaluate(json.loads(args.json_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8')))
    rendered=json.dumps(output,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n'
    if args.report:args.report.write_text(rendered,encoding='utf-8')
    print(rendered,end='')

# DATEI 043/107: scripts/cardmarket_scan_monitor.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Read-only, bounded DE/EN Cardmarket collector status for authenticated admin UI."""
import argparse
import csv
import json
import importlib.util
from datetime import datetime,timezone
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path

BASE=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector')
PROBLEMS={'ERROR','NO_EXACT_NUMBER_SEARCH_HIT','PRODUCT_SET_SLUG_MISMATCH','MULTIPLE_SEARCH_HITS','NO_PRODUCT_ID','PRODUCT_ID_NOT_IN_VERIFIED_SET','FILTER_UNAVAILABLE','FILTER_NOT_APPLIED','FILTER_UNVERIFIED_EMPTY','DEFERRED_SOCKET','DEFERRED_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT','NO_CONFIRMED_FILTERED_OFFERS'}

def snapshot(base:Path,language:str):
    if language not in ('de','en'):raise ValueError('unsupported language')
    queue=base/f'queue-{language}.csv'
    with queue.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:rows=list(csv.DictReader(f))
    expected={(r['cardyssey_karte'],r['variante_id']) for r in rows}
    browser={(r['cardyssey_karte'],r['variante_id']) for r in rows}
    completed={}
    events=base/f'browser-observations-{language}.jsonl'
    if events.exists():
        with events.open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
            for line in f:
                try:x=json.loads(line)
                except (ValueError,TypeError):continue
                k=(x.get('card_id'),x.get('variant_id'))
                if k in browser:completed[k]=x
    scanned={key:r for key,r in completed.items() if r.get('status') not in ('STARTED','ERROR','DEFERRED_SOCKET','DEFERRED_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT')}
    pending=[x for x in completed.values() if x.get('status') in PROBLEMS or str(x.get('status','')).startswith('DEFERRED')]
    progress_path=base/f'progress-{language}.json'
    progress={}
    if progress_path.exists():
        try:progress=json.loads(progress_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
        except ValueError:pass
    session={}
    session_path=base/'browser-session-status.json'
    if session_path.exists():
        try:
            raw=json.loads(session_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
            session={k:raw.get(k) for k in ('status','reason','login','checkedAt','profileExists')}
        except (ValueError,OSError):pass
    rescan_epoch=None
    rescan_done=None
    epoch_file=base/'full-rescan-epoch.json'
    if epoch_file.exists():
        from datetime import datetime,timezone
        try:
            epoch=json.loads(epoch_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig'))
            rescan_epoch=datetime.fromisoformat(epoch['requestedAt'].replace('Z','+00:00'))
            if rescan_epoch.tzinfo is None:rescan_epoch=rescan_epoch.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            rescan_done=sum(bool(x.get('seen_at')) and datetime.fromisoformat(str(x['seen_at']).replace('Z','+00:00'))>=rescan_epoch
                and str(x.get('status','')) not in ('STARTED','BLOCKED','LOGIN_REQUIRED','ERROR')
                and not str(x.get('status','')).startswith('DEFERRED') for x in completed.values())
        except (OSError,ValueError,TypeError):rescan_epoch=None;rescan_done=None
    total=len(expected);done=len(scanned)
    matched=sum(bool(r.get('identity_confirmed')) for r in completed.values())
    search_misses=sum(r.get('status')=='NO_EXACT_NUMBER_SEARCH_HIT' for r in completed.values())
    priced=sum(r.get('needs_price')=='NO' and r.get('existing_price_eur') not in ('',None) for r in rows)
    missing=sum(r.get('needs_price')=='YES' for r in rows)
    eligible_missing=sum(r.get('needs_price')=='YES' and str(r.get('scan_tier'))!='4' for r in rows)
    bulk_missing=sum(r.get('needs_price')=='YES' and str(r.get('scan_tier'))=='4' for r in rows)
    raw_status=str(progress.get('status') or 'NOT_STARTED')
    stalled=False
    if raw_status=='RUNNING' and progress.get('updated_at'):
        try:
            last=datetime.fromisoformat(str(progress['updated_at']).replace('Z','+00:00'))
            if last.tzinfo is None:last=last.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            stalled=(datetime.now(timezone.utc)-last).total_seconds()>300
        except ValueError:pass
    current_problems=sum(bool(x.get('seen_at')) and rescan_epoch is not None
         and str(x['seen_at'])[:19]>=rescan_epoch.isoformat()[:19]
         and (x.get('status') in PROBLEMS or str(x.get('status','')).startswith('DEFERRED'))
         for x in completed.values())
    # Surface missing vs non-filterable fields as proactive data guidance.
    spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('cardmarket_filter_readiness',Path(__file__).with_name('cardmarket-filter-readiness.py'))
    audit=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(audit)
    readiness=[audit.analyze(row) for row in rows]
    missing_filter_inputs=sum(bool(x['missing']) for x in readiness)
    nonfilterable_specials=sum(bool(x['unsupported']) for x in readiness)
    return {'language':language,'totalVariants':total,'uniqueCards':len({r['cardyssey_karte'] for r in rows}),
       'browserTotal':len(browser),'rescanStartedAt':rescan_epoch.isoformat() if rescan_epoch else None,'rescanProcessed':rescan_done,'feedOnly':sum(str(r.get('scan_tier'))=='4' and r.get('needs_price')=='NO' for r in rows),'browserObserved':len(completed),
       'browserProcessed':done,'productMapped':matched,'noExactSearchMatch':search_misses,
       'pricedVariants':priced,'missingVariants':missing,'browserMissingPriority':eligible_missing,'feedMissingPriority':bulk_missing,
       'browserRemaining':max(0,len(browser)-done),
       'missingFilterInputs':missing_filter_inputs,'nonfilterableSpecialPrints':nonfilterable_specials,
       'progressPercent':round(done/len(browser)*100,2) if browser else 100,
       'totalReviewedPercent':round((total-len(browser)+done)/total*100,2) if total else 100,
       'status':'RUNNING_STALLED' if stalled else raw_status,
       'problemCountCurrentCycle':current_problems,
       'browserSession':session,
       'lastCard':str(progress.get('last_card') or ''),'updatedAt':progress.get('updated_at'),
       'problemCountInObserved':len(pending),
       'recentProblems':[{'cardId':x.get('card_id'),'variantId':x.get('variant_id'),'status':x.get('status'),
          'setId':x.get('set_id'),'detail':str(x.get('error') or x.get('pagination_failure') or '')[:160]}
          for x in reversed(pending[-8:])],
       'recentEvents':[{'cardId':x.get('card_id'),'status':x.get('status'),'at':x.get('seen_at')} for x in list(completed.values())[-6:][::-1]]}

if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--language',choices=('de','en'),default='de');a=p.parse_args()
    try:print(json.dumps(snapshot(BASE,a.language),ensure_ascii=False))
    except (OSError,ValueError) as e:print(json.dumps({'error':'scan_data_unavailable','detail':str(e)[:100]}));raise SystemExit(2)


############################################################################################
# DATEI 044/107: scripts/cardmarket_variant_recheck.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Rescan the local DE/EN Cardmarket catalog and browser evidence safely.

This does NOT infer language availability from website locale or a product ID,
NOR guess that V2 is always Holo. No price, review decision or routing writes.
"""
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import re
from collections import Counter
from datetime import datetime,timezone
from pathlib import Path

ROOT=Path('/home/tv/cardshop')
BASE=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector')
FEEDS=Path('/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/price-feeds')
URL='https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json'
URL_VERSION=re.compile(r'(?i)(?:^|[-/])V([1-9][0-9]?)(?=[-/]|$)')
COMPLETED={'COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED','FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE','NO_CONFIRMED_FILTERED_OFFERS','FILTER_UNAVAILABLE','FILTER_UNVERIFIED_EMPTY','MAPPED_NO_PRICE_REQUIRED','FILTER_NOT_APPLIED'}

def read_latest(lang,base=BASE):
    path=base/f'browser-observations-{lang}.jsonl'
    result={}
    if path.exists():
        for line in path.open(encoding='utf-8-sig'):
            try:
                x=json.loads(line)
                if x.get('card_id') and x.get('variant_id'):
                    result[(x['card_id'],x['variant_id'])]=x
            except (ValueError,TypeError):pass
    return result

def candidate_audit(row,products,obs,supplemental=None,user_reference_url=None):
    ids=[v for v in row.get('matched_set_ids','').split(',') if v]
    product_records=[products.get(i) for i in ids]
    evidence=(obs or {}).get('url') or ''
    version=URL_VERSION.search(evidence.split('?')[0])
    has_filtered=bool(obs and obs.get('identity_confirmed') is True and obs.get('status') in COMPLETED)
    lang_proof=bool(has_filtered and int(obs.get('verified_lang_nm_offers') or 0)>0)
    evidence_pid=str(obs.get('product_id') or '') if obs else ''
    # Version in a verified URL is evidence for the OBSERVED product only,
    # not for every similarly named product in the source catalog.
    confirmed_url=bool(version and has_filtered and evidence_pid in ids)
    version_pages=(supplemental or {}).get('versionPages') or []
    independent_versions={x.get('version') for x in version_pages if
        isinstance(x,dict) and str(x.get('url','')).startswith('https://www.cardmarket.com/')}
    user_version_compare=len(independent_versions)>1
    # A user-supplied URL is a useful lookup hint, not a reason to require
    # manual set assignment. One catalog product in the independently
    # verified set must first go through automatic print/language checking.
    if 'CARD_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_NOT_IDENTIFIED' in row.get('classification','') and ids:
        category='AUTO_PRINT_VERIFICATION'
        reason='Kartennummer und Cardmarket-Set sind bekannt; Produktkandidaten sind noch keiner physischen Druckvariante zugewiesen.'
    elif not ids or 'SET_NOT_VERIFIED' in row.get('classification','') or 'NO_MATCH' in row.get('classification','') or 'INFERRED_SET' in row.get('classification',''):
        category='AUTO_CATALOG_RECHECK'
        reason='Set oder Produktidentität zunächst durch Automatik klären'
    elif len(ids)>1:
        category='MANUAL_COMPARISON_READY' if (confirmed_url or user_version_compare) else 'AUTO_VERSION_DISCOVERY'
        reason=('Version auf einer bestätigten Produktseite gefunden; andere Kandidaten müssen noch verglichen werden' if confirmed_url else
                'V1/V2-Bilder wurden bereitgestellt, die numerischen Produkt-IDs sind aber noch keiner Version verlässlich zugeordnet' if user_version_compare else
                'Mehrere Produkte: erst V1/V2, Bilder und Druck der Kandidaten nachweisen')
    elif obs and obs.get('status')=='PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED':
        category='AUTO_PRINT_VERIFICATION'
        reason='Produkt ist im bestätigten Set mit passender Kartennummer gefunden; nur die konkrete Druck-/Editionsvariante ist noch offen.'
    elif has_filtered and lang_proof:
        category='AUTO_PRINT_VERIFICATION'
        reason='Produktseite und Angebote in dieser Sprache gefunden; Druck/Edition noch zu verifizieren'
    else:
        category='AUTO_LANGUAGE_AND_PRINT_RECHECK'
        reason='Sprache/Edition/Druck noch nicht unabhängig nachgewiesen'
    meta_ids={str(p.get('idMetacard')) for p in product_records if p and p.get('idMetacard')}
    return {'category':category,'reason':reason,'versionObserved':'V'+version.group(1) if confirmed_url else None,
            'versionProductId':evidence_pid if confirmed_url else None,
            'languageOffersObserved':lang_proof,
            'languageAvailability':'OFFERS_OBSERVED' if lang_proof else 'UNKNOWN_NOT_PROVEN_ABSENT',
            'sameMetacard':len(ids)>1 and len(meta_ids)==1,
            'printVerified':False,'priceApproved':False,
            'versionPages':version_pages if user_version_compare else [],
            'lastScanStatus':obs.get('status') if obs else 'NOT_SCANNED'}

def run(lang,root=ROOT,base=BASE,feeds=FEEDS):
    meta=json.loads((feeds/(hashlib.sha256(URL.encode()).hexdigest()+'.json')).read_text())
    products={str(x['idProduct']):x for x in meta['products']}
    latest=read_latest(lang,base)
    evidence_path=root/'config/cardmarket-page-version-evidence.json'
    supplied=json.loads(evidence_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')) if evidence_path.exists() else {}
    user_links_path=root/'config/cardmarket-user-reference-links.json'
    user_links=json.loads(user_links_path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')) if user_links_path.exists() else {}
    src=root/f'reports/cardmarket-identity-worklist-{lang}.csv'
    out=root/f'reports/cardmarket-variant-recheck-{lang}.json'
    counts=Counter(); cases={}; seen=0
    with src.open(newline='',encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
        for row in csv.DictReader(f):
            seen+=1
            key=(row['card_id'],row['variant_id'])
            info=candidate_audit(row,products,latest.get(key),supplied.get(lang+'|'+row['card_id']),user_links.get(row['card_id']))
            counts[info['category']]+=1
            if info['versionObserved']:counts['observed_version_url']+=1
            if info['languageOffersObserved']:counts['observed_language_offers']+=1
            # Restrict report to candidates requiring follow-up; metadata is
            # bounded per-card, never store account cookies or offer photos.
            if row.get('classification')!='ONE_PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET' or user_links.get(row['card_id']):
                cases['|'.join(key)]=info
    result={'language':lang,'createdAt':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
            'sourceCatalogCreatedAt':meta.get('createdAt'),
            'scannedWorklistRows':seen,'browserRecords':len(latest),
            'counts':dict(counts),'cases':cases,
            'note':'Only independently verified version URL is retained. No V1=normal / V2=holo assumption. Missing offers never proves nonexistence.'}
    tmp=out.with_suffix('.json.tmp')
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(',',':')),encoding='utf-8')
    tmp.replace(out)
    return {k:v for k,v in result.items() if k!='cases'}

if __name__=='__main__':
    for language in ('de','en'):
        print(json.dumps(run(language),ensure_ascii=False))


############################################################################################
# DATEI 045/107: scripts/cardmarket_variant_resolver.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Conservative Cardmarket print resolution and language-specific offer pricing.

Input must come from an independently verified Cardmarket product-page / catalog
record. An image hash, V1/V2, a high price, or an unlabelled JP reverse does
NOT independently identify a print. This module NEVER writes price caches.
No external dependencies; optional Pillow/imagehash support is diagnostic.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import argparse
import dataclasses
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation, ROUND_HALF_UP
from collections import Counter
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
import io
import json
import re
import statistics
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

PRINTS={"normal","reverse","pokeball","masterball","stamp","holo","cosmos","unknown"}
LANGUAGES={"de","en","ja","ko","zh-cn","zh-tw"}
CONDITIONS={"NM","MT","NEAR MINT","MINT"}
BALL_PATTERNS={
 "masterball":re.compile(r"(?i)(?<!\w)(?:master\s*ball|meister\s*ball|masterball|meisterball)(?!\w)"),
 "pokeball":re.compile(r"(?i)(?<!\w)(?:pok[eé]\s*ball|pok[eé]ball)(?!\w)"),
}
NEGATIONS=re.compile(r"(?i)(?:\b(?:no|not|kein(?:e|en|er|es)?|ohne|non|nicht|sin|sans)\b|\b(?:wrong|falsch)\b)")
NEGATED_BALL=re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:no|not|kein(?:e|en|er|es)?|ohne|non|nicht|sin|sans)\s+(?:(?:a|ein|eine|an)\s+)?(?:master\s*ball|meister\s*ball|masterball|pok[eé]\s*ball|pok[eé]ball)")
STAMP_WORDS={
 "prerelease":re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:prerelease|pre.release|vorabversion)\b"),
 "play-pokemon":re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:play!?\s*pok[eé]mon|play!\s*pokemon)\b"),
 "professor-program":re.compile(r"(?i)\b(?:professor.?s?\s+program(?:me)?|professorenprogramm)\b"),
 "cosmos":re.compile(r"(?i)\bcosmos\s*(?:holo|foil)?\b"),
}
def labels(text:str)->set[str]:
    text=str(text or "")
    out={key for key,rx in BALL_PATTERNS.items() if rx.search(text)}
    out|={key for key,rx in STAMP_WORDS.items() if rx.search(text)}
    return out

def classified_comment(comment:str, target:str)->str:
    """'positive', 'contradiction', 'missing', 'ambiguous'.

    An absent comment never proves a special print, even in JP 151.
    Bare PB/MB abbreviations are deliberately NOT accepted as evidence.
    """
    target=str(target or "").casefold()
    found=labels(comment)
    if not comment or not comment.strip():return "missing"
    if NEGATED_BALL.search(comment):return "ambiguous"
    if target in {"pokeball","masterball"}:
        other={"pokeball","masterball"}-{target}
        if found&other:return "contradiction"
        if target in found:return "positive"
        return "missing"
    if target.startswith("stamp:"):
        stamp=target.partition(":")[2]
        if stamp in found and not NEGATIONS.search(comment):return "positive"
        return "missing"
    return "not_applicable"

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Product:
    product_id:str
    set_slug:str
    product_url:str
    number:str
    name:str=""
    title:str=""
    version:str=""
    price_trend:float|None=None
    image_url:str|None=None
    confirmed_product_page:bool=False
    official_expansion_id:str|None=None

@dataclass
class Decision:
    product_id:str
    print_type:str="unknown"
    confidence:str="LOW"
    evidence:list[str]=field(default_factory=list)
    review_required:bool=True
    safe_to_publish_price:bool=False
    # HIGH refers to identity only; price requires independent offer validation

def valid_product_url(u:str)->bool:
    try:x=urlsplit(u)
    except (TypeError,ValueError):return False
    return bool(x.scheme=="https" and x.hostname=="www.cardmarket.com" and
      x.port in (None,443) and not x.username and not x.password and
      re.fullmatch(r"/(?:de|en)/Pokemon/Products/Singles/[A-Za-z0-9-]+/[A-Za-z0-9-]+",x.path))

def classify_product(product:Product,*,valid_set_slugs:set[str],
                     matched_number:str, confirmed_print_evidence:str|None=None,
                     foil_image_evidence:dict|None=None)->Decision:
    """Classify a page only if set+number were independently validated.

    A high-cost product is not evidence of a Master Ball. A generic phash
    cannot distinguish shared artwork with different foil; image proof must
    explicitly be 'human_verified' and identify the printed feature.
    """
    result=Decision(product_id=str(product.product_id))
    if not product.confirmed_product_page or not valid_product_url(product.product_url):
        result.evidence.append("PRODUCT_PAGE_NOT_VERIFIED");return result
    if product.set_slug not in valid_set_slugs or not valid_set_slugs:
        result.evidence.append("SET_NOT_VERIFIED");return result
    a=re.search(r"0*(\d+)$",str(product.number))
    b=re.search(r"0*(\d+)$",str(matched_number))
    if not a or not b or a.group(1)!=b.group(1):
        result.evidence.append("NUMBER_MISMATCH");return result
    result.evidence+=["SET_VERIFIED","COLLECTOR_NUMBER_VERIFIED","PRODUCT_PAGE_VERIFIED"]
    # A Trainer card can itself be named "Master Ball". Only a structured
    # provider version/print field, not the card name/title, is foil evidence.
    explicit=labels(product.version)
    if "masterball" in explicit and "pokeball" in explicit:
        result.evidence.append("CONFLICTING_PRINT_LABELS");return result
    if "masterball" in explicit or "pokeball" in explicit:
        result.print_type="masterball" if "masterball" in explicit else "pokeball"
        result.confidence="HIGH"
        result.evidence.append("EXPLICIT_PROVIDER_PRINT_LABEL")
        result.review_required=False
        return result
    if confirmed_print_evidence in PRINTS-{"unknown"}:
        # Human/manual evidence must be supplied by a verified review action,
        # not by untrusted comments or price heuristics.
        result.print_type=confirmed_print_evidence
        result.confidence="HIGH";result.review_required=False
        result.evidence.append("HUMAN_CONFIRMED_PRINT")
        return result
    if foil_image_evidence:
        if (foil_image_evidence.get("method")=="human_verified_roi"
                and foil_image_evidence.get("subjectProductId")==str(product.product_id)
                and foil_image_evidence.get("print") in PRINTS-{"unknown"}
                and foil_image_evidence.get("reviewer")):
            result.print_type=foil_image_evidence["print"]
            result.confidence="HIGH";result.review_required=False
            result.evidence.append("HUMAN_VERIFIED_FOIL_REGION")
            return result
        result.evidence.append("IMAGE_SIMILARITY_ONLY_NOT_FOIL_PROOF")
    result.evidence.append("V1_V2_OR_PRICE_NOT_PRINT_PROOF")
    return result

def phash_distance(a:bytes,b:bytes)->int|None:
    """Optional diagnostic; near-equal artwork != equal foil."""
    try:
        from PIL import Image
        import imagehash
        x=imagehash.phash(Image.open(io.BytesIO(a)).convert("RGB"))
        y=imagehash.phash(Image.open(io.BytesIO(b)).convert("RGB"))
        return int(x-y)
    except (ImportError,OSError,ValueError,TypeError):
        return None

def price_hint(products:list[Product])->dict:
    """Trend-price only ranks candidates for manual review; never maps prints."""
    valid=sorted([p for p in products if p.price_trend and p.price_trend>0],
                 key=lambda p:p.price_trend or 0)
    ratio=(valid[-1].price_trend/valid[0].price_trend) if len(valid)==2 else None
    return {"orderedProductIds":[p.product_id for p in valid],
            "priceRatio":round(ratio,2) if ratio else None,
            "reviewSuggestion":"CHEAPER_POKEBALL_MORE_EXPENSIVE_MASTERBALL_POSSIBLE" if ratio and ratio>=2.5 else None,
            "printVerified":False,"safeToAutoPublish":False}

def parse_money(value)->Decimal:
    if isinstance(value,bool):raise ValueError("price")
    if isinstance(value,str):
        value=value.replace("€","").replace("\u00a0","").replace(" ","").strip()
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9.,]+",value):raise ValueError("price")
        if "," in value and "." in value:
            value=value.replace(".","").replace(",",".") if value.rfind(",")>value.rfind(".") else value.replace(",","")
        elif "," in value:value=value.replace(",",".")
    try:amount=Decimal(str(value))
    except (InvalidOperation,TypeError,ValueError):raise ValueError("price")
    if not amount.is_finite() or amount<=0:raise ValueError("price")
    return amount

def percentile(values:list[Decimal],p:Decimal)->Decimal:
    values=sorted(values)
    i=Decimal(len(values)-1)*p
    low=int(i)
    return values[low]+(values[min(low+1,len(values)-1)]-values[low])*(i-Decimal(low))

def price_diagnostic(data:dict)->dict:
    """Read-only robust asking median; requires complete list for certification.

    Same idProduct, language, condition, special print positive comments where
    required, reverse/edition flags, no duplicates. IQR only with >=8 offers.
    No automatic cross-language conversion or price-feed publication.
    """
    exp=data.get("expected") or {}
    target=str(exp.get("print") or "").casefold()
    lang=exp.get("language")
    pid=str(exp.get("product_id") or "")
    if lang not in LANGUAGES or target not in PRINTS-{"unknown"} or not pid.isdigit():
        raise ValueError("language, print and numeric product_id required")
    rows=data.get("offers")
    if not isinstance(rows,list):raise ValueError("offers must be list")
    accepted=[];excluded=Counter();seen=set()
    for row in rows:
        if not isinstance(row,dict):
            excluded["INVALID_ROW"]+=1;continue
        oid=str(row.get("offer_id") or "")
        if not oid or oid in seen:
            excluded["DUPLICATE_OR_MISSING_OFFER_ID"]+=1;continue
        seen.add(oid)
        if str(row.get("product_id"))!=pid:
            excluded["WRONG_PRODUCT"]+=1;continue
        if row.get("language")!=lang:
            excluded["WRONG_LANGUAGE"]+=1;continue
        if str(row.get("condition") or "").upper() not in CONDITIONS:
            excluded["NOT_NM_OR_BETTER"]+=1;continue
        if row.get("signed") is not False or row.get("altered") is not False or row.get("graded") is not False:
            excluded["MODIFIED_OR_UNVERIFIED"]+=1;continue
        if row.get("bundle") is not False:
            excluded["BUNDLE_OR_UNVERIFIED"]+=1;continue
        reverse=row.get("is_reverse")
        if not isinstance(reverse,bool):
            excluded["UNKNOWN_REVERSE_FLAG"]+=1;continue
        if target=="normal" and reverse or target=="reverse" and not reverse:
            excluded["WRONG_REVERSE_FLAG"]+=1;continue
        if target in {"pokeball","masterball"}:
            comment_status=classified_comment(row.get("comment") or "",target)
            if comment_status!="positive":
                excluded["PRINT_COMMENT_"+comment_status.upper()]+=1;continue
            if lang=="ja" and not reverse:
                excluded["JAPANESE_SPECIAL_REQUIRES_REVERSE"]+=1;continue
        if target=="stamp" and not (exp.get("confirmed_print_identity") is True):
            excluded["STAMP_TYPE_UNVERIFIED"]+=1;continue
        try:accepted.append(parse_money(row["price_eur"]))
        except (KeyError,ValueError):excluded["INVALID_PRICE"]+=1
    complete_info=data.get("pagination") or {}
    all_complete=(complete_info.get("all_pages_fetched") is True
                  and complete_info.get("capped") is False
                  and isinstance(complete_info.get("total_offers"),int)
                  and complete_info["total_offers"]==len(seen)
                  and excluded["DUPLICATE_OR_MISSING_OFFER_ID"]==0)
    med=statistics.median(accepted) if accepted else None
    cleaned=list(accepted)
    outlier_count=0
    if len(cleaned)>=8:
        q1=percentile(cleaned,Decimal("0.25"))
        q3=percentile(cleaned,Decimal("0.75"))
        iqr=q3-q1
        if iqr>0:
            low=q1-Decimal("1.5")*iqr
            high=q3+Decimal("1.5")*iqr
            cleaned=[x for x in cleaned if low<=x<=high]
            outlier_count=len(accepted)-len(cleaned)
    cleaned_median=statistics.median(cleaned) if cleaned else None
    trend=data.get("product_wide_trend")
    ratio=None
    try:
        tr=parse_money(trend)
        if cleaned_median is not None:ratio=float(cleaned_median/tr)
    except ValueError:pass
    result={
       "basis":"ACTIVE_ASKING_NOT_SOLD",
       "language":lang,"product_id":pid,"print":target,
       "total_rows":len(rows),"unique_offers":len(seen),
       "accepted_offers":len(accepted),"rejected":dict(excluded),
       "all_pages_complete":all_complete,"iqr_removed":outlier_count,
       "median_all_accepted_eur":str(med.quantize(Decimal(".01"),rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)) if med else None,
       "median_iqr_diagnostic_eur":str(cleaned_median.quantize(Decimal(".01"),rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)) if cleaned_median else None,
       "product_guide_ratio":round(ratio,2) if ratio is not None else None,
       "guide_outlier_warning":ratio is not None and (ratio<0.5 or ratio>2),
       "exact_print_verified":exp.get("confirmed_print_identity") is True,
       "safe_to_publish":bool(all_complete and accepted and exp.get("confirmed_print_identity") is True
          and not excluded["PRINT_COMMENT_AMBIGUOUS"]
          and (target not in {"pokeball","masterball"} or all_complete)),
    }
    return result

def main():
    a=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    a.add_argument("input",type=Path)
    a.add_argument("--output",type=Path)
    args=a.parse_args()
    data=json.loads(args.input.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig"))
    if data.get("operation")=="price":result=price_diagnostic(data)
    else:
        p=Product(**data["product"])
        d=classify_product(p,valid_set_slugs=set(data.get("verified_set_slugs",[])),
            matched_number=data["card_number"],confirmed_print_evidence=data.get("manual_print"),
            foil_image_evidence=data.get("foil_image_evidence"))
        result=dataclasses.asdict(d)
    rendered=json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n"
    if args.output:args.output.write_text(rendered,encoding="utf-8")
    print(rendered,end="")
if __name__=="__main__":main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 046/107: scripts/export-cardmarket-full-queue.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Export complete Cardmarket DE/EN variant queues from Cardyssey PostgreSQL.

Read only. Product IDs from catalog candidates or TCGdex are *unverified* hints.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import json
import os
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

SQL = r"""
COPY (
WITH source AS (
 SELECT c.language_code,c.card_id,c.set_id,c.local_id,c.name,c.variants,
        k.provider_product_ids,k.candidate_status
 FROM catalog_cards_mirror c
 LEFT JOIN cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates k
   ON k.language_code='de' AND k.card_id=c.card_id
   -- The Cardmarket expansion/product is defined by the set and collector
   -- card identity, not the requested offering language. DE is the verified
   -- set-candidate source for both DE/EN queues. A physical print is still
   -- unverified when only the card-level ID is available.
 WHERE c.language_code='{language}'
)
SELECT
 c.card_id AS cardyssey_karte,
 v.value->>'id' AS variante_id,
 c.set_id,
 c.local_id AS kartennummer,
 c.name AS kartenname_suche,
 COALESCE(v.value->>'type','') AS drucktyp,
 COALESCE(v.value->>'subtype','') AS sonderdruck,
 COALESCE(v.value->>'foil','') AS foil,
 COALESCE(v.value->>'stamps','[]') AS stempel,
 CASE WHEN r.primary_provider='CARDMARKET'
      AND r.verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'
      AND COALESCE(r.provider_product_id,'') ~ '^[0-9]+$'
   THEN r.provider_product_id
 -- Cardyssey stores the exact Cardmarket ID on EACH physical variant.
 -- Never merge all products with the same card name: Undaunted Houndoom
 -- UD5 and UD82 are different cards even though both are called Houndoom.
 ELSE COALESCE(
   CASE WHEN v.value->>'cardmarketProductId' ~ '^[0-9]{{3,12}}$'
        THEN v.value->>'cardmarketProductId' END,
   CASE WHEN r.primary_provider='CARDMARKET'
             AND r.provider_product_id ~ '^[0-9]{{3,12}}$'
        THEN r.provider_product_id END,
   CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(c.provider_product_ids)='array'
             AND jsonb_array_length(c.provider_product_ids)=1
        THEN c.provider_product_ids->>0 END,
   ''
 ) END AS cardmarket_ids,
 -- A previously observed URL belongs to one product ID; never pair it with
 -- a different per-variant ID without a new visit to the product page.
 CASE WHEN r.verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'
           OR NULLIF(v.value->>'cardmarketProductId','') IS NULL
           OR r.provider_product_id=v.value->>'cardmarketProductId'
      THEN COALESCE(r.provider_product_url,'') ELSE '' END AS known_url,
 CASE WHEN r.primary_provider='CARDMARKET' AND r.verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'
  AND r.source_evidence LIKE 'Human confirmed exact%'
  THEN COALESCE(to_char(r.updated_at AT TIME ZONE 'UTC','YYYY-MM-DD"T"HH24:MI:SS"Z"'),'')
  ELSE '' END AS manual_scan_requested_at,
 COALESCE(r.verification_state,'UNVERIFIED') AS routing_state,
 COALESCE(c.candidate_status,'NO_CATALOG_CANDIDATE') AS candidate_status,
 -- Cardmarket candidates for the CARD, used only for print-uncertain hints.
 -- Never promote these into a variant-level verified route.
 COALESCE((SELECT string_agg(x.id,',' ORDER BY x.id) FROM (
     SELECT DISTINCT jsonb_array_elements_text(
       CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(c.provider_product_ids)='array'
         THEN c.provider_product_ids ELSE '[]'::jsonb END) AS id
   ) x), '') AS set_card_candidate_ids,
 CASE WHEN lower(coalesce(v.value->>'type','')) IN ('normal','nonholo','common') THEN '4' ELSE '2' END AS scan_tier,
 'YES' AS needs_price
FROM source c
CROSS JOIN LATERAL jsonb_array_elements(COALESCE(c.variants,'[]'::jsonb)) AS v(value)
LEFT JOIN price_source_routing r
 ON r.language_code=c.language_code AND r.card_id=c.card_id
 AND r.variant_id=v.value->>'id'
ORDER BY c.set_id,c.local_id,c.card_id,v.value->>'id'
) TO STDOUT WITH (FORMAT csv, HEADER, ENCODING 'UTF8');
"""

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--language",choices=("de","en"),required=True)
    p.add_argument("--output",type=Path,required=True)
    args=p.parse_args()
    sql=SQL.format(language=args.language)
    process=subprocess.run(["docker","exec","cardyssey-db","psql","-X","-qAt",
                            "-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-c",sql],
                           capture_output=True,check=True)
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    tmp=args.output.with_suffix(args.output.suffix+".tmp")
    tmp.write_bytes(process.stdout)
    with tmp.open(encoding="utf-8",newline="") as f:
        data=list(csv.DictReader(f))
    if not data or any(not r["variante_id"] or not r["kartennummer"] for r in data):
        tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise RuntimeError("Invalid queue: empty or missing variant ID/card number")
    keys={(r["cardyssey_karte"],r["variante_id"]) for r in data}
    if len(keys)!=len(data):
        tmp.unlink(missing_ok=True)
        raise RuntimeError("Duplicate variant identities in queue")
    os.replace(tmp,args.output)
    result={"language":args.language,"variants":len(data),
            "candidate_rows":sum(bool(r["cardmarket_ids"]) for r in data),
            "with_existing_url":sum(bool(r["known_url"]) for r in data),
            "output":str(args.output)}
    print(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False))
if __name__=="__main__":
    main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 047/107: scripts/import-cardmarket-browser-observations.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Add browser observations to admin review cases without treating samples as prices."""
import argparse
import csv
import hashlib
import json
import math
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

VALID = {"FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE", "NO_CONFIRMED_FILTERED_OFFERS",
         "COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED"}

def select_observations(records, worklist):
    allowed = {(r["cardyssey_karte"], r["variante_id"]):
               set(filter(None, (r.get("cardmarket_ids") or "").split(",")))
               for r in worklist}
    refresh_days = {(r["cardyssey_karte"],r["variante_id"]):
                    (7 if str(r.get("scan_tier")) in ("0","1") else
                     14 if str(r.get("scan_tier")) == "2" else 30)
                    for r in worklist}
    prepared = {}
    for r in records:
        key = (str(r.get("card_id") or ""), str(r.get("variant_id") or ""))
        pid = str(r.get("product_id") or "")
        if r.get("status") not in VALID or pid not in allowed.get(key, set()):
            continue
        sample = r.get("sample_median_eur")
        if sample is not None and (not isinstance(sample, (float,int)) or not math.isfinite(sample) or sample <= 0):
            continue
        count = r.get("verified_lang_nm_offers", r.get("de_nm_offers"))
        lang = r.get("language", "de")
        if lang not in ("de", "en") or type(count) is not int or count < 0 or count > 300:
            continue
        complete = r.get("all_pages_complete") is True
        if r.get("status") == "COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED" and not complete:
            continue
        if r.get("status") != "COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED":
            complete = False
        prepared[key] = {
            "language_code": lang, "card_id": key[0], "variant_id": key[1],
            "refresh_days":refresh_days[key],
            "query": str(r.get("query") or "")[:255],
            "candidate": {"provider": "CARDMARKET", "product_id": pid,
                          "match_type": "CARDMARKET_FILTERED_ASKING_SAMPLE",
                          "language": lang, "min_condition": "NM",
                          "sample_count": count, "sample_median_eur": sample,
                          "all_pages_complete": complete,
                          "sample_not_market_wide": not complete,
                          "access_mode": str(r.get("access_mode") or "UNKNOWN"),
                          "exact_print_verified": False,
                          "no_verified_print_identity": True,
                          "url": str(r.get("url") or "")[:500]}
        }
    return list(prepared.values())

def sql_for(rows):
    serialized = json.dumps(rows, ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")).replace("'", "''")
    return """
WITH incoming AS (
 SELECT jsonb_array_elements('""" + serialized + """'::jsonb) AS data
), prepared AS (
 SELECT data->>'language_code' AS language_code,
 data->>'card_id' AS card_id, data->>'variant_id' AS variant_id,
 data->>'query' AS search_query, data->'candidate' AS candidate
 FROM incoming
)
INSERT INTO price_review_cases(language_code,card_id,variant_id,search_status,search_query,search_steps,candidates,failure_reason,checked_at)
SELECT language_code,card_id,variant_id,'CARDMARKET_BROWSER_REVIEW',search_query,
 '["CARDMARKET_BROWSER","LANGUAGE_NM_FILTER"]'::jsonb,
 jsonb_build_array(candidate),
 'Filtered active asking offers only; exact printing/edition/foil/stamp not independently confirmed',NOW()
FROM prepared
ON CONFLICT(language_code,card_id,variant_id) DO UPDATE SET
 candidates=(SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(c.value), '[]'::jsonb)
             FROM jsonb_array_elements(price_review_cases.candidates) c(value)
             WHERE c.value->>'match_type'<>'CARDMARKET_FILTERED_ASKING_SAMPLE')
           || EXCLUDED.candidates,
 checked_at=NOW();
"""

def main():
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--results", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--queue", type=Path, required=True)
    p.add_argument("--apply", action="store_true")
    args = p.parse_args()
    if not args.results.exists():
        print("browser_results_missing")
        return
    with args.queue.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as f:
        queue = list(csv.DictReader(f))
    source_bytes = args.results.read_bytes()
    fingerprint = hashlib.sha256(source_bytes).hexdigest()
    state_path = args.results.with_suffix(".imported_sha256")
    if args.apply and state_path.exists() and state_path.read_text().strip() == fingerprint:
        print("browser_results_unchanged")
        return
    records = []
    for line in source_bytes.decode("utf-8-sig").splitlines():
        try:
            records.append(json.loads(line))
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            continue
    ready = select_observations(records, queue)
    print(json.dumps({"browser_records":len(records),
                      "validated_review_candidates":len(ready), "apply":args.apply}))
    if not args.apply or not ready:
        return
    for i in range(0, len(ready), 100):
        command = ["docker","exec","-i","cardyssey-db","psql","-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1",
                   "-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-q"]
        subprocess.run(command, input=sql_for(ready[i:i+100]), check=True,
                       text=True, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    # Retain the stable product ID and canonical URL for later revisit.
    # SOURCE_ONLY never implies an authenticated, print-verified price.
    for i in range(0,len(ready),100):
        batch=ready[i:i+100]
        payload=json.dumps(batch,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(",", ":")).replace("'", "''")
        routing_sql="""
WITH incoming AS (
 SELECT jsonb_array_elements('""" + payload + """'::jsonb) AS data
), fields AS (
 SELECT data->>'language_code' AS language_code,
        data->>'card_id' AS card_id, data->>'variant_id' AS variant_id,
        data->'candidate'->>'product_id' AS product_id,
        (data->>'refresh_days')::integer AS refresh_days,
        split_part(data->'candidate'->>'url','?',1) AS product_url
 FROM incoming
)
INSERT INTO price_source_routing
(language_code,card_id,variant_id,primary_provider,provider_product_id,
 provider_product_url,source_evidence,verification_state,fallback_provider,
 refresh_days,last_attempt_at,next_attempt_at)
SELECT language_code,card_id,variant_id,'CARDMARKET',product_id,
       product_url,'Browser filtered asking offers; exact print unverified',
       'SOURCE_ONLY','TCGCSV_TCGPLAYER',refresh_days,NOW(),
       NOW()+make_interval(days=>refresh_days)
FROM fields
WHERE product_url LIKE ('https://www.cardmarket.com/' || language_code || '/Pokemon/Products/Singles/%')
ON CONFLICT(language_code,card_id,variant_id) DO UPDATE SET
 provider_product_id=CASE
   WHEN price_source_routing.verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'
   THEN price_source_routing.provider_product_id
   ELSE EXCLUDED.provider_product_id END,
 provider_product_url=CASE
   WHEN price_source_routing.verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'
   THEN price_source_routing.provider_product_url
   ELSE EXCLUDED.provider_product_url END,
 refresh_days=EXCLUDED.refresh_days,
 next_attempt_at=EXCLUDED.next_attempt_at,
 updated_at=NOW()
WHERE price_source_routing.verification_state<>'VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID';
"""
        subprocess.run(["docker","exec","-i","cardyssey-db","psql", "-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1",
                        "-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-q"],input=routing_sql,
                       text=True,check=True,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    state_path.write_text(fingerprint + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print("review_cases_synced", len(ready))

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# DATEI 048/107: scripts/import-cardmarket-filtered-prices.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Promote COMPLETE filtered asking medians only for a human-confirmed print.

The browser samples are NOT sold prices. Unverified catalog mappings or
incomplete pagination never write a card price.
"""
import argparse,json,math,subprocess
from datetime import datetime,timezone,timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit,parse_qs

def collect(path,language,now=None):
    if language not in ('de','en'):raise ValueError('language')
    now=now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    latest={}
    with path.open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
        for line in f:
            try:
                x=json.loads(line)
                if x.get('card_id') and x.get('variant_id'):
                    latest[(x['card_id'],x['variant_id'])]=x
            except (ValueError,TypeError):continue
    results=[]
    for (card,variant),x in latest.items():
        if (x.get('language')!=language or x.get('identity_confirmed') is not True or
            x.get('status')!='COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED' or x.get('all_pages_complete') is not True):
            continue
        median=x.get('median_all_offers_eur')
        if isinstance(median,bool) or not isinstance(median,(float,int)) or not math.isfinite(median) or not 0<median<1e6:continue
        if not isinstance(x.get('verified_lang_nm_offers'),int) or x['verified_lang_nm_offers']<1:continue
        url=str(x.get('url') or '')
        u=urlsplit(url)
        if u.scheme!='https' or u.netloc!='www.cardmarket.com' or not u.path.startswith('/'+language+'/Pokemon/Products/Singles/'):
            continue
        q=parse_qs(u.query)
        if q.get('language')!=(['3'] if language=='de' else ['1']) or q.get('minCondition')!=['2']:
            continue
        # Only publish if edition/foil filters are explicit, not broad mixed offers.
        reverse=x.get('expected_reverse_holo')
        first=x.get('expected_first_edition')
        if reverse not in ('Y','N') or first not in ('Y','N'):continue
        # Do not import a mixed print's median under the guise of an exact
        # copyright, textured foil, promo stamp, or other unsupported print.
        if x.get('non_filterable_print_attributes') or x.get('exact_print_filter_supported') is False:continue
        if q.get('isReverseHolo')!=[reverse] or q.get('isFirstEd')!=[first]:continue
        try:seen=datetime.fromisoformat(str(x.get('seen_at') or '').replace('Z','+00:00')).astimezone(timezone.utc)
        except (ValueError,TypeError):continue
        if seen<now-timedelta(days=8) or seen>now+timedelta(hours=1):continue
        pid=str(x.get('product_id') or '')
        if not pid.isdecimal():continue
        results.append({'language':language,'card':card,'variant':variant,
            'product_id':pid,'url':url.split('?')[0],'price':round(median,4),'observed_at':seen.isoformat()})
    return results

SQL="""
WITH rows AS (
 SELECT item->>'language' AS lang,item->>'card' AS card,item->>'variant' AS variant,
 item->>'product_id' AS pid,item->>'url' AS url,(item->>'price')::numeric AS price,
 (item->>'observed_at')::timestamptz AS observed
 FROM jsonb_array_elements({payload}::jsonb) item
), approved AS (
 SELECT r.* FROM rows r JOIN price_source_routing p
 ON p.language_code=r.lang AND p.card_id=r.card AND p.variant_id=r.variant
 WHERE p.primary_provider='CARDMARKET' AND p.verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'
 AND p.provider_product_id=r.pid
 AND (p.provider_product_url IS NULL OR p.provider_product_url='' OR split_part(p.provider_product_url,'?',1)=r.url)
 AND p.source_evidence LIKE 'Human confirmed exact%'
)
INSERT INTO tcg_price_cache(card_id,language_code,variant_id,source,base_eur,updated_at,resolution_status)
SELECT card,lang,variant,
 CASE WHEN lang='de' THEN 'CARDMARKET_DE_NM_WEB' ELSE 'CARDMARKET_EN_NM_WEB' END,
 price,observed,'PRICE_FOUND' FROM approved
ON CONFLICT(card_id,language_code,variant_id) DO UPDATE SET
 source=EXCLUDED.source,base_eur=EXCLUDED.base_eur,
 updated_at=EXCLUDED.updated_at,resolution_status='PRICE_FOUND',failure_detail=NULL
WHERE tcg_price_cache.updated_at<=EXCLUDED.updated_at;
"""

def apply(rows):
    for i in range(0,len(rows),100):
        data=json.dumps(rows[i:i+100],ensure_ascii=False,separators=(',',':')).replace("'","''")
        sql='BEGIN;\n'+SQL.format(payload="'"+data+"'")+'\nCOMMIT;'
        subprocess.run(['docker','exec','-i','cardyssey-db','psql','-X','-q','-v','ON_ERROR_STOP=1',
                        '-U','cardyssey','-d','cardyssey'],input=sql,text=True,check=True,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
if __name__=='__main__':
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--language',choices=('de','en'),required=True)
    p.add_argument('--results',type=Path,required=True);p.add_argument('--apply',action='store_true');a=p.parse_args()
    rows=collect(a.results,a.language)
    if a.apply and rows:apply(rows)
    print(json.dumps({'language':a.language,'full_filtered_candidates':len(rows),
       'applied_with_human_print_verification_only':a.apply,'basis':'complete_active_asking_median_not_sold'}))

# DATEI 049/107: scripts/import-cardmarket-language-probes.py
