#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Stage language-filtered Cardmarket asking quotes without publishing them.

Input MUST be produced by verify-cardmarket-language-browser-smoke.py.
No tcg_price_cache, routing or collection writes occur here.
"""
import argparse
import json
import subprocess
from decimal import Decimal
from pathlib import Path

ROOT=Path("/home/tv/cardshop")
DEFAULT=ROOT/"reports/cardmarket-language-smoke-results.json"

def clean(rows):
    result=[]
    for row in rows:
        product=str(row.get("product_id") or "")
        lang=row.get("language")
        basis=row.get("basis")
        lo=Decimal(str(row.get("lowest_ask_eur")))
        entry=Decimal(str(row.get("entry_five_median_eur")))
        seen=int(row.get("visible_offers",0))
        validated=int(row.get("verified_sample_rows",0))
        if not product.isdigit() or lang not in ("de","en"):
            raise ValueError("invalid product identity or language")
        if basis!="active_asking_5_lowest_visible_offers_NOT_SOLD_OR_NM_ONLY":
            raise ValueError("unverified price basis")
        if row.get("printing_verified") is not False or row.get("market_wide") is not False:
            raise ValueError("unsafe printing/market flag")
        if validated<5 or seen<validated or lo<=0 or entry<=0:
            raise ValueError("insufficient eligible offers")
        result.append({"product_id":product,"language":lang,"low":str(lo),
                       "entry5":str(entry),"validated":validated,"visible":seen})
    if not result:raise ValueError("empty result")
    if len({(x["product_id"],x["language"]) for x in result})!=len(result):
        raise ValueError("duplicate product-language pair")
    return result

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--input",type=Path,default=DEFAULT)
    p.add_argument("--apply",action="store_true")
    args=p.parse_args()
    rows=clean(json.loads(args.input.read_text(encoding="utf-8")))
    if args.apply:
        quoted=json.dumps(rows,separators=(",",":")).replace("'","''")
        sql="""WITH incoming AS (
            SELECT x->>'product_id' product_id,x->>'language' language_code,
                   (x->>'low')::numeric low,(x->>'entry5')::numeric entry5,
                   (x->>'validated')::int verified_preview_rows,
                   (x->>'visible')::int visible_offers
            FROM jsonb_array_elements('"""+quoted+"""'::jsonb) x
          )
          INSERT INTO cardmarket_language_offer_samples
            (product_id,language_code,lowest_ask_eur,entry_five_median_eur,
             verified_preview_rows,visible_offers,source_note)
          SELECT product_id,language_code,low,entry5,verified_preview_rows,visible_offers,
                 'Six-card-side browser probe, language + minCondition=2, printing unverified'
          FROM incoming
          ON CONFLICT(product_id,language_code,minimum_condition,sample_date) DO UPDATE SET
            captured_at=NOW(),lowest_ask_eur=EXCLUDED.lowest_ask_eur,
            entry_five_median_eur=EXCLUDED.entry_five_median_eur,
            verified_preview_rows=EXCLUDED.verified_preview_rows,
            visible_offers=EXCLUDED.visible_offers,
            source_note=EXCLUDED.source_note;"""
        subprocess.run(["docker","exec","cardyssey-db","psql","-X","-q","-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1",
                        "-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-c",sql],
                       check=True,capture_output=True,text=True)
    print(json.dumps({"validated_records":len(rows),"product_ids":len(set(x["product_id"] for x in rows)),
                      "languages":["de","en"],"staged":args.apply,
                      "published_to_card_cache":False},ensure_ascii=False))

if __name__=="__main__":main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 050/107: scripts/import-cardmarket-mapping-safe.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Safely stage Cardmarket browser ID/URL observations in existing routing.

Only confirmed browser records with a valid URL and an eligible candidate ID are accepted.
Never writes price values, never promotes candidate evidence to verified variant identity.
Preserves existing verified rows, conflicting IDs, and other primary providers.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import json
import re
import subprocess
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlsplit

def valid_url(url, lang):
    try:
        p=urlsplit(url)
        return (p.scheme=="https" and p.netloc=="www.cardmarket.com"
                and p.path.startswith(f"/{lang}/Pokemon/Products/Singles/"))
    except ValueError:
        return False

def load_queue(path):
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig",newline="") as f:
        return {(r["cardyssey_karte"],r["variante_id"]):r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}

def collect(files, queue, language, set_slugs=None):
    found={}
    reasons=Counter()
    set_slugs=set_slugs or {}
    for path in files:
        with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
            for line in f:
                try:
                    r=json.loads(line)
                    key=(str(r.get("card_id") or ""),str(r.get("variant_id") or ""))
                    q=queue.get(key)
                    pid=str(r.get("product_id") or "")
                    url=str(r.get("url") or "").split("?")[0]
                    if not q:
                        reasons["NOT_IN_QUEUE"]+=1
                        continue
                    if r.get("language",language)!=language or r.get("identity_confirmed") is not True:
                        reasons["UNCONFIRMED"]+=1
                        continue
                    if not pid.isdigit() or not valid_url(url,language):
                        reasons["INVALID_ID_OR_URL"]+=1
                        continue
                    set_slug=set_slugs.get(q.get('set_id'))
                    if set_slug and not urlsplit(url).path.split('/')[5].startswith(set_slug):
                        reasons['WRONG_CARDMARKET_SET']+=1
                        continue
                    allowed={x.strip() for x in (q.get("cardmarket_ids") or "").split(",")}
                    if pid not in allowed:
                        reasons["ID_NOT_IN_CANDIDATES"]+=1
                        continue
                    if q.get("known_url") and q["known_url"].split("?")[0]!=url:
                        reasons["URL_CONFLICT"]+=1
                        continue
                    found[key]={"language_code":language,"card_id":key[0],"variant_id":key[1],
                                "product_id":pid,"url":url}
                except (json.JSONDecodeError, KeyError, TypeError):
                    reasons["BAD_RECORD"]+=1
    return list(found.values()), reasons

SQL=r"""
WITH incoming AS (
 SELECT item->>'language_code' AS language_code,
        item->>'card_id' AS card_id,
        item->>'variant_id' AS variant_id,
        item->>'product_id' AS product_id,
        item->>'url' AS product_url
 FROM jsonb_array_elements({payload}::jsonb) item
), eligible AS (
 SELECT i.* FROM incoming i
 JOIN catalog_cards_mirror c ON c.language_code=i.language_code AND c.card_id=i.card_id
 WHERE EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM jsonb_array_elements(c.variants) v
               WHERE v->>'id'=i.variant_id)
)
INSERT INTO price_source_routing
 (language_code,card_id,variant_id,primary_provider,provider_product_id,
  provider_product_url,source_evidence,verification_state)
SELECT language_code,card_id,variant_id,'CARDMARKET',product_id,product_url,
       'Cardmarket browser product ID and URL matched candidate; print and condition NOT verified',
       'SOURCE_ONLY'
FROM eligible
ON CONFLICT (language_code,card_id,variant_id) DO UPDATE
SET provider_product_id=EXCLUDED.provider_product_id,
    provider_product_url=EXCLUDED.provider_product_url,
    source_evidence=EXCLUDED.source_evidence,
    updated_at=NOW()
WHERE price_source_routing.primary_provider='CARDMARKET'
  AND price_source_routing.verification_state='SOURCE_ONLY'
  AND (price_source_routing.provider_product_id IS NULL
       OR price_source_routing.provider_product_id=EXCLUDED.provider_product_id)
  AND (price_source_routing.provider_product_url IS NULL
       OR price_source_routing.provider_product_url=EXCLUDED.provider_product_url);
"""

def apply(rows):
    for i in range(0,len(rows),100):
        payload=json.dumps(rows[i:i+100],ensure_ascii=False,separators=(",",":")).replace("'","''")
        sql="BEGIN;\n"+SQL.format(payload="'"+payload+"'")+"\nCOMMIT;"
        subprocess.run(["docker","exec","-i","cardyssey-db","psql","-X","-q",
                        "-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey"],
                       input=sql,text=True,check=True,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)
        # Human-confirmed print mappings may have an ID but no canonical URL.
        # Fill that URL only when this same ID was verified on a browser page;
        # never change a different ID or a conflicting existing URL.
        url_sql="""WITH source AS (
 SELECT data->>'language_code' lang,data->>'card_id' card,
 data->>'variant_id' variant,data->>'product_id' pid,data->>'url' url
 FROM jsonb_array_elements('""" + payload + """'::jsonb) data
)
UPDATE price_source_routing p SET provider_product_url=s.url,updated_at=NOW()
FROM source s WHERE p.language_code=s.lang AND p.card_id=s.card
 AND p.variant_id=s.variant AND p.primary_provider='CARDMARKET'
 AND p.verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'
 AND p.source_evidence LIKE 'Human confirmed exact%'
 AND p.provider_product_id=s.pid
 AND (p.provider_product_url IS NULL OR p.provider_product_url='');"""
        subprocess.run(['docker','exec','-i','cardyssey-db','psql','-X','-q','-v','ON_ERROR_STOP=1',
                        '-U','cardyssey','-d','cardyssey'],input=url_sql,check=True,
                       text=True,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL)

def main():
    a=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    a.add_argument("--queue",type=Path,required=True)
    a.add_argument("--results",type=Path,nargs="+",required=True)
    a.add_argument("--language",choices=["de","en"],required=True)
    a.add_argument("--apply",action="store_true")
    args=a.parse_args()
    guards=Path('/home/tv/cardshop/config/cardmarket-set-url-guards.json')
    slugs=json.loads(guards.read_text(encoding='utf-8')) if guards.exists() else {}
    rows,reasons=collect(args.results,load_queue(args.queue),args.language,slugs)
    print(json.dumps({"language":args.language,"valid_observations":len(rows),
                      "rejected":dict(reasons),"applied":args.apply},ensure_ascii=False))
    if args.apply and rows:apply(rows)
if __name__=="__main__":
    main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 051/107: scripts/map-all-de-cardmarket-catalog.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Catalog-wide German Cardmarket product crosswalk (no unverified price writes).

Batch-match distinct cards against the official product feed and validated
expansion mappings. Store candidates separately from exact print identity.
"""
import argparse
import collections
import json
import subprocess
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from price_feed_client import fetch_json
from price_identity_validation import split_by_verified_expansion
from importlib.machinery import SourceFileLoader

PRODUCT_URL="https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json"
PRICE_URL="https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/priceGuide/price_guide_6.json"
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent

def db(sql):
    proc=subprocess.run(["docker","exec","-i","cardyssey-db","psql","-X","-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1",
                         "-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-At"],
                        input=sql,text=True,capture_output=True,check=True,timeout=120)
    return proc.stdout.strip()

def quote(x):
    return "'"+str(x).replace("'","''")+"'"

def sem_name(v):
    import re
    s=str(v or "").lower().replace("’","'").replace("☆"," gold star ").replace("★"," gold star ")
    s=re.sub(r"δ\s*delta\s*species"," delta species ",s)
    s=s.replace("δ"," delta species ")
    s=re.sub(r"\bgold\s+star\b","goldstar",s)
    s=re.sub(r"\bdelta\s+species\b","deltaspecies",s)
    s=re.sub(r"\[[^\]]*\]","",s)
    s=re.sub(r"\s*\([^)]*\)\s*$","",s)
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+","",s)

def calculate_set_map(en_cards,products):
    names=collections.defaultdict(list)
    for card in en_cards:
        key=sem_name(card.get("name"))
        if key:names[card["set_id"]].append(key)
    by_exp=collections.defaultdict(collections.Counter)
    for product in products:
        name=sem_name(product.get("name"))
        if name:by_exp[str(product.get("idExpansion"))][name]+=1
    maps={}
    for sid,card_names in names.items():
        wanted=collections.Counter(card_names)
        scored=[]
        for exp,available in by_exp.items():
            count=sum(min(n,available.get(name,0)) for name,n in wanted.items())
            if count:scored.append((count,count/max(1,len(card_names)),exp))
        scored.sort(reverse=True)
        if not scored:continue
        first=scored[0]
        second=scored[1] if len(scored)>1 else (0,0,None)
        # A full name-list tie is NOT a verified expansion. This previously
        # sent 2019 McDonalds to the French printing: both contain 12 names.
        # Only an independently confirmed set override may resolve that tie.
        if first[1]>=0.90 and first[0] > second[0] and (first[0]-second[0]>=3 or first[1]>=0.98):
            maps[sid]=first[2]
    return maps

def calculate_variant_set_consensus(en_cards, products):
    """Find set expansions using *distinct card identities* carrying Cardmarket
    product IDs, not the overlapping lists of names of reprints.

    Different variants of a Cardyssey set may legitimately use other Cardmarket
    expansions: majority votes identify the principal expansion only. Exact
    variant IDs remain separate; do not promote secondary expansions blindly.
    """
    product_exp={str(p['idProduct']):str(p['idExpansion']) for p in products}
    by_set=collections.defaultdict(dict)
    for c in en_cards:
        expansions=set()
        variants=c.get('variants') or []
        if isinstance(variants,str):
            try:variants=json.loads(variants)
            except ValueError:variants=[]
        for variant in variants:
            pid=str(variant.get('cardmarketProductId') or '') if isinstance(variant,dict) else ''
            if pid in product_exp:expansions.add(product_exp[pid])
        if expansions:by_set[c['set_id']][c['card_id']]=expansions
    maps={}
    for sid,cards in by_set.items():
        count=len(cards)
        if count<5:continue
        votes=collections.Counter(exp for expansions in cards.values() for exp in expansions)
        top=votes.most_common(2)
        win_exp,win_count=top[0]
        second=top[1][1] if len(top)>1 else 0
        if (win_count>=8 and win_count/count>=.98 and win_count-second>=7) or (win_count>=5 and win_count==count and second==0):
            maps[sid]=win_exp
    return maps

def candidates_for_card(card,by_exp_name,set_map):
    eid=set_map.get(card["set_id"])
    if eid is None:return [],"SET_NOT_VERIFIED"
    names=by_exp_name.get((eid,sem_name(card.get("english_name"))),[])
    if not names:return [],"NAME_NOT_FOUND_IN_VERIFIED_SET"
    ids=sorted({str(p["idProduct"]) for p in names})
    return ids,"SINGLE_SET_PRODUCT" if len(ids)==1 else "MULTIPLE_SET_PRODUCTS"

def collect():
    products=fetch_json(PRODUCT_URL).get("products") or []
    price_rows=fetch_json(PRICE_URL).get("priceGuides") or []
    priced_ids={str(p.get("idProduct")) for p in price_rows}
    data=db("""SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(x)),'[]'::jsonb) FROM (
        SELECT card_id,set_id,local_id,name,variants
        FROM catalog_cards_mirror WHERE language_code='en') x;""")
    en=json.loads(data)
    data=db("""SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(x)),'[]'::jsonb) FROM (
        SELECT d.card_id,d.set_id,d.local_id,d.name AS german_name,
            COALESCE(e.name,d.name) AS english_name,
            jsonb_array_length(d.variants) AS variant_count
        FROM catalog_cards_mirror d
        LEFT JOIN catalog_cards_mirror e ON e.language_code='en'
          AND e.set_id=d.set_id AND e.local_id=d.local_id
        WHERE d.language_code='de') x;""")
    de=json.loads(data)
    # Name overlap is merely a suggestion, not a trusted set identity.
    # 2019 McDonalds and Base Set were previously mapped to the wrong
    # expansion by that heuristic. Require multiple exact per-card IDs.
    set_map=calculate_variant_set_consensus(en,products)
    # Exact, manually confirmed SET-level corrections override fuzzy name
    # overlap. Never turn a set override into a verified foil/print mapping.
    override_file=ROOT.parent/'config/cardmarket-verified-set-overrides.json'
    if override_file.is_file():
        overrides=json.loads(override_file.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        known_expansions={str(p.get('idExpansion')) for p in products}
        for set_id,evidence in overrides.items():
            eid=str(evidence.get('expansionId') or '')
            if eid not in known_expansions:raise RuntimeError('Missing verified Cardmarket expansion for '+set_id)
            set_map[set_id]=eid
    names=collections.defaultdict(list)
    for p in products:
        key=sem_name(p.get("name"))
        if key:names[(str(p.get("idExpansion")),key)].append(p)
    # A single card name can appear at several printed numbers in the SAME
    # expansion. Catalog products do not carry a machine-readable card number.
    # Never merge their IDs just because the names are identical.
    number_sets=collections.defaultdict(set)
    for card in en:
        number_sets[(card['set_id'],sem_name(card['name']))].add(str(card['local_id']))
    rows=[]
    for card in de:
        ids,status=candidates_for_card(card,names,set_map)
        if len(number_sets.get((card['set_id'],sem_name(card['english_name'])),set()))>1:
            ids=[]
            status='SAME_NAME_DIFFERENT_NUMBERS_REQUIRE_VARIANT_ID'
        rows.append({"card_id":card["card_id"],"set_id":card["set_id"],
                     "local_id":card["local_id"],"english_name":card["english_name"],
                     "variant_count":card["variant_count"],"set_expansion":set_map.get(card["set_id"]),
                     "product_ids":ids,"with_guide":sum(x in priced_ids for x in ids),
                     "status":status})
    return rows,{"sets_matched":len(set_map),"set_evidence":"MULTIPLE_DISTINCT_CARD_VARIANT_IDS_PLUS_EXPLICIT_OVERRIDES","feed_products":len(products),
                 "guide_products":len(priced_ids)}

DDL="""
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates (
  language_code text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'de',
  card_id text NOT NULL,
  set_id text NOT NULL,
  printed_number text,
  english_name text,
  provider_expansion_id text,
  provider_product_ids jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,
  candidate_status text NOT NULL,
  priceguide_candidate_count integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  catalog_variant_count integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  checked_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),
  PRIMARY KEY(language_code,card_id)
);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates_status_idx
 ON cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates(candidate_status,checked_at);
"""

def apply(rows):
    db(DDL)
    for i in range(0,len(rows),400):
        records=[{"language_code":"de","card_id":r["card_id"],"set_id":r["set_id"],
                  "printed_number":r["local_id"],"english_name":r["english_name"],
                  "provider_expansion_id":r["set_expansion"],"provider_product_ids":r["product_ids"],
                  "candidate_status":r["status"],"priceguide_candidate_count":r["with_guide"],
                  "catalog_variant_count":r["variant_count"]} for r in rows[i:i+400]]
        data=quote(json.dumps(records,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(",",":")))
        sql=f"""
WITH data AS (SELECT jsonb_array_elements({data}::jsonb) AS x),
fields AS (
 SELECT x->>'language_code' AS language_code,x->>'card_id' AS card_id,
 x->>'set_id' AS set_id,x->>'printed_number' AS printed_number,
 x->>'english_name' AS english_name,
 x->>'provider_expansion_id' AS provider_expansion_id,
 x->'provider_product_ids' AS provider_product_ids,
 x->>'candidate_status' AS candidate_status,
 (x->>'priceguide_candidate_count')::integer AS priceguide_candidate_count,
 (x->>'catalog_variant_count')::integer AS catalog_variant_count FROM data)
INSERT INTO cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates
(language_code,card_id,set_id,printed_number,english_name,provider_expansion_id,
 provider_product_ids,candidate_status,priceguide_candidate_count,catalog_variant_count)
SELECT language_code,card_id,set_id,printed_number,english_name,
 provider_expansion_id,provider_product_ids,candidate_status,
 priceguide_candidate_count,catalog_variant_count FROM fields
ON CONFLICT(language_code,card_id) DO UPDATE SET
 set_id=EXCLUDED.set_id, printed_number=EXCLUDED.printed_number,
 english_name=EXCLUDED.english_name,provider_expansion_id=EXCLUDED.provider_expansion_id,
 provider_product_ids=EXCLUDED.provider_product_ids,candidate_status=EXCLUDED.candidate_status,
 priceguide_candidate_count=EXCLUDED.priceguide_candidate_count,
 catalog_variant_count=EXCLUDED.catalog_variant_count,checked_at=NOW();
"""
        db(sql)
    # Stage a unique product bucket as SOURCE_ONLY. This is a product pointer,
    # NOT verified condition/foil/stamp identity and cannot publish a price.
    # Existing verified routes or TCGplayer routes are left untouched.
    db('''
INSERT INTO price_source_routing
(language_code,card_id,variant_id,primary_provider,provider_product_id,
 source_evidence,verification_state,refresh_days,fallback_provider,
 next_attempt_at)
SELECT p.language_code,p.card_id,p.variant_id,'CARDMARKET',
       link.provider_product_ids->>0,
       'Official catalogue: unique name within high-confidence expansion; print unverified',
       'SOURCE_ONLY',
       CASE WHEN p.resolution_status<>'PRICE_FOUND' OR p.base_eur>=20 THEN 7
            WHEN c.rarity IN ('Häufig','Ungewöhnlich') THEN 30
            ELSE 14 END,
       CASE WHEN p.source IN ('TCGCSV_TCGPLAYER','TCGDEX_TCGPLAYER')
            THEN p.source ELSE 'TCGDEX' END,
       NOW()
FROM cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates link
JOIN tcg_price_cache p ON p.card_id=link.card_id AND p.language_code=link.language_code
JOIN catalog_cards_mirror c ON c.card_id=p.card_id AND c.language_code=p.language_code
JOIN LATERAL jsonb_array_elements(c.variants) v(value) ON v.value->>'id'=p.variant_id
WHERE link.language_code='de' AND link.candidate_status='SINGLE_SET_PRODUCT'
 -- A singleton name-bucket must never override a different Cardmarket ID
 -- explicitly attached to the physical print/variant in Cardyssey.
 AND (COALESCE(v.value->>'cardmarketProductId','') !~ '^[0-9]+$'
      OR v.value->>'cardmarketProductId'=link.provider_product_ids->>0)
 AND jsonb_array_length(link.provider_product_ids)=1
ON CONFLICT(language_code,card_id,variant_id) DO NOTHING;
''')

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--apply",action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--report",type=Path,default=Path("/tmp/cardyssey-all-de-cardmarket-catalog-report.json"))
    args=p.parse_args()
    rows,feed=collect()
    if args.apply:apply(rows)
    counts=dict(collections.Counter(r["status"] for r in rows))
    summary={"checked_at":datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
             "applied":args.apply,"distinct_de_cards":len(rows),
             "total_de_variants":sum(r["variant_count"] for r in rows),
             "candidate_statuses":counts,"cards_with_any_candidates":sum(bool(r["product_ids"]) for r in rows),
             "variants_with_any_candidate":sum(r["variant_count"] for r in rows if r["product_ids"]),
             "note":"Catalog matches are candidate product buckets, NOT verified German NM print prices.",
             **feed}
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(summary,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
    print(json.dumps(summary,ensure_ascii=False))
if __name__=="__main__": main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 052/107: scripts/prepare-cardmarket-browser-review.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build a safe, auditable Cardmarket German print-identity review queue.

Candidate IDs are suggestions, never automatic assignments. The product page
number, set, language, edition, finish and special stamps need independent proof.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import json
from collections import Counter
from pathlib import Path


def triage(item):
    products = item.get("products") or []
    same_set = bool(item.get("set_verified"))
    external = item.get("exact_variant_provider_ids") or {}
    direct = str(external.get("cardmarket") or "").strip()
    candidate_ids = {str(p.get("id")) for p in products}
    hinted = item.get("name_only_search_hints") or []
    variant = item.get("variant") or {}
    special = any((variant.get(k) for k in ("subtype", "foil", "stamps")))
    if direct and direct in candidate_ids and same_set:
        return "P1", "EXTERNAL_CARDMARKET_ID_IN_VERIFIED_SET", "Produktnummer und exakte Druckidentitaet auf Produktseite pruefen"
    if direct and direct not in candidate_ids:
        return "P1", "EXTERNAL_CARDMARKET_ID_CONFLICT", "Externe Varianten-ID gegen Set und Karte pruefen; keinen Kandidaten automatisch zuordnen"
    if same_set and len(products) == 1:
        return "P2" if not special else "P3", "SINGLE_SET_CANDIDATE", "Kartennummer, Sprache, Edition und Sonderdruck pruefen"
    if same_set and len(products) > 1:
        return "P3", "MULTIPLE_SET_CANDIDATES", "Kartennummer und Artwork/Attacken zur Unterscheidung pruefen"
    if external:
        return "P3", "EXTERNAL_VARIANT_REFERENCE_ONLY", "Externe Varianten-ID nach Set und Druck pruefen"
    if hinted:
        return "P4", "NAME_ONLY_SEARCH_HINTS", "Set zuerst belegen; Namensgleichheit ist kein Produktmatch"
    return "P5", "NO_PRODUCT_CANDIDATE", "Produktkatalog nach Set, Nummer und Namen manuell recherchieren"


def make_rows(items):
    rows = []
    for x in items:
        candidates = x.get("products") or []
        variant = x.get("variant") or {}
        external = x.get("exact_variant_provider_ids") or {}
        priority, action, instruction = triage(x)
        rows.append({
            "prioritaet": priority, "status": "ZU_PRUEFEN", "prueffall": action,
            "naechster_schritt": instruction, "set_id": x.get("set_code") or "",
            "kartennummer": x.get("number") or "",
            "kartenname_suche": x.get("english_name") or "",
            "cardyssey_karte": x.get("card_id") or "", "variante_id": x.get("variant_id") or "",
            "drucktyp": variant.get("type") or "", "sonderdruck": variant.get("subtype") or "",
            "foil": variant.get("foil") or "", "stempel": ",".join(variant.get("stamps") or []),
            "produktkandidaten": len(candidates),
            "cardmarket_ids": ",".join(str(c.get("id") or "") for c in candidates),
            "cardmarket_namen": " | ".join(str(c.get("name") or "") for c in candidates),
            "externe_cardmarket_id": str(external.get("cardmarket") or ""),
            "externe_tcgplayer_id": str(external.get("tcgplayer") or ""),
            "externe_cardtrader_id": str(external.get("cardtrader") or ""),
            "namenstreffer_nur_hinweis": int(x.get("name_only_match_count") or 0),
            "namenstreffer_ids": ",".join(str(c.get("id") or "") for c in (x.get("name_only_search_hints") or [])),
            "offen_zu_pruefen": ",".join(x.get("unverified_identity_fields") or []),
            "suche": x.get("search_query") or "",
            "filter_sprache": "Deutsch (language[3])",
            "filter_zustand": "mind. Near Mint (minCondition=2)",
            "filter_signiert": "Nein", "filter_veraendert": "Nein",
            "produktnummer_bestaetigt": "", "set_bestaetigt": "",
            "sprache_bestaetigt": "", "edition_bestaetigt": "",
            "foil_bestaetigt": "", "stempel_bestaetigt": "",
            "manuell_bestaetigte_id": "", "manuell_bestaetigte_edition": "",
            "manuell_bestaetigte_sprache": "", "notizen": "",
        })
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: (r["prioritaet"], r["set_id"], r["cardyssey_karte"], r["variante_id"]))
    return rows


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--input", type=Path, required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output-dir", type=Path, required=True)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    items = json.loads(args.input.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["unresolved"]
    rows = make_rows(items)
    args.output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    if rows:
        path = args.output_dir / "de_offene_cardmarket_varianten.csv"
        with path.open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
            writer = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=list(rows[0]))
            writer.writeheader()
            writer.writerows(rows)
    summary = {
        "total": len(rows),
        "with_product_candidates": sum(bool(r["produktkandidaten"]) for r in rows),
        "with_exact_variant_provider_ids": sum(bool(x.get("exact_variant_provider_ids")) for x in items),
        "with_direct_cardmarket_variant_id": sum(bool((x.get("exact_variant_provider_ids") or {}).get("cardmarket")) for x in items),
        "name_only_hint_without_set_candidate": sum(bool(x.get("name_only_search_hints")) and not x.get("products") for x in items),
        "priorities": dict(Counter(r["prioritaet"] for r in rows)),
        "review_actions": dict(Counter(r["prueffall"] for r in rows)),
        "safety": "No product ID or price is confirmed/published by this review queue.",
        "first_examples": [{k: r[k] for k in ("set_id", "cardyssey_karte", "prueffall", "cardmarket_ids")} for r in rows[:5]],
    }
    (args.output_dir / "status.json").write_text(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(summary, ensure_ascii=False))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 053/107: scripts/prioritize-cardmarket-scan-queue.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Put actually unpriced German variants first, then valuable/special print cases.

The queue is rebuilt without changing the stable variant IDs or old observations.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

def db_rows(language='de'):
    if language not in ('de','en'):raise ValueError('Invalid language')
    sql = """SELECT json_agg(json_build_object(
        'card_id',p.card_id,'variant_id',p.variant_id,
        'status',p.resolution_status,'price',p.base_eur,'source',p.source,
        'rarity',c.rarity))
    FROM tcg_price_cache p LEFT JOIN catalog_cards_mirror c
    ON c.card_id=p.card_id AND c.language_code=p.language_code
    WHERE p.language_code='{language}'""".format(language=language)
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["docker","exec","cardyssey-db","psql","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey",
         "-At","-c",sql], capture_output=True,text=True,check=True)
    return json.loads(result.stdout.strip() or "[]") or []

def prioritize(rows, cache):
    lookup = {(r["card_id"],r["variant_id"]): r for r in cache}
    expensive = 0
    for row in rows:
        state = lookup.get((row["cardyssey_karte"],row["variante_id"]),{})
        value = float(state.get("price") or 0)
        missing = state.get("status") != "PRICE_FOUND" or value <= 0
        stamp = str(row.get("stempel") or "").strip()
        # JSON [] is an empty stamp list, not a special printing.
        special = bool(row.get("sonderdruck") or row.get("foil") or (stamp not in ("", "[]", "null")))
        rare = str(state.get("rarity") or "").casefold() in {
            "selten","rare","ultra rare","secret rare","illustration rare",
            "special illustration rare","hyper rare","promo"}
        expensive_row = value >= 20
        bulk = str(state.get("rarity") or "").casefold() in {
            "häufig","ungewöhnlich","common","uncommon"}
        # Bulk stays on price feeds EVEN when no price exists yet.
        # Existing price is a usable interim value; browser scans are for
        # missing non-bulk or high-value print-specific verification.
        if bulk and not special: tier=4
        elif missing: tier=0
        elif expensive_row: tier=1
        elif special or rare: tier=2
        else: tier=3
        row["scan_tier"] = tier
        row["existing_price_eur"] = f"{value:.4f}" if not missing else ""
        row["existing_source"] = str(state.get("source") or "")
        row["needs_price"] = "YES" if missing else "NO"
        expensive += int(expensive_row)
    rows.sort(key=lambda r:(r["scan_tier"],r.get("prioritaet",""),
                            r.get("set_id",""),r["cardyssey_karte"],r["variante_id"]))
    return rows

def main():
    parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--queue",type=Path,required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--output",type=Path,required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--language',choices=('de','en'),default='de')
    args=parser.parse_args()
    with args.queue.open(encoding="utf-8-sig",newline="") as f:
        rows=list(csv.DictReader(f))
    prioritized=prioritize(rows,db_rows(args.language))
    if len(prioritized)!=len(rows):raise ValueError("lost queue rows")
    unique={(r["cardyssey_karte"],r["variante_id"]) for r in prioritized}
    if len(unique)!=len(rows):raise ValueError("duplicate variant key")
    args.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    with args.output.open("w",encoding="utf-8-sig",newline="") as f:
        writer=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=prioritized[0].keys())
        writer.writeheader();writer.writerows(prioritized)
    print(json.dumps({"rows":len(prioritized),
                      "missing_first":sum(x["needs_price"]=="YES" and x["scan_tier"]!=4 for x in prioritized),
                      "feed_only":sum(x["scan_tier"]==4 for x in prioritized),
                      "already_priced":sum(x["needs_price"]=="NO" for x in prioritized),
                      "first_cards":[x["cardyssey_karte"] for x in prioritized[:27]]},
                     ensure_ascii=False))
if __name__=="__main__":main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 054/107: scripts/reconcile-cardmarket-variant-identity-review.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Correct previously merged Cardmarket candidates using explicit per-variant IDs.

The official product feed validates the expansion. The existing Cardyssey
variant_id distinguishes identical names printed under different card numbers.
This does NOT verify language/foil/edition or publish a price.
"""
import argparse,hashlib,json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path('/home/tv/cardshop')
FEED='https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json'
PRODUCTS=Path('/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/price-feeds')/(hashlib.sha256(FEED.encode()).hexdigest()+'.json')

def reconcile(apply=False):
 products=json.loads(PRODUCTS.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))['products']
 # Only IDs actually carried by a Cardyssey physical variant, not every
 # similarly named candidate in the product catalog.
 query="""SELECT DISTINCT v->>'cardmarketProductId' FROM catalog_cards_mirror c
 CROSS JOIN LATERAL jsonb_array_elements(c.variants) v
 WHERE c.language_code IN ('de','en')
 AND v->>'cardmarketProductId' ~ '^[0-9]{3,12}$';"""
 cmd=['docker','exec','cardyssey-db','psql','-X','-qAt','-v','ON_ERROR_STOP=1','-U','cardyssey','-d','cardyssey']
 p=subprocess.run(cmd+['-c',query],text=True,capture_output=True,check=True,timeout=60)
 variant_ids=set(p.stdout.split())
 catalog={str(x['idProduct']):str(x['idExpansion']) for x in products if str(x['idProduct']) in variant_ids}
 vals=','.join("('%s','%s')"%(pid,exp) for pid,exp in sorted(catalog.items()))
 if not vals:raise RuntimeError('no official product IDs')
 sql="""BEGIN;
 CREATE TEMP TABLE cm_official_variant_product (id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, expansion TEXT) ON COMMIT DROP;
 INSERT INTO cm_official_variant_product(id,expansion) VALUES """+vals+""";
 WITH exact AS (
 SELECT r.language_code,r.card_id,r.variant_id,v.value->>'cardmarketProductId' product_id
 FROM cardmarket_print_identity_review r
 JOIN catalog_cards_mirror c ON c.language_code=r.language_code AND c.card_id=r.card_id
 CROSS JOIN LATERAL jsonb_array_elements(c.variants) v(value)
 JOIN cm_official_variant_product p ON p.id=v.value->>'cardmarketProductId'
 WHERE v.value->>'id'=r.variant_id
 AND r.status='PENDING_REVIEW'
 AND p.expansion=r.provider_expansion_id
 AND (r.matched_set_ids<>jsonb_build_array(p.id) OR r.candidate_ids<>jsonb_build_array(p.id))
 ), updated AS (
 UPDATE cardmarket_print_identity_review r
 SET candidate_ids=jsonb_build_array(e.product_id),matched_set_ids=jsonb_build_array(e.product_id),
 classification='CATALOG_VARIANT_ID_PRINT_PENDING',
 product_names='Exact Cardyssey variant product ID; language/print still pending',
 checked_at=NOW()
 FROM exact e WHERE r.language_code=e.language_code AND r.card_id=e.card_id
 AND r.variant_id=e.variant_id RETURNING r.language_code,r.card_id,r.variant_id
 )
 SELECT json_build_object('corrected',count(*),'de',count(*) FILTER(WHERE language_code='de'),
 'en',count(*) FILTER(WHERE language_code='en'))::text FROM updated;
 -- Reconcile stale official-name-only SOURCE_ONLY routes only when an exact
 -- physical variant ID and its official product expansion agree.
 WITH exact_route AS (
 SELECT r.language_code,r.card_id,r.variant_id,p.id AS product_id
 FROM price_source_routing r
 JOIN catalog_cards_mirror c ON c.language_code=r.language_code AND c.card_id=r.card_id
 CROSS JOIN LATERAL jsonb_array_elements(c.variants) v(value)
 JOIN cm_official_variant_product p ON p.id=v.value->>'cardmarketProductId'
 JOIN cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates k ON k.language_code='de' AND k.card_id=c.card_id
 WHERE v.value->>'id'=r.variant_id
   AND p.expansion=k.provider_expansion_id
   AND r.primary_provider='CARDMARKET' AND r.verification_state='SOURCE_ONLY'
   AND r.source_evidence LIKE 'Official catalogue:%'
   AND r.provider_product_id IS DISTINCT FROM p.id
 ), updated_route AS (
 UPDATE price_source_routing r SET provider_product_id=e.product_id,provider_product_url=NULL,
 source_evidence='Official Cardyssey variant-specific Cardmarket product ID in matched expansion; print and language unverified',
 updated_at=NOW(),next_attempt_at=NOW()
 FROM exact_route e
 WHERE r.language_code=e.language_code AND r.card_id=e.card_id AND r.variant_id=e.variant_id
 RETURNING r.language_code
 )
 SELECT json_build_object('sourceOnlyRoutesCorrected',count(*))::text FROM updated_route;
 """+('COMMIT;' if apply else 'ROLLBACK;')
 out=subprocess.run(['docker','exec','-i','cardyssey-db','psql','-X','-qAt','-v','ON_ERROR_STOP=1','-U','cardyssey','-d','cardyssey'],input=sql,capture_output=True,text=True,timeout=120)
 if out.returncode:raise RuntimeError(out.stderr[-950:])
 responses=[json.loads(line) for line in out.stdout.splitlines() if line.startswith('{')]
 result={}
 for response in responses:result.update(response)
 return {'applied':apply,'catalogVariants':len(variant_ids),'officialProducts':len(catalog),**result}
if __name__=='__main__':
 a=argparse.ArgumentParser();a.add_argument('--apply',action='store_true');args=a.parse_args()
 print(json.dumps(reconcile(args.apply),ensure_ascii=False))


############################################################################################
# DATEI 055/107: scripts/recover-cardmarket-broken-scan.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Recover actionable Cardmarket verification jobs from a failed browser run.

This is offline: no HTTP or price writes. Exact catalogue product IDs are
*not* evidence of the requested language, near-mint condition or special print.
"""
import argparse
import collections
import csv
import json
from pathlib import Path

def recovery(queue, observations):
    latest={}
    for line in observations:
        try:
            r=json.loads(line)
            if r.get("key"):latest[r["key"]]=r
        except (ValueError,TypeError):
            continue
    result=[]
    stats=collections.Counter()
    for row in queue:
        key=f'{row["cardyssey_karte"]}|{row["variante_id"]}'
        old=latest.get(key,{})
        if old.get("status")!="ERROR":continue
        ids=list(dict.fromkeys(i.strip() for i in row["cardmarket_ids"].split(",") if i.strip().isdigit()))
        stats["failed"]+=1
        if len(ids)==1:
            kind="UNIQUE_CATALOG_PRODUCT_REQUIRES_PRINT_VERIFICATION"
            stats["single_product"]+=1
        elif len(ids)>1:
            kind="AMBIGUOUS_PRODUCTS_REQUIRES_IDENTITY_REVIEW"
            stats["ambiguous_product"]+=1
        else:
            kind="NO_CATALOG_PRODUCT"
            stats["no_product"]+=1
        result.append({
            "key":key,"card_id":row["cardyssey_karte"],
            "variant_id":row["variante_id"],
            "set_id":row["set_id"],"printed_number":row["kartennummer"],
            "english_name":row["kartenname_suche"],
            "product_ids":",".join(ids),"category":kind,
            "special":bool(row["sonderdruck"] or row["foil"] or row["stempel"]),
            "price_source":row["existing_source"],
            "existing_price_eur":row["existing_price_eur"],
            "requires_de_nm_variant_verification":True,
        })
    return result,stats

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--queue",type=Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument("--observations",type=Path,required=True)
    p.add_argument("--output",type=Path,required=True)
    a=p.parse_args()
    with a.queue.open(encoding="utf-8-sig",newline="") as f:
        rows=list(csv.DictReader(f))
    recovered,stats=recovery(rows,a.observations.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig").splitlines())
    a.output.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    temp=a.output.with_suffix(".tmp")
    temp.write_text(json.dumps({"summary":dict(stats),"records":recovered,
      "policy":"No candidate is certified as German NM or exact print by this report; no production prices changed."},
      ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
    temp.replace(a.output)
    print(json.dumps({"summary":dict(stats),"unique_ids":len({
       r["product_ids"] for r in recovered if r["category"].startswith("UNIQUE")}),
       "output":str(a.output)},ensure_ascii=False))

if __name__=="__main__":main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 056/107: scripts/refresh-cardmarket-official-weekly.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Weekly refresh of already mapped official Cardmarket guide prices.

No scraping or individual card network calls. Exact existing product IDs only.
DE/EN are Cardyssey catalog language labels, not language-filtered guide prices.
"""
import argparse
import json
import math
import re
import subprocess
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from price_feed_client import fetch_json

PRICE_URL="https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/priceGuide/price_guide_6.json"
DETAIL=re.compile(r"Cardmarket product (\d+); key ([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)")
SAFE_KEYS={"avg30","avg30-holo","trend","trend-holo","avg7","avg7-holo","avg","avg-holo","low","low-holo"}
def db(sql,apply=False):
    cmd=["docker","exec"]+(["-i"] if apply else [])+["cardyssey-db","psql","-X","-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-At","-c",sql] if not apply else ["docker","exec","-i","cardyssey-db","psql","-X","-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-q"]
    return subprocess.run(cmd,input=(sql if apply else None),capture_output=True,text=True,check=True).stdout

def sql_quote(s):
    return str(s).replace("'","''")

def value_for(guide,oldkey):
    suffix="-holo" if oldkey.endswith("-holo") else ""
    for key in ("avg30"+suffix,"trend"+suffix,"avg7"+suffix,"avg"+suffix,"low"+suffix):
        v=guide.get(key)
        if isinstance(v,(int,float)) and not isinstance(v,bool) and math.isfinite(v) and v>0:
            return round(float(v),4),key
    return None,None

def get_rows(lang):
    q=f"""SELECT json_build_object(
      'card_id',p.card_id,'language',p.language_code,'variant_id',p.variant_id,
      'old_price',p.base_eur,'failure_detail',p.failure_detail,
      'variant',v.value
      )::text
      FROM tcg_price_cache p
      JOIN catalog_cards_mirror c ON c.language_code=p.language_code AND c.card_id=p.card_id
      JOIN LATERAL jsonb_array_elements(c.variants) v(value) ON v.value->>'id'=p.variant_id
      WHERE p.language_code='{lang}' AND p.source='CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL'
      ORDER BY p.card_id,p.variant_id"""
    return [json.loads(x) for x in db(q).splitlines() if x.strip()]

def prepare(rows,guide):
    stats={"source_rows":len(rows),"no_stored_id":0,"not_in_guide":0,"no_valid_price":0,
           "ambiguous_variant":0,"ready":0,"avg30":0,"fallback":0}
    updates=[]
    for row in rows:
        m=DETAIL.search(row["failure_detail"] or "")
        if not m or m.group(2) not in SAFE_KEYS:
            stats["no_stored_id"]+=1;continue
        variant=row["variant"] or {}
        # Do not silently assign a generic guide bucket to a special physical printing.
        if variant.get("foil") or variant.get("stamps") or variant.get("subtype"):
            stats["ambiguous_variant"]+=1;continue
        item=guide.get(m.group(1))
        if not item:
            stats["not_in_guide"]+=1;continue
        price,key=value_for(item,m.group(2))
        if price is None:
            stats["no_valid_price"]+=1;continue
        stats["ready"]+=1
        stats["avg30" if key.startswith("avg30") else "fallback"]+=1
        updates.append(dict(card_id=row["card_id"],language=row["language"],
                            variant_id=row["variant_id"],product_id=m.group(1),
                            price=price,guide_key=key,old_price=row["old_price"]))
    return updates,stats

def apply_updates(rows,guide_date):
    for start in range(0,len(rows),250):
        chunk=rows[start:start+250]
        literal=sql_quote(json.dumps(chunk,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(",",":")))
        date=sql_quote(guide_date)
        sql=f"""BEGIN;
WITH incoming AS (
 SELECT x->>'card_id' card_id,x->>'language' language_code,x->>'variant_id' variant_id,
        (x->>'price')::numeric price,x->>'product_id' product_id,x->>'guide_key' guide_key
 FROM jsonb_array_elements('{literal}'::jsonb) x
), changed AS (
 UPDATE tcg_price_cache p
 SET base_eur=i.price,updated_at=NOW(),
     resolution_status='PRICE_FOUND',
     failure_detail='Cardmarket product '||i.product_id||'; key '||i.guide_key||
       '; via weekly-official-guide; aggregate not language-filtered NM offer; feed {date}',
     next_attempt_at=NOW()+INTERVAL '7 days'
 FROM incoming i
 WHERE p.card_id=i.card_id AND p.language_code=i.language_code
   AND p.variant_id=i.variant_id AND p.source='CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL'
   AND p.failure_detail LIKE 'Cardmarket product '||i.product_id||';%'
 RETURNING p.card_id,p.language_code,p.variant_id,p.base_eur
)
INSERT INTO tcg_price_history(card_id,language_code,variant_id,source,base_eur,captured_at)
SELECT card_id,language_code,variant_id,'CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL',base_eur,NOW() FROM changed;
COMMIT;"""
        db(sql,apply=True)

def main():
    a=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    a.add_argument("--language",choices=("de","en","both"),default="both")
    a.add_argument("--apply",action="store_true")
    a.add_argument("--report",type=Path,default=Path("/home/tv/cardshop/reports/cardmarket-weekly-official.json"))
    args=a.parse_args()
    payload=fetch_json(PRICE_URL,max_age=20*3600)
    prices={str(x["idProduct"]):x for x in (payload.get("priceGuides") or [])}
    stamp=str(payload.get("createdAt") or "")
    if len(prices)<50000 or not stamp:
        raise RuntimeError("Official Cardmarket price guide invalid or empty")
    langs=["de","en"] if args.language=="both" else [args.language]
    report={"guide_date":stamp,"products":len(prices),"mode":"apply" if args.apply else "dry-run",
            "language_specific_prices":False,"languages":{}}
    for lang in langs:
        updates,stats=prepare(get_rows(lang),prices)
        report["languages"][lang]=stats
        if args.apply and updates:
            apply_updates(updates,stamp)
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    tmp=args.report.with_suffix(".tmp")
    tmp.write_text(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    tmp.replace(args.report)
    print(json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False))
if __name__=="__main__":
    main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 057/107: scripts/refresh-cardmarket-price-cache.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Backfill and refresh verified Cardmarket prices from Cardmarket's public Pokémon JSON files.

Identity resolution is fail-closed:
1) prefer a Cardmarket product id exposed by TCGdex for the exact card;
2) otherwise use a high-confidence Cardmarket expansion mapping plus exact English card name.

Special physical variants are only resolved when the Cardmarket identity cannot
collapse multiple distinct holo-like variants into one bucket.
"""
import argparse
import collections
import concurrent.futures
import json
from pathlib import Path
import math
import re
import subprocess
import requests
from price_feed_client import fetch_json as cached_feed_json
from price_identity_validation import print_reasons,candidate_status,split_by_verified_expansion

UA = {"User-Agent": "Cardyssey/0.4 cardmarket-price-refresh"}
PRICE_URL = "https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/priceGuide/price_guide_6.json"
PRODUCT_URL = "https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json"

def db(sql):
    return subprocess.run(
        ["docker","exec","cardyssey-db","psql","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-At","-F","\t","-c",sql],
        check=True,capture_output=True,text=True
    ).stdout.strip()

def q(value):
    return str(value).replace("'","''")

def positive(value):
    return isinstance(value,(int,float)) and math.isfinite(value) and value > 0

def norm(value):
    s=str(value or "").lower().replace("’","'")
    for a,b in (("é","e"),("è","e"),("á","a"),("ö","o"),("ü","u"),("ä","a")):
        s=s.replace(a,b)
    s=re.sub(r"\[[^\]]*\]","",s)
    s=re.sub(r"\s*\([^)]*\)\s*$","",s)
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+","",s)

def semantic_name(value):
    s=str(value or "").lower().replace("’","'")
    # Cardmarket spells legacy rarity/Delta markers out while TCGdex often
    # uses the printed symbols. Normalize both representations.
    s=s.replace("☆"," gold star ").replace("★"," gold star ")
    s=re.sub(r"δ\s*delta\s*species", " delta species ", s)
    s=s.replace("δ"," delta species ")
    s=re.sub(r"gold\s+star", "goldstar", s)
    s=re.sub(r"delta\s+species", "deltaspecies", s)
    s=re.sub(r"\[[^\]]*\]","",s)
    s=re.sub(r"\s*\([^)]*\)\s*$","",s)
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+","",s)

def norm_full(value):
    s=str(value or "").lower().replace("’","'")
    for a,b in (("é","e"),("è","e"),("á","a"),("ö","o"),("ü","u"),("ä","a")):
        s=s.replace(a,b)
    return re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+","",s)

def variant_is_holo(legacy, variant):
    typ=str(variant.get("type") or "").lower()
    return (
        str(legacy).upper() in ("HOLO","REVERSE")
        or typ in ("holo","reverse")
        or bool(variant.get("foil"))
    )

def variant_is_special(variant):
    return bool(variant.get("foil") or variant.get("stamps") or variant.get("subtype"))

def holo_variant_count(variants):
    count=0
    for v in variants:
        if variant_is_holo(v.get("legacyVariant"),v):
            count+=1
    return count

def price_from_guide(guide, legacy, variant):
    if variant_is_holo(legacy,variant):
        keys=("avg30-holo","trend-holo","avg7-holo","avg-holo","low-holo")
    else:
        keys=("avg30","trend","avg7","avg","low")
    for key in keys:
        value=guide.get(key)
        if positive(value):
            return float(value),key
    return None,None

def explicit_special_product(name, variant):
    text=str(name or "").lower()
    foil=str(variant.get("foil") or "").lower().replace("_","-")
    stamps=" ".join(str(x).lower() for x in (variant.get("stamps") or []))
    subtype=str(variant.get("subtype") or "").lower()
    if foil in ("cosmos","kosmos"):
        return "cosmos" in text or "cosmo holo" in text
    if foil in ("cracked-ice","cracked ice"):
        return "cracked ice" in text
    if foil=="league":
        return "league" in text
    if "staff" in stamps:
        return "staff" in text
    if subtype and subtype in text:
        return True
    return not variant_is_special(variant)

parser=argparse.ArgumentParser()
parser.add_argument("--dry-run",action="store_true")
parser.add_argument("--language",choices=("de","en"),default=None)
parser.add_argument("--fallback-report",type=Path,help="JSON audit of unresolved identity candidates, no guessed prices")
args=parser.parse_args()

print("cardmarket_download=products_start",flush=True)
products_payload=cached_feed_json(PRODUCT_URL,headers=UA)
print(f"cardmarket_download=products_done items={len(products_payload.get('products') or [])}",flush=True)
print("cardmarket_download=prices_start",flush=True)
price_payload=cached_feed_json(PRICE_URL,headers=UA)
print(f"cardmarket_download=prices_done items={len(price_payload.get('priceGuides') or [])}",flush=True)
products=products_payload.get("products") or []
price_rows=price_payload.get("priceGuides") or []
prices={str(x.get("idProduct")):x for x in price_rows}

by_exp=collections.defaultdict(list)
by_name=collections.defaultdict(list)
by_semantic_name=collections.defaultdict(list)
for product in products:
    by_exp[product.get("idExpansion")].append(product)
    key=norm(product.get("name"))
    if key:
        by_name[key].append(product)
    semantic_key=semantic_name(product.get("name"))
    if semantic_key:
        by_semantic_name[semantic_key].append(product)

# Build a high-confidence Cardyssey set -> Cardmarket idExpansion map from full EN set-name overlap.
set_cards=collections.defaultdict(list)
for line in db("SELECT set_id,name FROM catalog_cards_mirror WHERE language_code='en';").splitlines():
    if "\t" not in line:
        continue
    sid,name=line.split("\t",1)
    key=semantic_name(name)
    if key:
        set_cards[sid].append(key)

exp_names={
    eid:collections.Counter(
        semantic_name(p.get("name"))
        for p in plist
        if semantic_name(p.get("name"))
    )
    for eid,plist in by_exp.items()
}
set_map={}
for sid,names in set_cards.items():
    wanted=collections.Counter(names)
    scores=[]
    for eid,available in exp_names.items():
        overlap=sum(min(count,available.get(name,0)) for name,count in wanted.items())
        if overlap:
            scores.append((overlap,overlap/max(1,len(names)),eid))
    scores.sort(reverse=True)
    if not scores:
        continue
    top=scores[0]
    second=scores[1] if len(scores)>1 else (0,0,None)
    if top[1] >= 0.90 and (top[0]-second[0] >= 3 or top[1] >= 0.98):
        set_map[sid]=top[2]

rows=[]
query="""
SELECT p.card_id,p.language_code,c.set_id,c.local_id,
       COALESCE(e.name,c.name) AS en_name,
       p.variant_id,p.legacy_variant,c.variants::text,
       p.source,p.failure_detail
FROM tcg_price_cache p
JOIN catalog_cards_mirror c
  ON c.card_id=p.card_id AND c.language_code=p.language_code
LEFT JOIN catalog_cards_mirror e
  ON e.language_code='en' AND e.set_id=c.set_id AND e.local_id=c.local_id
WHERE p.language_code IN ('de','en')
  AND (p.resolution_status IN ('NO_PRICE','NO_PUBLIC_PRICE')
       OR p.source='CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL'
       OR (p.language_code='de' AND p.source IN ('TCGCSV_TCGPLAYER','TCGDEX_TCGPLAYER')))
  AND (p.language_code='de' OR NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM price_source_routing routing
    WHERE routing.language_code=p.language_code AND routing.card_id=p.card_id
      AND routing.variant_id=p.variant_id
      AND routing.verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'
      AND routing.primary_provider<>'CARDMARKET'
  ))
ORDER BY p.language_code,p.card_id,p.variant_id
"""
for line in db(query).splitlines():
    parts=line.split("\t",9)
    if len(parts)!=10:
        continue
    cid,lang,sid,local_id,en_name,vid,legacy,variants_json,source,failure=parts
    if args.language and lang != args.language:
        continue
    variants=json.loads(variants_json or "[]")
    selected=next((v for v in variants if str(v.get("id") or "")==vid),None)
    if selected is None:
        continue
    rows.append({
        "card_id":cid,"language":lang,"set_id":sid,"local_id":local_id,
        "en_name":en_name,"variant_id":vid,"legacy":legacy,"variant":selected,
        "variants":variants,"source":source,"failure":failure,
    })

# Refresh already verified Cardmarket rows from stored product id without re-resolving identity.
# German TCGplayer-derived cache rows are *search targets*, never a German price
# until an independently matched Cardmarket guide/product identity is found.
updates=[]
pending=[]
pattern=re.compile(r"Cardmarket product (\d+); key ([A-Za-z0-9_-]+)")
for row in rows:
    if row["source"]=="CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL":
        match=pattern.search(row["failure"] or "")
        if match:
            product_id=match.group(1)
            guide=prices.get(product_id)
            if guide:
                value,key=price_from_guide(guide,row["legacy"],row["variant"])
                if value is not None:
                    updates.append((row,product_id,value,key,"refresh"))
                    continue
    pending.append(row)

# Fetch TCGdex details only for unresolved rows; this is identity metadata, not the price source.
card_keys=sorted({(r["card_id"],r["language"]) for r in pending})
def fetch_card(key):
    cid,lang=key
    try:
        response=requests.get(
            f"https://api.tcgdex.net/v2/{lang}/cards/{cid}",
            headers=UA,timeout=20
        )
        return key,(response.json() if response.status_code==200 else None)
    except requests.RequestException:
        return key,None

cards={}
with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=12) as pool:
    for index,(key,payload) in enumerate(pool.map(fetch_card,card_keys),1):
        cards[key]=payload
        if index%250==0 or index==len(card_keys):
            print(f"cardmarket_identity={index}/{len(card_keys)}",flush=True)

resolved_keys={(u[0]["card_id"],u[0]["language"],u[0]["variant_id"]) for u in updates}
for row in pending:
    identity=(row["card_id"],row["language"],row["variant_id"])
    if identity in resolved_keys:
        continue

    guide=None
    product_id=None
    product_name=None
    method=None
    card=cards.get((row["card_id"],row["language"]))

    # Preferred: exact Cardmarket product bucket supplied by TCGdex card details.
    if card:
        top_cm=((card.get("pricing") or {}).get("cardmarket") or {})
        top_id=top_cm.get("idProduct")
        if top_id is not None and str(top_id) in prices:
            # Special variants can use a shared Cardmarket foil bucket only if there is
            # exactly one holo-like physical variant on this card.
            if not variant_is_special(row["variant"]) or holo_variant_count(row["variants"])==1:
                product_id=str(top_id)
                guide=prices[product_id]
                method="tcgdex-cardmarket-id"

    # Fallback: high-confidence set mapping + exact EN card name.
    # This name-based path is intentionally limited to Western languages.
    # Asian cards use only TCGdex's exact Cardmarket product identity.
    if guide is None and row["language"] in ("de","en") and row["set_id"] in set_map:
        eid=set_map[row["set_id"]]
        candidates=[
            p for p in by_exp[eid]
            if (
                norm(p.get("name"))==norm(row["en_name"])
                or semantic_name(p.get("name"))==semantic_name(row["en_name"])
            )
        ]
        if len(candidates)>1 and card:
            attacks=[
                str(a.get("name") or "")
                for a in (card.get("attacks") or [])
                if str(a.get("name") or "").strip()
            ]
            if attacks:
                attack_matches=[
                    p for p in candidates
                    if all(norm_full(attack) in norm_full(p.get("name")) for attack in attacks)
                ]
                if len(attack_matches)==1:
                    candidates=attack_matches
        if len(candidates)==1:
            candidate=candidates[0]
            if (
                not variant_is_special(row["variant"])
                or explicit_special_product(candidate.get("name"),row["variant"])
            ):
                candidate_id=str(candidate.get("idProduct"))
                if candidate_id in prices:
                    product_id=candidate_id
                    product_name=candidate.get("name")
                    guide=prices[candidate_id]
                    method="set-name"

    if guide is None:
        continue
    value,key=price_from_guide(guide,row["legacy"],row["variant"])
    if value is None:
        continue
    updates.append((row,product_id,value,key,method))

# Second-stage fallback: use the real card name and set context to expose
# otherwise overlooked provider candidates. This is a diagnostic route, not
# automatic authority to assign a price across languages or printings.
found_keys={(u[0]["card_id"],u[0]["language"],u[0]["variant_id"]) for u in updates}
fallback=[]
for row in rows:
    identity=(row["card_id"],row["language"],row["variant_id"])
    if identity in found_keys:
        continue
    label=row["en_name"]
    # Search query for human/retailer fallback: number and name first.
    # Product-catalog names do not contain printed numbers, so the number
    # is a search hint, NOT evidence of a validated product identity.
    card_details=cards.get((row["card_id"],row["language"])) or {}
    total=((card_details.get("set") or {}).get("cardCount") or {}).get("official")
    # Promo set totals are not card-number denominators.
    promo_prefixes=("np","dpp","basep","svp","mep","bwp","swshp","smp","xyp")
    printed_number=str(row["local_id"])+("/"+str(total) if total and row["set_id"] not in promo_prefixes else "")
    search_name=str(label).replace("☆", "Gold Star").replace("★", "Gold Star")
    search_query=" ".join(str(part) for part in (search_name, printed_number, row["set_id"], "German" if row["language"]=="de" else "English" if row["language"]=="en" else row["language"]) if part)
    matching=[p for p in by_name.get(norm(label),[]) if str(p.get("idProduct")) in prices]
    # Also support semantic star/delta naming, only if the normalized name
    # yields no matches. Avoid a full catalogue scan on every card.
    if not matching:
        matching=[p for p in by_semantic_name.get(semantic_name(label),[])
                  if str(p.get("idProduct")) in prices]
    matched_set=set_map.get(row["set_id"])
    # Name matches outside a verified expansion are search hints only.
    candidates,related_name_matches=split_by_verified_expansion(matching,matched_set)
    same_set=candidates
    # The public product list does not establish a language-filtered or
    # first-edition-filtered price. Never mark a candidate as import-ready.
    reasons=print_reasons(row["variant"],candidates[0].get("name") if len(candidates)==1 else "",
                          set_verified=bool(same_set),number_verified=False,
                          language_verified=False,edition_verified=False)
    exact_variant=None
    detail_card=cards.get((row["card_id"],row["language"])) or {}
    for variant_detail in detail_card.get("variants_detailed") or []:
        if str(variant_detail.get("variantId") or "")==row["variant_id"]:
            exact_variant=variant_detail
            break
    exact_links=(exact_variant or {}).get("thirdParty") or {}
    exact_languages=(exact_variant or {}).get("languages") or []
    if exact_languages and row["language"] not in [str(x).lower() for x in exact_languages]:
        exact_links={}
    fallback.append(dict(language=row["language"],card_id=row["card_id"],
                         set_code=row["set_id"],number=row["local_id"],
                         english_name=label,search_query=search_query,
                         lookup_steps=["TCGdex exact card/variant ID","Cardmarket exact or semantic name",
                                       "Cardmarket expansion check","language/edition/print check"],
                         variant_id=row["variant_id"],
                         variant=row["variant"],total_candidates=len(candidates),
                         set_verified=bool(same_set),
                         status=candidate_status(len(candidates),reasons),
                         unverified_identity_fields=reasons,
                         exact_variant_provider_ids={
                             provider:identifier for provider,identifier in exact_links.items()
                             if provider in ("tcgplayer","cardmarket","cardtrader")
                             and identifier is not None},
                         products=[dict(id=x.get("idProduct"),name=x.get("name"),
                                        expansion=x.get("idExpansion")) for x in candidates[:8]],
                         name_only_match_count=len(related_name_matches),
                         name_only_search_hints=[dict(id=x.get("idProduct"),name=x.get("name"),
                                        expansion=x.get("idExpansion")) for x in related_name_matches[:5]]))
if args.fallback_report:
    args.fallback_report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.fallback_report.write_text(json.dumps({
        "source":"Cardmarket public product catalogue",
        "scope":"Unresolved after quick product-ID and safe set/name lookups",
        "warning":"Candidates are NOT verified language/edition/size/stamp prices",
        "unresolved":fallback},ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n")
print(f"cardmarket_fallback_unresolved={len(fallback)} review_candidates={sum(bool(x['products']) for x in fallback)}",flush=True)

# Deduplicate by cache primary key, preferring exact Cardmarket-id resolution.
best={}
rank={"refresh":3,"tcgdex-cardmarket-id":2,"set-name":1}
for update in updates:
    row=update[0]
    key=(row["card_id"],row["language"],row["variant_id"])
    if key not in best or rank.get(update[4],0)>rank.get(best[key][4],0):
        best[key]=update
updates=list(best.values())

print(
    f"cardmarket_created_at={price_payload.get('createdAt')} "
    f"catalog_products={len(products)} set_map={len(set_map)} "
    f"eligible_rows={len(rows)} updates={len(updates)} dry_run={args.dry_run}",
    flush=True
)

for row,product_id,value,price_key,method in updates:
    print(
        f"{row['language']} {row['card_id']} {row['variant_id']} "
        f"{value:.4f} EUR product={product_id} key={price_key} via={method}",
        flush=True
    )

if not args.dry_run and updates:
    sql=["BEGIN;"]
    for row,product_id,value,price_key,method in updates:
        detail=(f"Cardmarket product {product_id}; key {price_key}; via {method}; "
                "public price-guide aggregate; not a language-filtered NM offer")
        sql.append(f"""
UPDATE tcg_price_cache
SET source='CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL',
    base_eur={value:.4f},
    updated_at=NOW(),
    resolution_status='PRICE_FOUND',
    failure_detail='{q(detail)}',
    next_attempt_at=NOW()+INTERVAL '14 days'
WHERE card_id='{q(row["card_id"])}'
  AND language_code='{q(row["language"])}'
  AND variant_id='{q(row["variant_id"])}';
""")
        sql.append(f"""
INSERT INTO tcg_price_history
(card_id,language_code,variant_id,source,base_eur,captured_at)
VALUES (
  '{q(row["card_id"])}','{q(row["language"])}','{q(row["variant_id"])}',
  'CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL',{value:.4f},NOW()
);
""")
    sql.append("COMMIT;")
    subprocess.run(
        ["docker","exec","-i","cardyssey-db","psql","-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey"],
        input="\n".join(sql),text=True,check=True,stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL
    )
    print(f"applied={len(updates)}",flush=True)
