# DATEI 058/107: scripts/repair-cardmarket-verified-expansions.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Repair explicitly verified Cardyssey=>Cardmarket SET links only.

Recomputes product candidates from official Cardmarket catalog INSIDE the
confirmed expansion, preserving ambiguity between physical V1/V2 prints.
Does not touch prices, human verified routes, or confirmed review decisions.
"""
import argparse,collections,hashlib,json,subprocess,csv,os
from pathlib import Path
from importlib.util import spec_from_file_location,module_from_spec
ROOT=Path('/home/tv/cardshop')
URL='https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json'
FEEDS=Path('/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/price-feeds')
BASE=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector')

def sql_quote(x):return "'"+str(x).replace("'","''")+"'"
def pg(sql):
 p=subprocess.run(['docker','exec','-i','cardyssey-db','psql','-X','-v','ON_ERROR_STOP=1','-U','cardyssey','-d','cardyssey'],input=sql,text=True,capture_output=True,timeout=80)
 if p.returncode:raise RuntimeError(p.stderr[-850:])
 return p.stdout

def build(selected):
 spec=spec_from_file_location('mapper',ROOT/'scripts/map-all-de-cardmarket-catalog.py')
 m=module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(m)
 data=json.loads((FEEDS/(hashlib.sha256(URL.encode()).hexdigest()+'.json')).read_text())
 products=data['products'];by=collections.defaultdict(list)
 for p in products:
  if str(p.get('idExpansion'))==selected:by[m.sem_name(p.get('name'))].append(p)
 q="SELECT COALESCE(jsonb_agg(to_jsonb(t)),'[]'::jsonb)::text FROM (SELECT card_id,set_id,local_id,name FROM catalog_cards_mirror WHERE language_code='en' AND set_id="+sql_quote(SET)+")t;"
 output=subprocess.run(['docker','exec','cardyssey-db','psql','-X','-qAt','-U','cardyssey','-d','cardyssey','-c',q],capture_output=True,text=True,check=True)
 return [{'card':r['card_id'],'name':r['name'],'ids':sorted({str(x['idProduct']) for x in by.get(m.sem_name(r['name']),[])})} for r in json.loads(output.stdout)]

if __name__=='__main__':
 ap=argparse.ArgumentParser();ap.add_argument('--set',required=True);ap.add_argument('--apply',action='store_true');a=ap.parse_args();SET=a.set
 overrides=json.loads((ROOT/'config/cardmarket-verified-set-overrides.json').read_text())
 if SET not in overrides:raise SystemExit('set has no independently confirmed expansion')
 expansion=str(overrides[SET]['expansionId']);rows=build(expansion)
 if len(rows)<3:raise RuntimeError('insufficient known set cards')
 info={'set':SET,'expansion':expansion,'cards':len(rows),'single':sum(len(x['ids'])==1 for x in rows),'multiple':sum(len(x['ids'])>1 for x in rows),'missing':sum(len(x['ids'])==0 for x in rows),'applied':a.apply}
 if a.apply:
  payload=sql_quote(json.dumps(rows,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(',',':')))
  script=f"""BEGIN;
WITH incoming AS (SELECT j->>'card' card,j->'ids' ids,j->>'name' name FROM jsonb_array_elements({payload}::jsonb) j)
UPDATE cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates c SET provider_expansion_id={sql_quote(expansion)},provider_product_ids=i.ids,
 candidate_status=CASE WHEN jsonb_array_length(i.ids)=0 THEN 'NAME_NOT_FOUND_IN_VERIFIED_SET'
 WHEN jsonb_array_length(i.ids)=1 THEN 'SINGLE_SET_PRODUCT' ELSE 'MULTIPLE_SET_PRODUCTS' END,
 checked_at=NOW() FROM incoming i WHERE c.card_id=i.card AND c.set_id={sql_quote(SET)} AND c.language_code='de';
WITH incoming AS (SELECT j->>'card' card,j->'ids' ids,j->>'name' name FROM jsonb_array_elements({payload}::jsonb) j)
UPDATE cardmarket_print_identity_review r SET provider_expansion_id={sql_quote(expansion)},
 candidate_ids=i.ids,matched_set_ids=i.ids,
 product_names='Set-confirmed Cardmarket expansion; print not independently proven',
 classification=CASE WHEN jsonb_array_length(i.ids)=0 THEN 'NO_PRODUCT_ID'
 WHEN jsonb_array_length(i.ids)=1 THEN 'ONE_PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_SPECIAL_PRINT'
 ELSE 'MULTIPLE_PRODUCTS_IN_VERIFIED_SET_SPECIAL_PRINT' END, checked_at=NOW()
 FROM incoming i WHERE r.card_id=i.card AND r.set_id={sql_quote(SET)}
 AND r.language_code IN ('de','en') AND r.status='PENDING_REVIEW';
COMMIT;"""
  pg(script)
  # Strip known-false product identities only for the old catalogue SOURCE_ONLY route.
  # Keep row and fallback provider, but clear unverified Cardmarket product hint.
  q=f"""UPDATE price_source_routing r SET provider_product_id=NULL,provider_product_url=NULL,
 source_evidence='Catalog product identity quarantined after verified expansion correction; print pending',updated_at=NOW()
 FROM cardmarket_catalog_link_candidates l
 WHERE r.language_code='de' AND r.card_id=l.card_id AND l.set_id={sql_quote(SET)}
 AND r.primary_provider='CARDMARKET' AND r.verification_state='SOURCE_ONLY'
 AND r.source_evidence LIKE 'Official catalogue:%';"""
  pg('BEGIN;'+q+'COMMIT;')
  ids={r['card']:r['ids'] for r in rows}
  for lang in ('de','en'):
   path=BASE/f'queue-{lang}.csv'
   with path.open(encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
    rd=csv.DictReader(f);fields=list(rd.fieldnames or []);old=list(rd)
   changed=0
   for r in old:
    if r['cardyssey_karte'] not in ids or r.get('routing_state')=='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID':continue
    r['cardmarket_ids']=','.join(ids[r['cardyssey_karte']]);r['routing_state']='UNVERIFIED';r['known_url']=''
    if 'catalog_singleton_id' in fields:r['catalog_singleton_id']=''
    if 'catalog_search_name' in fields:r['catalog_search_name']=''
    changed+=1
   tmp=path.with_suffix(path.suffix+'.repair.tmp')
   with tmp.open('w',encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
    wr=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=fields);wr.writeheader();wr.writerows(old)
   os.replace(tmp,path)
   info[lang+'_queue']=changed
 print(json.dumps(info,ensure_ascii=False))


############################################################################################
# DATEI 059/107: scripts/repair-de-cardmarket-product-prices.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Recover German TCG prices by exact Cardmarket product identity.

For remaining physical NO_PRICE rows, fetch current TCGdex details and only
reuse a card-level Cardmarket price when the selected physical variant points
at exactly that same Cardmarket product id. This is safe even when several
physical metadata variants share one Cardmarket listing: Cardmarket itself
prices them as one market bucket.
"""
import concurrent.futures
import json
import math
import subprocess
import requests

BASE = "https://api.tcgdex.net/v2/de/cards"

def db(sql):
    return subprocess.run(
        ["docker","exec","cardyssey-db","psql","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-At","-F","\t","-c",sql],
        check=True,capture_output=True,text=True
    ).stdout.strip()

def q(value):
    return value.replace("'","''")

def positive(value):
    return isinstance(value,(int,float)) and math.isfinite(value) and value > 0

def cardmarket_price(cm, variant):
    if not isinstance(cm,dict):
        return None
    typ = str(variant.get("type") or "").strip().lower()
    foil = str(variant.get("foil") or "").strip()
    holo_like = typ in ("holo","reverse") or bool(foil)
    keys = (
        ["trend-holo","avg1-holo","avg7-holo","avg30-holo","avg-holo","low-holo"]
        if holo_like else []
    ) + ["trend","avg1","avg7","avg30","avg","low"]
    for key in keys:
        value = cm.get(key)
        if positive(value):
            return float(value)
    return None

rows = db("""
SELECT p.card_id,p.variant_id,p.legacy_variant
FROM tcg_price_cache p
JOIN catalog_cards_mirror c
  ON c.card_id=p.card_id AND c.language_code=p.language_code
WHERE p.language_code='de'
  AND p.resolution_status='NO_PRICE'
  AND NOT (c.set_id ~ '^(A[0-9]|B[0-9])' OR c.set_id='P-A')
ORDER BY p.card_id,p.variant_id
""").splitlines()

targets = [tuple(line.split("\t")) for line in rows if line.strip()]
card_ids = sorted({row[0] for row in targets})
print(f"rows={len(targets)} cards={len(card_ids)}", flush=True)

def fetch(cid):
    for attempt in range(3):
        try:
            response = requests.get(
                f"{BASE}/{cid}",
                headers={"User-Agent":"Cardyssey price repair"},
                timeout=20,
            )
            if response.status_code == 200:
                return cid, response.json()
            if response.status_code == 404:
                return cid, None
        except requests.RequestException:
            pass
    return cid, None

cards = {}
with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=8) as executor:
    for index,(cid,payload) in enumerate(executor.map(fetch,card_ids),1):
        cards[cid] = payload
        if index % 250 == 0 or index == len(card_ids):
            print(f"fetched={index}", flush=True)

resolved = []
for cid,variant_id,legacy in targets:
    card = cards.get(cid)
    if not card:
        continue
    detailed = [
        item for item in (card.get("variants_detailed") or [])
        if not item.get("languages") or "de" in [str(x).lower() for x in item.get("languages") or []]
    ]
    selected = next(
        (item for item in detailed if str(item.get("variantId") or "") == variant_id),
        None,
    )
    if not selected:
        continue

    own_cm = ((selected.get("pricing") or {}).get("cardmarket") or {})
    own_price = cardmarket_price(own_cm, selected)
    if own_price is not None:
        resolved.append((cid,variant_id,legacy,own_price,"TCGDEX"))
        continue

    top_cm = ((card.get("pricing") or {}).get("cardmarket") or {})
    top_product = top_cm.get("idProduct")
    variant_product = (selected.get("thirdParty") or {}).get("cardmarket")
    if top_product is None or variant_product is None:
        continue
    if str(top_product) != str(variant_product):
        continue

    price = cardmarket_price(top_cm, selected)
    if price is not None:
        resolved.append((cid,variant_id,legacy,price,"TCGDEX_PRODUCT"))

print(f"resolved={len(resolved)}", flush=True)

sql = ["BEGIN;"]
for cid,variant_id,legacy,price,source in resolved:
    sql.append(f"""
UPDATE tcg_price_cache
SET source='{source}',
    base_eur={price},
    updated_at=NOW(),
    resolution_status='PRICE_FOUND',
    failure_detail=NULL,
    next_attempt_at=NOW()+INTERVAL '7 days'
WHERE language_code='de'
  AND card_id='{q(cid)}'
  AND variant_id='{q(variant_id)}';
""")
    sql.append(f"""
INSERT INTO tcg_price_history
(card_id,language_code,variant_id,source,base_eur,captured_at)
VALUES ('{q(cid)}','de','{q(variant_id)}','{source}',{price},NOW());
""")
sql.append("COMMIT;")

if resolved:
    subprocess.run(
        ["docker","exec","-i","cardyssey-db","psql","-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey"],
        input="\n".join(sql), text=True, check=True, stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL
    )
print(f"applied={len(resolved)}", flush=True)


############################################################################################
# DATEI 060/107: scripts/resolve-cardmarket-cardtrader-bridge.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Resolve safe open prices via CardTrader identity -> official Cardmarket price guide."""
import json, math, re, subprocess, requests

PRICE_URL="https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/priceGuide/price_guide_6.json"
UA={"User-Agent":"Cardyssey/0.4 cardmarket-cardtrader-bridge"}

def db(sql):
    return subprocess.run(
        ["docker","exec","cardyssey-db","psql","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-At","-F","\t","-c",sql],
        check=True,capture_output=True,text=True
    ).stdout.strip()

def q(v): return str(v).replace("'","''")
def positive(v): return isinstance(v,(int,float)) and math.isfinite(v) and v>0

def guide_price(g,legacy,allow_generic_holo=False,allow_holo_for_exact_normal=False):
    generic=("trend","avg7","avg30","avg","low")
    holo=("trend-holo","avg7-holo","avg30-holo","avg-holo","low-holo")
    keys=holo if legacy in ("HOLO","REVERSE") else generic
    for key in keys:
        if positive(g.get(key)):
            return float(g[key]),key
    # Some exact products are inherently holo, so Cardmarket stores only one
    # of the two price series. Use the opposite series only for identities we
    # already resolved to an exact promo/product blueprint.
    if legacy in ("HOLO","REVERSE") and allow_generic_holo:
        for key in generic:
            if positive(g.get(key)):
                return float(g[key]),key
    if legacy=="NORMAL" and allow_holo_for_exact_normal:
        for key in holo:
            if positive(g.get(key)):
                return float(g[key]),key
    return None,None

raw=db("""SELECT p.card_id,p.language_code,p.variant_id,p.legacy_variant,
       COALESCE(vi.cardmarket_product_id,i.cardmarket_product_id),
       v.value::text,
       CASE WHEN vi.card_id IS NOT NULL THEN 'variant' ELSE 'card' END,
       COALESCE(vi.method,'')
FROM tcg_price_cache p
JOIN catalog_cards_mirror c
  ON c.card_id=p.card_id AND c.language_code=p.language_code
LEFT JOIN cardtrader_variant_identity_cache vi
  ON vi.card_id=p.card_id
 AND vi.language_code=p.language_code
 AND vi.variant_id=p.variant_id
LEFT JOIN cardtrader_identity_cache i
  ON i.card_id=p.card_id AND i.language_code=p.language_code
CROSS JOIN LATERAL jsonb_array_elements(c.variants) v(value)
WHERE p.language_code IN ('de','en','ja','zh-cn','zh-tw')
  AND (p.resolution_status='NO_PRICE' OR p.source='CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL_CT_IDENTITY')
  AND COALESCE(vi.cardmarket_product_id,i.cardmarket_product_id) IS NOT NULL
  AND NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM price_source_routing rt
    WHERE rt.language_code=p.language_code AND rt.card_id=p.card_id
      AND rt.variant_id=p.variant_id
      AND rt.verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'
      AND rt.primary_provider<>'CARDMARKET'
  )

  AND v.value->>'id'=p.variant_id
ORDER BY p.language_code,p.card_id,p.variant_id""")

rows=[]
for line in raw.splitlines():
    z=line.split("\t",7)
    if len(z)!=8: continue
    cid,lang,vid,legacy,pid,vjson,scope,variant_method=z
    try:v=json.loads(vjson)
    except Exception: continue
    # A card-level identity is safe only for the plain physical printing.
    # Variant-level identities may intentionally describe stamps/subtypes/foils.
    if scope=="card" and (v.get("foil") or v.get("stamps") or v.get("subtype")):
        continue
    # Cardmarket Japanese products do not distinguish CardTrader's editable
    # 1st-edition/unlimited property. Never bridge those edition identities.
    if variant_method in ("ja-classic-1st","ja-classic-unlimited"):
        continue
    if str(v.get("size") or "standard").lower()!="standard": continue
    rows.append((cid,lang,vid,legacy,pid,variant_method))

response=requests.get(PRICE_URL,headers=UA,timeout=45)
response.raise_for_status()
payload=response.json()
prices={str(x.get("idProduct")):x for x in payload.get("priceGuides") or []}

updates=[]
for cid,lang,vid,legacy,pid,variant_method in rows:
    guide=prices.get(str(pid))
    if not guide: continue
    value,key=guide_price(
        guide,
        legacy,
        allow_generic_holo=(
            lang in ("ja","zh-cn")
            or variant_method in ("ja-promo","western-special")
        ),
        allow_holo_for_exact_normal=variant_method in ("ja-promo","western-special"),
    )
    if value is None: continue
    updates.append((cid,lang,vid,pid,value,key))

print(
    f"cardmarket_ct_bridge_created={payload.get('createdAt')} "
    f"eligible={len(rows)} updates={len(updates)}",
    flush=True
)
for x in updates[:30]:
    print("bridge",*x,sep="\t",flush=True)

if updates:
    sql=["BEGIN;"]
    for cid,lang,vid,pid,value,key in updates:
        detail=f"CardTrader exact identity -> Cardmarket product {pid}; key {key}"
        sql.append(
            f"UPDATE tcg_price_cache SET source='CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL_CT_IDENTITY',"
            f"base_eur={value:.4f},updated_at=NOW(),resolution_status='PRICE_FOUND',"
            f"failure_detail='{q(detail)}',next_attempt_at=NOW()+INTERVAL '7 days' "
            f"WHERE card_id='{q(cid)}' AND language_code='{q(lang)}' "
            f"AND variant_id='{q(vid)}';"
        )
        sql.append(
            f"INSERT INTO tcg_price_history(card_id,language_code,variant_id,source,base_eur,captured_at) "
            f"VALUES('{q(cid)}','{q(lang)}','{q(vid)}','CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL_CT_IDENTITY',{value:.4f},NOW());"
        )
    sql.append("COMMIT;")
    subprocess.run(
        ["docker","exec","-i","cardyssey-db","psql","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1","-q"],
        input="\n".join(sql),text=True,check=True
    )
    print(f"cardmarket_ct_bridge_applied={len(updates)}",flush=True)

# Repair only obvious single-listing CardTrader outliers when the exact
# Cardmarket identity has a much lower official guide value.
outlier_raw=db("""SELECT p.card_id,p.language_code,p.variant_id,p.legacy_variant,
       p.base_eur,p.failure_detail,i.cardmarket_product_id
FROM tcg_price_cache p
JOIN cardtrader_identity_cache i
  ON i.card_id=p.card_id AND i.language_code=p.language_code
WHERE p.language_code IN ('de','en','ja','zh-cn','zh-tw')
  AND p.source='CARDTRADER_MARKETPLACE'
  AND p.base_eur > 500
  AND i.cardmarket_product_id IS NOT NULL""")

repairs=[]
for line in outlier_raw.splitlines():
    z=line.split("\t",6)
    if len(z)!=7: continue
    cid,lang,vid,legacy,current,detail,pid=z
    match=re.search(r"listings[= ](\d+)",detail or "")
    listing_count=int(match.group(1)) if match else 0
    if listing_count!=1: continue
    guide=prices.get(str(pid))
    if not guide: continue
    value,key=guide_price(guide,legacy)
    if value is None: continue
    current=float(current)
    if current > 500 and current >= value*20:
        repairs.append((cid,lang,vid,pid,current,value,key))

print(f"cardmarket_ct_outlier_repairs={len(repairs)}",flush=True)
if repairs:
    sql=["BEGIN;"]
    for cid,lang,vid,pid,current,value,key in repairs:
        detail=(
            f"CardTrader single-listing outlier {current:.4f} EUR replaced by "
            f"Cardmarket product {pid}; key {key}"
        )
        sql.append(
            f"UPDATE tcg_price_cache SET source='CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL_CT_IDENTITY',"
            f"base_eur={value:.4f},updated_at=NOW(),resolution_status='PRICE_FOUND',"
            f"failure_detail='{q(detail)}',next_attempt_at=NOW()+INTERVAL '7 days' "
            f"WHERE card_id='{q(cid)}' AND language_code='{q(lang)}' "
            f"AND variant_id='{q(vid)}';"
        )
        sql.append(
            f"INSERT INTO tcg_price_history(card_id,language_code,variant_id,source,base_eur,captured_at) "
            f"VALUES('{q(cid)}','{q(lang)}','{q(vid)}','CARDMARKET_OFFICIAL_CT_IDENTITY',{value:.4f},NOW());"
        )
    sql.append("COMMIT;")
    subprocess.run(
        ["docker","exec","-i","cardyssey-db","psql","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1","-q"],
        input="\n".join(sql),text=True,check=True
    )
    print(f"cardmarket_ct_outlier_applied={len(repairs)}",flush=True)


############################################################################################
# DATEI 061/107: scripts/run-cardmarket-identity-weekly.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Refresh public Cardmarket catalog and Cardyssey review worklist weekly.

Does not modify published prices, route mappings, or human confirmations.
Price guide weekly refresh remains a separate, existing job.
"""
import argparse
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from price_feed_client import fetch_json

ROOT=Path("/home/tv/cardshop")
BASE=Path("/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector")
PRODUCT_URL="https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json"
PRICE_URL="https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/priceGuide/price_guide_6.json"
def run(*args):
    p=subprocess.run([sys.executable,*map(str,args)],cwd=ROOT,capture_output=True,text=True)
    if p.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError(str(args[:2])+": "+p.stderr[-1000:]+p.stdout[-300:])
    return p.stdout.strip()
def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--apply",action="store_true")
    a=p.parse_args()
    products=fetch_json(PRODUCT_URL,max_age=20*3600)
    prices=fetch_json(PRICE_URL,max_age=20*3600)
    if len(products.get("products") or [])<50000 or len(prices.get("priceGuides") or [])<50000:
        raise RuntimeError("Official feed incomplete; previous queues and reviews preserved")
    # Recompute the expansion crosswalk from multiple distinct physical
    # card-product IDs BEFORE queue generation. Never trust name overlaps.
    crosswalk=json.loads(run(ROOT/'scripts/map-all-de-cardmarket-catalog.py',
        *(['--apply'] if a.apply else [])))
    set_audit=json.loads(run(ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-set-identity-audit.py'))
    report={"set_crosswalk":crosswalk,"set_audit":set_audit,"product_catalog_date":products.get("createdAt"),"guide_date":prices.get("createdAt"),
            "products":len(products["products"]),"guide_items":len(prices["priceGuides"])}
    for lang in ("de","en"):
        result=run(ROOT/"scripts/export-cardmarket-full-queue.py","--language",lang,"--output",BASE/f"queue-{lang}.csv")
        report[lang]=json.loads(result)
        # Critical: exporter emits needs_price=YES as a placeholder. Never
        # feed that unprioritized raw CSV to the browser scanner again.
        report[lang]['prioritization']=json.loads(run(
            ROOT/'scripts/prioritize-cardmarket-scan-queue.py',
            '--language',lang,'--queue',BASE/f'queue-{lang}.csv',
            '--output',BASE/f'queue-{lang}.csv'))
    result=run(ROOT/"scripts/build-cardmarket-identity-worklist.py")
    report["worklist"]=json.loads(result)
    # Set-verified catalog singleton hints reduce costly translated-name
    # searches. These are CANDIDATES only, never print-verified prices.
    for lang in ('de','en'):
        report[lang]['lookup_hints']=json.loads(run(
            ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-enrich-queue-hints.py','--language',lang))
    report["review_stage"]=json.loads(run(ROOT/"scripts/sync-cardmarket-print-review.py",*(["--apply"] if a.apply else [])))
    # Existing OPEN rows need the exact same per-variant corrections; the
    # review importer only stages complex cases and otherwise leaves old
    # multi-product candidates visible forever.
    report['variant_candidate_reconciliation']=json.loads(run(
        ROOT/'scripts/reconcile-cardmarket-variant-identity-review.py',
        *(['--apply'] if a.apply else [])))
    # The generic exporter intentionally does not promote ambiguous print
    # product IDs. Restore all *independently verified* set-local candidate
    # lists after regeneration; never change a human-approved print or price.
    overrides=json.loads((ROOT/'config/cardmarket-verified-set-overrides.json').read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
    report['verified_expansion_repairs']={}
    for set_id in sorted(overrides):
        report['verified_expansion_repairs'][set_id]=json.loads(run(
            ROOT/'scripts/repair-cardmarket-verified-expansions.py','--set',set_id,
            *(['--apply'] if a.apply else [])))
    # Durable exact-card URL leads supplied by the collector owner. Import
    # AFTER regenerating/merging queue columns; do not promote print prices.
    for lang in ('de','en'):
        report[lang]['known_page_hints']=json.loads(run(
            ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-apply-print-page-hints.py','--language',lang))
    # Compact read-only artifacts for safe browser scoring and guide
    # diagnostics; neither may assign prints or write price caches.
    if a.apply:
        report['observed_set_guards']=json.loads(run(
            ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-build-observed-set-guards.py',
            '--output',BASE/'observed-set-url-guards.json'))
        report['product_guide_diagnostics']=json.loads(run(
            ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-build-guide-diagnostics.py',
            '--output',BASE/'cardmarket-guide-diagnostic.json'))
    if a.apply:
        report['verified_print_mapping_cache']=json.loads(run(
            ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-sync-print-mapping-cache.py','--apply'))
        report['metacard_candidate_index']=json.loads(run(
            ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-build-metacard-family-index.py',
            '--output',BASE/'cardmarket-metacard-family-index.json'))
        # The main queue can be regenerated after a catalog refresh; put
        # exact-print review first again without modifying card/price data.
        report['recovery_priority']=json.loads(run(
            ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-prioritize-recovery-queue.py','--apply'))
    report["mode"]="apply" if a.apply else "dry-run"
    (ROOT/"reports/cardmarket-identity-pipeline-latest.json").write_text(
        json.dumps(report,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps({"mode":report["mode"],"de":report["de"]["variants"],
      "en":report["en"]["variants"],"stage":report["review_stage"]["rows"],
      "catalog_date":report["product_catalog_date"]},ensure_ascii=False))
if __name__=="__main__":
    main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 062/107: scripts/stage-cardmarket-print-review.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Stage verified-set but ambiguous Cardmarket matches as a focused admin review batch."""
import argparse,json,subprocess
from pathlib import Path

def main():
 p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
 p.add_argument("--triage",type=Path,required=True)
 p.add_argument("--apply",action="store_true")
 a=p.parse_args()
 rows=[x for x in json.loads(a.triage.read_text())["cases"] if x["next_action"]=="VERIFY_CARDMARKET_PRINT_IDENTITY"]
 payload=json.dumps(rows,ensure_ascii=False)
 sql="""WITH selected AS (SELECT jsonb_array_elements('"""+payload.replace("'","''")+"""'::jsonb) x)
 UPDATE price_review_cases r SET
   search_status='CARDMARKET_PRINT_REVIEW',
   candidates=COALESCE((SELECT jsonb_agg(v) FROM jsonb_array_elements(r.candidates) v
      WHERE v->>'match_type'<>'CARDMARKET_PRINT_REVIEW'),'[]'::jsonb)
    ||jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('match_type','CARDMARKET_PRINT_REVIEW',
      'status','MANUAL_PRINT_IDENTITY_REQUIRED','provider','CARDMARKET',
      'instruction','Set match exists; confirm exact language, number, edition, foil and stamp before assigning product ID.'))
 FROM selected
 WHERE r.language_code=selected.x->>'language'
 AND r.card_id=selected.x->>'card_id' AND r.variant_id=selected.x->>'variant_id'
 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM tcg_price_cache c WHERE c.language_code=r.language_code
  AND c.card_id=r.card_id AND c.variant_id=r.variant_id AND c.resolution_status<>'PRICE_FOUND')
 RETURNING r.language_code,r.card_id,r.variant_id;"""
 if a.apply:
  cmd=["docker","exec","cardyssey-db","psql","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-At","-F","|","-c",sql]
  results=subprocess.run(cmd,check=True,capture_output=True,text=True).stdout
  print("staged",len([r for r in results.splitlines() if r.count("|")==2]))
 else:print("preview",len(rows))
if __name__=="__main__":main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 063/107: scripts/sync-cardmarket-print-review.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Sync unresolved official Cardmarket product candidates to admin review safely.

Selected decisions and notes are preserved. Never writes tcg_price_cache.
"""
import argparse
import csv
import json
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path("/home/tv/cardshop")
REPORT=ROOT/"reports/cardmarket-print-review-all.csv"
def sql_quote(text):
    return str(text).replace("'","''")
def ingest(path,apply=False):
    stats={"rows":0,"with_set_ids":0,"no_set_ids":0}
    chunks=[]
    batch=[]
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8",newline="") as f:
        for x in csv.DictReader(f):
            ids=[y for y in x["candidate_ids"].split(",") if y.isdigit()]
            matched=[y for y in x["matched_set_ids"].split(",") if y in ids]
            item=dict(language=x["language"],card_id=x["card_id"],variant_id=x["variant_id"],
              set_id=x["set_id"],name=x["name"],number=x["number"],type=x["type"],
              foil=x["foil"],subtype=x["subtype"],stamps=x["stamps"],
              candidate_ids=ids,matched_set_ids=matched,expansion=x["expansion_id"],
              classification=x["classification"],product_names=x["product_names"])
            batch.append(item)
            stats["rows"]+=1
            stats["with_set_ids" if matched else "no_set_ids"]+=1
            if len(batch)>=500:
                chunks.append(batch);batch=[]
    if batch:chunks.append(batch)
    if not apply:return stats
    sql=["BEGIN;"]
    for group in chunks:
        payload=sql_quote(json.dumps(group,ensure_ascii=False,separators=(",",":")))
        sql.append("""WITH incoming AS (
 SELECT x->>'language' AS language_code,x->>'card_id' AS card_id,
   x->>'variant_id' AS variant_id,x->>'set_id' AS set_id,
   x->>'name' AS card_name,x->>'number' AS printed_number,
   x->>'type' AS print_type,x->>'foil' AS foil,x->>'subtype' AS subtype,
   x->>'stamps' AS stamps,x->'candidate_ids' AS candidate_ids,
   x->'matched_set_ids' AS matched_set_ids,x->>'expansion' AS provider_expansion_id,
   x->>'classification' AS classification,x->>'product_names' AS product_names
 FROM jsonb_array_elements('"""+payload+"""'::jsonb) x)
INSERT INTO cardmarket_print_identity_review
(language_code,card_id,variant_id,set_id,card_name,printed_number,print_type,
 foil,subtype,stamps,candidate_ids,matched_set_ids,provider_expansion_id,
 classification,product_names)
SELECT language_code,card_id,variant_id,set_id,card_name,printed_number,print_type,
 foil,subtype,stamps,candidate_ids,matched_set_ids,provider_expansion_id,
 classification,product_names FROM incoming
ON CONFLICT(language_code,card_id,variant_id) DO UPDATE SET
 set_id=EXCLUDED.set_id,card_name=EXCLUDED.card_name,
 printed_number=EXCLUDED.printed_number,print_type=EXCLUDED.print_type,
 foil=EXCLUDED.foil,subtype=EXCLUDED.subtype,stamps=EXCLUDED.stamps,
 candidate_ids=EXCLUDED.candidate_ids,matched_set_ids=EXCLUDED.matched_set_ids,
 provider_expansion_id=EXCLUDED.provider_expansion_id,
 classification=EXCLUDED.classification,product_names=EXCLUDED.product_names,
 checked_at=NOW()
WHERE cardmarket_print_identity_review.status='PENDING_REVIEW';
""")
    sql.append("COMMIT;")
    p=subprocess.run(["docker","exec","-i","cardyssey-db","psql","-X","-q",
       "-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1","-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey"],input="\n".join(sql),
       capture_output=True,text=True)
    if p.returncode:
        raise RuntimeError("DB import failed: "+p.stderr[-1200:])
    return stats
if __name__=="__main__":
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--apply",action="store_true")
    p.add_argument("--input",type=Path,default=REPORT)
    a=p.parse_args()
    print(json.dumps({**ingest(a.input,a.apply),"applied":a.apply},ensure_ascii=False))

# DATEI 064/107: scripts/test-cardmarket-search-score.ps1
############################################################################################

$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
. (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'cardmarket-search-score.ps1')
. (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'cardmarket-filter-url.ps1')
function Check([bool]$value,[string]$why) {if(!$value){throw ('TEST_FAIL: '+$why)}}
$felori=@(
 @{url='https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Scarlet-Violet/Sprigatito-V1-SVI013';text='Sprigatito [Scratch | Leafage]'},
 @{url='https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Scarlet-Violet/Sprigatito-V2-SVI013';text='Sprigatito [Scratch | Leafage]'},
 @{url='https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Gem-Pack-Vol-1/Sprigatito-V1-CBB1C01';text='Sprigatito'},
 @{url='https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Scarlet-Violet/Sprigatito-V3-SVI113';text='Sprigatito'}
)
$a=Resolve-CardmarketSearchCandidates -Links $felori -PrintedNumber '013' -SetSlug 'Scarlet-Violet' -PrintType Reverse -Stamp 'grass-symbol'
Check ($a.candidateCount -eq 2 -and $null -eq $a.uniquePageCandidate) 'Felori V1 and V2 must be ambiguous'
Check ($a.reason -eq 'MULTIPLE_SAME_SET_NUMBER_CANDIDATES') 'Felori state'
Check (!$a.exactPrintVerified -and !$a.priceVerified) 'no print or price inferred'
$b=Resolve-CardmarketSearchCandidates -Links $felori -PrintedNumber '113' -SetSlug 'Scarlet-Violet'
Check ($b.candidateCount -eq 1 -and $b.uniquePageCandidate.productSlug -match 'SVI113$') '13 may not match 113'
$c=Resolve-CardmarketSearchCandidates -Links $felori -PrintedNumber '013'
Check ($c.candidateCount -eq 2 -and $null -eq $c.uniquePageCandidate -and $c.reason -eq 'SET_NOT_INDEPENDENTLY_VERIFIED') 'unknown set requires independent guard'
$joltik=@(
 @{url='https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/White-Flare-Additionals/Joltik-V1-xWHT033';text='Joltik [Surprise Attack]'},
 @{url='https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/White-Flare-Additionals/Joltik-V2-xWHT033';text='Joltik [Surprise Attack]'},
 @{url='https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/White-Flare/Joltik-V1-WHT033';text='Joltik [Surprise Attack]'}
)
$d=Resolve-CardmarketSearchCandidates -Links $joltik -PrintedNumber '033' -SetSlug 'White-Flare-Additionals' -Foil 'Pokéball'
Check ($d.candidateCount -eq 2 -and !$d.uniquePageCandidate) 'Pokeball keyword missing: do not guess V1/V2'
$e=Resolve-CardmarketSearchCandidates -Links @(@{url='https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Wizards-Black-Star-Promos/Pikachu-WP1';text='Pikachu Promo'}) -PrintedNumber '1' -SetSlug 'Wizards-Black-Star-Promos' -Stamp 'grauer stern'
Check ($e.candidateCount -eq 1 -and !$e.exactPrintVerified) 'Promo title does not confirm stamp'
$f=Resolve-CardmarketSearchCandidates -Links @(@{url='https://evil.example/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Base-Set/Machamp-V1-BS8';text='Machamp 8'}) -PrintedNumber '8' -SetSlug 'Base-Set'
Check ($f.candidateCount -eq 0) 'reject fake Cardmarket domain'
$u=New-CardmarketOfferFilterUrl -ProductUrl 'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Scarlet-Violet/Sprigatito-V1-SVI013?language=1' -LanguageId 3 -ReverseCode Y -FirstEditionCode N
Check ($u -eq 'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Scarlet-Violet/Sprigatito-V1-SVI013?language=3&minCondition=2&isSigned=N&isAltered=N&isReverseHolo=Y&isFirstEd=N') 'canonical filter URL parameters'
$failed=$false
try{$null=New-CardmarketOfferFilterUrl -ProductUrl 'https://evil.example/Pokemon/Products' -LanguageId 3}catch{$failed=$true}
Check $failed 'reject hostile domain'
$failed=$false
try{$null=New-CardmarketOfferFilterUrl -ProductUrl 'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Scarlet-Violet/Sprigatito-V1-SVI013' -LanguageId 1}catch{$failed=$true}
Check $failed 'reject mismatched language'
Write-Output 'CARDMARKET_SCORE_FILTER_TESTS_OK (9 checks)'

# DATEI 065/107: scripts/test_audit_all_de_cardmarket_prices.py
############################################################################################

import importlib.util
import sys
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("audit_all_cm",Path(__file__).with_name("audit-all-de-cardmarket-prices.py"))
module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(module)

class AuditAllGermanPrices(unittest.TestCase):
    def base(self,**kwargs):
        row=dict(card_id="a",variant_id="one",product_ids=["77"],variant={"type":"Normal"},
                 variant_count=1,legacy_variant="NORMAL",source="TCGDEX",price=1)
        row.update(kwargs)
        return row
    def test_one_product_with_guide_staged_not_promoted(self):
        result=module.calculate([self.base()],{"77":{"trend":1.5}})
        self.assertEqual(result[0]["status"],"GUIDE_CANDIDATE_NOT_LANGUAGE_NM_VERIFIED")
        self.assertEqual(result[0]["guide_value_eur"],1.5)
        self.assertEqual(result[0]["prior_source"],"TCGDEX")
    def test_multicandidate_never_invent_price(self):
        item=module.calculate([self.base(product_ids=["77","88"])],
                              {"77":{"trend":1},"88":{"trend":2}})[0]
        self.assertEqual(item["status"],"MULTIPLE_PRODUCT_CANDIDATES")
        self.assertIsNone(item["guide_value_eur"])
    def test_special_print_not_same_as_regular(self):
        item=module.calculate([self.base(variant={"type":"Normal","stamps":["staff"]})],
                              {"77":{"trend":10}})[0]
        self.assertEqual(item["status"],"SPECIAL_PRINT_IDENTITY_UNVERIFIED")
    def test_reverse_holo_not_assumed_separate(self):
        item=module.calculate([self.base(variant={"type":"Reverse"},variant_count=3)],
                              {"77":{"trend-holo":5}})[0]
        self.assertEqual(item["status"],"HOLO_REVERSE_SPLIT_UNVERIFIED")
    def test_no_priceguide_does_not_override(self):
        item=module.calculate([self.base()],{})[0]
        self.assertEqual(item["status"],"PRODUCT_HAS_NO_GUIDE")
        self.assertIsNone(item["guide_value_eur"])

if __name__=="__main__":unittest.main()

# DATEI 066/107: scripts/test_audit_cardmarket_catalog_crossrefs.py
############################################################################################

import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
import unittest

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("cm_crossrefs", Path(__file__).with_name("audit-cardmarket-catalog-crossrefs.py"))
module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(module)

class CrossRefs(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_same_metacard_different_set_not_approved(self):
        cases = [{"language": "de", "card_id": "x", "variant_id": "v", "exact_variant_provider_ids": {"cardmarket": 2}, "products": [{"id": 1}]}]
        products = [{"idProduct": 1, "idExpansion": 10, "idMetacard": 5}, {"idProduct": 2, "idExpansion": 20, "idMetacard": 5}]
        results, summary = module.crosscheck(cases, products)
        self.assertEqual(results[0]["crossref_status"], "SAME_METACARD_DIFFERENT_PRODUCT_OR_EXPANSION")
        self.assertFalse(results[0]["safe_to_assign_product_id"])
        self.assertEqual(summary["automatically_assigned"], 0)

    def test_same_product_still_requires_print_review(self):
        cases = [{"exact_variant_provider_ids": {"cardmarket": 2}, "products": [{"id": 2}]}]
        result, _ = module.crosscheck(cases, [{"idProduct": 2, "idExpansion": 20}])
        self.assertEqual(result[0]["crossref_status"], "DIRECT_ID_IN_SET_REVIEW_PRINT")
        self.assertFalse(result[0]["safe_to_assign_product_id"])

    def test_missing_direct_id(self):
        result, _ = module.crosscheck([{"products": []}], [])
        self.assertEqual(result[0]["crossref_status"], "NO_DIRECT_VARIANT_ID")

    def test_direct_not_in_catalog(self):
        result, _ = module.crosscheck([{"exact_variant_provider_ids": {"cardmarket": 12345}}], [])
        self.assertEqual(result[0]["crossref_status"], "DIRECT_ID_NOT_IN_CURRENT_CATALOG")

if __name__ == "__main__": unittest.main()

# DATEI 067/107: scripts/test_audit_cardmarket_feed_first.py
############################################################################################

import unittest
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('cm_feed_first',Path(__file__).with_name('audit-cardmarket-feed-first.py'))
mod=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
classify,audit=mod.classify,mod.audit

class FeedFirstTests(unittest.TestCase):
    row={'cardyssey_karte':'x','variante_id':'v','cardmarket_ids':'123','routing_state':'VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID','drucktyp':'Normal','sonderdruck':'','foil':'','stempel':'[]'}
    guide={'123':{'avg30':1.25,'avg30-holo':2.5}}
    def test_simple_verified_preview(self):
        status,result=classify(self.row,self.guide)
        self.assertEqual(status,'VERIFIED_PRODUCT_GUIDE_PREVIEW')
        self.assertEqual(result['aggregate_eur'],1.25)
    def test_holo_bucket(self):
        status,result=classify({**self.row,'drucktyp':'Reverse'},self.guide)
        self.assertEqual(result['guide_bucket'],'avg30-holo')
    def test_holo_is_not_automatically_reverse_holo(self):
        status,result=classify({**self.row,'drucktyp':'Holo'},self.guide)
        self.assertEqual(status,'VERIFIED_PRODUCT_GUIDE_PREVIEW')
        self.assertEqual(result['guide_bucket'],'avg30')
    def test_candidate_not_verified(self):
        self.assertEqual(classify({**self.row,'routing_state':'SOURCE_ONLY'},self.guide)[0],'SINGLE_ID_NEEDS_IDENTITY_VERIFICATION')
    def test_special_never_auto(self):
        self.assertEqual(classify({**self.row,'stempel':'["staff"]'},self.guide)[0],'SPECIAL_PRINT_NEEDS_REVIEW')
    def test_multiple_not_assumed(self):
        self.assertEqual(classify({**self.row,'cardmarket_ids':'123,456'},self.guide)[0],'MULTIPLE_PRODUCT_IDS')
    def test_missing_id(self):
        self.assertEqual(classify({**self.row,'cardmarket_ids':''},self.guide)[0],'NO_PRODUCT_ID')
    def test_aggregate_counts(self):
        stats,quotes=audit([self.row,{**self.row,'cardmarket_ids':''}],self.guide)
        self.assertEqual(stats['NO_PRODUCT_ID'],1)
        self.assertEqual(len(quotes),1)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 068/107: scripts/test_audit_cardmarket_variant_evidence.py
############################################################################################

import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
import unittest

spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("cm_audit", Path(__file__).with_name("audit-cardmarket-variant-evidence.py"))
module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(module)


class AuditTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def row(self, **overrides):
        d = {"language": "de", "card_id": "x", "variant_id": "holo", "set_verified": True,
             "products": [{"id": 111}], "exact_variant_provider_ids": {"cardmarket": 111}}
        d.update(overrides)
        return d

    def test_matching_id_is_not_autoverified(self):
        rows, s = module.audit([self.row()])
        self.assertEqual(rows[0]["status"], "DIRECT_ID_SET_MATCH_REVIEW")
        self.assertEqual(s["fully_verified_prints"], 0)

    def test_conflict(self):
        self.assertEqual(module.classify(self.row(exact_variant_provider_ids={"cardmarket": 999})), "DIRECT_ID_CONFLICT_REVIEW")

    def test_name_hint_is_not_same_set(self):
        row = self.row(set_verified=False, products=[], exact_variant_provider_ids={}, name_only_search_hints=[{"id": 111}])
        self.assertEqual(module.classify(row), "NAME_ONLY_HINT_NOT_VERIFIED")

    def test_duplicate_key(self):
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):
            module.audit([self.row(), self.row()])


if __name__ == "__main__": unittest.main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 069/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_architecture_upgrade.py
############################################################################################

"""Regression checks for safe scoring, observed-set guards and guide-only data."""
import csv,hashlib,importlib.util,json,tempfile,unittest
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

def load(filename):
    spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(filename.replace('-','_'),ROOT/'scripts'/filename)
    module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(module);return module
guards=load('cardmarket-build-observed-set-guards.py')
guide=load('cardmarket-build-guide-diagnostics.py')

class CardmarketUpgrade(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_observed_set_path_requires_five_distinct_cards_and_no_conflicts(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
            b=Path(t)
            rows=[]
            events=[]
            for n in range(6):
                card=f'sv09-{n+1:03d}';pid=str(800000+n)
                rows.append({'cardyssey_karte':card,'variante_id':'normal',
                             'set_id':'sv09','catalog_singleton_id':pid,
                             'catalog_expansion_id':'6001'})
                events.append({'card_id':card,'variant_id':'normal','product_id':pid,
                               'catalog_direct_id_verified':True,'card_number_url_verified':True,
                               'url':f'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Journey-Together/Test-V1-SVI{n+1:03d}'})
            with (b/'queue-de.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
                writer=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=rows[0]);writer.writeheader();writer.writerows(rows)
            with (b/'browser-observations-de.jsonl').open('w') as f:
                for x in events:f.write(json.dumps(x)+'\n')
            result=guards.derive(base=b)
            self.assertEqual(result['setSlugs']['sv09']['distinctLiveCards'],6)
            self.assertEqual(result['setSlugs']['sv09']['setSlug'],'Journey-Together')
            events[-1]['url']=events[-1]['url'].replace('Journey-Together','Different-Expansion')
            with (b/'browser-observations-de.jsonl').open('w') as f:
                for x in events:f.write(json.dumps(x)+'\n')
            result=guards.derive(base=b)
            self.assertNotIn('sv09',result['setSlugs'])
            self.assertIn('sv09',result['ambiguousExcluded'])
    def test_price_guide_is_only_diagnostic_not_eligible_for_print_price(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
            b=Path(t);cache=b/'cache';cache.mkdir()
            for lang in ('de','en'):
                with (b/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
                    w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=['cardmarket_ids','catalog_singleton_id'])
                    w.writeheader();w.writerow({'cardmarket_ids':'836551,836552','catalog_singleton_id':''})
            p=cache/(hashlib.sha256(guide.URL.encode()).hexdigest()+'.json')
            items=[{'idProduct':836551,'trend':7.58,'avg30':7.80,'trend-holo':7.58,'avg30-holo':7.80}]
            items += [{'idProduct':i+1000000,'trend':0} for i in range(50000)]
            p.write_text(json.dumps({'createdAt':'2026-11-09T02:50:26+0100','priceGuides':items}))
            result=guide.build(base=b,cache=cache)
            self.assertFalse(result['canPublishAsLanguagePrintPrice'])
            self.assertEqual(result['products']['836551']['avg30'],7.8)
            self.assertNotIn('language',result['products']['836551'])
            self.assertNotIn('836552',result['products'])
    def test_live_smoke_no_price_publication(self):
        p=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector/probe-score-filter-20261110/browser-observations-de.jsonl')
        if not p.is_file():self.skipTest('No local browser probe')
        seen={}
        with p.open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
            for line in f:
                x=json.loads(line);seen[x['card_id']]=x
        self.assertEqual(len(seen),3)
        self.assertEqual(seen['sve-015']['status'],'MULTIPLE_SEARCH_HITS')
        self.assertEqual(seen['sv09-128']['score_matching_status'],'UNIQUE_SET_NUMBER_PAGE_CANDIDATE')
        self.assertFalse(seen['sv09-128']['official_guide_product_aggregate']['can_publish_language_print_price'])
        self.assertEqual(seen['sv01-013']['filter_mode'],'FORM_SUBMISSION_DOM_VERIFIED')
        self.assertIsNone(seen['sv01-013'].get('median_all_offers_eur'))
    def test_fail_closed_wiring(self):
        source=(ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-language-browser-collector.ps1').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
        for marker in ('CARDMARKET_SCORE_MODULE_MISSING','CANONICAL_URL_DOM_VERIFIED',
                       'FORM_SUBMISSION_DOM_VERIFIED','PROVIDER_URL_FILTER_NOT_VERIFIED',
                       'SCORED_PAGE_NOT_IN_CATALOG_CANDIDATES','SET_NOT_INDEPENDENTLY_VERIFIED',
                       'OFFICIAL_CARDMARKET_PRODUCT_GUIDE_NOT_VARIANT_PRICE',
                       'can_publish_language_print_price=$false',
                       'DIRECT_ID_LOOKUP_TIMEOUT_OR_UNAVAILABLE'):
            self.assertIn(marker,source)
        ui=(ROOT/'web/app/admin/prices/page.tsx').read_text()
        self.assertIn('kein DE/EN-Variantenpreis',ui)
        self.assertIn('Kandidat {i+1} ansehen',ui)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 070/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_card_vs_print_evidence.py
############################################################################################

"""Regression for Quappo base1-13 copyright-edition false set mismatch."""
import csv,re,unittest
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
SCANNER=(ROOT/'cardmarket-language-browser-collector.ps1').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
class ProductCardVsPrintEvidence(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_quappo_variant_has_card_candidates_not_fake_print_id(self):
  path=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector/queue-de.csv')
  with path.open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
   row=next(r for r in csv.DictReader(f) if r['cardyssey_karte']=='base1-13' and r['variante_id'].startswith('zqq5g2u9'))
  # This DE special print was subsequently *human-confirmed* as 273708.
  # Do not confuse this newer, explicit decision with catalog inference.
  if row.get('routing_state')=='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID':
   self.assertEqual(row['cardmarket_ids'],'273708')
  else:self.assertEqual(row['cardmarket_ids'],'')
  self.assertEqual(set(row['set_card_candidate_ids'].split(',')),{'273708','660215'})
 def test_print_uncertainty_never_confirms_identity_or_price(self):
  assert "'PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'" in SCANNER
  branch=SCANNER.split("$out.status = 'PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'",1)[1].split('} else {',1)[0]
  self.assertNotIn('identity_confirmed = $true',branch)
  self.assertNotIn('sample_median_eur',branch)
 def test_page_collector_number_matches_card_number(self):
  pattern=r'(?i)-[A-Z]{1,8}([0-9]{1,4})$'
  self.assertEqual(int(re.search(pattern,'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Base-Set/Poliwrath-V1-BS13').group(1)),13)
  self.assertNotEqual(int(re.search(pattern,'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Undaunted/Houndoom-V1-UD82').group(1)),5)
  self.assertIn('$out.card_number_url_verified=$numberEvidence',SCANNER)
 def test_invalid_set_is_never_accepted_as_print_candidate(self):
  self.assertIn('$setSlugGuards.ContainsKey([string]$case.set_id)',SCANNER)
  self.assertIn('$candidateId -in $cardCandidates',SCANNER)
  self.assertIn('$numberEvidence',SCANNER)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 071/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_catalog_direct_id_lookup.py
############################################################################################

"""Regression checks for evidence-first Cardmarket product lookup."""
import csv,unittest
from pathlib import Path
from importlib.util import spec_from_file_location,module_from_spec
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
s=(ROOT/'cardmarket-language-browser-collector.ps1').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
spec=spec_from_file_location('scan_failures',ROOT/'cardmarket-live-failures.py');m=module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(m)
class CatalogIdTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_queue_has_official_expansion_evidence_for_singleton(self):
  for lang in ('de','en'):
   p=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector')/f'queue-{lang}.csv'
   with p.open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
    r=csv.DictReader(f)
    self.assertIn('catalog_expansion_id',r.fieldnames)
    rows=[x for x in r if x['catalog_singleton_id']]
    self.assertGreater(len(rows),18000)
    self.assertTrue(all(x['catalog_expansion_id'].isdigit() for x in rows))
 def test_direct_result_requires_real_card_page_id_and_number(self):
  for marker in ('catalog_singleton_id','catalog_expansion_id',"$state.id -ne $directId",'sameNumber -and $sameSet','CARDMARKET_PROVIDER_CHALLENGE','NO_VERIFIED_REDIRECT_FALLBACK_TO_SEARCH'):
   self.assertIn(marker,s)
  self.assertIn('catalogIdLookupUnsupported=$true',s)
  self.assertIn('$landing.GetLeftPart([UriPartial]::Path)',s)
  self.assertIn("$out.catalog_direct_id_verified=$true",s)
 def test_direct_id_never_auto_proves_physical_print_or_price(self):
  branch=s.split("} elseif ($out.catalog_direct_id_verified -eq $true -and",1)[1].split('} elseif (',1)[0]
  self.assertIn('PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED',branch)
  self.assertNotIn('identity_confirmed = $true',branch)
  self.assertNotIn('median_all_offers_eur',branch)
  self.assertIn('exact_print_verified=$false',s)
  self.assertIn('if (-not $priceNeeded)',s)
  self.assertIn('expected_reverse_holo',s)
 def test_print_review_requires_both_catalog_and_live_evidence(self):
  row={'catalog_singleton_id':'275420','catalog_expansion_id':'1607'}
  observed={'product_id':'275420','catalog_direct_id_verified':True}
  standard={**row,'set_id':'basep','kartennummer':'1','variante_id':'v1','drucktyp':'normal'}
  self.assertEqual(m.classify('PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED',observed,standard),'PREISPRUEFUNG')
  special={**standard,'stempel':'[\"grauer stern\"]'}
  self.assertEqual(m.classify('PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED',observed,special),'DRUCKPRUEFUNG')
  self.assertEqual(m.classify('PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED',{**observed,'product_id':'999999'},row),'ZUORDNUNG')
  self.assertEqual(m.classify('PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED',{**observed,'catalog_direct_id_verified':False},row),'ZUORDNUNG')
  self.assertEqual(m.classify('PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED',observed,{**row,'catalog_expansion_id':''}),'ZUORDNUNG')
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

"""Live-feed grounded invariants for Felori SVI013 V1/V2 + special prints."""
import csv,hashlib,json,re,unittest
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
FEED_URL='https://downloads.s3.cardmarket.com/productCatalog/productList/products_singles_6.json'
FEED=Path('/home/tv/.local/state/cardyssey-queue/price-feeds')/(hashlib.sha256(FEED_URL.encode()).hexdigest()+'.json')
BASE=Path('/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector')
class FeloriVersionTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_v1_v2_belong_to_same_verified_physical_set_not_gem_pack(self):
  products={str(p['idProduct']):p for p in json.loads(FEED.read_text())['products']}
  overrides=json.loads((ROOT/'config/cardmarket-verified-set-overrides.json').read_text())
  self.assertEqual(overrides['sv01']['expansionId'],'5223')
  self.assertEqual({str(products[i]['idExpansion']) for i in ('689763','691000')},{'5223'})
  self.assertEqual(str(products['689763']['idMetacard']),str(products['691000']['idMetacard']))
  self.assertNotEqual(str(products['806289']['idExpansion']),'5223')
 def test_narrowly_allow_offer_filters_and_never_special_stamp_foil(self):
  spec=json.loads((ROOT/'config/cardmarket-known-print-page-hints.json').read_text())['sv01-013']
  self.assertEqual(spec['setSlug'],'Scarlet-Violet')
  for var in ('endfynwn4n10gzq','cm4kqul3x1bwlz1f'):
   x=spec['variants'][var]
   self.assertEqual(x['slug'],'Sprigatito-V1-SVI013')
   self.assertEqual(x['standardFilterProductId'],'689763')
   self.assertFalse(x['printVerified'])
  for var in ('curated:de:sv01:013:grass-stamp','curated:de:sv01:013:holo'):
   x=spec['variants'][var]
   self.assertFalse(x['printVerified'])
   self.assertNotIn('standardFilterProductId',x)
 def test_images_match_observed_product_ids(self):
  a=json.loads((ROOT/'config/cardmarket-verified-version-page-images.json').read_text())['sv01-013']
  for pid in ('689763','691000'):
   self.assertIn('/'+pid+'/'+pid+'.jpg',a[pid]['imageUrl'])
   self.assertTrue(a[pid]['imageUrl'].startswith('https://product-images.s3.cardmarket.com/51/SVI/'))
   self.assertIn(a[pid]['version'],('V1','V2'))
 def test_both_queues_keep_real_candidate_products_and_protect_special_prints(self):
  for lang in ('de','en'):
   with (BASE/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
    rows={r['variante_id']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f) if r['cardyssey_karte']=='sv01-013'}
   for var in ('endfynwn4n10gzq','cm4kqul3x1bwlz1f'):
    self.assertEqual(rows[var]['catalog_singleton_id'],'689763')
    self.assertEqual(rows[var]['reference_standard_filter_supported'],'YES')
    self.assertEqual(rows[var]['reference_print_verified'],'NO')
   self.assertIn('691000',rows['endfynwn4n10gzq']['cardmarket_ids'])
   if lang=='de':
    for var in ('curated:de:sv01:013:grass-stamp','curated:de:sv01:013:holo'):
     self.assertEqual(rows[var]['reference_standard_filter_supported'],'NO')
     self.assertNotEqual(rows[var]['routing_state'],'VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID')
 def test_weekly_refresh_persists_all_verified_set_expansions_before_page_hints(self):
  s=(ROOT/'scripts/run-cardmarket-identity-weekly.py').read_text()
  self.assertIn('verified_expansion_repairs',s)

 def test_review_shows_both_product_images_not_one_guessed_stamp(self):

  self.assertIn('Sonderstempel und Holo-Muster sind dadurch noch nicht automatisch bestätigt',s)

 def test_no_implicit_print_pricing_from_standard_product_hint(self):

import csv,importlib.util,json,tempfile,unittest
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sp=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('print_hints',ROOT/'cardmarket-apply-print-page-hints.py');h=importlib.util.module_from_spec(sp);sp.loader.exec_module(h)
class Tests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_known_urls_are_never_print_verified(self):
  data=json.loads((ROOT.parent/'config/cardmarket-known-print-page-hints.json').read_text())
  self.assertEqual(set(data),{'basep-1','basep-4','base3-1','sv01-013'})
  for card,rule in data.items():
   for variant,x in rule['variants'].items():
    self.assertFalse(x['printVerified']);self.assertTrue(x['slug'].endswith(rule['collectorSuffix']))
  self.assertEqual(data['basep-1']['variants']['zqq5g2u9n315kaa1q7ordzv42kgf7pxg']['slug'],'Pikachu-WP1')
  self.assertEqual(data['basep-4']['variants']['us0ioug2avlzsvzo3l1p89ztq94zgllrr']['slug'],'Pikachu-WP4')
  self.assertEqual(data['base3-1']['variants']['qi4hl156ztevhio39njr8t2eqgmqjdlals']['slug'],'Aerodactyl-V3-FO1')
  self.assertEqual(data['base3-1']['variants']['677yasikk9iggi3g0o8t']['slug'],'Aerodactyl-V1-FO1')
 def test_both_languages_and_no_conflation(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   root=Path(td);(root/'config').mkdir()
   (root/'config/cardmarket-known-print-page-hints.json').write_text((ROOT.parent/'config/cardmarket-known-print-page-hints.json').read_text())
   fields=['cardyssey_karte','variante_id','set_id','cardmarket_ids','routing_state']
   for lang in ('de','en'):
    with (root/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open('w',encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
     w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=fields);w.writeheader()
     for card,vals in [('base3-1',['113thq6t8j5zpn9aq2h12bi21oykxyl2','qi4hl156ztevhio39njr8t2eqgmqjdlals']),('basep-4',['3a83wf50ts0izkobrpvrjbdwt','us0ioug2avlzsvzo3l1p89ztq94zgllrr'])]:
      for var in vals:w.writerow({'cardyssey_karte':card,'variante_id':var,'set_id':card.rsplit('-',1)[0],'cardmarket_ids':'12345','routing_state':'SOURCE_ONLY'})
    result=h.apply(lang,base=root,root=root)
    self.assertEqual(result['knownPages'],4)
    with (root/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:rows=list(csv.DictReader(f))
    self.assertTrue(all(x['reference_print_verified']=='NO' and x['cardmarket_ids']=='12345' for x in rows))
    self.assertTrue(all('/'+lang+'/Pokemon/' in x['reference_product_url'] for x in rows))
    self.assertTrue('V1-FO1' in rows[0]['reference_product_url']);self.assertTrue('V3-FO1' in rows[1]['reference_product_url'])
 def test_felori_standard_vs_special_are_independent(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   root=Path(td);(root/'config').mkdir()
   (root/'config/cardmarket-known-print-page-hints.json').write_text((ROOT.parent/'config/cardmarket-known-print-page-hints.json').read_text())
   fields=['cardyssey_karte','variante_id','set_id','cardmarket_ids','routing_state','drucktyp','stempel','foil','sonderdruck']
   names=[('endfynwn4n10gzq','Normal','[]',''),('cm4kqul3x1bwlz1f','Reverse','[]',''),('curated:de:sv01:013:grass-stamp','Reverse','["grass-symbol"]','Grass symbol'),('curated:de:sv01:013:holo','Holo','[]','')]
   for lang in ('de','en'):
    with (root/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open('w',encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
     w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=fields);w.writeheader()
     for var,typ,st,foil in names:
      w.writerow({'cardyssey_karte':'sv01-013','variante_id':var,'set_id':'sv01','cardmarket_ids':'689763,691000','routing_state':'UNVERIFIED','drucktyp':typ,'stempel':st,'foil':foil,'sonderdruck':''})
    result=h.apply(lang,base=root,root=root)
    self.assertEqual(result['standardOfferFilterCandidates'],2)
    with (root/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:rows={x['variante_id']:x for x in csv.DictReader(f)}
    for var in ('endfynwn4n10gzq','cm4kqul3x1bwlz1f'):
     self.assertEqual(rows[var]['catalog_singleton_id'],'689763')
     self.assertEqual(rows[var]['catalog_expansion_id'],'5223')
     self.assertEqual(rows[var]['reference_standard_filter_supported'],'YES')
     self.assertEqual(rows[var]['reference_print_verified'],'NO')
     self.assertEqual(rows[var]['cardmarket_ids'],'689763,691000')
    for var in ('curated:de:sv01:013:grass-stamp','curated:de:sv01:013:holo'):
     self.assertEqual(rows[var]['catalog_singleton_id'],'')
     self.assertEqual(rows[var]['reference_standard_filter_supported'],'NO')
     self.assertIn('/Sprigatito-V2-SVI013',rows[var]['reference_product_url'])
 def test_scanner_checks_reference_before_search_and_never_confirms_price(self):
  s=(ROOT/'cardmarket-language-browser-collector.ps1').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
  self.assertIn('reference_page_used=$true',s)
  self.assertIn("$out.status='PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'",s)
  self.assertIn("[string]$case.reference_print_verified -eq 'NO'",s)
  a=s.index("$out.reference_page_used -eq $true");b=s.index("} elseif ($candidateId -ne",a)
  self.assertNotIn('identity_confirmed = $true',s[a:b]);self.assertNotIn('sample_median_eur=',s[a:b])
  self.assertIn("[string]$case.reference_standard_filter_supported -ne 'YES'",s)
  self.assertIn("$out.standard_offer_filter_candidate=$true",s)
  self.assertIn('expected_reverse_holo',s)
  self.assertIn('all_pages_complete',s)

# DATEI 076/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_live_failures.py
############################################################################################

import csv,importlib.util,json,tempfile,unittest
from datetime import datetime,timezone,timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
def load(name):
 spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('module_'+name.replace('-','_'),ROOT/name)
 module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(module);return module
fail=load('cardmarket-live-failures.py');retry=load('cardmarket-retry-one.py')
class Tests(unittest.TestCase):
 def setup(self,td):
  base=Path(td);cols=['cardyssey_karte','variante_id','set_id','kartenname_suche','manual_scan_requested_at']
  for lang in ('de','en'):
   with (base/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=cols);w.writeheader()
    for card in ('hgss3-5','2019sm-1'):w.writerow({'cardyssey_karte':card,'variante_id':'v1','set_id':card.rsplit('-',1)[0],'kartenname_suche':card})
  return base
 def test_current_failures_paged_and_historical_hidden(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   b=self.setup(td);start=datetime.now(timezone.utc);(b/'full-rescan-epoch.json').write_text(json.dumps({'requestedAt':start.isoformat()}))
   with (b/'browser-observations-de.jsonl').open('w') as f:
    for card,status,seen in [('2019sm-1','NO_EXACT_NUMBER_SEARCH_HIT',start-timedelta(days=2)),('hgss3-5','FILTER_UNAVAILABLE',start+timedelta(seconds=1))]:
     f.write(json.dumps({'card_id':card,'variant_id':'v1','status':status,'seen_at':seen.isoformat()})+'\n')
   result=fail.list_failures(b,'de',kind='alle')
   self.assertEqual(result['total'],1);self.assertEqual(result['items'][0]['type'],'TECHNISCH')
   self.assertEqual(fail.list_failures(b,'de',scope='all',kind='alle')['total'],2)
   self.assertEqual(fail.list_failures(b,'de')['total'],0)
   self.assertEqual(fail.list_failures(b,'de',kind='technik')['total'],1)
 def test_30th_verified_product_with_incomplete_offers_is_price_only(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   b=self.setup(td);p=b/'queue-de.csv'
   # A fixture with the real queue shape is preferable to guesswork about
   # optional fields of the old synthetic queue.
   with p.open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=['cardyssey_karte','variante_id','set_id','kartenname_suche','cardmarket_ids','routing_state','known_url']);w.writeheader()
    w.writerow({'cardyssey_karte':'30th-c-008','variante_id':'v30','set_id':'30th-c','kartenname_suche':'Pikachu & Zekrom GX','cardmarket_ids':'907963','routing_state':'SOURCE_ONLY','known_url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/30th-Celebration/Pikachu-Zekrom-GX-30CTEU-33'})
    w.writerow({'cardyssey_karte':'30th-c-009','variante_id':'v31','set_id':'30th-c','kartenname_suche':'Andere Karte','cardmarket_ids':'907964','routing_state':'SOURCE_ONLY','known_url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/30th-Celebration/Other'})
   now=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
   with (b/'browser-observations-de.jsonl').open('w') as f:
    for card,var,pid in [('30th-c-008','v30','907963'),('30th-c-009','v31','999999')]:
     f.write(json.dumps({'card_id':card,'variant_id':var,'status':'FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE','product_id':pid,'identity_confirmed':True,'pagination_failure':'LOAD_MORE_DISABLED_OR_LIMIT_REACHED','seen_at':now})+'\n')
   price=fail.list_failures(b,'de',kind='preise');manual=fail.list_failures(b,'de',kind='karten')
   self.assertEqual([x['cardId'] for x in price['items']],['30th-c-008'])
   self.assertEqual([x['cardId'] for x in manual['items']],['30th-c-009'])
   self.assertEqual(price['priceChecks'],1)
   self.assertEqual(manual['assignment'],1)
   self.assertEqual(fail.list_failures(b,'de',kind='alle')['total'],2)
 def test_known_page_with_distinct_stamp_is_not_missing_card_identity(self):

 def test_other_sets_are_not_auto_exempt(self):
  self.assertEqual(fail.classify('FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE',{'product_id':'907963','identity_confirmed':True},{'set_id':'other','cardmarket_ids':'907963','known_url':'https://example.com'}),'TECHNISCH')
 def test_confirmed_card_disappears_from_current_and_historical_review(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   b=self.setup(td)
   t=datetime(2026,11,9,19,30,tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   with (b/'browser-observations-de.jsonl').open('w') as f:
    f.write(json.dumps({'card_id':'hgss3-5','variant_id':'v1','status':'MULTIPLE_SEARCH_HITS','seen_at':t.isoformat()})+'\n')
   decisions={('hgss3-5','v1'):{'status':'CONFIRMED_PRINT','reviewedAt':(t+timedelta(minutes=20)).isoformat()}}
   self.assertEqual(fail.list_failures(b,'de',kind='karten',decisions=decisions)['total'],0)
   self.assertEqual(fail.list_failures(b,'de',scope='all',kind='alle',decisions=decisions)['total'],0)
   self.assertEqual(fail.list_failures(b,'de',kind='karten',decisions={})['total'],1)
 def test_human_confirmation_outweighs_later_name_only_failure(self):
  t=datetime(2026,11,9,19,30,tzinfo=timezone.utc)
  decision={'status':'CONFIRMED_PRINT','reviewedAt':t.isoformat(),'selectedProductId':'273703'}
  later=(t+timedelta(minutes=3)).isoformat()
  self.assertTrue(fail.resolved_after_scan({'seen_at':later,'status':'MULTIPLE_SEARCH_HITS','product_id':''},decision))
  self.assertTrue(fail.resolved_after_scan({'seen_at':later,'status':'PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED','product_id':'273703'},decision))
  self.assertFalse(fail.resolved_after_scan({'seen_at':later,'status':'PRODUCT_ID_NOT_VERIFIED_FOR_CARD','product_id':'999999'},decision))
 def test_new_scan_after_confirmation_remains_visible(self):
  t=datetime(2026,11,9,19,30,tzinfo=timezone.utc)
  decision={'status':'CONFIRMED_PRINT','reviewedAt':t.isoformat()}
  obs={'seen_at':(t+timedelta(minutes=5)).isoformat()}
  self.assertFalse(fail.resolved_after_scan(obs,decision))
  self.assertTrue(fail.resolved_after_scan({'seen_at':(t-timedelta(seconds=10)).isoformat()},decision))
 def test_retry_only_requested_variant(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   b=self.setup(td);r=retry.retry('de','hgss3-5','v1',base=b)
   self.assertTrue(r['ok'])
   with (b/'queue-de.csv').open() as f:rows=list(csv.DictReader(f))
   self.assertTrue(rows[0]['manual_scan_requested_at'])
   self.assertFalse(rows[1]['manual_scan_requested_at'])
 def test_missing_required_fields_are_visible_instead_of_card_missing(self):
  self.assertEqual(fail.classify('MISSING_REQUIRED_SCAN_FIELDS'),'DATEN_FEHLEN')
  self.assertEqual(fail.classify('FILTER_UNAVAILABLE'),'TECHNISCH')
 def test_no_unknown_queue_row(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   b=self.setup(td)
   with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'card_variant_not_in_current_queue'):retry.retry('de','fake-1','v1',base=b)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 077/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_login_pause_policy.py
############################################################################################

import unittest
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
class LoginPausePolicyTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_runner_requires_login_and_stops_on_block(self):
  runner=(ROOT/'Cardmarket-Queue-Runner.ps1').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
  self.assertIn('-RequireLogin:$RequireLogin',runner)
  self.assertIn("'LOGIN_REQUIRED'",runner)
  self.assertIn("'BLOCKED'",runner)
 def test_collector_stops_instead_of_scanning_as_guest(self):
  scanner=(ROOT/'cardmarket-language-browser-collector.ps1').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
  self.assertIn("if ($RequireLogin -and -not $auth) { Assert-CardmarketAuthenticated }",scanner)
  self.assertIn("function Assert-CardmarketAuthenticated",scanner)
  self.assertIn("throw 'CARDMARKET_PROVIDER_CHALLENGE'",scanner)
  self.assertIn("Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'LOGIN_REQUIRED'",scanner)
  self.assertIn("Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'BLOCKED'",scanner)
 def test_resume_gate_does_not_retry_blocked_or_logged_out_session(self):
  launcher=(ROOT/'Cardmarket-Collector-Launcher.ps1').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
  self.assertIn("$health.login -ne 'AUTHENTICATED'",launcher)
  self.assertIn("'LOGIN_UNVERIFIED'",launcher)
  self.assertIn('"-Languages","both"',launcher)
  self.assertIn('"-RequireLogin"',launcher)
 def test_manual_resume_does_not_override_access_gate(self):
  wrapper=(ROOT/'Cardmarket-Manual-Resume.ps1').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
  self.assertIn('& $launcher -ManualResume',wrapper)
  self.assertNotIn('-ForcePublicMode',wrapper)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 078/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_manual_product_review.py
############################################################################################

import importlib.util, json, tempfile, unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
s=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('manual',Path(__file__).with_name('cardmarket-manual-product-review.py'))
m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(s);s.loader.exec_module(m)
class ManualProductReviewTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_only_exact_cardmarket_domains_allowed(self):
  self.assertTrue(m.valid_link('https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Set/Caterpie-V2-Z1'))
  self.assertTrue(m.valid_link('https://www.cardmarket.com/Pokemon/Products?idProduct=581845'))
  for u in ('http://www.cardmarket.com/Pokemon/Products?idProduct=123','https://cardmarket.com/Pokemon/Products?idProduct=123',
     'https://www.cardmarket.com.evil.org/Pokemon/Products?idProduct=123',
     'https://www.cardmarket.com/Pokemon/Products?idProduct=not_an_id',
     'https://www.cardmarket.com@evil.org/Pokemon/Products?idProduct=581845',
     'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Search?searchString=Raupy'):
   self.assertFalse(m.valid_link(u),u)
 def test_curated_variant_id_is_valid_and_wrong_foreign_set_is_rejected(self):
  # Real Cardyssey IDs contain colons; rejecting them before the browser
  # hid the actual issue: Gem Pack Chinese is not SV01 Scarlet-Violet.
  case={'language_code':'de','card_id':'sv01-013','variant_id':'curated:de:sv01:013:grass-stamp','set_id':'sv01'}
  with patch.object(m,'record',return_value=case),patch.object(m.subprocess,'run') as runner:
   with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'wrong_set_url_expected_Scarlet-Violet'):
    m.preview({'language':'de','cardId':'sv01-013','variantId':case['variant_id'],
      'url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Gem-Pack-Vol-1/Sprigatito-V1-CBB1C01?isReverseHolo=Y'})
   runner.assert_not_called()
 def test_other_set_url_rejected_before_browser(self):
  case={'language_code':'de','card_id':'2019sm-1','variant_id':'a','set_id':'2019sm'}
  with patch.object(m,'record',return_value=case),patch.object(m.subprocess,'run') as runner:
   with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'wrong_set_url_expected_McDonalds-Collection-2019'):
    m.preview({'language':'de','cardId':'2019sm-1','variantId':'a','url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Burning-Shadows/Caterpie-BUS1'})
   runner.assert_not_called()
 def test_mcDonalds_and_burning_shadows_are_separate_set_guards(self):
  import json
  guard=json.loads((Path('/home/tv/cardshop')/'config/cardmarket-set-url-guards.json').read_text())
  self.assertEqual(guard['2019sm'],'McDonalds-Collection-2019')
  self.assertEqual(guard['sm3'],'Burning-Shadows')
 def test_blocked_browser_never_creates_preview(self):
  case={'language_code':'de','card_id':'2019sm-1','variant_id':'a','matched_set_ids':['479679'],'set_id':'2019sm'}
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td,patch.object(m,'STORE',Path(td)),patch.object(m,'record',return_value=case),patch.object(m.subprocess,'run') as run:
   run.return_value.returncode=1;run.return_value.stderr='CARDMARKET_PROVIDER_CHALLENGE'
   with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'cloudflare_blocked'):
    m.preview({'language':'de','cardId':'2019sm-1','variantId':'a','url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/Pokemon/Products?idProduct=479679'})
   self.assertEqual(list(Path(td).iterdir()),[])
 def test_preview_creates_untrusted_price_reference_and_explicit_conflict(self):
  case={'language_code':'de','card_id':'2019sm-1','variant_id':'a','matched_set_ids':['479679'],'set_id':'2019sm','provider_expansion_id':'3354','printed_number':'1','card_name':'Raupy','print_type':'Holo'}
  page={'id':'581845','url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/McDonalds-Collection-2019/Caterpie-V2','heading':'Caterpie','crumbs':['2019 McDonalds','Caterpie']}
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td,patch.object(m,'STORE',Path(td)),patch.object(m,'record',return_value=case),patch.object(m,'catalog',side_effect=[{'581845':{'idExpansion':3354,'name':'Caterpie'}},{'581845':{'avg30':0.4}}]),patch.object(m.subprocess,'run') as run:
   run.return_value.returncode=0;run.return_value.stdout=json.dumps(page)
   result=m.preview({'language':'de','cardId':'2019sm-1','variantId':'a','url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/Pokemon/Products?idProduct=581845'})
   self.assertEqual(result['productId'],'581845')
   self.assertIsNone(result['languageSpecificPrice'])
   self.assertTrue(any('PRODUCT_NOT_IN_PREVIOUS_CANDIDATES' in w for w in result['warnings']))
   self.assertTrue((Path(td)/(result['token']+'.json')).exists())
 def test_confirm_requires_human_checkbox(self):
  with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'invalid_preview_token'):m.confirm({'token':'../escape','exactPrintConfirmed':True})
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 079/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_median_all_offers.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import importlib.util,json,unittest
from pathlib import Path
path=Path(__file__).with_name("cardmarket-median-all-offers.py")
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("cm_all_median",path)
m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(m)
def sample():
    def offer(i,price,quantity=1):
        return {"offer_id":str(i),"product_id":"895610","language":"de","condition":"NM",
            "finish":"cosmos","edition":"other","signed":False,"altered":False,
            "graded":False,"bundle":False,"quantity":quantity,"price_eur":price}
    return {"expected":{"product_id":"895610","language":"de","finish":"cosmos","edition":"other"},
        "offers":[offer(1,"0,05",1),offer(2,"0,20",1),offer(3,"0,49",5),
                  offer(4,"0,99",1),offer(5,"1,50",1)],
        "pagination":{"all_pages_fetched":True,"total_offers":5,"capped":False},
        "print_identity_independently_verified":True}
class MedianTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_all_offers_not_five_cheapest(self):
    data=sample()
    self.assertEqual(m.evaluate(data)["median_all_offers_eur"],"0.49")
    self.assertEqual(m.evaluate(data)["median_quantity_weighted_eur"],"0.49")
 def test_one_listing_not_weighted_by_stock(self):
    d=sample();d["offers"][2]["quantity"]=200000
    self.assertEqual(m.evaluate(d)["median_all_offers_eur"],"0.49")
 def test_incomplete_pages_not_published(self):
    d=sample();d["pagination"]["all_pages_fetched"]=False
    x=m.evaluate(d)
    self.assertFalse(x["can_publish_print_price"])
    self.assertEqual(x["status"],"PARTIAL_ASKING_MEDIAN")
 def test_language_mismatch_rejected(self):
    d=sample();d["offers"][0]["language"]="en"
    x=m.evaluate(d)
    self.assertEqual(x["accepted_offers"],4)
    self.assertFalse(x["can_publish_print_price"])
 def test_reverse_mismatch_rejected(self):
    d=sample();d["offers"][1]["finish"]="reverse"
    self.assertFalse(m.evaluate(d)["can_publish_print_price"])
 def test_missing_print_confirmation(self):
    d=sample();d["print_identity_independently_verified"]=False
    self.assertFalse(m.evaluate(d)["can_publish_print_price"])
 def test_missing_quantity_does_not_count(self):
    d=sample();d["offers"][1].pop("quantity")
    self.assertEqual(m.evaluate(d)["accepted_offers"],4)
 def test_german_thousands(self):
    self.assertEqual(m.money("1.234,56 €"),m.money("1,234.56 €"))
if __name__=="__main__":unittest.main()
