# DATEI 080/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_offer_median.py
############################################################################################

import unittest
from scripts.cardmarket_offer_median import evaluate

def example():
 return {'expected':{'language':'de','cardmarket_product_id':'273710','finish':'holo','first_edition':False},
  'pagination':{'all_pages_fetched':True,'capped':False,'total_offers':3},'offers':[
  {'id':str(i),'product_id':'273710','language':'de','finish':'holo','first_edition':False,
    'signed':False,'altered':False,'graded':False,'price_eur':p,'condition':c}
  for i,p,c in [(1,'83.43','NM'),(2,'89.00',None),(3,'95.00','PL')]]}
class TestOffers(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_missing_condition_is_flagged(self):
  result=evaluate(example());self.assertEqual(result['median_eur'],'86.22');self.assertEqual(result['assumed_nm'],1);self.assertFalse(result['safe_to_auto_publish'])
 def test_all_exact_explicit_allows_publish(self):
  d=example();d['offers'][1]['condition']='Near Mint';d['pagination']['total_offers']=3
  result=evaluate(d);self.assertTrue(result['safe_to_auto_publish']);self.assertEqual(result['accepted'],2)
 def test_unverified_pagination_not_full_market_median(self):
  d=example();d['pagination']['capped']=True
  self.assertEqual(evaluate(d)['status'],'PARTIAL_SAMPLE_MEDIAN_NOT_MARKET_WIDE')
 def test_missing_language_not_assumed(self):
  d=example();d['offers'][0].pop('language');self.assertEqual(evaluate(d)['accepted'],1)
 def test_mixed_editions_rejected(self):
  d=example();d['offers'][0]['first_edition']=True;self.assertEqual(evaluate(d)['accepted'],1)
 def test_bad_product_does_not_match(self):
  d=example();d['offers'][0]['product_id']='3';self.assertEqual(evaluate(d)['accepted'],1)
 def test_invalid_expected_rejected(self):
  d=example();d['expected']['finish']='unknown'
  with self.assertRaises(ValueError):evaluate(d)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 081/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_pagination_completion.py
############################################################################################

"""Do not interpret a missing/stalled next-page load as a verified full market."""
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
SRC=(Path(__file__).with_name('cardmarket-language-browser-collector.ps1')).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
class PaginationTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_more_than_old_300_limit_is_supported(self):
  self.assertIn('$offerCap=1200',SRC)
  self.assertIn('$maxOfferPages=24',SRC)
  self.assertIn('.slice(0,1200)',SRC)
  self.assertNotIn('CARDMARKET_300_CAP',SRC)
 def test_only_stable_absent_button_can_convert_old_error_to_complete(self):
  self.assertIn("$lastButton -ne 'ABSENT'",SRC)
  self.assertIn('BUTTON_REMOVED_STABLE_4_CHECKS',SRC)
  self.assertIn('if($stableEnd)',SRC)
  self.assertIn('if($settle -eq 3)',SRC)
  self.assertNotIn("if($clicked -eq 'DISABLED') {$out.pagination_failure=''}",SRC)
 def test_stop_is_always_incomplete_if_offer_cap_or_visible_more(self):
  self.assertIn("$out.pagination_failure='CARDMARKET_COLLECTOR_CAP'",SRC)
  self.assertIn("$out.pagination_failure='MORE_OFFERS_STILL_VISIBLE'",SRC)
  self.assertIn('not $data.hasMore',SRC)
  self.assertIn('$out.all_pages_complete=($auth',SRC)
 def test_median_requires_verified_full_market_and_all_offer_flags(self):
  self.assertIn('$out.median_all_offers_eur=if($out.all_pages_complete)',SRC)
  self.assertIn('$uniqueOfferIds.Count -ne [int]$data.totalRows',SRC)
  self.assertIn('$accepted.Count -ne [int]$data.totalRows',SRC)
  self.assertIn('MISMATCHED_OFFER_FILTER_FLAGS',SRC)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 082/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_print_review_admin.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Live DB integration smoke test for the separate Cardmarket review table.

Creates a uniquely named synthetic review row only; always deletes it.
"""
import json
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).parent
CARD_ID="cardmarket-review-smoke-test-20261106"

def sql(statement):
    p=subprocess.run(["docker","exec","cardyssey-db","psql","-X","-v","ON_ERROR_STOP=1",
         "-U","cardyssey","-d","cardyssey","-At","-c",statement],
         capture_output=True,text=True,check=True)
    return p.stdout.strip()
def cli(payload):
    return subprocess.run([sys.executable,str(ROOT/"cardmarket-print-review-admin.py"),
         "--action","review"],input=json.dumps(payload),capture_output=True,text=True)
def main():
    sql(f"DELETE FROM cardmarket_print_identity_review WHERE card_id='{CARD_ID}'")
    try:
        sql(f"""INSERT INTO cardmarket_print_identity_review
           (language_code,card_id,variant_id,set_id,classification,candidate_ids,matched_set_ids)
           VALUES ('de','{CARD_ID}','v-test','s-test','TEST',
                   '["1234","9876"]'::jsonb,'["1234"]'::jsonb)""")
        base={"language":"de","cardId":CARD_ID,"variantId":"v-test",
              "status":"CONFIRMED_PRINT","exactPrintConfirmed":True,"note":"test"}
        invalid=cli({**base,"productId":"9876"})
        assert invalid.returncode==2 and "not matched" in invalid.stdout, invalid.stdout
        count=sql(f"SELECT status FROM cardmarket_print_identity_review WHERE card_id='{CARD_ID}'")
        assert count=="PENDING_REVIEW",count
        valid=cli({**base,"productId":"1234"})
        assert valid.returncode==0,valid.stdout
        state=sql(f"SELECT status||':'||selected_product_id FROM cardmarket_print_identity_review WHERE card_id='{CARD_ID}'")
        assert state=="CONFIRMED_PRINT:1234",state
        route=sql(f"SELECT verification_state||':'||provider_product_id FROM price_source_routing WHERE card_id='{CARD_ID}'")
        assert route=="VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID:1234",route
        print("PASS: wrong ID rejected; correct ID confirmed in review + verified routing; no price write.")
    finally:
        sql(f"DELETE FROM price_source_routing WHERE card_id='{CARD_ID}'")
        sql(f"DELETE FROM cardmarket_print_identity_review WHERE card_id='{CARD_ID}'")
if __name__=="__main__":
    main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 083/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_prioritize_recovery_queue.py
############################################################################################

"""Queue-priority invariants: stable keys, special prints, no lost entries."""
import importlib.util
import tempfile
import unittest
from datetime import datetime,timezone,timedelta
from pathlib import Path
import csv,json
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('recovery_priority',Path(__file__).with_name('cardmarket-prioritize-recovery-queue.py'))
mod=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
class PrioritizeRecoveryTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def make(self,card,special='',tier='4',manual=''):
  return {'cardyssey_karte':card,'variante_id':'v1','set_id':'sv','drucktyp':'Normal',
    'foil':special,'sonderdruck':'','stempel':'[]','scan_tier':tier,
    'manual_scan_requested_at':manual,'existing_price_eur':'0.15'}
 def test_prints_and_manual_failures_before_bulk(self):
  now=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
  rows=[self.make('bulk'),self.make('fail',tier='2',manual=now.isoformat()),
    self.make('pokeball','Pokeball'),self.make('mapped','Meisterball'),
    self.make('old-error',tier='2')]
  latest={('old-error','v1'):{'status':'NO_EXACT_NUMBER_SEARCH_HIT','seen_at':now.isoformat()},
    ('fail','v1'):{'status':'ERROR','seen_at':(now-timedelta(days=1)).isoformat()}}
  output,report=mod.prioritize(rows,latest,{('mapped','v1')})
  self.assertEqual([r['cardyssey_karte'] for r in output],
                   ['mapped','pokeball','fail','old-error','bulk'])
  self.assertEqual(len(output),5)
  self.assertEqual(output[0]['existing_price_eur'],'0.15')
 def test_same_card_keeps_distinct_variant_rows(self):
  rows=[self.make('wattzapf','Meisterball'),self.make('wattzapf','Pokeball')]
  rows[1]['variante_id']='v2'
  output,report=mod.prioritize(rows,{},set())
  self.assertEqual(len(output),2)
  self.assertEqual(len({(r['cardyssey_karte'],r['variante_id']) for r in output}),2)
 def test_normal_reverse_not_special_by_default(self):
  row=self.make('regular')
  self.assertFalse(mod.is_special(row))
  row['drucktyp']='Reverse'
  self.assertFalse(mod.is_special(row))
  row['stempel']='["Professorenprogramm"]'
  self.assertTrue(mod.is_special(row))
 def test_dry_run_no_mutations_and_apply_atomic(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
   base=Path(t)
   fields=list(self.make('x').keys())
   for lang in ('de','en'):
    with (base/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open('w',newline='',encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
     w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=fields);w.writeheader();w.writerows([
         self.make('bulk'),self.make('rare','Pokeball')])
    (base/f'browser-observations-{lang}.jsonl').write_text('')
   a=(base/'queue-de.csv').read_bytes()
   report=mod.run(base=base,apply=False,candidates={('de','rare','v1')})
   self.assertEqual(a,(base/'queue-de.csv').read_bytes())
   self.assertEqual(report['languages']['de']['openMappingCasesInQueue'],1)
   mod.run(base=base,apply=True,candidates={('de','rare','v1')})
   with (base/'queue-de.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:rows=list(csv.DictReader(f))
   self.assertEqual([x['cardyssey_karte'] for x in rows],['rare','bulk'])
   self.assertEqual(rows[0]['existing_price_eur'],'0.15')
 def test_one_time_recheck_promotes_only_open_failed_case(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
   base=Path(t)
   stamp=datetime(2026,11,11,13,0,tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   previous=stamp-timedelta(hours=2)
   fields=list(self.make('a').keys())
   for lang in ('de','en'):
    with (base/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open('w',newline='',encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
     w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=fields);w.writeheader();w.writerows([
       self.make('open','Pokeball'),self.make('success','Masterball'),self.make('bulk')])
    with (base/f'browser-observations-{lang}.jsonl').open('w') as f:
     for cid,status in [('open','PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'),
                        ('success','COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED')]:
      f.write(json.dumps({'card_id':cid,'variant_id':'v1','status':status,'seen_at':previous.isoformat()})+'\n')
   cases={(lang,'open','v1') for lang in ('de','en')}
   first=mod.run(base=base,apply=True,candidates=cases,recheck_open=True,now=stamp)
   self.assertEqual(first['languages']['de']['specialRecheckMarked'],1)
   self.assertEqual(first['languages']['en']['specialRecheckMarked'],1)
   self.assertTrue((base/'open-print-recheck-once-20261111.json').exists())
   for lang in ('de','en'):
    with (base/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
     rows={r['cardyssey_karte']:r for r in csv.DictReader(f)}
    self.assertEqual(rows['open']['manual_scan_requested_at'],stamp.isoformat())
    self.assertEqual(rows['success']['manual_scan_requested_at'],'')
   next_run=mod.run(base=base,apply=True,candidates=cases,recheck_open=True,now=stamp+timedelta(hours=6))
   self.assertTrue(next_run['recheckAlreadyScheduled'])
   self.assertEqual(next_run['languages']['de']['specialRecheckMarked'],0)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 084/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_rescan_epoch.py

import csv,json,tempfile,unittest
from datetime import datetime,timezone,timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from scripts.cardmarket_scan_monitor import snapshot
class RescanEpochTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_old_observations_preserved_but_not_in_new_cycle(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   p=Path(td);keys=[('a','v1'),('a','v2')]
   with (p/'queue-de.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=['cardyssey_karte','variante_id','scan_tier','needs_price']);w.writeheader()
    for card,variant in keys:w.writerow({'cardyssey_karte':card,'variante_id':variant,'scan_tier':'2','needs_price':'NO'})
   start=datetime(2026,11,9,13,0,tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   (p/'full-rescan-epoch.json').write_text(json.dumps({'requestedAt':start.isoformat()}))
   with (p/'browser-observations-de.jsonl').open('w') as f:
    for i,(card,variant) in enumerate(keys):f.write(json.dumps({'card_id':card,'variant_id':variant,'status':'MAPPED_NO_PRICE_REQUIRED','seen_at':(start+timedelta(seconds=1) if i else start-timedelta(days=1)).isoformat()})+'\n')
   x=snapshot(p,'de')
   self.assertEqual(x['browserProcessed'],2)
   self.assertEqual(x['rescanProcessed'],1)
   self.assertIsNotNone(x['rescanStartedAt'])
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 085/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_reset_rescan.py

import csv,importlib.util,json,tempfile,unittest
from datetime import datetime,timezone
from pathlib import Path
m_path=Path(__file__).with_name('cardmarket-reset-rescan.py')
s=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('reset_cm',m_path);m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(s);s.loader.exec_module(m)
class ResetTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_reset_starts_new_epoch_but_preserves_previous_scan_history(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
   base=Path(tmp)
   for lang in ('de','en'):
    (base/f'queue-{lang}.csv').write_text('card,variant\n'+('x,v\n'*1000))
    (base/f'progress-{lang}.json').write_text(json.dumps({'status':'BLOCKED','done':42,'total':1000,'file':'history'}))
    (base/f'browser-observations-{lang}.jsonl').write_text('historic row\n')
   now=datetime(2026,11,9,14,0,tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   result=m.reset(base=base,check_process=False,now=now)
   self.assertEqual(result['startedAt'],now.isoformat())
   for lang in ('de','en'):
    p=json.loads((base/f'progress-{lang}.json').read_text())
    self.assertEqual(p['done'],0);self.assertEqual(p['status'],'QUEUED');self.assertEqual(p['file'],'history')
    self.assertEqual((base/f'browser-observations-{lang}.jsonl').read_text(),'historic row\n')
   self.assertEqual(json.loads((base/'full-rescan-epoch.json').read_text())['requestedAt'],now.isoformat())
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

import csv,importlib.util,tempfile,unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('review',Path(__file__).with_name('cardmarket-print-review-admin.py'))
review=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(review)
class UndoTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_queue_undo_restores_candidate_without_touching_price(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as td:
   base=Path(td);p=base/'queue-de.csv';cols=['cardyssey_karte','variante_id','routing_state','cardmarket_ids','known_url','manual_scan_requested_at']
   with p.open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=cols);w.writeheader();w.writerow(dict(zip(cols,['2019sm-1','v1','VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID','777777','https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Wrong','2026-11-09T12:00:00Z'])))
   self.assertTrue(review.revert_queue_identity('de','2019sm-1','v1',['412849'],base))
   with p.open() as f:r=next(csv.DictReader(f))
   self.assertEqual(r['cardmarket_ids'],'412849')
   self.assertEqual(r['routing_state'],'UNVERIFIED')
   self.assertEqual(r['known_url'],'')
   self.assertEqual(r['manual_scan_requested_at'],'')
 def test_already_pending_case_cannot_be_undone(self):
  with patch.object(review,'pg',return_value='{"undone":0,"routeRemoved":0,"candidateIds":null}'):
   with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError,'no_review_decision_to_undo'):
    review.undo_case({'language':'de','cardId':'2019sm-1','variantId':'jr7oetx1mqug9'})
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 090/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_schedule_all_unfinished.py
############################################################################################

"""One-time failed-only requeue leaves successful and historical rows untouched."""
import csv,importlib.util,json,tempfile,unittest
from datetime import datetime,timezone,timedelta
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('outstanding',ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-schedule-all-unfinished.py')
mod=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
class RecoveryTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_all_open_current_epoch_once_old_and_success_untouched(self):
  with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as t:
   base=Path(t)
   epoch=datetime(2026,11,9,19,30,tzinfo=timezone.utc)
   (base/'full-rescan-epoch.json').write_text(json.dumps({'requestedAt':epoch.isoformat()}))
   cols=['cardyssey_karte','variante_id','set_id','drucktyp','cardmarket_ids',
         'manual_scan_requested_at','existing_price_eur']
   for lang in ('de','en'):
    rows=[{'cardyssey_karte':str(i),'variante_id':'v1','set_id':'sve','drucktyp':'Reverse',
           'cardmarket_ids':'321'+str(i),'manual_scan_requested_at':'','existing_price_eur':'0.02'} for i in range(5)]
    with (base/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open('w',newline='',encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
     writer=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=cols);writer.writeheader();writer.writerows(rows)
    cases=[
     ('0','MULTIPLE_SEARCH_HITS',epoch+timedelta(hours=1)),
     ('1','FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE',epoch+timedelta(hours=1)),
     ('2','COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED',epoch+timedelta(hours=1)),
     ('3','NO_EXACT_NUMBER_SEARCH_HIT',epoch-timedelta(days=1))]
    with (base/f'browser-observations-{lang}.jsonl').open('w') as f:
     for cid,status,when in cases:
      f.write(json.dumps({'card_id':cid,'variant_id':'v1',
         'status':status,'seen_at':when.isoformat()})+'\n')
   before=(base/'queue-de.csv').read_bytes()
   out=mod.audit_and_schedule(base=base,apply=False,now=epoch+timedelta(hours=2))
   self.assertEqual(out['languages']['de']['requeuedCurrentFailures'],2)
   self.assertEqual(out['languages']['en']['requeuedCurrentFailures'],2)
   self.assertEqual(out['languages']['de']['unscannedAlreadyDue'],1)
   self.assertEqual(out['languages']['de']['historicalAlreadyDue'],1)
   self.assertEqual(out['languages']['de']['completedUnchanged'],1)
   self.assertEqual(before,(base/'queue-de.csv').read_bytes())
   old_report=mod.REPORT;mod.REPORT=base/'reports'
   try:out=mod.audit_and_schedule(base=base,apply=True,now=epoch+timedelta(hours=2))
   finally:mod.REPORT=old_report
   for lang in ('de','en'):
    with (base/f'queue-{lang}.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
     rows={x['cardyssey_karte']:x for x in csv.DictReader(f)}
    self.assertTrue(rows['0']['manual_scan_requested_at'])
    self.assertTrue(rows['1']['manual_scan_requested_at'])
    for n in ('2','3','4'):self.assertFalse(rows[n]['manual_scan_requested_at'])
    self.assertEqual(rows['0']['cardmarket_ids'],'3210')
    self.assertEqual(rows['0']['existing_price_eur'],'0.02')
 def test_all_retryable_known_statuses_explicit(self):
  self.assertIn('PRODUCT_ID_NOT_VERIFIED_FOR_CARD',mod.RETRYABLE)
  self.assertIn('BLOCKED',mod.RETRYABLE)
  self.assertIn('DEFERRED_SOCKET',mod.RETRYABLE)
  self.assertNotIn('COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED',mod.RETRYABLE)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 091/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_schedule_variant_rechecks.py
############################################################################################

"""Targeted recheck queue is lossless and never reclassifies a print or price."""
import csv, importlib.util, json, tempfile, unittest
from datetime import datetime,timezone
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('schedule',ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-schedule-variant-rechecks.py')
mod=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec); spec.loader.exec_module(mod)
class SchedulerTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_marks_only_actual_failures_and_keeps_ids_and_prices(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:
            b=Path(tmp)
            cols=['cardyssey_karte','variante_id','set_id','kartennummer','kartenname_suche','drucktyp','foil',
                  'stempel','sonderdruck','cardmarket_ids','routing_state','existing_price_eur','manual_scan_requested_at']
            rows=[
                dict(zip(cols,['sv10.5w-033','poke','sv10.5w','033','Wattzapf','Reverse','Pokéball','[]','','836551','UNVERIFIED','7.58',''])),
                dict(zip(cols,['sv10.5w-033','master','sv10.5w','033','Wattzapf','Reverse','Meisterball','[]','','836552','UNVERIFIED','0.21',''])),
                dict(zip(cols,['sv10.5w-034','regular','sv10.5w','034','Test','Normal','','[]','','9999','VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID','0.03','']))]
            with (b/'queue-de.csv').open('w',encoding='utf-8-sig',newline='') as f:
                w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=cols);w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)
            with (b/'browser-observations-de.jsonl').open('w') as f:
                for cid,var,status in [('sv10.5w-033','poke','MULTIPLE_SEARCH_HITS'),
                                      ('sv10.5w-033','master','MULTIPLE_SEARCH_HITS'),
                                      ('sv10.5w-034','regular','MAPPED_NO_PRICE_REQUIRED')]:
                    f.write(json.dumps({'card_id':cid,'variant_id':var,'status':status,'seen_at':'2026-11-10T09:00:00Z'})+'\n')
            at=datetime(2026,11,10,14,30,tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            draft=mod.select('de',limit=2,base=b,now=at,dry_run=True)
            self.assertEqual(draft['scheduled'],2)
            with (b/'queue-de.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f:
                self.assertTrue(all(not x['manual_scan_requested_at'] for x in csv.DictReader(f)))
            previous_root=mod.ROOT
            mod.ROOT=b
            try:real=mod.select('de',limit=2,base=b,now=at,dry_run=False)
            finally:mod.ROOT=previous_root
            self.assertEqual(real['scheduled'],2)
            with (b/'queue-de.csv').open(encoding='utf-8-sig') as f: out={x['variante_id']:x for x in csv.DictReader(f)}
            for r in rows:
                self.assertEqual(out[r['variante_id']]['cardmarket_ids'],r['cardmarket_ids'])
                self.assertEqual(out[r['variante_id']]['existing_price_eur'],r['existing_price_eur'])
                self.assertEqual(out[r['variante_id']]['routing_state'],r['routing_state'])
            self.assertEqual(out['regular']['manual_scan_requested_at'],'')
            self.assertEqual(out['poke']['manual_scan_requested_at'],at.isoformat())
    def test_epoch_respects_manual_retry_and_family_leads_never_price(self):
        src=(ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-language-browser-collector.ps1').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
        self.assertIn('-not $manualRefreshDue',src)
        self.assertIn('BROWSER_OBSERVED_PRODUCT_CANDIDATE_PRINT_UNVERIFIED',src)
        self.assertIn('VERSION_FAMILY_PAGE_AND_NUMBER_PROVED_PRINT_UNVERIFIED',src)
        self.assertIn("$out.status='PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'",src)
        self.assertNotIn("$out.status='PRICE_FOUND'",src)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 092/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_session_pacing_priority.py
############################################################################################

"""Safeguards for persistent profile, provider cooldown, and paced requests."""
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
class SessionPacingPolicyTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def source(self,name):
  return (ROOT/name).read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig')
 def test_persistent_profile_no_cookie_replay(self):
  launcher=self.source('Cardmarket-Collector-Launcher.ps1')
  self.assertIn('CardmarketEdgeProfile',launcher)
  self.assertIn('--user-data-dir=$profile',launcher)
  self.assertIn('Cardmarket-Session-Status.ps1',launcher)
  for token in ['PHPSESSID','browser-cookie3','storage_state','Set-Cookie','CookieHeader']:
   self.assertNotIn(token,launcher)
  self.assertNotIn('$cookies=',launcher)
 def test_per_request_budget_including_ajax(self):
  scanner=self.source('cardmarket-language-browser-collector.ps1')
  self.assertIn('[int]$MinRequestGapSeconds = 10',scanner)
  self.assertIn('function Wait-CardmarketRequestBudget',scanner)
  self.assertIn("$script:lastProviderRequestUtc=[DateTime]::MinValue",scanner)
  self.assertIn("Wait-CardmarketRequestBudget\n  Send-Cdp 'Page.navigate'",scanner)
  self.assertIn("Wait-CardmarketRequestBudget\n          Send-Cdp 'Page.navigate'",scanner)
  self.assertIn("Wait-CardmarketRequestBudget\n                  $clicked=Js",scanner)
  self.assertIn("Wait-CardmarketRequestBudget\n              $filterOk = Js",scanner)
 def test_runner_shares_gap_with_both_languages(self):
  runner=self.source('Cardmarket-Queue-Runner.ps1')
  self.assertIn('MinRequestGapSeconds $MinRequestGapSeconds',runner)
  self.assertIn('BatchLimit = 16',runner)
  self.assertIn('RunMinutes = 40',runner)
  launcher=self.source('Cardmarket-Collector-Launcher.ps1')
  self.assertIn('"-BatchLimit","16"',launcher)
  self.assertIn('"-MinRequestGapSeconds","10"',launcher)
  self.assertIn('"-Languages","both"',launcher)
 def test_backoff_persists_between_runner_invocations(self):
  for name in ('Cardmarket-Collector-Launcher.ps1','Cardmarket-Resume-Watchdog.ps1'):
   source=self.source(name)
   self.assertIn("'BLOCKED' {240;break}",source)
   self.assertIn("'BATCH_COMPLETE_MORE_PENDING' {45;break}",source)
   self.assertIn("'LOGIN_REQUIRED' {20;break}",source)
  watchdog=self.source('Cardmarket-Resume-Watchdog.ps1')
  self.assertIn("foreach($language in @('de','en'))",watchdog)
  self.assertIn("$diag.login -ne 'AUTHENTICATED'",watchdog)
 def test_access_denied_not_mislabeled_logout(self):
  status=self.source('Cardmarket-Session-Status.ps1')
  scanner=self.source('cardmarket-language-browser-collector.ps1')
  self.assertIn('you have been blocked',status)
  self.assertIn('you have been blocked',scanner)
  self.assertIn('Assert-CardmarketAuthenticated',scanner)
  self.assertIn('CARDMARKET_PROVIDER_CHALLENGE',scanner)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 093/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_set_identity_audit.py
############################################################################################

import unittest
import importlib.util,sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0,str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('audit',Path(__file__).with_name('cardmarket-set-identity-audit.py'))
m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec);spec.loader.exec_module(m)
assess=m.assess
class SetIdentityAuditTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def test_identical_reprints_are_ambiguous_not_automatically_wrong(self):
  names=['Caterpie','Pikachu','Eevee']
  expansions={'2829':{'caterpie':1,'pikachu':1,'eevee':1},'3354':{'caterpie':1,'pikachu':1,'eevee':1}}
  x=assess(names,'2829',expansions)
  self.assertEqual(x['status'],'AMBIGUOUS_OVERLAPPING_EXPANSIONS')
 def test_unique_matching_expansion_not_flagged(self):
  names=['A','B','C','D']
  x=assess(names,'42',{'42':{'a':1,'b':1,'c':1,'d':1},'44':{'a':1}})
  self.assertEqual(x['status'],'NO_STRONG_CONFLICT_FOUND')
 def test_bad_existing_choice_flagged_without_reassigning_it(self):
  names=['A','B','C','D','E','F']
  x=assess(names,'42',{'42':{'a':1},'44':{'a':1,'b':1,'c':1,'d':1,'e':1,'f':1}})
  self.assertEqual(x['status'],'SELECTED_EXPANSION_NEEDS_REVIEW')
  self.assertEqual(x['top'][0]['expansionId'],'44')
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 094/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_variant_filters.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Regression checks against actual queued Cardyssey print variants."""
import csv
import re
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

QUEUE=Path("/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector/queue-de.csv")
SCRIPT=Path(__file__).with_name("cardmarket-language-browser-collector.ps1")

def filters(row):
    kind=row["drucktyp"]
    reverse="Y" if re.search("reverse",kind,re.I) else "N" if re.search("normal",kind,re.I) else "0"
    stamp=row["stempel"]
    first="Y" if re.search(r"1\. auflage|first.edition",stamp,re.I) else (
        "N" if re.fullmatch(r"unbegrenzt|unlimited",row["sonderdruck"],re.I) else "0")
    return reverse,first
def selected():
    wanted={"base1-8","sm10-16","mep-095"}
    with QUEUE.open(encoding="utf-8-sig",newline="") as f:
        for r in csv.DictReader(f):
            if r["cardyssey_karte"] in wanted:yield r

class CheckVariants(unittest.TestCase):
    @classmethod
    def setUpClass(cls):
        cls.rows=list(selected())
    def test_first_edition_machamp(self):
        rows=[r for r in self.rows if r["cardyssey_karte"]=="base1-8" and "1. Auflage" in r["stempel"]]
        self.assertEqual(len(rows),1)
        self.assertEqual(filters(rows[0]),("0","Y"))
    def test_unlimited_machamp(self):
        rows=[r for r in self.rows if r["cardyssey_karte"]=="base1-8" and r["sonderdruck"]=="unbegrenzt"]
        self.assertEqual(len(rows),1)
        self.assertEqual(filters(rows[0]),("0","N"))
    def test_tangela_reverse_does_not_imply_first(self):
        rows=[r for r in self.rows if r["cardyssey_karte"]=="sm10-16" and "reverse" in r["drucktyp"].lower()]
        self.assertEqual(len(rows),1)
        self.assertEqual(filters(rows[0]),("Y","0"))
    def test_tangela_normal(self):
        rows=[r for r in self.rows if r["cardyssey_karte"]=="sm10-16" and "normal" in r["drucktyp"].lower()]
        self.assertEqual(len(rows),1)
        self.assertEqual(filters(rows[0]),("N","0"))
    def test_lucario_not_auto_cosmos_filter(self):
        rows=[r for r in self.rows if r["cardyssey_karte"]=="mep-095"]
        self.assertEqual(len(rows),1)
        self.assertEqual(filters(rows[0]),("0","0"))
    def test_pagination_requires_full_and_unique_offer_ids(self):
        s=SCRIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        for marker in (
            "loadMoreButton", "LOAD_MORE_NO_NEW_OFFERS", "LOAD_MORE_DISABLED_OR_LIMIT_REACHED",
            "CARDMARKET_COLLECTOR_CAP", "DUPLICATE_OR_MISSING_OFFER_ID",
            "MISMATCHED_OFFER_FILTER_FLAGS", "all_pages_complete",
            "median_all_offers_eur", "totalRows",
        ):
            self.assertIn(marker,s)
    def test_search_cache_only_reuses_search_links_not_filtered_offers(self):
        s=SCRIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        self.assertIn('$searchCache.ContainsKey($searchUrl)',s)
        self.assertIn('$searchCache[$searchUrl] = $links',s)
        self.assertIn('$out.search_cache_hit=$true',s)
        self.assertIn('search_cache_hits = $searchCacheHits',s)
        self.assertIn('DEFERRED_PROVIDER_TIMEOUT',s)
        self.assertNotIn('$searchCache[$key] = $accepted',s)
    def test_elapsed_ms_collected_without_changing_filters(self):
        s=SCRIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        self.assertIn('$caseTimer=[Diagnostics.Stopwatch]::StartNew()',s)
        self.assertIn('$out.elapsed_ms=[int]$caseTimer.ElapsedMilliseconds',s)
        self.assertIn('extra[isReverseHolo]',s)
    def test_priced_bulk_is_routed_to_feed_not_browser(self):
        s=SCRIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        self.assertIn("$bulkFeedOnly = @($allCases | Where-Object {",s)
        self.assertIn("$priceNeeded = [string]$case.scan_tier -ne '4'",s)
        self.assertIn("bulk_feed_only_variants = $bulkFeedOnly",s)
    def test_batch_has_pending_limit_and_resume(self):
        s=SCRIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        for token in ("[int]$BatchLimit = 0", "-not $processed.Contains($k)", "Select-Object -First $BatchLimit", "BATCH_COMPLETE_MORE_PENDING"):
            self.assertIn(token,s)
    def test_qc_reports_complete_prices_as_print_unverified(self):
        s=SCRIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        self.assertIn("complete_filtered_asking_medians_print_unverified",s)
        self.assertIn("verified_language_variant_prices = 0",s)
    def test_public_session_fails_closed_and_continues(self):
        s=SCRIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        self.assertIn("access_mode='UNKNOWN'",s)
        self.assertIn("'PUBLIC_LIMITED'",s)
        self.assertIn('a[href*=User_Logout]',s)
        self.assertIn("PUBLIC_SESSION_LIMITED_SAMPLE",s)
        self.assertIn("$auth -and $data.filtered",s)
        self.assertIn("Save-Progress $processed.Count $runTotal 'LOGIN_REQUIRED'",s)
        self.assertIn("if ($RequireLogin -and -not $auth) { Assert-CardmarketAuthenticated }",s)
        self.assertIn("throw 'CARDMARKET_LOGIN_REQUIRED'",s)
        self.assertIn("throw 'CARDMARKET_PROVIDER_CHALLENGE'",s)
        self.assertIn("if ($data.filtered -and $auth)",s)
    def test_verified_queue_mapping_precedes_unreliable_text_search(self):
        s=SCRIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        self.assertIn("$case.known_url",s)
        self.assertIn("$case.routing_state -eq 'VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'",s)
        self.assertIn("$out.product_id = $candidateId",s)
        self.assertIn("PRODUCT_ID_NOT_IN_VERIFIED_SET",s)
    def test_live_script_independent_filters_and_query_verification(self):
        s=SCRIPT.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
        for token in ("expected_reverse_holo","expected_first_edition","extra[isReverseHolo]","extra[isFirstEd]","searchParams.get(k)","all_pages_complete=$false"):
            self.assertIn(token,s)
if __name__=="__main__":
    unittest.main()

import csv,importlib.util,io,unittest

  sql=m.SQL.format(language='en')

  self.assertIn("v.value->>'cardmarketProductId'",sql)
  self.assertIn("jsonb_array_length(c.provider_product_ids)=1",sql)
  self.assertNotIn('string_agg(DISTINCT pid',sql)
 def test_real_undaunted_houndoom_5_and_82_separated(self):
  p=Path('/tmp/cm-queue-new-de.csv')
  if not p.exists():self.skipTest('local verification export not available')
  with p.open(encoding='utf-8') as f:
   rows=[r for r in csv.DictReader(f) if r['cardyssey_karte'] in ('hgss3-5','hgss3-82')]
  self.assertEqual({r['cardmarket_ids'] for r in rows if r['cardyssey_karte']=='hgss3-5'},{'279258','371567'})
  self.assertEqual({r['cardmarket_ids'] for r in rows if r['cardyssey_karte']=='hgss3-82'},{'279335'})

  sql=m.SQL.format(language='de')
  self.assertIn('set_card_candidate_ids',sql)
  self.assertIn('jsonb_array_elements_text',sql)
 def test_human_verified_identity_wins(self):
  self.assertIn("r.verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'",m.SQL)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()

# DATEI 096/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_variant_recheck.py
############################################################################################

import unittest
from scripts.cardmarket_variant_recheck import candidate_audit
class VariantRecheckTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def setUp(self):
  self.products={'479679':{'idMetacard':226279},'581845':{'idMetacard':226279}}
  self.row={'matched_set_ids':'479679,581845','classification':'MULTIPLE_PRODUCTS_IN_VERIFIED_SET'}
 def test_card_level_candidates_are_not_verified_print_or_price(self):
  row={'matched_set_ids':'273708,660215','classification':'CARD_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_NOT_IDENTIFIED_SPECIAL_PRINT'}
  x=candidate_audit(row,{'273708':{'idExpansion':1523},'660215':{'idExpansion':1523}},None)
  self.assertEqual(x['category'],'AUTO_PRINT_VERIFICATION')
  self.assertFalse(x['printVerified'])
  self.assertFalse(x['priceApproved'])
 def test_card_set_number_confirmed_but_print_unverified_stays_automatic(self):
  row={'matched_set_ids':'','classification':'NO_PRODUCT_ID_SPECIAL_PRINT'}
  obs={'status':'PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET_PRINT_UNVERIFIED','product_id':'273708',
       'url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Base-Set/Poliwrath-V1-BS13',
       'identity_confirmed':False}
  x=candidate_audit(row,{'273708':{'idExpansion':1523}},obs)
  self.assertEqual(x['category'],'AUTO_CATALOG_RECHECK')
  self.assertFalse(x['printVerified'])
  self.assertFalse(x['priceApproved'])
 def test_user_supplied_link_is_separate_manual_review_not_verified_price(self):
  row={'matched_set_ids':'412849','classification':'ONE_PRODUCT_IN_VERIFIED_SET'}
  x=candidate_audit(row,{'412849':{'idExpansion':2829}},None,user_reference_url='https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/McDonalds-Collection-2019/Caterpie-MCD191')
  self.assertEqual(x['category'],'AUTO_LANGUAGE_AND_PRINT_RECHECK')
  self.assertFalse(x['printVerified'])
  self.assertFalse(x['priceApproved'])
 def test_without_matching_version_proof_is_automatic(self):
  x=candidate_audit(self.row,self.products,None)
  self.assertEqual(x['category'],'AUTO_VERSION_DISCOVERY')
  self.assertEqual(x['languageAvailability'],'UNKNOWN_NOT_PROVEN_ABSENT')
  self.assertFalse(x['printVerified'])
 def test_observed_version_does_not_certify_holo(self):
  obs={'url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/McDonalds-Collection-2019-2/Caterpie-V2-MCD19F2?language=3',
   'product_id':'581845','identity_confirmed':True,'status':'FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE',
   'verified_lang_nm_offers':2}
  x=candidate_audit(self.row,self.products,obs)
  self.assertEqual(x['versionObserved'],'V2')
  self.assertEqual(x['category'],'MANUAL_COMPARISON_READY')
  self.assertEqual(x['languageAvailability'],'OFFERS_OBSERVED')
  self.assertFalse(x['printVerified'])
 def test_zero_offers_never_means_language_does_not_exist(self):
  obs={'url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/en/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Set/Caterpie-V1-Z1',
  'product_id':'479679','identity_confirmed':True,'status':'FILTER_UNVERIFIED_EMPTY','verified_lang_nm_offers':0}
  x=candidate_audit(self.row,self.products,obs)
  self.assertEqual(x['languageAvailability'],'UNKNOWN_NOT_PROVEN_ABSENT')
 def test_wrong_product_id_version_is_not_proof(self):
  obs={'url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Set/Caterpie-V2-Z1',
  'product_id':'111111','identity_confirmed':True,'status':'FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE','verified_lang_nm_offers':1}
  x=candidate_audit(self.row,self.products,obs)
  self.assertEqual(x['category'],'AUTO_VERSION_DISCOVERY')
  self.assertIsNone(x['versionObserved'])
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 097/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_variant_resolver_production.py
############################################################################################

"""Safety regressions for user-supplied variant-resolver design."""
import importlib.util
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
p=ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket_variant_resolver.py'
s=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('resolver_prod',p)
m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(s)
import sys;sys.modules[s.name]=m;s.loader.exec_module(m)

class ResolverTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def product(self,**extra):
        args=dict(product_id='836551',set_slug='White-Flare-Additionals',
          product_url='https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/White-Flare-Additionals/Joltik-V2-xWHT033',
          number='033',title='Wattzapf [Surprise Attack]',
          version='V2',price_trend=7.58,confirmed_product_page=True)
        args.update(extra);return m.Product(**args)
    def test_v2_and_high_price_not_masterball_proof(self):
        p=self.product()
        x=m.classify_product(p,valid_set_slugs={'White-Flare-Additionals'},matched_number='033')
        self.assertEqual(x.print_type,'unknown')
        self.assertEqual(x.confidence,'LOW')
        self.assertTrue(x.review_required)
    def test_hard_wrong_set_number_or_host(self):
        for props,slugs,number in [
            ({'set_slug':'White-Flare'}, {'White-Flare-Additionals'}, '033'),
            ({}, {'White-Flare-Additionals'}, '133'),
            ({'product_url':'https://evil.example/x'}, {'White-Flare-Additionals'}, '033'),
            ({'confirmed_product_page':False},{'White-Flare-Additionals'},'033')]:
            x=m.classify_product(self.product(**props),valid_set_slugs=slugs,matched_number=number)
            self.assertEqual(x.print_type,'unknown')
    def test_unambiguous_title_and_human_verified_roi(self):
        x=m.classify_product(self.product(version='Master Ball Foil'),valid_set_slugs={'White-Flare-Additionals'},matched_number='033')
        self.assertEqual((x.print_type,x.confidence),('masterball','HIGH'))
        x=m.classify_product(self.product(),valid_set_slugs={'White-Flare-Additionals'},matched_number='033',
            foil_image_evidence={'method':'phash','print':'masterball','subjectProductId':'836551','reviewer':'bot'})
        self.assertEqual(x.print_type,'unknown')
        x=m.classify_product(self.product(),valid_set_slugs={'White-Flare-Additionals'},matched_number='033',
            foil_image_evidence={'method':'human_verified_roi','print':'masterball','subjectProductId':'836551','reviewer':'human'})
        self.assertEqual(x.print_type,'masterball')
    def test_master_ball_trainer_name_is_not_masterball_foil(self):
        x=m.classify_product(self.product(name='Master Ball',title='Master Ball',version='V2'),
            valid_set_slugs={'White-Flare-Additionals'},matched_number='033')
        self.assertEqual(x.print_type,'unknown')
    def test_price_heuristic_does_not_classify(self):
        x=m.price_hint([self.product(product_id='111',price_trend=.21),
                        self.product(product_id='222',price_trend=7.58)])
        self.assertTrue(x['reviewSuggestion'])
        self.assertFalse(x['printVerified'])
        self.assertFalse(x['safeToAutoPublish'])
    def test_comment_regex_and_negations(self):
        self.assertEqual(m.classified_comment('Masterball near mint','masterball'),'positive')
        self.assertEqual(m.classified_comment('nur Pokéball','masterball'),'contradiction')
        self.assertEqual(m.classified_comment('no master ball','masterball'),'ambiguous')
        self.assertEqual(m.classified_comment('Pokeball / Masterball','masterball'),'contradiction')
        self.assertEqual(m.classified_comment('MB','masterball'),'missing')
        self.assertEqual(m.classified_comment('','masterball'),'missing')
        self.assertEqual(m.classified_comment('play mat new','stamp:play-pokemon'),'missing')
    def offer(self,oid,price,**kw):
        x=dict(offer_id=oid,product_id='836551',language='de',
               condition='NM',signed=False,altered=False,graded=False,
               bundle=False,is_reverse=True,comment='Master Ball',price_eur=price)
        x.update(kw);return x
    def price(self,offers,**kw):
        x=dict(operation='price',expected=dict(language='de',product_id='836551',
                 print='masterball',confirmed_print_identity=False),offers=offers,
                 pagination=dict(all_pages_fetched=True,capped=False,total_offers=len(offers)))
        x.update(kw);return x
    def test_unconfirmed_not_published_even_complete(self):
        x=m.price_diagnostic(self.price([self.offer('1',5),self.offer('2',8)]))
        self.assertEqual(x['accepted_offers'],2)
        self.assertFalse(x['safe_to_publish'])
    def test_complete_verified_exclusions_and_iqr(self):
        p=self.price([self.offer(str(i),v) for i,v in
                      enumerate([5.6,5.7,5.8,5.9,6,6.1,6.2,6.3,.02],1)])
        p['expected']['confirmed_print_identity']=True
        p['product_wide_trend']=7.4
        x=m.price_diagnostic(p)
        self.assertEqual(x['accepted_offers'],9)
        self.assertEqual(x['iqr_removed'],1)
        self.assertEqual(x['median_iqr_diagnostic_eur'],'5.95')
        self.assertTrue(x['safe_to_publish'])
    def test_incomplete_blocks_publication(self):
        p=self.price([self.offer('a',7),self.offer('b',7.8)])
        p['expected']['confirmed_print_identity']=True
        p['pagination']['all_pages_fetched']=False
        x=m.price_diagnostic(p)
        self.assertFalse(x['safe_to_publish'])
    def test_japanese_masterball_without_explicit_comment_unknown(self):
        p=self.price([self.offer('a',8,language='ja',comment=''),
                      self.offer('b',9,language='ja',comment='masterball holo')],
                     expected={'language':'ja','product_id':'836551','print':'masterball',
                               'confirmed_print_identity':True})
        x=m.price_diagnostic(p)
        self.assertEqual(x['accepted_offers'],1)
        self.assertEqual(x['rejected'].get('PRINT_COMMENT_MISSING'),1)
    def test_negative_listing_on_masterball_page(self):
        p=self.price([self.offer('a',.5,comment='only Pokeball reverse'),
                      self.offer('b',6.4)])
        x=m.price_diagnostic(p)
        self.assertEqual(x['accepted_offers'],1)
        self.assertEqual(x['rejected']['PRINT_COMMENT_CONTRADICTION'],1)
    def test_stamps_not_prematurely_accepted(self):
        p=self.price([self.offer('a',3,comment='play!')],expected={
           'language':'de','product_id':'836551','print':'stamp','confirmed_print_identity':False})
        self.assertEqual(m.price_diagnostic(p)['accepted_offers'],0)

if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 098/107: scripts/test_cardmarket_version_family_resolver.py
############################################################################################

"""Version-page resolution must never turn a reprint candidate into a priced print."""
import importlib.util
import json
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
p=ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-version-family-resolver.py'
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('cardmarket_version_family',p)
mod=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(mod)

def observed():
    def link(slug):
        return {'url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/'+slug+'?language=3',
                'text':slug}
    return {'href':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Cards/Joltik-Surprise-Attack/Versions',
            'challenge':False,'ready':'complete','heading':'Wattzapf - 3 versions',
            'anchors':[link('White-Flare/Joltik-V1-WHT033'),
                       link('White-Flare-Additionals/Joltik-V1-xWHT033'),
                       link('White-Flare-Additionals/Joltik-V2-xWHT033')]}

class VersionFamilyChecks(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_one_expansion_and_number_only_no_print_guess(self):
        x=mod.resolve_version_family(observed(),card_id='sv10.5w-033',
                  set_slug='White-Flare-Additionals',collector_suffix='xWHT033')
        self.assertEqual(x['totalVersionsObserved'],3)
        self.assertEqual(x['otherSetVersionsExcluded'],1)
        self.assertEqual([c['version'] for c in x['sameSetCardNumberCandidates']],['V1','V2'])
        self.assertFalse(x['safeToAutoPrice'])
        self.assertFalse(x['exactPrintVerified'])
        self.assertTrue(all(not v['productIdChecked'] and not v['priceVerified'] for v in x['sameSetCardNumberCandidates']))
    def test_id_requires_exact_live_product_page(self):
        obs=[{'url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/White-Flare-Additionals/Joltik-V1-xWHT033',
              'id':'836552','imgs':[{'src':'https://product-images.s3.cardmarket.com/51/xWHT/836552/836552.jpg'}]},
             {'url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Scarlet-Violet/Joltik-V2-xWHT033',
              'id':'836551'}]
        catalog={'836552':{'idExpansion':6198,'idMetacard':450173},
                 '836551':{'idExpansion':6198,'idMetacard':450173}}
        x=mod.resolve_version_family(observed(),card_id='sv10.5w-033',
                 set_slug='White-Flare-Additionals',collector_suffix='xWHT033',
                 observations=obs,catalog=catalog)
        ids=[z['productId'] for z in x['sameSetCardNumberCandidates']]
        self.assertEqual(ids,['836552',None])
        self.assertTrue(x['sameSetCardNumberCandidates'][0]['imageUrl'].endswith('/836552/836552.jpg'))
        self.assertFalse(x['allCandidatesHaveCheckedProductId'])
    def test_additional_expansion_can_be_included_without_collapsing_prints(self):
        x=mod.resolve_version_family(observed(),card_id='sv10.5w-033',
                set_slug='White-Flare-Additionals',collector_suffix='xWHT033',
                additional_print_groups=[{'setSlug':'White-Flare','collectorSuffix':'WHT033'}])
        self.assertEqual(x['candidateCount'],3)
        self.assertEqual(x['otherSetVersionsExcluded'],0)
        self.assertFalse(x['safeToAutoPrice'])
    def test_challenge_wrong_host_and_wrong_family_rejected(self):
        x=observed();x['challenge']=True
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):mod.resolve_version_family(x,card_id='x',set_slug='White-Flare',collector_suffix='WHT033')
        x=observed();x['href']='https://cardmarket.example/de/Pokemon/Cards/Joltik/Versions'
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):mod.resolve_version_family(x,card_id='x',set_slug='White-Flare',collector_suffix='WHT033')
    def test_real_observations_21_and_11_expected(self):
        results=json.loads((ROOT/'config/cardmarket-version-family-evidence.json').read_text())
        for card,count,ids in [('sv01-013',21,{'689763','691000'}),('sv10.5w-033',11,{'835967','836551','836552'})]:
            x=results[card]
            self.assertEqual(x['totalVersionsObserved'],count)
            self.assertEqual({p['productId'] for p in x['sameSetCardNumberCandidates']},ids)
            self.assertTrue(x['allCandidatesHaveCheckedProductId'])
            self.assertFalse(x['safeToAutoPrice'])
            self.assertTrue(all(p['imageUrl'].startswith('https://product-images.s3.cardmarket.com/') for p in x['sameSetCardNumberCandidates']))
    def test_admin_has_immutable_version_leads(self):
        src=(ROOT/'scripts/cardmarket-print-review-admin.py').read_text()
        self.assertIn('cardmarket-version-family-evidence.json',src)
        self.assertIn("family_observed.get('status')=='VERSION_FAMILY_PRINT_UNVERIFIED'",src)
        ui=(ROOT/'web/components/CardmarketReviewCard.tsx').read_text()
        self.assertIn('Drucktyp und Sprachpreis sind weiterhin unbestätigt',ui)

if __name__=='__main__':
    unittest.main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 099/107: scripts/test_import_cardmarket_browser_observations.py
############################################################################################

import importlib.util
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("cm_import",Path(__file__).with_name("import-cardmarket-browser-observations.py"))
module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(module)

class ImportTests(unittest.TestCase):
    queue=[{"cardyssey_karte":"mep-095","variante_id":"legacy:holo","cardmarket_ids":"895610"}]
    good={"card_id":"mep-095","variant_id":"legacy:holo","product_id":"895610",
          "status":"FILTERED_SAMPLE_REVIEW_NOT_MARKET_WIDE",
          "de_nm_offers":50,"sample_median_eur":0.15}
    def test_good_sample_is_staged_only(self):
        rows=module.select_observations([self.good],self.queue)
        self.assertEqual(len(rows),1)
        self.assertTrue(rows[0]["candidate"]["no_verified_print_identity"])
        self.assertIn("price_review_cases",module.sql_for(rows))
        self.assertNotIn("tcg_price_cache",module.sql_for(rows))
    def test_wrong_product_never_staged(self):
        self.assertEqual(module.select_observations([{**self.good,"product_id":"999"}],self.queue),[])
    def test_bad_price_not_staged(self):
        self.assertEqual(module.select_observations([{**self.good,"sample_median_eur":float("nan")}],self.queue),[])
    def test_new_complete_english_offer_is_staged_but_not_published(self):
        q=[{"cardyssey_karte":"mep-095","variante_id":"legacy:holo","cardmarket_ids":"895610","scan_tier":"2"}]
        obs={**self.good,"status":"COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED",
             "language":"en","verified_lang_nm_offers":26,"all_pages_complete":True,
             "access_mode":"AUTHENTICATED","sample_median_eur":1.86}
        rows=module.select_observations([obs],q)
        self.assertEqual(len(rows),1)
        self.assertEqual(rows[0]["language_code"],"en")
        self.assertTrue(rows[0]["candidate"]["all_pages_complete"])
        self.assertTrue(rows[0]["candidate"]["no_verified_print_identity"])
        self.assertNotIn("tcg_price_cache",module.sql_for(rows))
    def test_public_limited_never_looks_complete(self):
        obs={**self.good,"language":"de","access_mode":"PUBLIC_LIMITED",
             "verified_lang_nm_offers":50,"all_pages_complete":False}
        rows=module.select_observations([obs],self.queue)
        self.assertFalse(rows[0]["candidate"]["all_pages_complete"])
    def test_complete_status_requires_completeness_flag(self):
        obs={**self.good,"status":"COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED",
             "verified_lang_nm_offers":26,"all_pages_complete":False}
        self.assertEqual(module.select_observations([obs],self.queue),[])
    def test_duplicate_idempotent(self):
        self.assertEqual(len(module.select_observations([self.good,self.good],self.queue)),1)

if __name__=="__main__":unittest.main()

# DATEI 100/107: scripts/test_import_cardmarket_filtered_prices.py
############################################################################################

import importlib.util,json,tempfile,unittest
from datetime import datetime,timezone
from pathlib import Path
s=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('cmprice',Path(__file__).with_name('import-cardmarket-filtered-prices.py'))
m=importlib.util.module_from_spec(s);s.loader.exec_module(m)
class FilteredImportTests(unittest.TestCase):
 def setUp(self):
  self.t=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory();self.addCleanup(self.t.cleanup);self.p=Path(self.t.name)/'observations.jsonl'
  self.x={'language':'de','card_id':'a','variant_id':'v','product_id':'123',
   'url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Set/A-V1-N?language=3&minCondition=2&isReverseHolo=N&isFirstEd=N',
   'identity_confirmed':True,'status':'COMPLETE_FILTERED_ASKING_MEDIAN_PRINT_UNVERIFIED',
   'all_pages_complete':True,'median_all_offers_eur':1.25,'verified_lang_nm_offers':6,
   'expected_reverse_holo':'N','expected_first_edition':'N','seen_at':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}
 def run_case(self,**changes):
  a={**self.x,**changes};self.p.write_text(json.dumps(a)+'\n');return m.collect(self.p,'de')
 def test_full_explicit_filter_is_eligible_for_human_route_join(self):
  self.assertEqual(len(self.run_case()),1)
 def test_incomplete_market_sample_never_publishes(self):
  self.assertEqual(self.run_case(all_pages_complete=False),[])
 def test_missing_first_ed_filter_never_publishes(self):
  self.assertEqual(self.run_case(url=self.x['url'].split('&isFirstEd')[0]),[])
 def test_wrong_language_never_publishes(self):
  self.assertEqual(self.run_case(language='en'),[])
 def test_db_requires_human_print_validation(self):
  self.assertIn("source_evidence LIKE 'Human confirmed exact%'",m.SQL)
  self.assertIn('VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID',m.SQL)
if __name__=='__main__':unittest.main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 101/107: scripts/test_import_cardmarket_mapping_safe.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import importlib.util
import json
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("cm_import",Path(__file__).with_name("import-cardmarket-mapping-safe.py"))
module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(module)

class SafeMappingTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.tmp=tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(self.tmp.cleanup)
        self.path=Path(self.tmp.name)/"browser.jsonl"
        self.queue={("card-a","var-a"):{"cardmarket_ids":"1234,5678","known_url":""}}
    def test_verified_browser_identity_maps_only_source_evidence(self):
        rec={"card_id":"card-a","variant_id":"var-a","language":"de",
             "identity_confirmed":True,"product_id":"1234",
             "url":"https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/SomeSet/SomeCard",
             "status":"MAPPED_NO_PRICE_REQUIRED"}
        self.path.write_text(json.dumps(rec)+"\n")
        rows,reasons=module.collect([self.path],self.queue,"de")
        self.assertEqual(len(rows),1)
        self.assertEqual(rows[0]["product_id"],"1234")
    def test_candidate_mismatch_rejected(self):
        rec={"card_id":"card-a","variant_id":"var-a","language":"de",
             "identity_confirmed":True,"product_id":"9999",
             "url":"https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Set/Card"}
        self.path.write_text(json.dumps(rec)+"\n")
        rows,reasons=module.collect([self.path],self.queue,"de")
        self.assertEqual(rows,[])
        self.assertEqual(reasons["ID_NOT_IN_CANDIDATES"],1)
    def test_wrong_language_rejected(self):
        rec={"card_id":"card-a","variant_id":"var-a","language":"en",
             "identity_confirmed":True,"product_id":"1234",
             "url":"https://www.cardmarket.com/en/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Set/Card"}
        self.path.write_text(json.dumps(rec)+"\n")
        rows,_=module.collect([self.path],self.queue,"de")
        self.assertEqual(rows,[])
    def test_wrong_verified_set_slug_rejected(self):
        self.queue[('card-a','var-a')]['set_id']='base1'
        rec={'card_id':'card-a','variant_id':'var-a','language':'de',
             'identity_confirmed':True,'product_id':'1234',
             'url':'https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Expansion-Pack/Machamp-EXP'}
        self.path.write_text(json.dumps(rec)+'\n')
        rows,reasons=module.collect([self.path],self.queue,'de',{'base1':'Base-Set'})
        self.assertEqual(rows,[])
        self.assertEqual(reasons['WRONG_CARDMARKET_SET'],1)
    def test_no_browser_confirmation_rejected(self):
        rec={"card_id":"card-a","variant_id":"var-a","language":"de",
             "identity_confirmed":False,"product_id":"1234",
             "url":"https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Set/Card"}
        self.path.write_text(json.dumps(rec)+"\n")
        rows,_=module.collect([self.path],self.queue,"de")
        self.assertEqual(rows,[])
    def test_human_confirmed_missing_url_filled_only_for_matching_id(self):
        source=Path(__file__).with_name('import-cardmarket-mapping-safe.py').read_text()
        self.assertIn("p.verification_state='VERIFIED_PRODUCT_ID'",source)
        self.assertIn('p.provider_product_id=s.pid',source)
        self.assertIn("p.source_evidence LIKE 'Human confirmed exact%'",source)
    def test_protects_existing_verified_data(self):
        self.assertIn("verification_state='SOURCE_ONLY'",module.SQL)
        self.assertIn("primary_provider='CARDMARKET'",module.SQL)
        self.assertIn("ON CONFLICT (language_code,card_id,variant_id)",module.SQL)
if __name__=="__main__":
    unittest.main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 102/107: scripts/test_map_all_de_cardmarket_catalog.py
############################################################################################

import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
import sys
import unittest
sys.path.insert(0,str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
    "cardmarket_full_map",Path(__file__).with_name("map-all-de-cardmarket-catalog.py"))
module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(module)

class FullCatalogMapTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_unique_exact_name_in_verified_expansion(self):
        names={("1","pikachu"):[{"idProduct":123,"idExpansion":1,"name":"Pikachu"}]}
        ids,status=module.candidates_for_card(
            {"set_id":"base1","english_name":"Pikachu"},names,{"base1":"1"})
        self.assertEqual((ids,status),(["123"],"SINGLE_SET_PRODUCT"))

    def test_full_set_overlap_tie_does_not_choose_wrong_expansion(self):
        en=[{'set_id':'2019sm','name':n} for n in ('Caterpie','Pikachu','Eevee')]
        products=[{'idExpansion':2829,'name':n} for n in ('Caterpie','Pikachu','Eevee')]
        products += [{'idExpansion':3354,'name':n} for n in ('Caterpie','Pikachu','Eevee')]
        self.assertNotIn('2019sm',module.calculate_set_map(en,products))

    def test_set_consensus_uses_distinct_variant_product_ids_not_names(self):
        products=[{'idProduct':100+i,'idExpansion':42,'name':'Houndoom'} for i in range(10)]
        products += [{'idProduct':200+i,'idExpansion':99,'name':'Houndoom'} for i in range(10)]
        cards=[{'set_id':'hgss3','card_id':f'hgss3-{i}','name':'Houndoom',
                'variants':[{'cardmarketProductId':100+i}]} for i in range(10)]
        self.assertEqual(module.calculate_variant_set_consensus(cards,products),{'hgss3':'42'})
    def test_split_name_same_set_has_no_automatic_print_identity(self):
        cards=[{'set_id':'hgss3','card_id':'hgss3-5','local_id':'5','name':'Houndoom'},
               {'set_id':'hgss3','card_id':'hgss3-82','local_id':'82','name':'Houndoom'}]
        number_sets=__import__('collections').defaultdict(set)
        for card in cards:number_sets[(card['set_id'],module.sem_name(card['name']))].add(card['local_id'])
        self.assertEqual(len(number_sets[('hgss3','houndoom')]),2)
    def test_multiple_distinct_products_not_assumed_unique(self):
        names={("1","pikachu"):[{"idProduct":123},{"idProduct":456}]}
        ids,status=module.candidates_for_card(
            {"set_id":"base1","english_name":"Pikachu"},names,{"base1":"1"})
        self.assertEqual((ids,status),(["123","456"],"MULTIPLE_SET_PRODUCTS"))

    def test_no_verified_expansion_rejects_same_name(self):
        names={("1","pikachu"):[{"idProduct":123}]}
        ids,status=module.candidates_for_card(
            {"set_id":"unknown","english_name":"Pikachu"},names,{"base1":"1"})
        self.assertEqual((ids,status),([],"SET_NOT_VERIFIED"))

    def test_expansion_mapping_requires_overlap(self):
        en=[{"set_id":"set-a","name":"A"},{"set_id":"set-a","name":"B"},{"set_id":"set-a","name":"C"}]
        products=[{"idExpansion":10,"name":"A"},{"idExpansion":10,"name":"B"},
                  {"idExpansion":10,"name":"C"},{"idExpansion":20,"name":"A"}]
        self.assertEqual(module.calculate_set_map(en,products),{"set-a":"10"})

if __name__=="__main__":unittest.main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 103/107: scripts/test_prepare_cardmarket_browser_review.py
############################################################################################

import unittest
import importlib.util
from pathlib import Path
_spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("cardmarket_review", Path(__file__).with_name("prepare-cardmarket-browser-review.py"))
_module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_spec)
_spec.loader.exec_module(_module)
make_rows, triage = _module.make_rows, _module.triage


class ReviewTriageTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def example(self, **kwargs):
        row = {"set_code": "mep", "card_id": "mep-095", "variant_id": "legacy:holo",
               "english_name": "Lucario", "variant": {"type": "Holo"},
               "set_verified": True, "products": [{"id": 895610, "name": "Lucario"}],
               "unverified_identity_fields": ["LANGUAGE_UNVERIFIED"]}
        row.update(kwargs)
        return row

    def test_single_candidate_is_review_not_confirmed(self):
        row = make_rows([self.example()])[0]
        self.assertEqual(row["prioritaet"], "P2")
        self.assertEqual(row["status"], "ZU_PRUEFEN")
        self.assertFalse(row["manuell_bestaetigte_id"])

    def test_direct_external_id_is_prioritized_but_not_assigned(self):
        row = make_rows([self.example(exact_variant_provider_ids={"cardmarket": "895610"})])[0]
        self.assertEqual(row["prueffall"], "EXTERNAL_CARDMARKET_ID_IN_VERIFIED_SET")
        self.assertEqual(row["prioritaet"], "P1")
        self.assertEqual(row["manuell_bestaetigte_id"], "")

    def test_conflicting_direct_id_is_flagged(self):
        priority, action, _ = triage(self.example(exact_variant_provider_ids={"cardmarket": 123}))
        self.assertEqual((priority, action), ("P1", "EXTERNAL_CARDMARKET_ID_CONFLICT"))

    def test_stamped_single_candidate_not_simple_case(self):
        row = make_rows([self.example(variant={"type": "Holo", "stamps": ["Set-Logo"]})])[0]
        self.assertEqual(row["prioritaet"], "P3")

    def test_name_only_hints_never_become_candidates(self):
        row = make_rows([self.example(set_verified=False, products=[],
                      name_only_match_count=2, name_only_search_hints=[{"id": 123}])])[0]
        self.assertEqual(row["prueffall"], "NAME_ONLY_SEARCH_HINTS")
        self.assertEqual(row["produktkandidaten"], 0)
        self.assertEqual(row["namenstreffer_ids"], "123")

    def test_no_evidence_is_low_priority(self):
        self.assertEqual(triage(self.example(set_verified=False, products=[]))[0], "P5")

    def test_multiple_candidates(self):
        self.assertEqual(triage(self.example(products=[{"id": 1}, {"id": 2}]))[1], "MULTIPLE_SET_CANDIDATES")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 104/107: scripts/test_prioritize_cardmarket_scan_queue.py
############################################################################################

import importlib.util
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("cm_priority",Path(__file__).with_name("prioritize-cardmarket-scan-queue.py"))
module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(module)

class PriorityTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_missing_price_precedes_high_value(self):
        rows=[
          {"cardyssey_karte":"a","variante_id":"1","set_id":"one","prioritaet":"P2","sonderdruck":"","foil":"","stempel":""},
          {"cardyssey_karte":"b","variante_id":"2","set_id":"two","prioritaet":"P4","sonderdruck":"","foil":"","stempel":""},
          {"cardyssey_karte":"c","variante_id":"3","set_id":"three","prioritaet":"P2","sonderdruck":"Staff","foil":"","stempel":""}
        ]
        cache=[
          {"card_id":"a","variant_id":"1","status":"PRICE_FOUND","price":100,"source":"TCGCSV_TCGPLAYER"},
          {"card_id":"b","variant_id":"2","status":"NO_PRICE","price":0,"source":None},
          {"card_id":"c","variant_id":"3","status":"PRICE_FOUND","price":1,"source":"TCGDEX"}
        ]
        ordered=module.prioritize(rows,cache)
        self.assertEqual([x["cardyssey_karte"] for x in ordered],["b","a","c"])
        self.assertEqual([x["scan_tier"] for x in ordered],[0,1,2])
        self.assertEqual(ordered[0]["needs_price"],"YES")
    def test_priced_bulk_uses_price_feeds_not_browser(self):
        row={"cardyssey_karte":"bulk","variante_id":"v","set_id":"x",
             "sonderdruck":"","foil":"","stempel":""}
        cache=[{"card_id":"bulk","variant_id":"v","status":"PRICE_FOUND",
                "price":0.05,"source":"TCGDEX","rarity":"Häufig"}]
        self.assertEqual(module.prioritize([row],cache)[0]["scan_tier"],4)
    def test_empty_json_stamps_do_not_make_bulk_special(self):
        row={"cardyssey_karte":"bulk","variante_id":"v","set_id":"x",
             "sonderdruck":"","foil":"","stempel":"[]"}
        cache=[{"card_id":"bulk","variant_id":"v","status":"PRICE_FOUND",
                "price":0.05,"source":"TCGDEX","rarity":"Ungewöhnlich"}]
        out=module.prioritize([row],cache)[0]
        self.assertEqual(out["scan_tier"],4)
        self.assertEqual(out["needs_price"],"NO")
    def test_missing_bulk_stays_feed_only(self):
        row={"cardyssey_karte":"bulk","variante_id":"v","set_id":"x", "sonderdruck":"","foil":"","stempel":"[]"}
        cache=[{"card_id":"bulk","variant_id":"v","status":"NO_PRICE", "price":0, "rarity":"Häufig"}]
        out=module.prioritize([row],cache)[0]
        self.assertEqual(out['scan_tier'],4)
        self.assertEqual(out['needs_price'],'YES')

    def test_one_row_one_variant(self):
        rows=[{"cardyssey_karte":"a","variante_id":"1","set_id":"s","sonderdruck":"","foil":"","stempel":""},
              {"cardyssey_karte":"a","variante_id":"2","set_id":"s","sonderdruck":"","foil":"","stempel":""}]
        result=module.prioritize(rows,[])
        self.assertEqual(len(result),2)
        self.assertEqual({r["variante_id"] for r in result},{"1","2"})

if __name__=="__main__":unittest.main()

# DATEI 105/107: scripts/test_refresh_cardmarket_official_weekly.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
import importlib.util
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
    "weekly",Path(__file__).with_name("refresh-cardmarket-official-weekly.py"))
# Importing module is safe: argparse only runs in __main__.
mod=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(mod)

class OfficialGuideTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_30day_preferred(self):
        self.assertEqual(mod.value_for({"avg30":1.25,"trend":2.7},"trend"),(1.25,"avg30"))
    def test_holo_separate(self):
        self.assertEqual(mod.value_for({"avg30":1.25,"avg30-holo":3.5},"trend-holo"),(3.5,"avg30-holo"))
    def test_missing_uses_trend(self):
        self.assertEqual(mod.value_for({"avg30":None,"trend":2.1},"avg30"),(2.1,"trend"))
    def test_no_price_preserves_existing(self):
        self.assertEqual(mod.value_for({"avg30":None,"trend":None},"avg30"),(None,None))
    def test_special_print_not_written(self):
        rows=[{"card_id":"x","language":"de","variant_id":"v",
               "failure_detail":"Cardmarket product 123; key avg30; via refresh",
               "variant":{"id":"v","type":"normal","stamps":["staff"]},"old_price":5}]
        updates,stats=mod.prepare(rows,{"123":{"avg30":9}})
        self.assertEqual(updates,[])
        self.assertEqual(stats["ambiguous_variant"],1)
    def test_regular_print(self):
        rows=[{"card_id":"x","language":"en","variant_id":"v",
               "failure_detail":"Cardmarket product 123; key trend; via refresh",
               "variant":{"id":"v","type":"normal","stamps":[],"foil":None},"old_price":5}]
        updates,stats=mod.prepare(rows,{"123":{"avg30":9,"trend":4}})
        self.assertEqual(updates[0]["price"],9)
        self.assertEqual(stats["avg30"],1)

if __name__=="__main__":
    unittest.main()

# DATEI 106/107: scripts/test_verify_cardmarket_language_browser_smoke.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Regression tests: don't accept the wrong language or condition as pricing."""
import importlib.util
import json
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

module_path=Path(__file__).with_name("verify-cardmarket-language-browser-smoke.py")
spec=importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("language_probe",module_path)
module=importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(module)

def pair():
    def row(lang,price):
        language_id,display=module.LANGS[lang]
        return {"card":"test Mewtwo","lang":lang,"data":{
            "url":"https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Base-Set/Mewtwo-V1-BS10?language="+language_id+"&minCondition=2",
            "checked":["language["+language_id+"]"],"condition":"2","productId":"273705",
            "rows":6,"offers":[{"condition":"Near Mint","labels":[display],"price":price} for _ in range(6)]}}
    return [row("de","1.234,56 €"),row("en","1,234.56 €")]

class BrowserLanguageTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_separated_languages(self):
        x=module.check(pair())
        self.assertEqual(len(x),2)
        self.assertEqual(x[0]["entry_five_median_eur"],"1234.56")
        self.assertEqual(x[1]["entry_five_median_eur"],"1234.56")
    def test_reject_mixed_language(self):
        rows=pair()
        rows[0]["data"]["checked"].append("language[1]")
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):module.check(rows)
    def test_reject_language_in_offer(self):
        rows=pair()
        rows[1]["data"]["offers"][0]["labels"]=["Deutsch"]
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):module.check(rows)
    def test_reject_no_committed_query(self):
        rows=pair()
        rows[0]["data"]["url"]="https://www.cardmarket.com/de/Pokemon/Products/Singles/Base-Set/Mewtwo-V1-BS10"
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):module.check(rows)
    def test_reject_bad_condition(self):
        rows=pair()
        rows[0]["data"]["offers"][0]["condition"]="Excellent"
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):module.check(rows)
    def test_reject_product_drift(self):
        rows=pair()
        rows[1]["data"]["productId"]="999999"
        with self.assertRaises(ValueError):module.check(rows)
if __name__=="__main__":unittest.main()


############################################################################################
# DATEI 107/107: scripts/verify-cardmarket-language-browser-smoke.py
############################################################################################

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Validate the DE/EN Cardmarket browser smoke-test. No price cache writes.

A correct product URL and checkbox are not enough: every sampled offer must
explicitly display the requested language and at least Near Mint.
The metric is the median of the five lowest *asking* prices, not a sold price.
"""
import argparse
import json
import re
import statistics
from decimal import Decimal
from pathlib import Path

DEFAULT=Path("/mnt/c/Users/TV/AppData/Local/Cardyssey/CardmarketCollector/language-nm-three-cards-result.json")
REPORT=Path("/home/tv/cardshop/reports/cardmarket-language-smoke-results.json")
LANGS={"de":("3","Deutsch"),"en":("1","Englisch")}

def euros(text):
    m=re.search(r"\d[\d., ]*",str(text or ""))
    if not m:raise ValueError("missing offer price")
    raw=m.group().strip().replace(" ","")
    if "." in raw and "," in raw:
        if raw.rfind(",")>raw.rfind("."):raw=raw.replace(".","").replace(",",".")
        else:raw=raw.replace(",","")
    elif "," in raw:
        if re.search(r",\d{1,2}$",raw):raw=raw.replace(",",".")
        else:raw=raw.replace(",","")
    elif "." in raw and re.fullmatch(r"\d{1,3}(?:\.\d{3})+",raw):
        raw=raw.replace(".","")
    amount=Decimal(raw)
    if not amount.is_finite() or amount<=0:raise ValueError("invalid offer price")
    return amount

def check(rows):
    entries={}
    for row in rows:
        if "data" not in row:raise ValueError("browser probe failed")
        data=row["data"]
        language=row["lang"]
        if language not in LANGS:raise ValueError("unexpected language")
        code,display=LANGS[language]
        if data.get("checked")!=[f"language[{code}]"]:
            raise ValueError("mixed or missing language checkbox")
        if str(data.get("condition"))!="2":
            raise ValueError("wrong condition filter")
        from urllib.parse import urlsplit,parse_qs
        query=parse_qs(urlsplit(data.get("url","")).query)
        if query.get("language")!=[code] or query.get("minCondition")!=["2"]:
            raise ValueError("filtered URL not committed")
        pid=str(data.get("productId") or "")
        if not pid.isdigit():raise ValueError("no actual product id")
        samples=data.get("offers") or []
        if len(samples)<5:raise ValueError("too few verified rows")
        five=[]
        for offer in samples:
            if display not in offer.get("labels",[]):
                raise ValueError(f"offer language mismatch for {language}")
            if offer.get("condition") not in ("Mint","Near Mint"):
                raise ValueError("offer worse than NM")
            five.append(euros(offer.get("price")))
        five=sorted(five)[:5]
        key=(row["card"],language)
        if key in entries:raise ValueError("duplicate card-language")
        entries[key]={"card":row["card"],"language":language,"product_id":pid,
            "visible_offers":int(data.get("rows",0)),"verified_sample_rows":len(samples),
            "lowest_ask_eur":str(five[0].quantize(Decimal("0.01"))),
            "entry_five_median_eur":str(statistics.median(five).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))),
            "basis":"active_asking_5_lowest_visible_offers_NOT_SOLD_OR_NM_ONLY",
            "printing_verified":False,"market_wide":False}
    cards={c for c,_ in entries}
    for card in cards:
        if (card,"de") not in entries or (card,"en") not in entries:
            raise ValueError("missing language")
        if entries[(card,"de")]["product_id"]!=entries[(card,"en")]["product_id"]:
            raise ValueError("different product IDs for language pair")
    return sorted(entries.values(),key=lambda x:(x["card"],x["language"]))

def main():
    p=argparse.ArgumentParser()
    p.add_argument("--input",type=Path,default=DEFAULT)
    p.add_argument("--report",type=Path,default=REPORT)
    args=p.parse_args()
    result=check(json.loads(args.input.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")))
    args.report.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True)
    args.report.write_text(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+"\n",encoding="utf-8")
    print(json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2))
if __name__=="__main__":main()
